/**
 * MockLlm: khÃ´ng gá»i máº¡ng; láº¥y output tá»« req.mockResponse() rá»“i váº«n validate báº±ng req.parse
 * â‡’ cháº¡y Ä‘Æ°á»£c toÃ n bá»™ luá»“ng LLM khi chÆ°a cÃ³ API key, vÃ  báº¯t lá»—i schema ngay trong cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock.
 */
import { providersConfig } from '../../shared/config'
import type { LlmJsonRequest, LlmJsonResult, LlmProvider } from '../providers/interfaces'

export class MockLlmProvider implements LlmProvider {
  readonly id = 'mock'
  readonly model = providersConfig.llm.mock.model

  async generateJson<T>(req: LlmJsonRequest<T>): Promise<LlmJsonResult<T>> {
    if (req.signal?.aborted) throw new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError')
    if (!req.mockResponse) throw new Error(`Mock LLM chÆ°a cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u máº«u cho tÃ¡c vá»¥ "${req.task}"`)
    const data = req.parse(req.mockResponse())
    return { data, inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0, cost: 0 }
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\llm\openaiLlm.ts ===
/**
 * LlmProvider cho OpenAI Chat Completions â€“ output luÃ´n lÃ  JSON (response_format json_object),
 * validate báº±ng hÃ m parse (zod). Sai schema â‡’ gá»­i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lá»—i láº¡i cho model vÃ  thá»­ láº¡i.
 */
import { providersConfig, type LlmConfig } from '../../shared/config'
import { logger } from '../logger'
import { fetchWithRetry } from '../providers/http'
import type { LlmJsonRequest, LlmJsonResult, LlmProvider } from '../providers/interfaces'

interface ChatResponse {
  choices: { message: { content: string | null } }[]
  usage?: { prompt_tokens: number; completion_tokens: number }
}

type ContentPart = { type: 'text'; text: string } | { type: 'image_url'; image_url: { url: string; detail: 'low' } }
type Msg = { role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant'; content: string | ContentPart[] }

export function describeZodLikeError(err: unknown): string {
  if (err && typeof err === 'object' && 'issues' in err && Array.isArray((err as { issues: unknown[] }).issues)) {
    return (err as { issues: { path: (string | number)[]; message: string }[] }).issues
      .slice(0, 8)
      .map((i) => `${i.path.join('.') || '(gá»‘c)'}: ${i.message}`)
      .join('; ')
  }
  return err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
}

export class OpenAiLlmProvider implements LlmProvider {
  readonly id = 'openai'
  readonly model: string
  private readonly cfg: LlmConfig & { endpoint: string }

  constructor(
    private readonly getKey: () => string | null,
    private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
    cfg = providersConfig.llm.openai
  ) {
    this.cfg = cfg
    this.model = cfg.model
  }

  async generateJson<T>(req: LlmJsonRequest<T>): Promise<LlmJsonResult<T>> {
    const key = this.getKey()
    if (!key) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ API key OpenAI. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: nháº­p key á»Ÿ mÃ n CÃ i Ä‘áº·t hoáº·c báº­t láº¡i Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock.')
    const messages: Msg[] = [
      { role: 'system', content: `${req.system}\n\nReturn ONLY one valid JSON object. No markdown, no commentary.` },
      {
        role: 'user',
        content: req.images?.length
          ? [
              { type: 'text', text: req.user },
              // Má»—i áº£nh cÃ³ nhÃ£n Ä‘á»©ng trÆ°á»›c Ä‘á»ƒ model biáº¿t áº£nh nÃ o lÃ  gÃ¬
              ...req.images.flatMap((im): ContentPart[] => [
                { type: 'text', text: `Image: ${im.label}` },
                { type: 'image_url', image_url: { url: `data:${im.mimeType};base64,${im.base64}`, detail: 'low' } }
              ])
            ]
          : req.user
      }
    ]
    const maxRetries = req.maxRetries ?? this.cfg.maxRetriesOnSchemaError
    let inTok = 0
    let outTok = 0
    let lastErr = ''

    for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
      const res = await fetchWithRetry(
        this.cfg.endpoint,
        {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            model: this.model,
            messages,
            temperature: req.temperature ?? this.cfg.temperature ?? 0.8,
            response_format: { type: 'json_object' }
          })
        },
        {
          maxAttempts: providersConfig.queue.maxAttempts,
          baseMs: providersConfig.queue.backoffBaseMs,
          maxMs: providersConfig.queue.backoffMaxMs,
          timeoutMs: this.cfg.timeoutMs ?? 180000,
          signal: req.signal,
          fetchImpl: this.fetchImpl,
          onRetry: (a, d, r) => logger.warn(`LLM ${req.task}: thá»­ láº¡i láº§n ${a} sau ${d}ms (${r})`, 'llm')
        }
      )
      const json = (await res.json()) as ChatResponse
      inTok += json.usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0
      outTok += json.usage?.completion_tokens ?? 0
      const content = json.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? ''
      try {
        const data = req.parse(JSON.parse(content))
        const cost = (inTok / 1000) * this.cfg.pricePer1kInputTokens + (outTok / 1000) * this.cfg.pricePer1kOutputTokens
        logger.info(`LLM ${req.task}: ${inTok}+${outTok} token, ~$${cost.toFixed(4)}`, 'llm')
        return { data, inputTokens: inTok, outputTokens: outTok, cost }
      } catch (err) {
        lastErr = describeZodLikeError(err)
        logger.warn(`LLM ${req.task}: JSON sai schema (láº§n ${attempt + 1}): ${lastErr}`, 'llm')
        messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content })
        messages.push({
          role: 'user',
          content: `Your JSON did not match the required schema: ${lastErr}. Return the FULL corrected JSON object only.`
        })
      }
    }
    throw new Error(`LLM tráº£ vá» JSON sai cáº¥u trÃºc sau ${maxRetries + 1} láº§n thá»­ (${req.task}): ${lastErr}`)
  }
}

/**

 * NguyÃªn táº¯c ná»™i dung (vÄƒn hoÃ¡, thÃ¢n thiá»‡n quáº£ng cÃ¡o, tháº­t/hÆ° cáº¥u) Ä‘Æ°á»£c nhÃºng vÃ o system prompt.
 */
import { z } from 'zod'

const SHOTS = ['wide', 'medium', 'closeUp', 'overShoulder', 'topDown', 'insert'] as const

const POSITIONS = ['left', 'center', 'right', 'background'] as const

export const PRINCIPLES = `You write for a YouTube/TikTok channel telling Vietnamese & Southeast Asian folk horror stories.
Hard rules:
- Respect beliefs: never mock religion, rituals, ancestors or folk practices.
- Clearly separate documented folklore from the channel's fiction. Never attach stories to real people, recent real tragedies or real specific places in a misleading way; use fictional village names.
- Advertiser-friendly horror: dread through atmosphere, darkness, sound, suggestion. No gore, no blood detail, no graphic violence, no self-harm detail.
- Visual culture is Vietnamese (Mekong delta / northern villages, stilt houses, altars, incense, Ã¡o bÃ  ba, banyan trees, oil lamps). Never Japanese/Chinese architecture, torii, kimono.
- Keep Vietnamese folklore terms in Vietnamese with diacritics (ma da, cÃ¢y Ä‘aâ€¦).`

const pad = (n: number, w = 2): string => String(n).padStart(w, '0')

export function slug(s: string): string {
  return (
    s
      .normalize('NFD')
      .replace(/[Ì€-Í¯]/g, '')
      .replace(/Ä‘/g, 'd')
      .replace(/Ä/g, 'D')
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
      .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
      .slice(0, 32) || 'x'
  )
}

function folkloreBlock(folklore: FolkloreEntry[]): string {
  return folklore
    .map(
      (f) =>
        `- ${f.nameVi} / ${f.nameEn} (${f.region}) â€“ ${f.isDocumentedFolklore ? 'documented folklore' : 'channel fiction'}\n  ${f.summaryEn}\n  Fixed visual design: ${f.visualDesign}`
    )
    .join('\n')
}

function characterBlock(chars: Character[]): string {
  return chars
    .map(
      (c) =>
        `- id="${c.id}" | ${c.name} | role=${c.role} | scope=${c.scope}${c.age ? ` | age ${c.age}` : ''} | ${c.appearance} | outfit: ${c.defaultOutfit}` +
        (c.states.length ? ` | states: ${c.states.map((s) => `${s.id}="${s.name}"`).join(', ')}` : '')
    )
    .join('\n')
}

function beatBlock(beats: Beat[]): string {
  return beats
    .map((b) => `- beatId="${b.id}" order=${b.order} intensity=${b.emotionalIntensity}: ${b.summaryEn} / ${b.summaryVi}`)
    .join('\n')
}

export const OutlineSchema = z.object({
  fictionNoteVi: z.string().min(10),
  beats: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        summaryVi: z.string().min(5),
        summaryEn: z.string().min(5),
        emotionalIntensity: z.number().min(0).max(1),
        mustShow: z.array(z.string()).max(6)
      })
    )
    .min(5)
    .max(10)
})
export type OutlineOut = z.infer<typeof OutlineSchema>

export function outlineTask(story: Story, folklore: FolkloreEntry[]): LlmJsonRequest<OutlineOut> {
  const name = folklore[0]?.nameVi ?? 'thá»±c thá»ƒ'

    user: `Folklore:\n${folkloreBlock(folklore)}\n\nPremise (Vietnamese): ${story.premiseVi}\n\nReturn JSON: {"fictionNoteVi": string, "beats": [{"summaryVi": string, "summaryEn": string, "emotionalIntensity": number, "mustShow": string[]}]}`,
    parse: (raw) => OutlineSchema.parse(raw),
    mockResponse: () => ({

export function toBeats(out: OutlineOut): Beat[] {
  return out.beats.map((b, i) => ({
    id: `b${i + 1}`,
    order: i + 1,
    summaryVi: b.summaryVi,
    summaryEn: b.summaryEn,
    emotionalIntensity: Math.round(b.emotionalIntensity * 100) / 100,
    mustShow: b.mustShow
  }))
}

// ============================================================================
// 2. DÃ€N NHÃ‚N Váº¬T Tá»ª DÃ€N Ã (bÆ°á»›c 3a)
// ============================================================================

export const CastSchema = z.object({
  characters: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        name: z.string().min(1),
        role: z.enum(['character', 'entity']),
        age: z.number().int().min(1).max(120).nullable(),
        gender: z.enum(['female', 'male', 'other', 'unknown']),
        appearance: z.string().min(10),
        defaultOutfit: z.string().min(3),
        description: z.string().min(5),
        expressions: z.array(z.string()).max(6),
        states: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string().min(1), description: z.string().min(3) })).max(5)
      })
    )
    .min(1)
    .max(6)
})
export type CastOut = z.infer<typeof CastSchema>

export function castTask(story: Story, folklore: FolkloreEntry[], channelChars: Character[]): LlmJsonRequest<CastOut> {
  const entity = folklore[0]
  return {
    task: 'cast',
    system: `${PRINCIPLES}\nYou are a character designer. From the outline, propose the story's cast: 2â€“4 human characters (main + supporting) and the supernatural entity (role "entity", based on the folklore's fixed visual design but customised to this story). For each: Vietnamese name, age, gender, appearance (face, body, hair) and default outfit appropriate to the Vietnamese setting and era, a short description, 2â€“4 key expressions, and appearance states needed by the plot (e.g. "Æ°á»›t sÅ©ng", "bá»‹ thÆ°Æ¡ng nháº¹", "bá»‹ nháº­p"). Write descriptions in Vietnamese. Do NOT include the channel narrator (already exists).`,
    user: `Folklore:\n${folkloreBlock(folklore)}\n\nChannel characters (do not repeat):\n${characterBlock(channelChars)}\n\nPremise: ${story.premiseVi}\n\nOutline:\n${beatBlock(story.outline.beats)}\n\nReturn JSON: {"characters":[{"name","role","age","gender","appearance","defaultOutfit","description","expressions":[],"states":[{"name","description"}]}]}`,
    parse: (raw) => CastSchema.parse(raw),
    mockResponse: () => ({
      characters: [
        { name: 'Tháº£o', role: 'character', age: 19, gender: 'female', appearance: 'CÃ´ gÃ¡i gáº§y, tÃ³c dÃ i buá»™c tháº¥p, máº¯t má»™t mÃ­, da ngÄƒm.', defaultOutfit: 'Ão bÃ  ba tráº¯ng, quáº§n Ä‘en, nÃ³n lÃ¡.', description: 'NhÃ¢n váº­t chÃ­nh, gan dáº¡ nhÆ°ng Ä‘a nghi.', expressions: ['bÃ¬nh thÆ°á»ng', 'sá»£ hÃ£i', 'quyáº¿t tÃ¢m'], states: [{ name: 'Æ°á»›t sÅ©ng', description: 'TÃ³c vÃ  Ã¡o Æ°á»›t Ä‘áº«m.' }] },
        { name: 'Ã”ng Báº£y', role: 'character', age: 72, gender: 'male', appearance: 'Ã”ng cá»¥ gáº§y, rÃ¢u báº¡c lÆ°a thÆ°a, lÆ°ng hÆ¡i cÃ²ng.', defaultOutfit: 'Ão bÃ  ba nÃ¢u, khÄƒn ráº±n quáº¥n cá»•.', description: 'NgÆ°á»i giÃ  trong xÃ³m, hiá»ƒu chuyá»‡n tÃ¢m linh.', expressions: ['nghiÃªm', 'lo Ã¢u'], states: [] },
        { name: entity ? `${entity.nameVi} cá»§a truyá»‡n` : 'Thá»±c thá»ƒ', role: 'entity', age: null, gender: 'unknown', appearance: entity?.visualDesign ?? 'KhÃ´ng lá»™ toÃ n thÃ¢n, chá»‰ tháº¥y bÃ³ng tá»‘i vÃ  chi tiáº¿t nhá».', defaultOutfit: 'KhÃ´ng rÃµ â€“ chÃ¬m trong bÃ³ng tá»‘i.', description: 'Thá»±c thá»ƒ cá»§a truyá»‡n, chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c gá»£i qua bÃ³ng tá»‘i vÃ  chi tiáº¿t nhá».', expressions: [], states: [{ name: 'chá»‰ tháº¥y bÃ³ng', description: 'Chá»‰ tháº¥y bÃ³ng má».' }] }
      ]
    })
  }
}

export function toCharacters(out: CastOut, storyId: string, existingIds: Set<string>): Character[] {
  const short = slug(storyId.replace(/^story-/, '')).slice(0, 12)
  return out.characters.map((c) => {
    let id = `char-${short}-${slug(c.name)}`
    let k = 2
    while (existingIds.has(id)) id = `char-${short}-${slug(c.name)}-${k++}`
    existingIds.add(id)
    return {
      id,
      name: c.name,
      role: c.role,
      scope: 'story',
      storyId,
      age: c.age,
      gender: c.gender,
      appearance: c.appearance,
      defaultOutfit: c.defaultOutfit,
      description: c.description,
      referenceImages: [],
      characterSheet: { front: null, threeQuarter: null, profile: null, fullBody: null },
      expressionSheet: c.expressions.map((e) => ({ expression: e, prompt: e, imagePath: null })),
      states: c.states.map((s, i) => ({ id: `st-${slug(c.name)}-${i + 1}`, name: s.name, description: s.description, referenceImage: null })),
      voiceProfileIdByLanguage: {},
      locked: false,
      approval: { status: 'pending' }
    }
  })
}

// ============================================================================
// 3. NGÃ‚N HÃ€NG Cáº¢NH THEO BEAT (bÆ°á»›c 4)
// ============================================================================

const CastMemberSchema = z.object({
  characterId: z.string(),
  stateId: z.string().nullable(),
  expression: z.string(),
  position: z.enum(POSITIONS),
  action: z.string()
})

export const SceneBankSchema = z.object({
  scenes: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        beatId: z.string(),
        description: z.string().min(5),
        shotType: z.enum(SHOTS),
        cameraAngle: z.string(),
        imagePrompt: z.string().min(10),
        focusX: z.number().min(0).max(1),
        focusY: z.number().min(0).max(1),
        needsVerticalVersion: z.boolean(),
        cast: z.array(CastMemberSchema).max(3)
      })
    )
    .min(4)
    .max(40)
})
export type SceneBankOut = z.infer<typeof SceneBankSchema>

export function sceneBankTask(story: Story, chars: Character[], channel: ChannelPreset): LlmJsonRequest<SceneBankOut> {
  const beatIds = new Set(story.outline.beats.map((b) => b.id))
  const byId = new Map(chars.map((c) => [c.id, c]))
  const narrator = chars.find((c) => c.scope === 'channel')
  const parse = (raw: unknown): SceneBankOut => {
    const out = SceneBankSchema.parse(raw)
    for (const [i, s] of out.scenes.entries()) {
      if (!beatIds.has(s.beatId)) throw new Error(`scenes[${i}].beatId "${s.beatId}" khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i`)
      for (const m of s.cast) {
        const c = byId.get(m.characterId)
        if (!c) throw new Error(`scenes[${i}].cast characterId "${m.characterId}" khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i`)
        if (m.stateId && !c.states.some((st) => st.id === m.stateId)) throw new Error(`scenes[${i}] stateId "${m.stateId}" khÃ´ng thuá»™c ${c.id}`)
      }
    }
    return out
  }
  return {
    task: 'sceneBank',
    system: `${PRINCIPLES}\nYou are a storyboard artist. Propose a scene bank: 2â€“3 scenes per beat. Diversity rule: vary shotType (wide, medium, closeUp, overShoulder, topDown, insert) and camera angle; never two consecutive scenes with the same shotType. Max 2â€“3 clearly visible characters per scene (use cast with exact characterId and optional stateId). Frightening entities are suggested through darkness, shadows, small details â€“ never fully shown, no gore. imagePrompt in English describes only the scene action/composition (art style is added later). focusX/focusY (0..1) mark the main subject for vertical cropping. Set needsVerticalVersion=true for key scenes that need a dedicated 9:16 composition. ${narrator ? `Also add exactly 2 scenes of the channel narrator (characterId "${narrator.id}") for intro/transition/outro, attached to the 2nd and last beat.` : ''}`,
    user: `Visual bible: ${channel.culturalVisualBible.join('; ')}\n\nCharacters:\n${characterBlock(chars)}\n\nOutline:\n${beatBlock(story.outline.beats)}\n\nReturn JSON: {"scenes":[{"beatId","description" (Vietnamese),"shotType","cameraAngle","imagePrompt","focusX","focusY","needsVerticalVersion","cast":[{"characterId","stateId","expression","position","action"}]}]}`,
    parse,
    mockResponse: () => {
      const story_chars = chars.filter((c) => c.scope === 'story')
      const human = story_chars.find((c) => c.role === 'character')
      const entity = story_chars.find((c) => c.role === 'entity')
      const scenes: unknown[] = []
      let k = 0
      for (const b of story.outline.beats) {
        for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
          const shot = SHOTS[k % SHOTS.length]
          const who = j === 0 ? human : b.emotionalIntensity >= 0.7 ? entity : undefined
          scenes.push({
            beatId: b.id,
            description: `${j === 0 ? 'Cáº£nh chÃ­nh' : 'Cáº£nh phá»¥'} â€“ ${b.summaryVi.slice(0, 80)}`,
            shotType: shot,
            cameraAngle: j === 0 ? 'eye level' : 'low angle',
            imagePrompt: `${b.summaryEn} â€“ ${shot} shot`,
            focusX: 0.4 + (k % 3) * 0.1,
            focusY: 0.45,
            needsVerticalVersion: b.emotionalIntensity >= 0.9,
            cast: who ? [{ characterId: who.id, stateId: null, expression: who.expressionSheet[0]?.expression ?? '', position: 'center', action: '' }] : []
          })
          k++
        }
      }
      if (narrator) {
        const beats = story.outline.beats
        for (const b of [beats[1] ?? beats[0], beats[beats.length - 1]]) {
          scenes.push({
            beatId: b.id,
            description: `${narrator.name} ká»ƒ chuyá»‡n bÃªn Ä‘Ã¨n dáº§u`,
            shotType: 'medium',
            cameraAngle: 'eye level',
            imagePrompt: 'the channel storyteller sitting by an oil lamp on a stilt-house porch at night',
            focusX: 0.5,
            focusY: 0.42,
            needsVerticalVersion: false,
            cast: [{ characterId: narrator.id, stateId: null, expression: narrator.expressionSheet[0]?.expression ?? '', position: 'center', action: 'ká»ƒ chuyá»‡n' }]
          })
        }
      }
      return { scenes }
    }
  }
}

export function toSceneBank(out: SceneBankOut): SceneBankItem[] {
  return out.scenes.map((s, i) => ({
    id: `s${pad(i + 1)}`,
    beatId: s.beatId,
    description: s.description,
    shotType: s.shotType as ShotType,
    cameraAngle: s.cameraAngle,
    cast: s.cast,
    folkloreIds: [],
    expression: s.cast[0]?.expression ?? '',
    imagePrompt: s.imagePrompt,
    needsVerticalVersion: s.needsVerticalVersion,
    media: { '16:9': null, '9:16': null },
    focusPoint: { x: s.focusX, y: s.focusY },
    depthMapPath: null,
    heroClip: { '16:9': null, '9:16': null },
    variants: [],
    approval: { status: 'pending' }
  }))
}

// ============================================================================
// 4. CHUYá»‚N THá»‚ Ká»ŠCH Báº¢N THEO RELEASE (bÆ°á»›c 8)
// ============================================================================

const LineSchema = z.object({
  sectionKind: z.enum(KINDS),
  beatId: z.string(),
  speakerId: z.string(),
  text: z.string().min(1),
  translationVi: z.string().optional(),
  tags: z.array(z.string()).max(4),
  instructions: z.string(),
  pace: z.number().min(0.6).max(1.3),
  pauseBeforeMs: z.number().int().min(0).max(3000),
  pauseAfterMs: z.number().int().min(0).max(3000)
})
export type RawScriptLine = z.infer<typeof LineSchema>

export const ScriptSchema = z.object({
  sections: z.array(z.object({ kind: z.enum(KINDS), title: z.string().min(1), titleVi: z.string().optional() })).min(1),
  lines: z.array(LineSchema).min(3)
})
export type ScriptOut = z.infer<typeof ScriptSchema>

export interface AdaptInput {
  story: Story
  folklore: FolkloreEntry[]
  characters: Character[]
  narratorId: string
  platform: Platform
  language: Language
  /** Cáº¥u trÃºc section yÃªu cáº§u (TikTok series: chá»‰ 'story', hook/cliffhanger thÃªm sau) */
  structure: ScriptSectionKind[]
  targetSec: number
  glossary: PronunciationEntry[]
}

const PLATFORM_GUIDE: Record<Platform, Record<Language, string>> = {
  youtube: {
    en: 'International English-speaking audience unfamiliar with Vietnamese culture. Long-form, slow, dark, atmospheric narration. coldOpen: a gripping 20â€“40s scene. context: the channel narrator welcomes and gives cultural background (explain Vietnamese terms once). story: the full tale. folkloreExplainer: what is documented folklore vs the channel\'s fiction. outro: the channel narrator closes gently (no begging for likes).',
    vi: 'KhÃ¡n giáº£ Viá»‡t trÃªn YouTube, video dÃ i, giá»ng ká»ƒ cháº­m, u Ã¡m. Má»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u cuá»‘n hÃºt, pháº§n bá»‘i cáº£nh do ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ cá»§a kÃªnh dáº«n, pháº§n giáº£i thÃ­ch truyá»n thuyáº¿t nÃªu rÃµ Ä‘Ã¢u lÃ  dÃ¢n gian, Ä‘Ã¢u lÃ  hÆ° cáº¥u.'
  },
  tiktok: {
    vi: 'KhÃ¡n giáº£ Viá»‡t trÃªn TikTok. Giá»ng ká»ƒ gáº§n gÅ©i kiá»ƒu "chuyá»‡n ká»ƒ Ä‘Ãªm khuya", cÃ¢u ngáº¯n, nhá»‹p nhanh, kháº©u ngá»¯ miá»n Nam vá»«a pháº£i. KHÃ”NG giáº£i thÃ­ch nhá»¯ng Ä‘iá»u khÃ¡n giáº£ Viá»‡t Ä‘Ã£ biáº¿t (ma da, cÃ¢y Ä‘a, tháº¯p nhangâ€¦).',
    en: 'English TikTok audience: short punchy sentences, immediate tension.'
  },
  youtubeShorts: {
    en: 'YouTube Shorts, English: hook in the first 2 seconds, fast pace, end on a hanging line.',
    vi: 'YouTube Shorts tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t: mÃ³c trong 2 giÃ¢y Ä‘áº§u, nhá»‹p nhanh, káº¿t lá»­ng.'
  }
}

export function scriptParser(input: AdaptInput): (raw: unknown) => ScriptOut {
  const beatIds = new Set(input.story.outline.beats.map((b) => b.id))
  const speakers = new Set(input.characters.map((c) => c.id))
  const allowedTags = new Set(providersConfig.tts.elevenlabs.audioTags ?? [])
  const structure = new Set(input.structure)
  return (raw) => {
    const out = ScriptSchema.parse(raw)
    out.sections = out.sections.filter((s, i, arr) => arr.findIndex((x) => x.kind === s.kind) === i)
    const sectionKinds = new Set(out.sections.map((s) => s.kind))
    for (const k of sectionKinds) if (!structure.has(k)) throw new Error(`section "${k}" khÃ´ng thuá»™c cáº¥u trÃºc ${[...structure].join(', ')}`)
    out.lines.forEach((l, i) => {
      if (!beatIds.has(l.beatId)) throw new Error(`lines[${i}].beatId "${l.beatId}" khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i`)
      if (!speakers.has(l.speakerId)) throw new Error(`lines[${i}].speakerId "${l.speakerId}" khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i`)
      if (!sectionKinds.has(l.sectionKind)) throw new Error(`lines[${i}].sectionKind "${l.sectionKind}" khÃ´ng cÃ³ trong sections`)
      if (input.language !== 'vi' && !l.translationVi) throw new Error(`lines[${i}] thiáº¿u translationVi`)
      // Bá» tag khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ thay vÃ¬ báº¯t LLM lÃ m láº¡i
      l.tags = l.tags.map((t) => t.replace(/[[\]]/g, '').trim().toLowerCase()).filter((t) => allowedTags.has(t))
    })
    return out
  }
}

export function adaptScriptTask(input: AdaptInput): LlmJsonRequest<ScriptOut> {
  const rate = providersConfig.tts.mock.unitsPerSecond[input.language]
  const units = Math.round(input.targetSec * rate * 0.85)
  const unitWord = input.language === 'vi' ? 'Vietnamese syllables' : 'English words'
  const tags = (providersConfig.tts.elevenlabs.audioTags ?? []).join(', ')
  const glossary = input.glossary.map((g) => `${g.term}${g.glossEn ? ` (${g.glossEn})` : ''}`).join('; ')
  const narrator = input.characters.find((c) => c.id === input.narratorId)
  const langName = input.language === 'vi' ? 'Vietnamese' : 'English'
  return {
    task: 'adaptScript',
    system: `${PRINCIPLES}
You are a scriptwriter ADAPTING (not translating) a story outline for one release.
Language: ${langName}. Platform: ${input.platform}. ${PLATFORM_GUIDE[input.platform][input.language]}
Section structure, in order: ${input.structure.join(' â†’ ')}.
Length: about ${input.targetSec} seconds of narration â‰ˆ ${units} ${unitWord} in total.
Speakers: the narrator (speakerId "${input.narratorId}"${narrator ? `, ${narrator.name}` : ''}) tells the story and speaks ALL intro/transition/outro lines. Characters may have SHORT dialogue lines (their own voice) interleaved with narration â€“ at most ~15% of lines. Use exact speakerId values from the list.
Every line has: beatId from the outline, delivery tags from [${tags}] (0â€“2 per line), short acting instructions in English, pace 0.8â€“1.1 (1 = normal), pauseBeforeMs / pauseAfterMs (use longer silences before reveals).
${input.language !== 'vi' ? 'Every line MUST include translationVi: a faithful Vietnamese translation for the Vietnamese reviewer.' : 'Do not include translationVi.'}
Keep lines short (one or two sentences) so each can be re-recorded independently.`,
    user: `Folklore:\n${folkloreBlock(input.folklore)}\n\nFiction note: ${input.story.fictionNoteVi}\nGlossary terms to keep in Vietnamese: ${glossary || '(none)'}\n\nSpeakers:\n${characterBlock(input.characters)}\n\nOutline:\n${beatBlock(input.story.outline.beats)}\n\nReturn JSON: {"sections":[{"kind","title","titleVi"}],"lines":[{"sectionKind","beatId","speakerId","text",${input.language !== 'vi' ? '"translationVi",' : ''}"tags":[],"instructions","pace","pauseBeforeMs","pauseAfterMs"}]}`,
    parse: scriptParser(input),
    mockResponse: () => mockScript(input)
  }
}

function mockScript(input: AdaptInput): ScriptOut {
  const vi = input.language === 'vi'
  const beats = input.story.outline.beats
  const human = input.characters.find((c) => c.scope === 'story' && c.role === 'character')
  const entity = input.characters.find((c) => c.role === 'entity')
  const titles: Record<ScriptSectionKind, [string, string]> = {
    hook: ['Hook', 'MÃ³c má»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u'],
    coldOpen: ['Cold open', 'Má»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u láº¡nh'],
    context: ['Background', 'Bá»‘i cáº£nh'],
    story: ['The story', 'CÃ¢u chuyá»‡n'],
    folkloreExplainer: ['About the legend', 'Vá» truyá»n thuyáº¿t'],
    cliffhanger: ['Cliffhanger', 'CÃ¢u treo'],
    outro: ['Outro', 'Káº¿t']
  }
  const sections = input.structure.map((k) => ({ kind: k, title: vi ? titles[k][1] : titles[k][0], titleVi: titles[k][1] }))
  const lines: RawScriptLine[] = []
  const add = (kind: ScriptSectionKind, beat: Beat, speakerId: string, text: string, textVi: string, tags: string[] = []): void => {
    lines.push({
      sectionKind: kind,
      beatId: beat.id,
      speakerId,
      text: vi ? textVi : text,
      ...(vi ? {} : { translationVi: textVi }),
      tags,
      instructions: 'Mock delivery',
      pace: 0.95,
      pauseBeforeMs: 250,
      pauseAfterMs: 450
    })
  }
  const first = beats[0]
  const last = beats[beats.length - 1]
  for (const kind of input.structure) {
    if (kind === 'story') {
      // Láº·p lá»i ká»ƒ theo beat cho tá»›i khi Ä‘á»§ Ä‘á»™ dÃ i má»¥c tiÃªu
      const rate = providersConfig.tts.mock.unitsPerSecond[input.language]
      const perBeatUnits = Math.max(15, Math.round((input.targetSec * rate * 0.6) / beats.length))
      for (const b of beats) {
        let units = 0
        let k = 0
        while (units < perBeatUnits) {
          const textVi = k === 0 ? b.summaryVi : `Chi tiáº¿t ${k + 1} cá»§a Ä‘oáº¡n nÃ y: khÃ´ng khÃ­ cÃ ng lÃºc cÃ ng náº·ng ná».`
          const text = k === 0 ? b.summaryEn : `Detail ${k + 1}: the air grows heavier with every breath.`
          add('story', b, input.narratorId, text, textVi, k === 0 ? ['softly'] : [])
          units += (vi ? textVi : text).split(/\s+/).length
          k++
        }
        if (b.emotionalIntensity >= 0.7 && entity) add('story', b, entity.id, 'Come to meâ€¦', 'Láº¡i Ä‘Ã¢yâ€¦', ['whispers'])
        else if (b.emotionalIntensity >= 0.45 && human) add('story', b, human.id, 'Who is there?', 'Ai Ä‘Ã³?', ['nervous'])
      }
    } else {
      const b = kind === 'outro' || kind === 'cliffhanger' || kind === 'folkloreExplainer' ? last : first
      const texts: Record<string, [string, string]> = {
        hook: ['If someone calls your name tonightâ€¦ do not answer.', 'Náº¿u Ä‘Ãªm nay cÃ³ ai gá»i tÃªn báº¡nâ€¦ Ä‘á»«ng tráº£ lá»i.'],
        coldOpen: ['Some nights, the darkness listens back.', 'CÃ³ nhá»¯ng Ä‘Ãªm, bÃ³ng tá»‘i cÅ©ng láº¯ng nghe.'],
        context: ['Come closer to the lamp. This story comes from a village by the river.', 'Láº¡i gáº§n ngá»n Ä‘Ã¨n Ä‘i. Chuyá»‡n nÃ y xáº£y ra á»Ÿ má»™t lÃ ng ven sÃ´ng.'],
        folkloreExplainer: [input.story.fictionNoteVi, input.story.fictionNoteVi],
        cliffhanger: ['But the next morningâ€¦ it was not over.', 'NhÆ°ng sÃ¡ng hÃ´m sauâ€¦ má»i chuyá»‡n chÆ°a háº¿t.'],
        outro: ['The lamp is low now. Sleep well, and stay away from the water.', 'ÄÃ¨n sáº¯p táº¯t rá»“i. Ngá»§ ngon nhÃ©.']
      }
      const [t, tv] = texts[kind] ?? ['â€¦', 'â€¦']
      add(kind, b, input.narratorId, t, tv, ['softly'])
    }
  }
  return { sections, lines }
}

// ============================================================================
// 5. MÃ“C Má»ž Äáº¦U + CÃ‚U TREO CHO Tá»ªNG PHáº¦N TIKTOK
// ============================================================================

const ShortLine = z.object({ text: z.string().min(1), tags: z.array(z.string()).max(3), instructions: z.string() })
export const HooksSchema = z.object({ parts: z.array(z.object({ hook: ShortLine, ending: ShortLine })).min(1) })
export type HooksOut = z.infer<typeof HooksSchema>

export function seriesHooksTask(
  language: Language,
  parts: { index: number; summary: string; firstLine: string; lastLine: string }[]
): LlmJsonRequest<HooksOut> {
  const n = parts.length
  const hookSec = platformsConfig.series.hookSec
  return {
    task: 'seriesHooks',
    system: `${PRINCIPLES}\nYou write hooks and endings for a ${n}-part ${language === 'vi' ? 'Vietnamese' : 'English'} TikTok series. For EACH part: "hook" = the very first line, readable in ${hookSec - 1}â€“${hookSec} seconds (â‰¤ 12 ${language === 'vi' ? 'syllables' : 'words'}), grabs attention immediately (parts > 1 may briefly recall the previous part). "ending" = the last line: a hanging line that makes viewers want the next part; for the final part, a chilling closing line instead. Do not say "Part X" in the text (a label is added on screen). Write in ${language === 'vi' ? 'Vietnamese, intimate late-night storytelling tone' : 'English'}.`,
    user: `Parts:\n${parts.map((p) => `Part ${p.index}/${n}: ${p.summary}\n  starts: "${p.firstLine}"\n  ends: "${p.lastLine}"`).join('\n')}\n\nReturn JSON: {"parts":[{"hook":{"text","tags":[],"instructions"},"ending":{"text","tags":[],"instructions"}}]} with exactly ${n} items.`,
    parse: (raw) => {

const CueSchema = z.object({
  kind: z.enum(['ambience', 'sfx', 'stinger', 'music', 'silence']),
  assetId: z.string().nullable(),
  start: z.number(),
  end: z.number(),
  volume: z.number().min(0).max(1.5),
  fadeIn: z.number().min(0).max(10),
  fadeOut: z.number().min(0).max(10),
  reason: z.string()
})
export const SoundDesignSchema = z.object({ cues: z.array(CueSchema).max(80) })

export function soundDesignTask(input: DesignInput, language: Language): LlmJsonRequest<SoundCue[]> {
  const lines = input.lines
    .map((l) => `[${l.start.toFixed(1)}â€“${l.end.toFixed(1)}] (${l.sectionKind}, beat ${l.beatId}, intensity ${input.beatIntensity(l.beatId).toFixed(2)}) ${l.text}`)
    .join('\n')
  const assets = input.assets
    .filter((a) => a.licenseConfirmed)
    .map((a) => `- id="${a.id}" kind=${a.kind} ${a.durationSec.toFixed(1)}s tags=[${a.tags.join(', ')}] "${a.name}"`)
    .join('\n')
  return {
    task: 'soundDesign',
    system: `You are a horror sound designer for a ${language === 'vi' ? 'Vietnamese' : 'English'} narrated folk-horror video. Place SoundCues using ONLY the listed assets (exact assetId, matching kind). Rules: one continuous ambience bed fitting the setting; optional very quiet music drone; SFX only at key story moments that the narration mentions (not every line, at least ~10s apart, at most one per ~15s, none in the first 2.5s); a stinger at the climax; an intentional "silence" cue (assetId null) of ~0.5â€“1.2s right before the biggest reveal; never cover the hook's first 2 seconds with loud sounds. Volumes are relative 0..1 (1 = normal for that kind). Keep ad-friendly: no screams or gore sounds. Times in seconds within 0..${input.duration.toFixed(1)}.`,
    user: `Sound palette tags: ambience=[${input.palette.ambienceTags.join(', ')}], sfx=[${input.palette.sfxTags.join(', ')}], stinger=[${input.palette.stingerTags.join(', ')}], music=[${input.palette.musicTags.join(', ')}]\n\nAvailable licensed assets:\n${assets || '(none)'}\n\nNarration timeline:\n${lines}\n\nReturn JSON: {"cues":[{"kind","assetId","start","end","volume","fadeIn","fadeOut","reason" (Vietnamese)}]}`,
    parse: (raw) => {
      const out = SoundDesignSchema.parse(raw)
      const cues: SoundCue[] = out.cues.map((c, i) => ({ id: `cue-${i + 1}`, ...c }))
      const v = validateCues(cues, input.assets, input.duration)
      if (v.problems.length) throw new Error(v.problems.slice(0, 5).join('; '))
      return v.cues
    },
    mockResponse: () => ({ cues: designSoundCues(input).map(({ id: _id, ...c }) => ({ ...c, reason: c.reason ?? '' })) })
  }
}

export const RewriteSchema = z.object({

export function rewriteLineTask(args: {
  language: Language
  platform: Platform
  before: string[]
  line: { text: string; speakerName: string; tags: string[] }
  after: string[]
  instruction: string
}): LlmJsonRequest<RewriteOut> {
  const allowed = new Set(providersConfig.tts.elevenlabs.audioTags ?? [])
  return {
    task: 'rewriteLine',
    system: `${PRINCIPLES}\nRewrite ONE script line for a ${args.platform} release in ${args.language === 'vi' ?       tags: args.line.tags,
      instructions: args.instruction || 'Mock rewrite'
    })
  }

=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\depth.ts ===
/**
 * Æ¯á»›c lÆ°á»£ng Ä‘á»™ sÃ¢u (depth map) cháº¡y LOCAL báº±ng onnxruntime-node â€“ model cáº¥u hÃ¬nh trong providers.json â†’ "depth".
 * ChÆ°a cÃ³ file model â‡’ dÃ¹ng depth Æ°á»›c lÆ°á»£ng (heuristic) Ä‘á»ƒ pipeline váº«n cháº¡y; UI cÃ³ nÃºt táº£i model.
 * onnxruntime cháº¡y suy luáº­n trÃªn luá»“ng native riÃªng (session.run lÃ  async) â‡’ khÃ´ng lÃ m Ä‘á»©ng UI.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs'
import fsp from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import sharp from 'sharp'
import { providersConfig, type DepthConfig } from '../../shared/config'
import { heuristicDepth, normalizeDepth } from '../../shared/logic/parallaxMath'
import { logger } from '../logger'

interface OrtSession {
  inputNames: readonly string[]
  outputNames: readonly string[]
  run(feeds: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Record<string, { data: Float32Array; dims: readonly number[] }>>
}
interface OrtModule {
  InferenceSession: { create(path: string): Promise<OrtSession> }
  Tensor: new (type: 'float32', data: Float32Array, dims: number[]) => unknown
}

export interface DepthResult {
  /** 0..1, 1 = gáº§n; kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c width Ã— height */
  depth: Float32Array
  width: number
  height: number
  source: 'model' | 'heuristic'
}

export class DepthEstimator {
  private session: Promise<OrtSession> | null = null
  private ort: OrtModule | null = null

  constructor(
    private readonly modelsDir: string,
    private readonly cfg: DepthConfig = providersConfig.depth
  ) {}

  get modelPath(): string {
    return path.join(this.modelsDir, this.cfg.modelFile)
  }

  hasModel(): boolean {
    try {
      return fs.statSync(this.modelPath).size > 1_000_000
    } catch {
      return false
    }
  }

  private async getSession(): Promise<OrtSession> {
    if (!this.session) {
      this.session = (async () => {
        // Náº¡p lÆ°á»i: náº¿u module native lá»—i thÃ¬ váº«n dÃ¹ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c depth Æ°á»›c lÆ°á»£ng
        this.ort = (await import('onnxruntime-node')).default as unknown as OrtModule
        const s = await this.ort.InferenceSession.create(this.modelPath)
        logger.info(`ÄÃ£ náº¡p model depth ${this.cfg.modelFile}`, 'depth')
        return s
      })()
      this.session.catch(() => (this.session = null))
    }
    return this.session
  }

  /** Depth á»Ÿ kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c widthÃ—height cho áº£nh Ä‘áº§u vÃ o */
  async estimate(image: Buffer, width: number, height: number, focus: { x: number; y: number }): Promise<DepthResult> {
    if (!this.hasModel()) return { depth: heuristicDepth(width, height, focus), width, height, source: 'heuristic' }
    try {
      const s = await this.getSession()
      const S = this.cfg.inputSize
      const { data } = await sharp(image).removeAlpha().resize(S, S, { fit: 'fill' }).raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true })
      const input = new Float32Array(3 * S * S)
      for (let i = 0; i < S * S; i++) {
        for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) input[c * S * S + i] = (data[i * 3 + c] / 255 - this.cfg.mean[c]) / this.cfg.std[c]
      }
      const out = await s.run({ [s.inputNames[0]]: new this.ort!.Tensor('float32', input, [1, 3, S, S]) })
      const t = out[s.outputNames[0]]
      const [oh, ow] = t.dims.slice(-2)
      const norm = normalizeDepth(t.data, this.cfg.higherIsNearer)
      // PhÃ³ng depth vá» kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c áº£nh (qua áº£nh xÃ¡m 8-bit, ná»™i suy cá»§a sharp)
      const gray = Buffer.alloc(norm.length)
      for (let i = 0; i < norm.length; i++) gray[i] = Math.round(norm[i] * 255)
      const up = await sharp(gray, { raw: { width: ow, height: oh, channels: 1 } }).resize(width, height, { fit: 'fill' }).extractChannel(0).raw().toBuffer()
      const depth = new Float32Array(width * height)
      for (let i = 0; i < depth.length; i++) depth[i] = up[i] / 255
      return { depth, width, height, source: 'model' }
    } catch (err) {
      logger.warn(`Model depth lá»—i (${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}) â€“ dÃ¹ng depth Æ°á»›c lÆ°á»£ng`, 'depth')
      return { depth: heuristicDepth(width, height, focus), width, height, source: 'heuristic' }
    }
  }

  /** Táº£i model vá» thÆ° má»¥c models (má»™t láº§n, ~27MB) */
  async download(onProgress: (pct: number) => void): Promise<string> {
    await fsp.mkdir(this.modelsDir, { recursive: true })
    const res = await fetch(this.cfg.downloadUrl)
    if (!res.ok || !res.body) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c model depth (HTTP ${res.status})`)
    const total = Number(res.headers.get('content-length') ?? 0)
    const tmp = `${this.modelPath}.part`
    const file = await fsp.open(tmp, 'w')
    let got = 0
    try {
      const reader = res.body.getReader()
      for (;;) {
        const { done, value } = await reader.read()
        if (done) break
        await file.write(value)
        got += value.length
        if (total) onProgress(got / total)
      }
    } finally {
      await file.close()
    }
    await fsp.rename(tmp, this.modelPath)
    this.session = null
    logger.info(`ÄÃ£ táº£i model depth (${(got / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB) â†’ ${this.modelPath}`, 'depth')
    return this.modelPath
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\ffmpeg.ts ===
/**
 * Gá»i ffmpeg/ffprobe (báº£n tÄ©nh Ä‘i kÃ¨m qua ffmpeg-static / ffprobe-static).
 * DÃ¹ng spawn vá»›i máº£ng tham sá»‘ (khÃ´ng qua shell) â‡’ Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n cÃ³ dáº¥u tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t, khoáº£ng tráº¯ng váº«n cháº¡y Ä‘Ãºng.
 */
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process'
import ffmpegStatic from 'ffmpeg-static'
import ffprobeStatic from 'ffprobe-static'
import type { VoiceProcessChain } from '../../shared/types'

/** Khi Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i (GÄ6) file nhá»‹ phÃ¢n náº±m trong app.asar.unpacked */
const unpacked = (p: string): string => p.replace('app.asar', 'app.asar.unpacked')

export function ffmpegPath(): string {
  const p = ffmpegStatic as unknown as string | null
  if (!p) throw new Error('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ffmpeg. HÃ£y cháº¡y láº¡i "npm install" Ä‘á»ƒ táº£i ffmpeg-static.')
  return unpacked(p)
}

export function ffprobePath(): string {
  return unpacked(ffprobeStatic.path)
}

/** Cháº¡y ffmpeg á»Ÿ má»©c log "info" vÃ  tráº£ vá» stderr (cáº§n Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘á»c káº¿t quáº£ Ä‘o loudnorm) */
export function runFfmpegCapture(args: string[], signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<string> {
  return run(ffmpegPath(), ['-hide_banner', '-nostats', '-loglevel', 'info', '-y', ...args], signal, true)
}

function run(bin: string, args: string[], signal?: AbortSignal, returnStderr = false): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal?.aborted) return reject(new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError'))
    const child = spawn(bin, args, { windowsHide: true })
    let stdout = ''
    let stderr = ''
    child.stdout.on('data', (d: Buffer) => (stdout += d.toString('utf8')))
    child.stderr.on('data', (d: Buffer) => {
      stderr += d.toString('utf8')
      if (stderr.length > 200000) stderr = stderr.slice(-100000)
    })
    const onAbort = (): void => {
      child.kill('SIGKILL')
    }
    signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
    child.on('error', (err) => {
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
      reject(err)
    })
    child.on('close', (code) => {
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
      if (signal?.aborted) return reject(new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError'))
      if (code === 0) resolve(returnStderr ? stderr : stdout)
      else reject(new Error(`${bin.split(/[\\/]/).pop()} lá»—i (mÃ£ ${code}): ${stderr.split('\n').slice(-6).join(' ').trim()}`))
    })
  })
}

export function runFfmpeg(args: string[], signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<string> {
  return run(ffmpegPath(), ['-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error', '-y', ...args], signal)
}

export async function probeDuration(file: string): Promise<number> {
  const out = await run(ffprobePath(), [
    '-v',
    'error',
    '-show_entries',
    'format=duration',
    '-of',
    'default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1',
    file
  ])
  const d = Number.parseFloat(out.trim())
  if (!Number.isFinite(d)) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng Ä‘á»c Ä‘Æ°á»£c thá»i lÆ°á»£ng: ${file}`)
  return d
}

/** Cáº¯t má»™t Ä‘oáº¡n audio cÃ³ fade vÃ o/ra (dÃ¹ng cho Shorts teaser); thÃªm khoáº£ng láº·ng á»Ÿ cuá»‘i cho tháº» káº¿t */
export async function cutAudio(src: string, dst: string, start: number, len: number, tailSec: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  const fo = Math.min(1.2, len / 3)
  await runFfmpeg(
    ['-ss', start.toFixed(3), '-t', len.toFixed(3), '-i', src, '-af', `afade=t=in:d=0.25,afade=t=out:st=${(len - fo).toFixed(3)}:d=${fo.toFixed(3)},apad=pad_dur=${tailSec.toFixed(2)}`, '-ar', '48000', '-ac', '2', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', dst],
    signal
  )
}

/** Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ má»i audio provider tráº£ vá» (mp3, wav 24kHzâ€¦) vá» WAV 44.1kHz mono 16-bit */
export async function toWav44kMono(src: string, dst: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  await runFfmpeg(['-i', src, '-ac', '1', '-ar', '44100', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', dst], signal)
}

/** Chuá»—i filter xá»­ lÃ½ giá»ng: lá»c táº§n tháº¥p â†’ nÃ©n â†’ EQ áº¥m â†’ reverb nháº¹ */
export function buildVoiceFilter(chain: VoiceProcessChain): string {
  const filters = [
    `highpass=f=${Math.max(20, Math.round(chain.highpassHz))}`,
    `acompressor=threshold=${chain.compressor.thresholdDb}dB:ratio=${chain.compressor.ratio}:attack=10:release=200`,
    `equalizer=f=220:t=q:w=1:g=${chain.warmthDb}`
  ]
  if (chain.reverbAmount > 0) {
    const decay = Math.min(0.6, Math.max(0.05, chain.reverbAmount)).toFixed(2)
    filters.push(`aecho=0.85:0.7:45|90:${decay}|${(Number(decay) * 0.6).toFixed(2)}`)
  }
  return filters.join(',')
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\mediaServer.ts ===
/**
 * HTTP server ná»™i bá»™ (chá»‰ 127.0.0.1, cá»•ng ngáº«u nhiÃªn, cÃ³ token bÃ­ máº­t trong URL) phá»¥c vá»¥ media cá»§a Story
 * cho Remotion Player (renderer) vÃ  Chrome headless (khi render).
 * Há»— trá»£ HTTP Range â‡’ tua audio/video mÆ°á»£t.
 */
import { createReadStream } from 'node:fs'
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import http from 'node:http'
import path from 'node:path'
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net'

const MIME: Record<string, string> = {
  '.png': 'image/png',
  '.jpg': 'image/jpeg',
  '.jpeg': 'image/jpeg',
  '.webp': 'image/webp',
  '.wav': 'audio/wav',
  '.mp3': 'audio/mpeg',
  '.m4a': 'audio/mp4',
  '.mp4': 'video/mp4',
  '.webm': 'video/webm',
  '.json': 'application/json'
}

export interface MediaServer {
  baseUrl: string
  close(): Promise<void>
}

export async function startMediaServer(getStoriesRoot: () => string): Promise<MediaServer> {
  const token = randomBytes(12).toString('hex')
  const prefix = `/m/${token}/`

  const server = http.createServer(async (req, res) => {
    try {
      const url = new URL(req.url ?? '/', 'http://127.0.0.1')
      if (!url.pathname.startsWith(prefix) || (req.method !== 'GET' && req.method !== 'HEAD')) {
        res.writeHead(404).end()
        return
      }
      const segments = url.pathname
        .slice(prefix.length)
        .split('/')
        .filter(Boolean)
        .map((s) => decodeURIComponent(s))
      if (segments.length < 2 || segments.some((s) => s === '..' || s.includes('\\') || s.includes(':'))) {
        res.writeHead(400).end()
        return
      }
      const root = path.resolve(getStoriesRoot())
      const file = path.resolve(root, ...segments)
      if (!file.startsWith(root + path.sep)) {
        res.writeHead(403).end()
        return
      }
      const stat = await fs.stat(file).catch(() => null)
      if (!stat || !stat.isFile()) {
        res.writeHead(404).end()
        return
      }
      const type = MIME[path.extname(file).toLowerCase()] ?? 'application/octet-stream'
      const headers: Record<string, string | number> = {
        'Content-Type': type,
        'Accept-Ranges': 'bytes',
        'Cache-Control': 'no-cache'
      }
      const range = req.headers.range
      if (range) {
        const m = /bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)/.exec(range)
        let start = m && m[1] ? Number(m[1]) : 0
        let end = m && m[2] ? Number(m[2]) : stat.size - 1
        if (m && !m[1] && m[2]) {
          // dáº¡ng "bytes=-500" (500 byte cuá»‘i)
          start = Math.max(0, stat.size - Number(m[2]))
          end = stat.size - 1
        }
        if (start >= stat.size || end < start) {
          res.writeHead(416, { 'Content-Range': `bytes */${stat.size}` }).end()
          return
        }
        end = Math.min(end, stat.size - 1)
        res.writeHead(206, {
          ...headers,
          'Content-Range': `bytes ${start}-${end}/${stat.size}`,
          'Content-Length': end - start + 1
        })
        if (req.method === 'HEAD') return void res.end()
        createReadStream(file, { start, end }).pipe(res)
        return
      }
      res.writeHead(200, { ...headers, 'Content-Length': stat.size })
      if (req.method === 'HEAD') return void res.end()
      createReadStream(file).pipe(res)
    } catch {
      if (!res.headersSent) res.writeHead(500)
      res.end()
    }
  })

  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once('error', reject)
    server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => resolve())
  })
  const port = (server.address() as AddressInfo).port
  return {
    baseUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${port}/m/${token}`,
    close: () => new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.close(() => resolve()))
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\mix.ts ===
/**
 * MIX Ã‚M THANH báº±ng ffmpeg:
 *   giá»ng â”€â”¬â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”
 *          â””â”€(sidechain)â”€â”                                 â”œâ”€ amix â”€ loudnorm (2 lÆ°á»£t) â”€ WAV 48k
 *   ambience/nháº¡c/SFX/stinger â”€ fade/vá»‹ trÃ­ â”€ amix â”€ láº·ng â”€ sidechaincompress (ducking) â”˜
 * LÆ°á»£t 1 Ä‘o loudness, lÆ°á»£t 2 chuáº©n hoÃ¡ tuyáº¿n tÃ­nh tá»›i má»¥c tiÃªu (~ -14 LUFS) â‡’ Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh giá»¯a cÃ¡c video.
 */
import { platformsConfig } from '../../shared/config'
import { runFfmpegCapture } from './ffmpeg'

export interface MixCue {
  path: string
  start: number
  end: number
  /** Ã‚m lÆ°á»£ng tuyáº¿n tÃ­nh tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i (Ä‘Ã£ nhÃ¢n má»©c máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh theo loáº¡i) */
  volume: number
  fadeIn: number
  fadeOut: number
  /** Láº·p nguá»“n (ambience/nháº¡c ngáº¯n hÆ¡n Ä‘oáº¡n cáº§n phá»§) */
  loop: boolean
}

export interface MixPlan {
  voicePath: string
  duration: number
  cues: MixCue[]
  silences: { start: number; end: number }[]
}

export interface LoudnessTarget {
  lufs: number
  truePeak: number
  lra: number
}

const f3 = (n: number): string => (Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000).toString()

/** Tham sá»‘ ffmpeg (input + filter_complex) cho pháº§n trá»™n; nhÃ£n Ä‘áº§u ra [pre] */
export function buildMixGraph(plan: MixPlan): { inputArgs: string[]; graph: string } {
  const a = platformsConfig.audio
  const SR = a.sampleRate
  const D = f3(plan.duration)
  const fmt = `aformat=sample_fmts=fltp:sample_rates=${SR}:channel_layouts=stereo`
  const inputArgs: string[] = ['-i', plan.voicePath]
  const parts: string[] = []
  const cues = plan.cues.filter((c) => c.end - c.start > 0.05)

  if (cues.length === 0) {
    parts.push(`[0:a]${fmt},apad=whole_dur=${D},atrim=0:${D}[pre]`)
    return { inputArgs, graph: parts.join(';') }
  }
  parts.push(`[0:a]${fmt},apad=whole_dur=${D},atrim=0:${D},asplit=2[vmix][vsc]`)
  cues.forEach((c, i) => {
    if (c.loop) inputArgs.push('-stream_loop', '-1')
    inputArgs.push('-i', c.path)
    const len = c.end - c.start
    const chain = [fmt, `atrim=0:${f3(len)}`, 'asetpts=PTS-STARTPTS']
    if (c.fadeIn > 0) chain.push(`afade=t=in:st=0:d=${f3(Math.min(c.fadeIn, len / 2))}`)
    if (c.fadeOut > 0) chain.push(`afade=t=out:st=${f3(Math.max(0, len - Math.min(c.fadeOut, len / 2)))}:d=${f3(Math.min(c.fadeOut, len / 2))}`)
    chain.push(`volume=${f3(c.volume)}`)
    const ms = Math.round(c.start * 1000)
    if (ms > 0) chain.push(`adelay=delays=${ms}:all=1`)
    parts.push(`[${i + 1}:a]${chain.join(',')}[c${i}]`)
  })
  const ins = cues.map((_, i) => `[c${i}]`).join('')
  parts.push(`${ins}amix=inputs=${cues.length}:normalize=0:duration=longest,apad=whole_dur=${D},atrim=0:${D}[bed0]`)
  // Khoáº£ng láº·ng cÃ³ chá»§ Ä‘Ã­ch: táº¯t toÃ n bá»™ Ã¢m ná»n trong Ä‘oáº¡n
  let bed = 'bed0'
  plan.silences.forEach((s, i) => {
    parts.push(`[${bed}]volume=volume=0:enable='between(t,${f3(s.start)},${f3(s.end)})'[bs${i}]`)
    bed = `bs${i}`
  })
  // Ducking: giá»ng nÃ³i vang lÃªn â‡’ Ã¢m ná»n tá»± nhá» xuá»‘ng
  const th = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.000976563, Math.pow(10, a.duck.thresholdDb / 20)))
  parts.push(`[${bed}][vsc]sidechaincompress=threshold=${th.toFixed(6)}:ratio=${a.duck.ratio}:attack=${a.duck.attackMs}:release=${a.duck.releaseMs}[bedd]`)
  parts.push(`[vmix][bedd]amix=inputs=2:normalize=0:duration=first[pre]`)
  return { inputArgs, graph: parts.join(';') }
}

export interface LoudnormMeasure {
  input_i: string
  input_tp: string
  input_lra: string
  input_thresh: string
  target_offset: string
  output_i?: string
}

/** Láº¥y khá»‘i JSON loudnorm in ra á»Ÿ cuá»‘i stderr */
export function parseLoudnorm(stderr: string): LoudnormMeasure {
  const end = stderr.lastIndexOf('}')
  const start = stderr.lastIndexOf('{', end)
  if (start < 0 || end < 0) throw new Error('KhÃ´ng Ä‘á»c Ä‘Æ°á»£c káº¿t quáº£ Ä‘o loudness tá»« ffmpeg')
  return JSON.parse(stderr.slice(start, end + 1)) as LoudnormMeasure
}

export async function mixAudio(plan: MixPlan, outPath: string, target: LoudnessTarget, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<{ lufs: number }> {
  const { inputArgs, graph } = buildMixGraph(plan)
  const ln = `loudnorm=I=${target.lufs}:TP=${target.truePeak}:LRA=${target.lra}`
  // LÆ°á»£t 1: Ä‘o
  const pass1 = await runFfmpegCapture([...inputArgs, '-filter_complex', `${graph};[pre]${ln}:print_format=json[out]`, '-map', '[out]', '-f', 'null', '-'], signal)
  const m = parseLoudnorm(pass1)
  // LÆ°á»£t 2: chuáº©n hoÃ¡ tuyáº¿n tÃ­nh theo sá»‘ Ä‘o
  const ln2 = `${ln}:measured_I=${m.input_i}:measured_TP=${m.input_tp}:measured_LRA=${m.input_lra}:measured_thresh=${m.input_thresh}:offset=${m.target_offset}:linear=true:print_format=json`
  const tmp = `${outPath}.tmp.wav`
  const pass2 = await runFfmpegCapture(
    [...inputArgs, '-filter_complex', `${graph};[pre]${ln2},aresample=${platformsConfig.audio.sampleRate}[out]`, '-map', '[out]', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', '-ar', String(platformsConfig.audio.sampleRate), '-ac', '2', tmp],
    signal
  )
  const fs = await import('node:fs/promises')
  await fs.rename(tmp, outPath)
  const out = parseLoudnorm(pass2)
  return { lufs: Number(out.output_i ?? target.lufs) }
}

/** Äo loudness tÃ­ch há»£p cá»§a má»™t file (dÃ¹ng kiá»ƒm thá»­ / hiá»ƒn thá»‹) */
export async function measureLufs(file: string): Promise<number> {
  const s = await runFfmpegCapture(['-i', file, '-af', 'loudnorm=print_format=json', '-f', 'null', '-'])
  return Number(parseLoudnorm(s).input_i)
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\parallax.ts ===
/**
 * TÃ¡ch áº£nh thÃ nh 3 lá»›p theo depth: [háº­u cáº£nh (Ä‘áº·c), chá»§ thá»ƒ (alpha), tiá»n cáº£nh (alpha)].
 * Háº­u cáº£nh: vÃ¹ng phÃ­a sau chá»§ thá»ƒ Ä‘Æ°á»£c láº¥p báº±ng báº£n lÃ m má» Ä‘á»ƒ khi lá»›p trÆ°á»›c dá»‹ch chuyá»ƒn khÃ´ng lá»™ "bÃ³ng ma" sáº¯c nÃ©t.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import sharp from 'sharp'
import { platformsConfig } from '../../shared/config'
import { percentile, smoothstep } from '../../shared/logic/parallaxMath'
import type { DepthEstimator } from './depth'

export interface ParallaxFiles {
  depth: string
  layers: [string, string, string]
}

async function writeAtomic(file: string, buf: Buffer): Promise<void> {
  await fs.writeFile(`${file}.tmp`, buf)
  await fs.rename(`${file}.tmp`, file)
}

export async function buildParallaxLayers(
  srcAbs: string,
  out: ParallaxFiles,
  estimator: DepthEstimator,
  focus: { x: number; y: number }
): Promise<{ source: 'model' | 'heuristic'; width: number; height: number }> {
  const cfg = platformsConfig.parallax
  const input = await fs.readFile(srcAbs)
  const base = sharp(input).removeAlpha().resize(cfg.maxDimension, cfg.maxDimension, { fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true })
  const { data: rgb, info } = await base.raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true })
  const { width: w, height: h } = info
  const png = await sharp(rgb, { raw: { width: w, height: h, channels: 3 } }).png().toBuffer()
  const d = await estimator.estimate(png, w, h, focus)

  const tMid = percentile(d.depth, cfg.midPercentile)
  const tNear = percentile(d.depth, cfg.nearPercentile)
  const soft = cfg.edgeSoftness
  const midMask = Buffer.alloc(w * h)
  const nearMask = Buffer.alloc(w * h)
  const depthGray = Buffer.alloc(w * h)
  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {
    const v = d.depth[i]
    // Chá»‰ láº¥y vÃ¹ng gáº§n HÆ N ngÆ°á»¡ng â‡’ áº£nh cÃ³ depth pháº³ng (má»™t mÃ u, sÆ°Æ¡ng mÃ¹ dÃ y) khÃ´ng bá»‹ tÃ¡ch nháº§m
    midMask[i] = Math.round(smoothstep(tMid, tMid + 2 * soft, v) * 255)
    nearMask[i] = Math.round(smoothstep(tNear, tNear + 2 * soft, v) * 255)
    depthGray[i] = Math.round(v * 255)
  }
  // LÃ m má»m mÃ©p máº·t náº¡
  // extractChannel(0): sharp máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh xuáº¥t 3 kÃªnh dÃ¹ Ä‘áº§u vÃ o 1 kÃªnh
  const blurMask = (m: Buffer): Promise<Buffer> => sharp(m, { raw: { width: w, height: h, channels: 1 } }).blur(2.5).extractChannel(0).raw().toBuffer()
  const [mid, near] = await Promise.all([blurMask(midMask), blurMask(nearMask)])
  const blurred = await sharp(rgb, { raw: { width: w, height: h, channels: 3 } }).blur(28).raw().toBuffer()

  const far = Buffer.alloc(w * h * 3)
  const midRgba = Buffer.alloc(w * h * 4)
  const nearRgba = Buffer.alloc(w * h * 4)
  for (let i = 0; i < w * h; i++) {
    const a = mid[i] / 255
    for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) {
      const o = rgb[i * 3 + c]
      far[i * 3 + c] = Math.round(o * (1 - a) + blurred[i * 3 + c] * a)
      midRgba[i * 4 + c] = o
      nearRgba[i * 4 + c] = o
    }
    midRgba[i * 4 + 3] = mid[i]
    nearRgba[i * 4 + 3] = near[i]
  }
  const raw3 = { raw: { width: w, height: h, channels: 3 as const } }
  const raw4 = { raw: { width: w, height: h, channels: 4 as const } }
  await Promise.all([
    sharp(far, raw3).png().toBuffer().then((b) => writeAtomic(out.layers[0], b)),
    sharp(midRgba, raw4).png().toBuffer().then((b) => writeAtomic(out.layers[1], b)),
    sharp(nearRgba, raw4).png().toBuffer().then((b) => writeAtomic(out.layers[2], b)),
    sharp(depthGray, { raw: { width: w, height: h, channels: 1 } }).png().toBuffer().then((b) => writeAtomic(out.depth, b))
  ])
  return { source: d.source, width: w, height: h }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\thumbnail.ts ===
/**
 * Thumbnail YouTube (1280Ã—720) / áº£nh bÃ¬a TikTok (1080Ã—1920): áº£nh ná»n + lá»›p tá»‘i + CHá»® TO do app váº½
 * (prompt áº£nh luÃ´n cáº¥m chá»¯ â‡’ chá»¯ Ä‘Æ°á»£c ghÃ©p sau báº±ng SVG, Ä‘Ãºng dáº¥u tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t).
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import sharp from 'sharp'

export interface ThumbOptions {
  width: number
  height: number
  text: string
  /** DÃ²ng phá»¥ nhá» (vd tÃªn truyá»n thuyáº¿t) */
  sub?: string
  /** NhÃ£n gÃ³c (vd "PHáº¦N 1/3") */
  badge?: string
  layout: 'youtube' | 'cover'
  accent?: string
}

const xml = (s: string): string => s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')

/** Ngáº¯t dÃ²ng theo sá»‘ kÃ½ tá»± tá»‘i Ä‘a má»—i dÃ²ng */
export function wrapWords(text: string, maxChars: number, maxLines: number): string[] {
  const words = text.trim().split(/\s+/)
  const lines: string[] = []
  let cur = ''
  for (const w of words) {
    if (cur && (cur + ' ' + w).length > maxChars) {
      lines.push(cur)
      cur = w
    } else cur = cur ? `${cur} ${w}` : w
  }
  if (cur) lines.push(cur)
  if (lines.length > maxLines) {
    const head = lines.slice(0, maxLines - 1)
    return [...head, lines.slice(maxLines - 1).join(' ')]
  }
  return lines
}

export function thumbnailSvg(o: ThumbOptions): string {
  const { width: W, height: H } = o
  const accent = o.accent ?? '#b3122e'
  const yt = o.layout === 'youtube'
  const lines = wrapWords(o.text.toLocaleUpperCase('vi'), yt ? 14 : 11, 3)
  const longest = Math.max(...lines.map((l) => l.length), 1)
  const fs = Math.min(yt ? H * 0.2 : W * 0.15, (yt ? W * 0.6 : W * 0.88) / (longest * 0.62))
  const x = yt ? W * 0.05 : W / 2
  const anchor = yt ? 'start' : 'middle'
  const blockH = lines.length * fs * 1.05
  const y0 = yt ? (H - blockH) / 2 + fs * 0.85 : H * 0.62 - blockH / 2 + fs * 0.85
  const text = lines
    .map(
      (l, i) =>
        `<text x="${x.toFixed(0)}" y="${(y0 + i * fs * 1.05).toFixed(0)}" text-anchor="${anchor}" font-family="Segoe UI Black, Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif" font-weight="900" font-size="${fs.toFixed(0)}" fill="${i === lines.length - 1 ? '#ffd23f' : '#ffffff'}" stroke="#000" stroke-width="${(fs * 0.09).toFixed(1)}" paint-order="stroke" letter-spacing="-1">${xml(l)}</text>`
    )
    .join('')
  const sub = o.sub
    ? `<text x="${x.toFixed(0)}" y="${(y0 + lines.length * fs * 1.05 + fs * 0.15).toFixed(0)}" text-anchor="${anchor}" font-family="Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif" font-weight="700" font-size="${(fs * 0.32).toFixed(0)}" fill="#f3c77a" stroke="#000" stroke-width="3" paint-order="stroke">${xml(o.sub)}</text>`
    : ''
  const badge = o.badge
    ? `<rect x="${W * 0.04}" y="${H * 0.04}" rx="12" width="${(o.badge.length * W * (yt ? 0.022 : 0.04) + W * 0.04).toFixed(0)}" height="${(yt ? H * 0.11 : W * 0.11).toFixed(0)}" fill="${accent}"/><text x="${(W * 0.06).toFixed(0)}" y="${(H * 0.04 + (yt ? H * 0.08 : W * 0.08)).toFixed(0)}" font-family="Segoe UI Black, Arial" font-weight="900" font-size="${(yt ? H * 0.07 : W * 0.07).toFixed(0)}" fill="#fff">${xml(o.badge)}</text>`
    : ''
  const shade = yt
    ? `<linearGradient id="g" x1="0" x2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0.82"/><stop offset="0.55" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0.35"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient>`
    : `<linearGradient id="g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0.15"/><stop offset="0.45" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0.35"/><stop offset="0.8" stop-color="#000" stop-opacity="0.75"/></linearGradient>`
  return `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${W}" height="${H}"><defs>${shade}</defs><rect width="${W}" height="${H}" fill="url(#g)"/><rect x="0" y="${H - (yt ? 10 : 16)}" width="${W}" height="${yt ? 10 : 16}" fill="${accent}"/>${badge}${text}${sub}</svg>`
}

export async function composeThumbnail(srcAbs: string, outAbs: string, o: ThumbOptions, focus = { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }): Promise<void> {
  const base = await sharp(await fs.readFile(srcAbs))
    .resize(o.width, o.height, { fit: 'cover', position: focus.x < 0.4 ? 'west' : focus.x > 0.6 ? 'east' : 'centre' })
    .modulate({ brightness: 1.08, saturation: 1.15 })
    .toBuffer()
  const out = await sharp(base).composite([{ input: Buffer.from(thumbnailSvg(o), 'utf8') }]).png().toBuffer()
  await fs.writeFile(`${outAbs}.tmp`, out)
  await fs.rename(`${outAbs}.tmp`, outAbs)
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\verticalFrame.ts ===
/**
 * Dá»±ng áº£nh 9:16 tá»« áº£nh 16:9: cáº¯t quanh focusPoint (tiá»n cáº£nh) + ná»n má» phÃ³ng to (háº­u cáº£nh).
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import sharp from 'sharp'
import { computeVerticalLayout } from '../../shared/logic/focusCrop'
import type { FocusPoint } from '../../shared/types'

export interface VerticalFrameOptions {
  outW: number
  outH: number
  cropAspect: number
  blur: number
  brightness: number
}

export async function buildVerticalFrame(
  srcPath: string,
  outPath: string,
  focus: FocusPoint,
  opts: VerticalFrameOptions
): Promise<{ width: number; height: number }> {
  const input = await fs.readFile(srcPath)
  const meta = await sharp(input).metadata()
  if (!meta.width || !meta.height) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng Ä‘á»c Ä‘Æ°á»£c kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c áº£nh: ${srcPath}`)
  const layout = computeVerticalLayout(meta.width, meta.height, focus, {
    outW: opts.outW,
    outH: opts.outH,
    cropAspect: opts.cropAspect
  })

  const background = await sharp(input)
    .extract(layout.backgroundCrop)
    .resize(opts.outW, opts.outH, { fit: 'cover' })
    .blur(Math.max(0.3, opts.blur))
    .modulate({ brightness: opts.brightness })
    .toBuffer()

  const foreground = await sharp(input)
    .extract(layout.foregroundCrop)
    .resize(layout.fgWidth, layout.fgHeight, { fit: 'cover' })
    .toBuffer()

  const out = await sharp(background)
    .composite([{ input: foreground, left: layout.fgLeft, top: layout.fgTop }])
    .png({ compressionLevel: 8 })
    .toBuffer()

  const tmp = `${outPath}.tmp`
  await fs.writeFile(tmp, out)
  await fs.rename(tmp, outPath)
  return { width: opts.outW, height: opts.outH }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\wav.ts ===
/**
 * Äá»c/ghi WAV PCM 16-bit â€“ dÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ ghÃ©p cÃ¡c cÃ¢u thÃ nh voice track vá»›i má»‘c thá»i gian CHÃNH XÃC tá»›i tá»«ng máº«u.
 * (Má»i take Ä‘Æ°á»£c chuáº©n hoÃ¡ vá» cÃ¹ng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng trÆ°á»›c khi ghÃ©p.)
 */

export interface WavFormat {
  sampleRate: number
  channels: number
  bitsPerSample: number
}

export interface WavData extends WavFormat {
  data: Buffer
}

export function readWav(buf: Buffer): WavData {
  if (buf.toString('ascii', 0, 4) !== 'RIFF' || buf.toString('ascii', 8, 12) !== 'WAVE') {
    throw new Error('File khÃ´ng pháº£i WAV há»£p lá»‡')
  }
  let offset = 12
  let fmt: WavFormat | null = null
  while (offset + 8 <= buf.length) {
    const id = buf.toString('ascii', offset, offset + 4)
    const size = buf.readUInt32LE(offset + 4)
    const body = offset + 8
    if (id === 'fmt ') {
      const audioFormat = buf.readUInt16LE(body)
      if (audioFormat !== 1 && audioFormat !== 0xfffe) throw new Error('Chá»‰ há»— trá»£ WAV PCM')
      fmt = {
        channels: buf.readUInt16LE(body + 2),
        sampleRate: buf.readUInt32LE(body + 4),
        bitsPerSample: buf.readUInt16LE(body + 14)
      }
    } else if (id === 'data') {
      if (!fmt) throw new Error('WAV thiáº¿u chunk fmt')
      const end = Math.min(buf.length, body + size)
      return { ...fmt, data: buf.subarray(body, end) }
    }
    offset = body + size + (size % 2)
  }
  throw new Error('WAV thiáº¿u chunk data')
}

export function writeWav(fmt: WavFormat, data: Buffer): Buffer {
  const blockAlign = (fmt.channels * fmt.bitsPerSample) / 8
  const header = Buffer.alloc(44)
  header.write('RIFF', 0, 'ascii')
  header.writeUInt32LE(36 + data.length, 4)
  header.write('WAVE', 8, 'ascii')
  header.write('fmt ', 12, 'ascii')
  header.writeUInt32LE(16, 16)
  header.writeUInt16LE(1, 20)
  header.writeUInt16LE(fmt.channels, 22)
  header.writeUInt32LE(fmt.sampleRate, 24)
  header.writeUInt32LE(fmt.sampleRate * blockAlign, 28)
  header.writeUInt16LE(blockAlign, 32)
  header.writeUInt16LE(fmt.bitsPerSample, 34)
  header.write('data', 36, 'ascii')
  header.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 40)
  return Buffer.concat([header, data])
}

export function wavDuration(w: WavData): number {
  const blockAlign = (w.channels * w.bitsPerSample) / 8
  return w.data.length / blockAlign / w.sampleRate
}

export function sameFormat(a: WavFormat, b: WavFormat): boolean {
  return a.sampleRate === b.sampleRate && a.channels === b.channels && a.bitsPerSample === b.bitsPerSample
}

/**
 * Äáº·t tá»«ng Ä‘oáº¡n audio vÃ o Ä‘Ãºng giÃ¢y báº¯t Ä‘áº§u trÃªn má»™t ná»n im láº·ng dÃ i totalSec.
 * VÃ¬ vá»‹ trÃ­ tÃ­nh theo máº«u (sample) â‡’ má»‘c start/end cá»§a cÃ¢u khá»›p tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i vá»›i file ghÃ©p.
 */
export function placeClips(fmt: WavFormat, totalSec: number, clips: { start: number; wav: WavData }[]): Buffer {
  const blockAlign = (fmt.channels * fmt.bitsPerSample) / 8
  const totalFrames = Math.ceil(totalSec * fmt.sampleRate)
  const out = Buffer.alloc(totalFrames * blockAlign)
  for (const clip of clips) {
    if (!sameFormat(fmt, clip.wav)) throw new Error('CÃ¡c Ä‘oáº¡n audio khÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng â€“ cáº§n chuáº©n hoÃ¡ trÆ°á»›c')
    const startByte = Math.round(clip.start * fmt.sampleRate) * blockAlign
    const room = Math.max(0, out.length - startByte)
    clip.wav.data.copy(out, startByte, 0, Math.min(room, clip.wav.data.length))
  }
  return out
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\media\zip.ts ===
/**
 * Ghi file ZIP tá»‘i giáº£n (phÆ°Æ¡ng thá»©c STORE â€“ khÃ´ng nÃ©n láº¡i; PNG/JPG vá»‘n Ä‘Ã£ nÃ©n) cho nÃºt "Táº£i háº¿t (ZIP)".
 * TÃªn file UTF-8 (cá» bit 11) â‡’ tÃªn cÃ³ dáº¥u tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t hiá»ƒn thá»‹ Ä‘Ãºng trong Windows Explorer.
 * KhÃ´ng thÃªm thÆ° viá»‡n ngoÃ i cho má»™t tÃ¡c vá»¥ nhá».
 */
const CRC_TABLE = (() => {
  const t = new Uint32Array(256)
  for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) {
    let c = n
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1
    t[n] = c >>> 0
  }
  return t
})()

export function crc32(buf: Buffer): number {
  let c = 0xffffffff
  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) c = CRC_TABLE[(c ^ buf[i]) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8)
  return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0
}

function dosDateTime(d: Date): { time: number; date: number } {
  return {
    time: (d.getHours() << 11) | (d.getMinutes() << 5) | Math.floor(d.getSeconds() / 2),
    date: ((Math.max(1980, d.getFullYear()) - 1980) << 9) | ((d.getMonth() + 1) << 5) | d.getDate()
  }
}

export interface ZipEntry {
  name: string
  data: Buffer
  date?: Date
}

export function createZip(entries: ZipEntry[]): Buffer {
  const locals: Buffer[] = []
  const centrals: Buffer[] = []
  let offset = 0
  for (const e of entries) {
    const name = Buffer.from(e.name.replace(/\\/g, '/'), 'utf8')
    const crc = crc32(e.data)
    const { time, date } = dosDateTime(e.date ?? new Date())

    const local = Buffer.alloc(30)
    local.writeUInt32LE(0x04034b50, 0)
    local.writeUInt16LE(20, 4) // version needed
    local.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 6) // UTF-8 names
    local.writeUInt16LE(0, 8) // method: store
    local.writeUInt16LE(time, 10)
    local.writeUInt16LE(date, 12)
    local.writeUInt32LE(crc, 14)
    local.writeUInt32LE(e.data.length, 18)
    local.writeUInt32LE(e.data.length, 22)
    local.writeUInt16LE(name.length, 26)
    local.writeUInt16LE(0, 28)
    locals.push(local, name, e.data)

    const central = Buffer.alloc(46)
    central.writeUInt32LE(0x02014b50, 0)
    central.writeUInt16LE(20, 4) // made by
    central.writeUInt16LE(20, 6)
    central.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 8)
    central.writeUInt16LE(0, 10)
    central.writeUInt16LE(time, 12)
    central.writeUInt16LE(date, 14)
    central.writeUInt32LE(crc, 16)
    central.writeUInt32LE(e.data.length, 20)
    central.writeUInt32LE(e.data.length, 24)
    central.writeUInt16LE(name.length, 28)
    central.writeUInt32LE(offset, 42)
    centrals.push(central, name)

    offset += local.length + name.length + e.data.length
  }
  const cd = Buffer.concat(centrals)
  const end = Buffer.alloc(22)
  end.writeUInt32LE(0x06054b50, 0)
  end.writeUInt16LE(entries.length, 8)
  end.writeUInt16LE(entries.length, 10)
  end.writeUInt32LE(cd.length, 12)
  end.writeUInt32LE(offset, 16)
  return Buffer.concat([...locals, cd, end])
}

/**
 * PIPELINE RUNNER â€“ cháº¡y tuáº§n tá»± cÃ¡c bÆ°á»›c cá»§a má»™t Release, lÆ°u tráº¡ng thÃ¡i sau má»—i bÆ°á»›c.
 * - Äiá»ƒm duyá»‡t â˜…: cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ "Duyá»‡t tá»«ng bÆ°á»›c" dá»«ng láº¡i (waitingApproval) â‡’ báº¥m "Tiáº¿p tá»¥c" Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘i tiáº¿p.
 *   Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ "Full Auto" tá»± bá» qua Ä‘iá»ƒm duyá»‡t (chá»‰ Ä‘á»ƒ táº¡o báº£n nhÃ¡p).
 * - BÆ°á»›c lá»—i: báº¥m "Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u" láº§n ná»¯a sáº½ cháº¡y láº¡i ÄÃšNG bÆ°á»›c lá»—i (cÃ¡c bÆ°á»›c xong Ä‘Æ°á»£c giá»¯ nguyÃªn).
 * - Huá»·: AbortController truyá»n xuá»‘ng tá»«ng bÆ°á»›c (ffmpeg, queue TTS, render).
