import path from 'node:path'
import {
  createInitialPipelineState,
  isPipelineComplete,
  isStepFinished,
  RELEASE_PIPELINE,
  type StepDef
} from '../../shared/pipeline'
import type { PipelineState, Release, RunMode } from '../../shared/types'
import { isAbortError, toVietnameseError } from '../errors'
import { logger } from '../logger'
import type { PipelineDeps, StepContext } from './context'
import { STEP_HANDLERS } from './steps'

const key = (storyId: string, releaseId: string): string => `${storyId}/${releaseId}`

export class PipelineRunner {
  private active = new Map<string, { abort: AbortController; done: Promise<void> }>()

  constructor(private readonly deps: PipelineDeps) {}

  isRunning(storyId: string, releaseId: string): boolean {
    return this.active.has(key(storyId, releaseId))
  }

  /** Chá» lÆ°á»£t cháº¡y hiá»‡n táº¡i káº¿t thÃºc (dÃ¹ng cho test) */
  async waitIdle(storyId: string, releaseId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.active.get(key(storyId, releaseId))?.done
  }

  async start(storyId: string, releaseId: string, mode: RunMode): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isRunning(storyId, releaseId)) throw new Error('Pipeline cá»§a Release nÃ y Ä‘ang cháº¡y')
    const release = await this.deps.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    let state = release.pipelineState
    // ÄÃ£ xong toÃ n bá»™ â‡’ cháº¡y láº¡i tá»« Ä‘áº§u (cache giÃºp khÃ´ng táº¡o láº¡i media trÃ¹ng)
    if (!state || isPipelineComplete(state)) state = createInitialPipelineState(mode)
    state.mode = mode
    // BÆ°á»›c bá»‹ huá»·/lá»—i â‡’ Ä‘Æ°a vá» pending Ä‘á»ƒ cháº¡y láº¡i
    for (const def of RELEASE_PIPELINE) {
      const st = state.steps[def.no]
      if (st && (st.status === 'failed' || st.status === 'cancelled' || st.status === 'running')) {
        state.steps[def.no] = { status: 'pending', progress: 0 }
      }
    }
    release.pipelineState = state
    await this.persist(release, true)
    logger.info(`Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u pipeline (${mode === 'review' ? 'Duyá»‡t tá»«ng bÆ°á»›c' : 'Full Auto â€“ báº£n nhÃ¡p'})`, release.id)
    this.launch(storyId, releaseId)
  }

  async continue(storyId: string, releaseId: string, decision: 'approved' | 'declined' = 'approved'): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isRunning(storyId, releaseId)) throw new Error('Pipeline Ä‘ang cháº¡y')
    const release = await this.deps.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    const state = release.pipelineState
    const waiting = state && RELEASE_PIPELINE.find((d) => state.steps[d.no]?.status === 'waitingApproval')
    if (!state || !waiting) throw new Error('KhÃ´ng cÃ³ bÆ°á»›c nÃ o Ä‘ang chá» duyá»‡t')
    const now = new Date().toISOString()
    if (!waiting.approval) {
      // Duyá»‡t cÃ³ Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n (vd bÃ¡o giÃ¡ video AI): ghi quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh rá»“i cháº¡y láº¡i chÃ­nh bÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³
      state.approvals = { ...(state.approvals ?? {}), [waiting.no]: decision }
      state.steps[waiting.no] = { status: 'pending', progress: 0, message: decision === 'approved' ? 'ÄÃ£ Ä‘á»“ng Ã½' : 'ÄÃ£ bá» qua' }
      await this.persist(release, true)
      logger.info(`â˜… BÆ°á»›c ${waiting.no}: ${decision === 'approved' ? 'Äá»“ng Ã½' : 'Bá» qua'} (${waiting.title})`, release.id)
      this.launch(storyId, releaseId)
      return
    }
    state.steps[waiting.no] = { status: 'done', progress: 1, message: 'ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t', finishedAt: now }
    this.markApproved(release, waiting, now)
    await this.persist(release, true)
    logger.info(`â˜… ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t bÆ°á»›c ${waiting.no}: ${waiting.title}`, release.id)
    this.launch(storyId, releaseId)
  }

  cancel(storyId: string, releaseId: string): void {
    this.active.get(key(storyId, releaseId))?.abort.abort()
  }

  async resetFrom(storyId: string, releaseId: string, stepNo: number): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isRunning(storyId, releaseId)) throw new Error('HÃ£y huá»· pipeline trÆ°á»›c khi cháº¡y láº¡i tá»« má»™t bÆ°á»›c')
    const release = await this.deps.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    const state = release.pipelineState ?? createInitialPipelineState('review')
    for (const def of RELEASE_PIPELINE) {
      if (def.no >= stepNo) state.steps[def.no] = { status: 'pending', progress: 0 }
    }
    state.currentStep = null
    state.running = false
    release.pipelineState = state
    await this.persist(release, true)
    logger.info(`Äáº·t láº¡i pipeline tá»« bÆ°á»›c ${stepNo}`, release.id)
  }

  /** Ghi nháº­n duyá»‡t lÃªn dá»¯ liá»‡u tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng (ká»‹ch báº£n, giá»ng, storyboard) */
  private markApproved(release: Release, def: StepDef, at: string): void {
    if (def.key === 'approveScript' || def.key === 'approveVoice') {
      for (const l of release.script.lines) l.approval = { status: 'approved', at }
    }
  }

  private launch(storyId: string, releaseId: string): void {
    const abort = new AbortController()
    const done = this.loop(storyId, releaseId, abort.signal).finally(() => {
      this.active.delete(key(storyId, releaseId))
    })
    this.active.set(key(storyId, releaseId), { abort, done })
  }

  private async persist(release: Release, force = false): Promise<void> {
    if (release.pipelineState) release.pipelineState.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString()
    await this.deps.repo.saveRelease(release)
    if (release.pipelineState) this.deps.emitProgress(release.storyId, release.id, release.pipelineState, force)
    this.deps.emitDataChanged(release.storyId)
  }

  private async loop(storyId: string, releaseId: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
    const { repo } = this.deps
    let release = await repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    const state = (): PipelineState => release.pipelineState as PipelineState

    try {
      state().running = true
      for (const def of RELEASE_PIPELINE) {
        const st = state().steps[def.no]
        if (isStepFinished(st)) continue

        if (def.approval) {
          if (state().mode === 'fullAuto') {
            state().steps[def.no] = {
              status: 'skipped',
              progress: 1,
              message: 'Tá»± duyá»‡t (Full Auto â€“ báº£n nhÃ¡p)',
              finishedAt: new Date().toISOString()
            }
            await this.persist(release)
            continue
          }
          state().steps[def.no] = { status: 'waitingApproval', progress: 0, message: 'Chá» báº¡n duyá»‡t rá»“i báº¥m "Tiáº¿p tá»¥c"' }
          state().currentStep = def.no
          state().running = false
          await this.persist(release, true)
          logger.info(`â˜… Dá»«ng á»Ÿ bÆ°á»›c ${def.no}: ${def.title} â€“ chá» duyá»‡t`, release.id)
          return
        }

        const handler = STEP_HANDLERS[def.key]
        if (!handler) throw new Error(`Thiáº¿u handler cho bÆ°á»›c ${def.no}`)

        const startedAt = new Date().toISOString()
        state().currentStep = def.no
        state().steps[def.no] = { status: 'running', progress: 0, startedAt, message: 'Äang cháº¡yâ€¦' }
        await this.persist(release, true)
        logger.info(`â–¶ BÆ°á»›c ${def.no}: ${def.title}`, release.id)

        const story = await repo.loadStory(storyId)
        const library = await repo.loadLibrary()
        const ctx: StepContext = {
          deps: this.deps,
          story,
          release,
          library,
          signal,
          storyDir: repo.storyDir(storyId),
          releaseDir: repo.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId),
          progress: (p, message) => {
            const cur = state().steps[def.no]
            cur.progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p))
            if (message) cur.message = message
            this.deps.emitProgress(storyId, releaseId, state())
          },
          log: (message, level = 'info') => logger[level](message, release.id),
          saveStory: async () => {
            await repo.saveStory(story)
            this.deps.emitDataChanged(storyId)
          },
          mode: state().mode,
          approval: state().approvals?.[def.no]
        }

        try {
          const result = await handler(ctx)
          if (result.status === 'waitingApproval') {
            state().steps[def.no] = { status: 'waitingApproval', progress: 0, message: result.message, startedAt }
            state().currentStep = def.no
            state().running = false
            await this.persist(release, true)
            logger.info(`â˜… BÆ°á»›c ${def.no} chá» quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh: ${result.message ?? ''}`, release.id)
            return
          }
          state().steps[def.no] = {
            status: result.status,
            progress: 1,
            message: result.message,
            startedAt,
            finishedAt: new Date().toISOString()
          }
          logger.info(`âœ“ BÆ°á»›c ${def.no}: ${result.message ?? 'xong'}`, release.id)
          await this.persist(release, true)
        } catch (err) {
          const aborted = isAbortError(err) || signal.aborted
          const message = toVietnameseError(err)
          state().steps[def.no] = {
            status: aborted ? 'cancelled' : 'failed',
            progress: state().steps[def.no]?.progress ?? 0,
            startedAt,
            finishedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            error: message,
            message: aborted ? 'ÄÃ£ huá»·' : 'Lá»—i â€“ báº¥m "Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u" Ä‘á»ƒ cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c nÃ y'
          }
          state().running = false
          if (aborted) logger.warn(`â–  ÄÃ£ huá»· á»Ÿ bÆ°á»›c ${def.no}`, release.id)
          else {
            logger.error(`âœ— BÆ°á»›c ${def.no} lá»—i: ${message}`, release.id)
            if (err instanceof Error && err.stack) logger.error(err.stack, release.id)
          }
          await this.persist(release, true)
          return
        }
        // Äá»c láº¡i tá»« Ä‘Ä©a Ä‘á»ƒ luÃ´n Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ (bÆ°á»›c cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘Ã£ sá»­a story)
        release = await repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
      }

      state().running = false
      state().currentStep = null
      await this.persist(release, true)
      const exportsDir = path.join(repo.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId), 'exports')
      logger.info(`HoÃ n táº¥t pipeline. Video náº±m trong: ${exportsDir}`, release.id)
    } catch (err) {
      // Lá»—i ngoÃ i bÆ°á»›c (Ä‘á»c/ghi fileâ€¦)
      logger.error(`Pipeline dá»«ng do lá»—i: ${toVietnameseError(err)}`, releaseId)
      try {
        release = await repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
        if (release.pipelineState) {
          release.pipelineState.running = false
          await this.persist(release, true)
        }
      } catch {
        // bá» qua
      }
    }
  }
}

const TARGET_WAV = { sampleRate: 44100, channels: 1, bitsPerSample: 16 }

function channelOf(ctx: StepContext): ChannelPreset {
  const ch = ctx.library.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === ctx.story.channelPresetId)
  if (!ch) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ChannelPreset "${ctx.story.channelPresetId}"`)
  return ch
}

function beatIntensityFn(ctx: StepContext): (beatId: string) => number {
  const map = new Map(ctx.story.outline.beats.map((b) => [b.id, b.emotionalIntensity]))
  return (id) => map.get(id) ?? 0.5
}

const bankImages: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  // áº¢nh táº¡o qua hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i job (song song, retry, cache, ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch) â€“ xem Film Studio â†’ HÃ ng chá»
  const res = await ctx.deps.images.ensureSceneImages(ctx.story.id, ctx.signal, (p, msg) => ctx.progress(p, msg))
  // Äá»c láº¡i story (job áº£nh Ä‘Ã£ cáº­p nháº­t story.json)
  Object.assign(ctx.story, await ctx.deps.repo.loadStory(ctx.story.id))
  return {
    status: 'done',
    message: res.created > 0 ? `ÄÃ£ táº¡o ${res.created} áº£nh cáº£nh (${ctx.deps.providers.defaultImageProvider()})` : 'DÃ¹ng láº¡i áº£nh cÃ³ sáºµn'
  }
}

const adaptScript: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const n = ctx.release.script.lines.length
  if (n > 0) return { status: 'done', message: `Giá»¯ ká»‹ch báº£n hiá»‡n cÃ³ (${n} cÃ¢u). Muá»‘n viáº¿t láº¡i: Script Editor â†’ "Chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ láº¡i".` }
  ctx.progress(0.1, `Äang chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ báº±ng ${ctx.deps.providers.llm().id === 'mock' ? 'Mock LLM' : 'LLM'}â€¦`)
  const res = await adaptRelease({
    repo: ctx.deps.repo,
    llm: ctx.deps.providers.llm(),
    story: ctx.story,
    release: ctx.release,
    library: ctx.library,
    signal: ctx.signal,
    log: ctx.log
  })
  ctx.release.script = res.script
  // Series: lÆ°u cÃ¡c pháº§n 2..N, cáº­p nháº­t danh sÃ¡ch Release cá»§a Story
  if (res.siblings.length > 0 || res.removedIds.length > 0) {
    for (const s of res.siblings) await ctx.deps.repo.saveRelease(s)
    const updated = await ctx.deps.repo.updateStory(ctx.story.id, (st) => {
      const ids = st.releaseIds.filter((id) => !res.removedIds.includes(id))
      for (const s of res.siblings) if (!ids.includes(s.id)) ids.splice(ids.indexOf(ctx.release.id) + s.partIndex - 1, 0, s.id)
      st.releaseIds = ids
    })
    ctx.story.releaseIds = updated.releaseIds
    await ctx.deps.repo.ensureStoryFolders(updated)
    ctx.deps.emitDataChanged(ctx.story.id)
  }
  const dialog = res.script.lines.filter((l) => !isChannelCharacter(l.speakerId, ctx.library.characters)).length
  return {
    status: 'done',
    message: `${res.script.lines.length} cÃ¢u (${dialog} cÃ¢u thoáº¡i nhÃ¢n váº­t)${res.partCount > 1 ? `, chia ${res.partCount} pháº§n` : ''}`
  }
}

const pronunciation: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const lang = ctx.release.language
  let applied = 0
  for (const line of ctx.release.script.lines) {
    line.glossaryTermIds = findTermIds(line.text, ctx.library.pronunciations, lang)
    applied += line.glossaryTermIds.length
  }
  return { status: 'done', message: `Gáº¯n ${applied} thuáº­t ngá»¯ / tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn phÃ¡t Ã¢m (${lang})` }
}

const tts: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const { story, release, library } = ctx
  const lines = release.script.lines
  if (lines.length === 0) throw new Error('Ká»‹ch báº£n trá»‘ng â€“ cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c 8')
  const providers = ctx.deps.providers

  // Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh chi phÃ­ cÃ¡c cÃ¢u CHÆ¯A cÃ³ take (cache) â‡’ cháº·n náº¿u vÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story
  let estimate = 0
  const voices = new Map<string, { name: string; licensed: boolean; known: boolean }>()
  for (const line of lines) {
    const plan = plannedTts(story, release, line, library, providers)
    voices.set(plan.voice.id, { name: plan.voice.name, licensed: plan.voice.commercialLicenseConfirmed, known: plan.provider.commercialLicenseKnown })
    const cached = line.takes.find((t) => t.hash === plan.hash)
    if (!cached || !(await fileExists(ctx.deps.repo.resolveInStory(story.id, cached.path)))) {
      estimate += plan.provider.estimateCost(plan.text.length)
    }
  }
  const bundle = await ctx.deps.repo.loadBundle(story.id)
  const others = bundle.releases.filter((r) => r.id !== release.id).map((r) => r.costs)
  const budget = checkBudget(storySpent(story.costs, [...others, release.costs]), estimate, story.budget.limitUsd)
  if (!budget.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      `VÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story: Ä‘Ã£ chi ${formatUsdShort(budget.spent)} + Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh giá»ng ${formatUsdShort(estimate)} > ${formatUsdShort(budget.limit)}. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: tÄƒng ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch á»Ÿ mÃ n Story, rÃºt gá»n ká»‹ch báº£n, hoáº·c báº­t Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock.`
    )
  }
  release.costs.estimated += estimate
  if (estimate > 0) ctx.log(`Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh chi phÃ­ giá»ng: ${formatUsdShort(estimate)}`)
  // Cáº£nh bÃ¡o giáº¥y phÃ©p thÆ°Æ¡ng máº¡i (nguyÃªn táº¯c 8)
  for (const v of voices.values()) {
    if (!v.licensed && !v.known) ctx.log(`âš  Giá»ng "${v.name}" chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u "ÄÃ£ xÃ¡c nháº­n giáº¥y phÃ©p thÆ°Æ¡ng máº¡i"`, 'warn')
  }

  const queue = new PQueue({ concurrency: providersConfig.queue.defaultConcurrency })
  let done = 0
  let generated = 0
  let chars = 0
  await Promise.all(
    lines.map((line) =>
      queue.add(async () => {
        throwIfAborted(ctx.signal)
        const res = await synthesizeLine({ repo: ctx.deps.repo, providers, story, release, line, library, signal: ctx.signal })
        if (res.generated) {
          generated++
          chars += res.characters
          if (res.cost > 0) {
            release.costs.entries.push({ provider: res.provider, kind: 'tts', units: res.characters, cost: res.cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
            release.costs.actual += res.cost
          }
        }
        // Giá»¯ take ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng Ä‘Ã£ chá»n náº¿u nÃ³ váº«n cÃ²n; náº¿u khÃ´ng chá»n take vá»«a táº¡o/khá»›p cache
        if (!line.takes.some((t) => t.id === line.selectedTakeId) || res.generated) line.selectedTakeId = res.take.id
        done++
        ctx.progress(done / lines.length, `CÃ¢u ${done}/${lines.length}`)
      })
    )
  )
  return {
    status: 'done',
    message: generated > 0 ? `Táº¡o ${generated} take (${chars} kÃ½ tá»±, ${voices.size} giá»ng)` : 'DÃ¹ng láº¡i toÃ n bá»™ take (cache)'
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- BÆ°á»›c 12

async function readTargetWav(ctx: StepContext, abs: string, cacheName: string): Promise<WavData> {
  let wav = readWav(await fs.readFile(abs))
  if (!sameFormat(wav, TARGET_WAV)) {
    const norm = path.join(ctx.storyDir, 'cache', cacheName)
    if (!(await fileExists(norm))) await toWav44kMono(abs, norm, ctx.signal)
    wav = readWav(await fs.readFile(norm))
  }
  return wav
}

const assembleVoice: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const { story, release, library } = ctx
  const lines = release.script.lines
  const wavs: WavData[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    throwIfAborted(ctx.signal)
    const line = lines[i]
    const take = line.takes.find((t) => t.id === line.selectedTakeId)
    if (!take) throw new Error(`CÃ¢u ${line.id} chÆ°a cÃ³ take Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»n â€“ cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c 11`)
    // Xá»­ lÃ½ giá»ng theo chuá»—i cá»§a GIá»ŒNG NÃ“I cÃ¢u Ä‘Ã³ (ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ / nhÃ¢n váº­t / ma cÃ³ chuá»—i khÃ¡c nhau)
    const voice = voiceForLine(story, release, line, library)
    const processed = await processTake(ctx.deps.repo, story.id, take, voice.postProcessChain, ctx.signal)
    wavs.push(await readTargetWav(ctx, processed.abs, `norm_${path.basename(processed.abs)}`))
    ctx.progress(((i + 1) / lines.length) * 0.8, `Xá»­ lÃ½ giá»ng ${i + 1}/${lines.length}`)
  }

  const { leadInSec, tailSec } = platformsConfig.voiceAssembly
  const timing = computeLineTimings(
    lines.map((l, i) => ({
      id: l.id,
      duration: wavDuration(wavs[i]),
      pauseBeforeMs: l.delivery.pauseBeforeMs,
      pauseAfterMs: l.delivery.pauseAfterMs
    })),
    { leadInSec, tailSec }
  )
  timing.items.forEach((t, i) => {
    lines[i].start = t.start
    lines[i].end = t.end
  })
  const pcm = placeClips(
    TARGET_WAV,
    timing.totalDuration,
    timing.items.map((t, i) => ({ start: t.start, wav: wavs[i] }))
  )
  const outAbs = path.join(ctx.releaseDir, 'voice', 'voice_processed.wav')
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(outAbs), { recursive: true })
  await fs.writeFile(outAbs, writeWav(TARGET_WAV, pcm))
  const rel = toRelPath('releases', release.id, 'voice', 'voice_processed.wav')
  // GÄ4: mix ambience/SFX/nháº¡c + ducking + loudness; hiá»‡n dÃ¹ng tháº³ng giá»ng Ä‘Ã£ xá»­ lÃ½
  release.voiceTrack = { mode: 'tts', processedPath: rel, mixedPath: rel, duration: timing.totalDuration }

  if (release.platform === 'youtube') {
    release.chapters = release.script.sections
      .map((sec) => {
        const first = lines.find((l) => l.sectionId === sec.id)
        return first ? { id: `ch-${sec.id}`, title: sec.title, titleVi: sec.titleVi, start: first.start ?? 0 } : null
      })
      .filter((c): c is NonNullable<typeof c> => c !== null)
      .map((c, i) => (i === 0 ? { ...c, start: 0 } : c))
  }
  const m = Math.floor(timing.totalDuration / 60)
  const s = Math.round(timing.totalDuration % 60)
  return { status: 'done', message: `Voice track ${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, '0')} (${lines.length} cÃ¢u)` }
}

const matchScenesStep: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const { story, release, library } = ctx
  const lines = release.script.lines
  if (!release.voiceTrack || lines.some((l) => l.start == null || l.end == null)) {
    throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ má»‘c thá»i gian cÃ¢u â€“ cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c 12')
  }
  // CÃ¢u Ä‘áº§u cá»§a má»—i Ä‘oáº¡n "ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ xuáº¥t hiá»‡n" (bá»‘i cáº£nh, giáº£i thÃ­ch, káº¿tâ€¦) â‡’ dÃ¹ng cáº£nh cÃ³ nhÃ¢n váº­t kÃªnh
  const pd = platformDefaultsOf(channelOf(ctx), release)
  const narratorSectionIds = new Set(release.script.sections.filter((s) => pd.narratorSections.includes(s.kind)).map((s) => s.id))
  const firstLineOfSection = new Set<string>()
  for (const sid of narratorSectionIds) {
    const first = lines.find((l) => l.sectionId === sid)
    if (first) firstLineOfSection.add(first.id)
  }
  const storyChars = charactersForStory(story.id, library.characters)
  const { scenes, missing } = matchScenes(
    lines.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, beatId: l.beatId, start: l.start ?? 0, end: l.end ?? 0, narrator: firstLineOfSection.has(l.id) })),
    story.sceneBank,
    story.outline.beats,
    release.voiceTrack.duration,
    platformsConfig.sceneTiming,
    { isNarratorScene: (s) => s.cast.some((m) => isChannelCharacter(m.characterId, storyChars)) }
  )
  const bankById = new Map(story.sceneBank.map((b) => [b.id, b]))
  release.scenes = scenes.map((s) => ({
    id: s.id,
    sceneBankId: s.sceneBankId,
    start: s.start,
    end: s.end,
    lineIds: s.lineIds,
    mediaOverride: null,
    motion: {
      level: 'kenBurns',
      type: 'zoomIn',
      intensity: 0.4,
      easing: 'easeInOut',
      focusPoint: { ...(bankById.get(s.sceneBankId)?.focusPoint ?? { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }) }
    },
    transition: { type: 'cut', durationSec: 0 },
    isHero: false
  }))
  for (const m of missing) {
    const beat = story.outline.beats.find((b) => b.id === m.beatId)
    ctx.log(
      m.reason === 'noScenes'
        ? `Beat ${beat?.order ?? m.beatId} chÆ°a cÃ³ cáº£nh â€“ Ä‘ang mÆ°á»£n cáº£nh beat gáº§n nháº¥t. Gá»£i Ã½: táº¡o thÃªm ${m.needed} cáº£nh (Film Studio â€“ GÄ3)`
        : `Beat ${beat?.order ?? m.beatId} cáº§n ${m.needed} cáº£nh nhÆ°ng chá»‰ cÃ³ ${m.available} â€“ má»™t sá»‘ cáº£nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c dÃ¹ng láº¡i. Gá»£i Ã½ táº¡o thÃªm (GÄ3)`,
      'warn'
    )
  }
  const narratorScenes = release.scenes.filter((s) => bankById.get(s.sceneBankId)?.cast.some((m) => isChannelCharacter(m.characterId, storyChars))).length
  return { status: 'done', message: `${scenes.length} cáº£nh (${narratorScenes} cáº£nh ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ), ${missing.length} gá»£i Ã½ thiáº¿u cáº£nh` }
}

const framing: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  if (ctx.release.aspectRatio !== '9:16') return { status: 'skipped', message: 'Release 16:9 â€“ khÃ´ng cáº§n cáº¯t dá»c' }
  const used = [...new Set(ctx.release.scenes.map((s) => s.sceneBankId))]
  const { verticalFraming, aspectSizes } = platformsConfig
  let built = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < used.length; i++) {
    throwIfAborted(ctx.signal)
    const item = ctx.story.sceneBank.find((b) => b.id === used[i])
    if (!item?.media['16:9']) continue
    const existing = item.media['9:16']
    const fileOk = existing ? await fileExists(ctx.deps.repo.resolveInStory(ctx.story.id, existing.path)) : false
    if (!existing || !fileOk) {
      const src = ctx.deps.repo.resolveInStory(ctx.story.id, item.media['16:9'].path)
      const fileName = `${item.id}_9x16_crop.png`
      await buildVerticalFrame(src, path.join(ctx.storyDir, 'bank', fileName), item.focusPoint, {
        outW: aspectSizes['9:16'].width,
        outH: aspectSizes['9:16'].height,
        cropAspect: verticalFraming.cropAspect,
        blur: verticalFraming.backgroundBlur,
        brightness: verticalFraming.backgroundBrightness
      })
      const ref: MediaRef = {
        path: toRelPath('bank', fileName),
        mediaType: 'image',
        source: 'own',
        provider: 'focus-crop',
        model: `crop-${verticalFraming.cropAspect}+blur`,
        cost: 0,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        width: aspectSizes['9:16'].width,
        height: aspectSizes['9:16'].height
      }
      item.media['9:16'] = ref
      // Ghi nguyÃªn tá»­: chá»‰ Ä‘áº·t báº£n cáº¯t náº¿u trong lÃºc Ä‘Ã³ chÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh 9:16 váº½ riÃªng
      await ctx.deps.repo.updateStory(ctx.story.id, (st) => {
        const it = st.sceneBank.find((b) => b.id === item.id)
        if (it && !it.media['9:16']) it.media['9:16'] = ref
      })
      built++
    }
    ctx.progress((i + 1) / used.length, `Khung dá»c ${i + 1}/${used.length}`)
  }
  if (built > 0) ctx.deps.emitDataChanged(ctx.story.id)
  const pending = ctx.story.sceneBank.filter((b) => b.needsVerticalVersion && used.includes(b.id)).length
  if (pending > 0) ctx.log(`${pending} cáº£nh cáº§n báº£n 9:16 váº½ riÃªng â€“ Ä‘ang dÃ¹ng báº£n cáº¯t. Táº¡o báº£n dá»c riÃªng á»Ÿ Storyboard (phÃ­m V) hoáº·c Film Studio.`, 'info')
  return { status: 'done', message: built > 0 ? `Dá»±ng ${built} áº£nh dá»c theo focusPoint` : 'DÃ¹ng láº¡i áº£nh dá»c cÃ³ sáºµn' }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- BÆ°á»›c 16

const heroScenes: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const { story, release } = ctx
  const intensity = beatIntensityFn(ctx)
  const beatOf = (sceneBankId: string): string => story.sceneBank.find((b) => b.id === sceneBankId)?.beatId ?? ''
  const ids = new Set(selectHeroScenes(release.scenes.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, beatId: beatOf(s.sceneBankId) })), intensity, release.heroRatio))
  for (const s of release.scenes) s.isHero = ids.has(s.id)
  const heroBank = [...new Set(release.scenes.filter((s) => s.isHero).map((s) => s.sceneBankId))]
  if (heroBank.length === 0) return { status: 'done', message: `KhÃ´ng cÃ³ cáº£nh hero (heroRatio ${Math.round(release.heroRatio * 100)}%)` }

  const aspect = release.aspectRatio
  const q = await ctx.deps.motion.quote(story, heroBank, aspect)
  if (q.needed.length === 0) return { status: 'done', message: `${heroBank.length} cáº£nh hero dÃ¹ng láº¡i clip video AI Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³` }
  if (q.cost > 0) {
    if (ctx.approval === 'declined') return { status: 'done', message: 'ÄÃ£ bá» qua video AI â€“ cáº£nh hero dÃ¹ng Ken Burns / parallax' }
    if (ctx.approval !== 'approved') {
      if (ctx.mode === 'fullAuto') {
        return { status: 'done', message: 'Full Auto khÃ´ng tá»± chi tiá»n cho video AI â€“ cáº£nh hero dÃ¹ng Má»©c 1/2 (cháº¡y "Duyá»‡t tá»«ng bÆ°á»›c" Ä‘á»ƒ táº¡o)' }
      }
      return {
        status: 'waitingApproval',
        message: `BÃ¡o giÃ¡ video AI: ${q.needed.length} cáº£nh Ã— ${q.seconds}s â‰ˆ ${formatUsdShort(q.cost)} (${q.providerId})${q.reused.length ? `, dÃ¹ng láº¡i ${q.reused.length} clip cÃ³ sáºµn` : ''}. Äá»“ng Ã½ Ä‘á»ƒ táº¡o, hoáº·c Bá» qua Ä‘á»ƒ dÃ¹ng Ken Burns/parallax.`
      }
    }
    const bundle = await ctx.deps.repo.loadBundle(story.id)
    const b = checkBudget(storySpent(bundle.story.costs, bundle.releases.map((r) => r.costs)), q.cost, story.budget.limitUsd)
    if (!b.ok) {
      ctx.log(`Video AI vÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story (Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh ${formatUsdShort(q.cost)}) â€“ quay vá» Má»©c 1/2`, 'warn')
      return { status: 'done', message: 'VÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch â€“ cáº£nh hero dÃ¹ng Ken Burns/parallax' }
    }

const motion: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const channel = channelOf(ctx)
  const intensity = beatIntensityFn(ctx)
  const aspect = ctx.release.aspectRatio
  const story = await ctx.deps.repo.loadStory(ctx.story.id)
  const bankById = new Map(story.sceneBank.map((b) => [b.id, b]))
  const scenes = ctx.release.scenes
  const inputs = scenes.map((s) => {
    const b = bankById.get(s.sceneBankId)
    return { beatId: b?.beatId ?? '', focusPoint: b?.focusPoint ?? { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }, shotType: b?.shotType ?? 'wide' }
  })
  // Má»©c 3: cáº£nh hero Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ clip Ä‘Ãºng khung
  const ai = new Set(scenes.map((s, i) => (s.isHero && bankById.get(s.sceneBankId)?.heroClip[aspect] ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0))
  // Má»©c 2: chá»n theo parallaxRatio, rá»“i tÃ­nh lá»›p (cáº£nh lá»—i â‡’ Ken Burns)
  let parallax = chooseParallaxScenes(inputs, intensity, channel.motionProfile.parallaxRatio, ai)
  let heuristic = 0
  if (parallax.size > 0) {
    ctx.progress(0.05, `TÃ¡ch lá»›p parallax ${parallax.size} cáº£nhâ€¦`)
    const ids = [...parallax].map((i) => scenes[i].sceneBankId)
    const r = await ctx.deps.motion.ensureParallax(story.id, ids, aspect, ctx.signal, (p) => ctx.progress(0.05 + 0.9 * p, 'Depth map & tÃ¡ch lá»›p'))
    heuristic = r.heuristic
    if (r.failed.length) parallax = new Set([...parallax].filter((i) => !r.failed.includes(scenes[i].sceneBankId)))
  }
  const motions = assignMotions(inputs, intensity, channel.motionProfile, { parallax, aiVideo: ai })
  const transitions = assignTransitions(inputs, intensity, channel.motionProfile)
  scenes.forEach((s, i) => {
    s.motion = motions[i]
    s.transition = transitions[i]
  })
  const violations = findDiversityViolations(scenes.map((s) => ({ shotType: bankById.get(s.sceneBankId)?.shotType ?? 'wide', motionType: s.motion.type })))
  const shotV = violations.filter((v) => v.kind === 'shotType').length
  if (shotV > 0) ctx.log(`${shotV} cáº·p cáº£nh liá»n nhau trÃ¹ng shotType (ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh thiáº¿u lá»±a chá»n) â€“ nÃªn táº¡o thÃªm cáº£nh`, 'warn')
  if (heuristic > 0) ctx.log(`${heuristic} cáº£nh parallax dÃ¹ng depth Æ¯á»šC LÆ¯á»¢NG (chÆ°a cÃ³ model) â€“ táº£i model depth á»Ÿ CÃ i Ä‘áº·t Ä‘á»ƒ tÃ¡ch lá»›p chÃ­nh xÃ¡c`, 'warn')
  const n1 = motions.filter((m) => m.level === 'kenBurns').length
  return {
    status: 'done',
    message: `Má»©c 1 Ken Burns: ${n1} Â· Má»©c 2 parallax: ${parallax.size}${heuristic ? ' (depth Æ°á»›c lÆ°á»£ng)' : ''} Â· Má»©c 3 video AI: ${ai.size} Â· ${violations.length} vi pháº¡m Ä‘a dáº¡ng`
  }
}

const soundDesign: StepHandler = async (ctx) => {
  const { story, release } = ctx
  let note = ''
  if (release.soundCues.length === 0) {
    ctx.progress(0.1, 'Thiáº¿t káº¿ Ã¢m thanhâ€¦')

    release.soundCues = d.cues

      release.costs.entries.push({ provider: ctx.deps.providers.llm().id, kind: 'llm', units: 1, cost: d.cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
    }
    note = d.note

  }
  // Ghi cue xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a trÆ°á»›c khi mix (mix Ä‘á»c release tá»« Ä‘Ä©a)

/**
 * ImageProvider: Google Gemini image ("Nano Banana") qua @google/genai.
 * - áº¢nh tham chiáº¿u nhÃ¢n váº­t (Ä‘Ã£ khoÃ¡) gá»­i kÃ¨m dáº¡ng inlineData â‡’ giá»¯ Ä‘á»“ng nháº¥t khuÃ´n máº·t/trang phá»¥c.
 * - Tá»‰ lá»‡ khung qua config.imageConfig.aspectRatio (16:9, 9:16, 1:1â€¦).
 * - Gemini khÃ´ng cÃ³ negative prompt â‡’ prompt Ä‘Ã£ chá»©a "Avoid: â€¦" (xem shared/logic/imagePrompt.ts).
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import { GoogleGenAI } from '@google/genai'
import sharp from 'sharp'
import { providersConfig, type ImageProviderConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import type { ImageProvider, ImageRequest, ImageResult } from '../interfaces'

interface Part {
  text?: string
  inlineData?: { data?: string; mimeType?: string }
}
interface GenerateResponse {
  candidates?: { content?: { parts?: Part[] }; finishReason?: string }[]
  promptFeedback?: { blockReason?: string }
}
/** Pháº§n tá»‘i thiá»ƒu cá»§a SDK mÃ  adapter dÃ¹ng (Ä‘á»ƒ test báº±ng client giáº£) */
export interface GenAiClientLike {
  models: { generateContent(req: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<GenerateResponse> }
}

export class GeminiImageProvider implements ImageProvider {
  readonly id = 'gemini'
  readonly displayName: string
  readonly model: string

  constructor(
    private readonly getKey: () => string | null,
    private readonly clientFactory: (apiKey: string) => GenAiClientLike = (apiKey) => new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey }) as unknown as GenAiClientLike,
    private readonly cfg: ImageProviderConfig = providersConfig.image.gemini,
    model?: string
  ) {
    this.displayName = cfg.displayName
    this.model = model ?? cfg.model
  }

  estimateCost(count: number): number {
    return count * this.cfg.pricePerImage
  }

  /** Thu nhá» áº£nh tham chiáº¿u (cáº¡nh dÃ i 1024) â‡’ gá»­i nhanh, ráº» token */
  private async loadReference(file: string): Promise<string> {
    const buf = await sharp(await fs.readFile(file)).resize(1024, 1024, { fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true }).png().toBuffer()
    return buf.toString('base64')
  }

  buildRequest(req: ImageRequest, refs: string[], signal: AbortSignal): Record<string, unknown> {
    const refNote = refs.length
      ? `\nThe attached ${refs.length} reference image(s) show the locked characters/states: keep their faces, hair and outfits identical. Do not copy the reference backgrounds.`
      : ''
    return {
      model: this.model,
      contents: [
        {
          role: 'user',
          parts: [{ text: req.prompt + refNote }, ...refs.map((data) => ({ inlineData: { mimeType: 'image/png', data } }))]
        }
      ],
      config: {
        responseModalities: ['IMAGE'],
        imageConfig: { aspectRatio: req.aspect },
        abortSignal: signal
      }
    }
  }

  async generate(req: ImageRequest): Promise<ImageResult> {
    const key = this.getKey()
    if (!key) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ API key Google Gemini. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: nháº­p key á»Ÿ mÃ n CÃ i Ä‘áº·t hoáº·c báº­t Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock.')
    const max = this.cfg.maxReferenceImages ?? 4
    const refs = await Promise.all(req.referenceImagePaths.slice(0, max).map((p) => this.loadReference(p)))

    const timeout = AbortSignal.timeout(this.cfg.timeoutMs ?? 120000)
    const signal = req.signal ? AbortSignal.any([req.signal, timeout]) : timeout
    let res: GenerateResponse
    try {
      res = await this.clientFactory(key).models.generateContent(this.buildRequest(req, refs, signal))
    } catch (err) {
      if (req.signal?.aborted) throw new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError')
      if (timeout.aborted) throw new Error(`Gemini timeout sau ${(this.cfg.timeoutMs ?? 120000) / 1000}s`)
      throw err // ApiError cá»§a SDK cÃ³ .status â‡’ hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i tá»± quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh thá»­ láº¡i (429/5xx)
    }

    const parts = res.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts ?? []
    const img = parts.find((p) => p.inlineData?.data)
    if (!img?.inlineData?.data) {
      const reason = res.promptFeedback?.blockReason ?? res.candidates?.[0]?.finishReason ?? 'khÃ´ng rÃµ'
      const text = parts.map((p) => p.text).filter(Boolean).join(' ').slice(0, 200)
      throw new Error(`Gemini khÃ´ng tráº£ vá» áº£nh (lÃ½ do: ${reason}). ${text} CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: sá»­a prompt cáº£nh (bá»›t chi tiáº¿t nháº¡y cáº£m) rá»“i táº¡o láº¡i.`)
    }
    const png = await sharp(Buffer.from(img.inlineData.data, 'base64')).png().toBuffer()
    const meta = await sharp(png).metadata()
    const tmp = `${req.outputPath}.tmp`
    await fs.writeFile(tmp, png)
    await fs.rename(tmp, req.outputPath)
    return {
      path: req.outputPath,
      width: meta.width ?? 0,
      height: meta.height ?? 0,
      cost: this.cfg.pricePerImage,
      provider: this.id,
      model: this.model
    }
  }
}

/**
 * MockImageProvider: táº¡o áº£nh placeholder báº±ng sharp (SVG â†’ PNG).
 * áº¢nh cÃ³ sá»‘ cáº£nh, shotType, gÃ³c mÃ¡y, tÃ³m táº¯t prompt vÃ  dáº¥u focusPoint â€“
 * Ä‘á»§ "chi tiáº¿t" Ä‘á»ƒ tháº¥y rÃµ chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng Ken Burns vÃ  cáº¯t dá»c.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import sharp from 'sharp'
import { providersConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import type { ImageAspect } from '../../../shared/types'
import type { ImageProvider, ImageRequest, ImageResult } from '../interfaces'

const SIZES: Record<ImageAspect, { w: number; h: number }> = {
  '16:9': { w: 1920, h: 1080 },
  '9:16': { w: 1080, h: 1920 },
  '1:1': { w: 1440, h: 1440 }
}

const xml = (s: string): string =>
  s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')

function wrap(text: string, maxChars: number, maxLines: number): string[] {
  const words = text.split(/\s+/)
  const lines: string[] = []
  let cur = ''
  for (const w of words) {
    if ((cur + ' ' + w).trim().length > maxChars) {
      lines.push(cur.trim())
      cur = w
      if (lines.length === maxLines) break
    } else cur = `${cur} ${w}`
  }
  if (lines.length < maxLines && cur.trim()) lines.push(cur.trim())
  if (lines.length === maxLines && words.join(' ').length > lines.join(' ').length) {
    lines[maxLines - 1] = lines[maxLines - 1].replace(/\s*\S*$/, '') + ' â€¦'
  }
  return lines
}

/** Sá»‘ giáº£ ngáº«u nhiÃªn á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh theo seed (cÃ¹ng cáº£nh â‡’ cÃ¹ng bá»‘ cá»¥c) */
function rng(seed: number): () => number {
  let s = seed % 2147483647
  if (s <= 0) s += 2147483646
  return () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647
}

export class MockImageProvider implements ImageProvider {
  readonly id = 'mock'
  readonly displayName = providersConfig.image.mock.displayName
  readonly model = providersConfig.image.mock.model

  estimateCost(): number {
    return 0
  }

  buildSvg(req: ImageRequest): { svg: string; w: number; h: number } {
    const { w, h } = SIZES[req.aspect]
    const p = req.placeholder
    const pal = p?.palette.length ? p.palette : ['#0b0f14', '#1b2a2f', '#3d2b1f', '#8a6a3a', '#a61b29']
    const rand = rng((req.seed ?? 7) * 7919 + 13)
    const unit = Math.min(w, h)

    // Háº­u cáº£nh: trÄƒng, Ä‘Æ°á»ng chÃ¢n trá»i, hÃ ng cÃ¢y bÃ³ng tá»‘i, sÃ³ng nÆ°á»›c
    const moonX = w * (0.15 + rand() * 0.7)
    const moonY = h * (0.12 + rand() * 0.2)
    const horizon = h * (0.5 + rand() * 0.15)
    let trees = ''
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
      const x = (w / 9) * i + rand() * (w / 12)
      const th = unit * (0.12 + rand() * 0.22)
      trees += `<path d="M${x.toFixed(0)},${horizon.toFixed(0)} l${(unit * 0.05).toFixed(0)},-${th.toFixed(0)} l${(unit * 0.05).toFixed(0)},${th.toFixed(0)} z" fill="#05070a" opacity="0.9"/>`
    }
    let waves = ''
    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) {

    const fs1 = unit * 0.085
    const fs2 = unit * 0.035
    const fs3 = unit * 0.03
    const summary = wrap(p?.summary ?? req.prompt, req.aspect === '9:16' ? 30 : 58, 4)
    const textX = w * 0.09
    const textY = h * 0.86 - summary.length * fs3 * 1.35

    const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${w}" height="${h}" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}">
  <defs>
    <linearGradient id="sky" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
      <stop offset="0" stop-color="${pal[1]}"/>
      <stop offset="0.55" stop-color="${pal[0]}"/>
      <stop offset="1" stop-color="#020305"/>
    </linearGradient>
    <radialGradient id="moon" cx="0.5" cy="0.5" r="0.5">
      <stop offset="0" stop-color="#f6e7c1" stop-opacity="0.95"/>

      <stop offset="1" stop-color="#d9c08a" stop-opacity="0"/>

      <stop offset="1" stop-color="${pal[4]}" stop-opacity="0"/>
    </radialGradient>
  </defs>
  <rect width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="url(#sky)"/>
  ${grid}
  <circle cx="${moonX.toFixed(0)}" cy="${moonY.toFixed(0)}" r="${(unit * 0.16).toFixed(0)}" fill="url(#moon)"/>
  <rect x="0" y="${horizon.toFixed(0)}" width="${w}" height="${(h - horizon).toFixed(0)}" fill="#04060a" opacity="0.75"/>
  ${trees}
  ${waves}
  <circle cx="${fx.toFixed(0)}" cy="${fy.toFixed(0)}" r="${(unit * 0.18).toFixed(0)}" fill="url(#glow)"/>
  <circle cx="${fx.toFixed(0)}" cy="${fy.toFixed(0)}" r="${(unit * 0.035).toFixed(0)}" fill="none" stroke="#f3c77a" stroke-width="${(unit * 0.004).toFixed(1)}"/>
  <line x1="${(fx - unit * 0.06).toFixed(0)}" y1="${fy.toFixed(0)}" x2="${(fx + unit * 0.06).toFixed(0)}" y2="${fy.toFixed(0)}" stroke="#f3c77a" stroke-width="2"/>
  <line x1="${fx.toFixed(0)}" y1="${(fy - unit * 0.06).toFixed(0)}" x2="${fx.toFixed(0)}" y2="${(fy + unit * 0.06).toFixed(0)}" stroke="#f3c77a" stroke-width="2"/>
  <text x="${textX.toFixed(0)}" y="${(h * 0.1 + fs1).toFixed(0)}" font-family="Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${fs1.toFixed(0)}" font-weight="800" fill="#f4f1ea" fill-opacity="0.92">${xml(title)}</text>
  <text x="${textX.toFixed(0)}" y="${(h * 0.1 + fs1 + fs2 * 1.5).toFixed(0)}" font-family="Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${fs2.toFixed(0)}" fill="#f3c77a">${xml(meta)}</text>
  ${castText ? `<text x="${textX.toFixed(0)}" y="${(h * 0.1 + fs1 + fs2 * 3.1).toFixed(0)}" font-family="Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${fs2.toFixed(0)}" font-weight="700" fill="#8fd3c8">${xml(castText.length > (req.aspect === '9:16' ? 40 : 75) ? castText.slice(0, req.aspect === '9:16' ? 39 : 74) + 'â€¦' : castText)}</text>` : ''}
  ${summary
    .map(
      (l, i) =>
        `<text x="${textX.toFixed(0)}" y="${(textY + i * fs3 * 1.35).toFixed(0)}" font-family="Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${fs3.toFixed(0)}" fill="#d6d3cb" fill-opacity="0.9">${xml(l)}</text>`
    )
    .join('\n  ')}
  <text x="${(w - unit * 0.04).toFixed(0)}" y="${(unit * 0.07).toFixed(0)}" text-anchor="end" font-family="Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif" font-size="${(fs3 * 0.8).toFixed(0)}" fill="#ffffff" fill-opacity="0.45">PLACEHOLDER Â· ${req.aspect}</text>
</svg>`
    return { svg, w, h }
  }

  async generate(req: ImageRequest): Promise<ImageResult> {
    const { svg, w, h } = this.buildSvg(req)
    // Ghi qua Buffer (fs) thay vÃ¬ Ä‘á»ƒ sharp tá»± má»Ÿ Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n â‡’ an toÃ n vá»›i Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n Unicode trÃªn Windows
    const png = await sharp(Buffer.from(svg, 'utf8')).png({ compressionLevel: 8 }).toBuffer()
    const tmp = `${req.outputPath}.tmp`
    await fs.writeFile(tmp, png)
    await fs.rename(tmp, req.outputPath)
    return { path: req.outputPath, width: w, height: h, cost: 0, provider: this.id, model: this.model }
  }

=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\providers\mock\mockTts.ts ===
/**
 * MockTts: sinh "giá»ng" giáº£ báº±ng ffmpeg â€“ má»™t Ã¢m tráº§m nhá», nháº¥p nhÃ´ theo nhá»‹p Ã¢m tiáº¿t,
 * thá»i lÆ°á»£ng Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh theo sá»‘ tá»«/Ã¢m tiáº¿t. GiÃºp cháº¡y toÃ n bá»™ app khÃ´ng cáº§n API key.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import { providersConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import { estimateSpeechSeconds } from '../../../shared/logic/speech'
import type { Delivery, Language, VoiceProfile } from '../../../shared/types'
import { runFfmpeg } from '../../media/ffmpeg'
import type { TtsProvider, TtsRequest, TtsResult } from '../interfaces'

export class MockTtsProvider implements TtsProvider {
  readonly id = 'mock'
  readonly displayName = providersConfig.tts.mock.displayName
  readonly model = providersConfig.tts.mock.model
  readonly languages: Language[] = ['en', 'vi']
  readonly commercialLicenseKnown = true

  estimateCost(): number {
    return 0
  }

  toProviderPayload(text: string, delivery: Delivery, voice: VoiceProfile): Record<string, unknown> {
    return { text, tags: delivery.tags, pace: delivery.pace * voice.speed }
  }

  estimateDuration(text: string, language: Language, speed: number): number {
    return estimateSpeechSeconds(text, language, speed, providersConfig.tts.mock)
  }

  async synthesize(req: TtsRequest): Promise<TtsResult> {
    const cfg = providersConfig.tts.mock
    const speed = req.delivery.pace * req.voice.speed
    const duration = this.estimateDuration(req.text, req.language, speed)
    // CÃ¢u thÃ¬ tháº§m â‡’ nhá» hÆ¡n; cÃ¢u cÄƒng tháº³ng â‡’ cao hÆ¡n má»™t chÃºt (Ä‘á»ƒ nghe Ä‘Æ°á»£c khÃ¡c biá»‡t)
    const whisper = req.delivery.tags.includes('whispers')
    const tense = req.delivery.tags.some((t) => t === 'tense' || t === 'nervous' || t === 'fearful')
    // Má»—i giá»ng nhÃ¢n váº­t má»™t cao Ä‘á»™ (providerSettings.toneHz) â‡’ nghe phÃ¢n biá»‡t ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ / nhÃ¢n váº­t
    const custom = Number(req.voice.providerSettings['toneHz'])
    const baseHz = Number.isFinite(custom) && custom > 50 ? custom : cfg.toneHz[req.language]
    const freq = Math.round(baseHz * (tense ? 1.12 : 1))
    const volume = cfg.volume * (whisper ? 0.6 : 1)
    const fadeOutStart = Math.max(0, duration - 0.12).toFixed(3)
    const tmp = `${req.outputPath}.tmp.wav`
    await runFfmpeg(
      [
        '-f',
        'lavfi',
        '-i',
        `sine=frequency=${freq}:sample_rate=44100:duration=${duration.toFixed(3)}`,
        '-af',
        `tremolo=f=${cfg.syllableRateHz}:d=0.85,volume=${volume.toFixed(3)},afade=t=in:d=0.06,afade=t=out:st=${fadeOutStart}:d=0.12`,
        '-ac',
        '1',
        '-ar',
        '44100',
        '-c:a',
        'pcm_s16le',
        tmp
      ],
      req.signal
    )
    await fs.rename(tmp, req.outputPath)
    return {
      path: req.outputPath,
      duration,
      characters: req.text.length,
      cost: 0,
      provider: this.id,
      model: this.model
    }
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\providers\tts\common.ts ===
/** Pháº§n dÃ¹ng chung cho adapter TTS tháº­t: táº£i audio vá» file táº¡m â†’ chuáº©n hoÃ¡ WAV â†’ Ä‘o thá»i lÆ°á»£ng */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import { providersConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import { probeDuration, toWav44kMono } from '../../media/ffmpeg'
import { logger } from '../../logger'
import type { RetryOptions } from '../http'

export async function saveAsWav(audio: ArrayBuffer, ext: string, outputPath: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<number> {
  const tmp = `${outputPath}.dl.${ext}`
  const tmpWav = `${outputPath}.tmp.wav`
  await fs.writeFile(tmp, Buffer.from(audio))
  try {
    await toWav44kMono(tmp, tmpWav, signal)
    await fs.rename(tmpWav, outputPath)
  } finally {
    await fs.rm(tmp, { force: true })
  }
  return probeDuration(outputPath)
}

export function retryOptions(providerName: string, timeoutMs: number, signal?: AbortSignal): RetryOptions {
  return {
    maxAttempts: providersConfig.queue.maxAttempts,
    baseMs: providersConfig.queue.backoffBaseMs,
    maxMs: providersConfig.queue.backoffMaxMs,
    timeoutMs,
    signal,
    onRetry: (a, d, r) => logger.warn(`${providerName}: thá»­ láº¡i láº§n ${a} sau ${d}ms (${r})`, 'tts')
  }
}

/** Gá»™p tag máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cá»§a giá»ng + tag cá»§a cÃ¢u, bá» trÃ¹ng */
export function mergeTags(defaultTags: string[], tags: string[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set([...defaultTags, ...tags].map((t) => t.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean))]
}

export function paceHint(pace: number): string {
  if (pace <= 0.85) return 'Speak slowly.'
  if (pace < 0.97) return 'Speak a little slower than normal.'
  if (pace >= 1.1) return 'Speak a little faster than normal.'
  return ''
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\providers\tts\elevenlabs.ts ===
/**
 * ElevenLabs (eleven_v3): delivery chung â†’ audio tag dáº¡ng [whispers] Ä‘áº·t Ä‘áº§u cÃ¢u.
 * Endpoint dialogue (nhiá»u nhÃ¢n váº­t trong má»™t láº§n gá»i) khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng: app táº¡o Tá»ªNG CÃ‚U Ä‘á»™c láº­p Ä‘á»ƒ táº¡o láº¡i riÃªng Ä‘Æ°á»£c.
 */
import { providersConfig, type ElevenLabsConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import type { Delivery, Language, VoiceProfile } from '../../../shared/types'
import { fetchWithRetry } from '../http'
import type { TtsProvider, TtsRequest, TtsResult } from '../interfaces'
import { mergeTags, retryOptions, saveAsWav } from './common'

export class ElevenLabsTtsProvider implements TtsProvider {
  readonly id = 'elevenlabs'
  readonly languages: Language[] = ['en', 'vi']
  readonly commercialLicenseKnown = false
  readonly displayName: string
  readonly model: string

  constructor(
    private readonly getKey: () => string | null,
    private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
    private readonly cfg: ElevenLabsConfig = providersConfig.tts.elevenlabs
  ) {
    this.displayName = cfg.displayName
    this.model = cfg.model
  }

  estimateCost(characters: number): number {
    return (characters / 1000) * (this.cfg.pricePer1kChars ?? 0)
  }

  toProviderPayload(text: string, delivery: Delivery, voice: VoiceProfile): Record<string, unknown> {
    const allowed = new Set(this.cfg.audioTags ?? [])
    const tags = mergeTags(voice.defaultTags, delivery.tags).filter((t) => allowed.has(t))
    const prefix = tags.map((t) => `[${t}]`).join(' ')
    const custom = (voice.providerSettings['voice_settings'] ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>
    const speed = Math.min(1.2, Math.max(0.7, voice.speed * delivery.pace))
    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
      text: prefix ? `${prefix} ${text}` : text,
      model_id: this.model,
      voice_settings: { ...this.cfg.defaultVoiceSettings, ...custom, speed: Math.round(speed * 100) / 100 }
    }
    if (this.cfg.sendLanguageCode) payload['language_code'] = voice.language
    return payload
  }

  async synthesize(req: TtsRequest): Promise<TtsResult> {
    const key = this.getKey()
    if (!key) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ API key ElevenLabs. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: nháº­p key á»Ÿ mÃ n CÃ i Ä‘áº·t, hoáº·c Ä‘á»•i giá»ng sang Mock.')
    if (!req.voice.providerVoiceId) throw new Error(`Giá»ng "${req.voice.name}" chÆ°a cÃ³ voice_id ElevenLabs`)
    const payload = this.toProviderPayload(req.text, req.delivery, req.voice)
    const url = `${this.cfg.endpoint}/${encodeURIComponent(req.voice.providerVoiceId)}?output_format=${encodeURIComponent(this.cfg.outputFormat)}`
    const res = await fetchWithRetry(
      url,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'xi-api-key': key, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'audio/mpeg' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      },
      { ...retryOptions('ElevenLabs', this.cfg.timeoutMs ?? 90000, req.signal), fetchImpl: this.fetchImpl }
    )
    const duration = await saveAsWav(await res.arrayBuffer(), 'mp3', req.outputPath, req.signal)
    const characters = String(payload['text']).length
    return { path: req.outputPath, duration, characters, cost: this.estimateCost(characters), provider: this.id, model: this.model }
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\providers\tts\openaiTts.ts ===
/**
 * OpenAI gpt-4o-mini-tts: Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn giá»ng báº±ng trÆ°á»ng "instructions"
 * (baseInstructions cá»§a giá»ng + chá»‰ dáº«n cÃ¢u + tag + gá»£i Ã½ nhá»‹p).
 */
import { providersConfig, type OpenAiTtsConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import type { Delivery, Language, VoiceProfile } from '../../../shared/types'
import { fetchWithRetry } from '../http'
import type { TtsProvider, TtsRequest, TtsResult } from '../interfaces'
import { mergeTags, paceHint, retryOptions, saveAsWav } from './common'

export class OpenAiTtsProvider implements TtsProvider {
  readonly id = 'openai'
  readonly languages: Language[] = ['en', 'vi']
  readonly commercialLicenseKnown = false
  readonly displayName: string
  readonly model: string

  constructor(
    private readonly getKey: () => string | null,
    private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
    private readonly cfg: OpenAiTtsConfig = providersConfig.tts.openai
  ) {
    this.displayName = cfg.displayName
    this.model = cfg.model
  }

  estimateCost(characters: number): number {
    return (characters / 1000) * (this.cfg.pricePer1kChars ?? 0)
  }

  toProviderPayload(text: string, delivery: Delivery, voice: VoiceProfile): Record<string, unknown> {
    const tags = mergeTags(voice.defaultTags, delivery.tags)
    const parts = [
      voice.baseInstructions,
      delivery.instructions,
      tags.length ? `Delivery: ${tags.join(', ')}.` : '',
      paceHint(voice.speed * delivery.pace),
      voice.language === 'vi' ? 'Speak natural Vietnamese with correct tones.' : ''
    ]
    return {
      model: this.model,
      voice: voice.providerVoiceId || 'ballad',
      input: text,
      instructions: parts.filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim(),
      response_format: 'wav'
    }
  }

  async synthesize(req: TtsRequest): Promise<TtsResult> {
    const key = this.getKey()
    if (!key) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ API key OpenAI. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: nháº­p key á»Ÿ mÃ n CÃ i Ä‘áº·t, hoáº·c Ä‘á»•i giá»ng sang Mock.')
    const payload = this.toProviderPayload(req.text, req.delivery, req.voice)
    const res = await fetchWithRetry(
      this.cfg.endpoint,
      {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${key}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      },
      { ...retryOptions('OpenAI TTS', this.cfg.timeoutMs ?? 90000, req.signal), fetchImpl: this.fetchImpl }
    )
    const duration = await saveAsWav(await res.arrayBuffer(), 'wav', req.outputPath, req.signal)
    return {
      path: req.outputPath,
      duration,
      characters: req.text.length,
      cost: this.estimateCost(req.text.length),
      provider: this.id,
      model: this.model
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Mock image-to-video: dá»±ng clip ngáº¯n tá»« áº£nh báº±ng ffmpeg (zoom cháº­m + háº¡t phim + Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng cháº­p chá»n) â€“ khÃ´ng tá»‘n phÃ­.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import { providersConfig, type VideoProviderConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import { probeDuration, runFfmpeg } from '../../media/ffmpeg'
import type { VideoProvider, VideoRequest, VideoResult } from '../interfaces'

export class MockVideoProvider implements VideoProvider {
  readonly id = 'mock'
  readonly displayName: string
  readonly model: string

  constructor(private readonly cfg: VideoProviderConfig = providersConfig.video.mock) {
    this.displayName = cfg.displayName
    this.model = cfg.model
  }

  get clipSeconds(): number {
    return this.cfg.clipSeconds
  }

  estimateCost(durationSec: number): number {
    return durationSec * this.cfg.pricePerSecond
  }

  async imageToVideo(req: VideoRequest): Promise<VideoResult> {
    const [w, h] = req.aspect === '9:16' ? [720, 1280] : [1280, 720]
    const fps = 30
    const d = Math.max(1, req.durationSec)
    const frames = Math.round(d * fps)
    const tmp = `${req.outputPath}.tmp.mp4`
    await runFfmpeg(
      [
        '-loop',
        '1',
        '-i',
        req.imagePath,
        '-t',
        d.toFixed(2),
        '-vf',
        [
          `scale=${w * 2}:${h * 2}:force_original_aspect_ratio=increase`,
          `crop=${w * 2}:${h * 2}`,
          `zoompan=z='min(1+0.10*on/${frames},1.10)':x='iw/2-(iw/zoom/2)':y='ih/2-(ih/zoom/2)':d=${frames}:s=${w}x${h}:fps=${fps}`,
          'noise=alls=8:allf=t',
          "eq=brightness='0.03*sin(2*PI*t*0.9)':eval=frame",
          'format=yuv420p'
        ].join(','),
        '-c:v',
        'libx264',
        '-preset',
        'veryfast',
        '-crf',
        '22',
        '-an',
        '-movflags',
        '+faststart',
        tmp
      ],
      req.signal
    )
    await fs.rename(tmp, req.outputPath)
    const duration = await probeDuration(req.outputPath).catch(() => d)
    return { path: req.outputPath, duration, cost: this.estimateCost(d), provider: this.id, model: this.model }
  }
}

/**
 * VideoProvider: Google Veo (image-to-video) qua @google/genai â€“ dÃ¹ng chung API key Gemini.
 * generateVideos (thao tÃ¡c dÃ i) â†’ há»i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘á»‹nh ká»³ â†’ táº£i file video vá».

 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import { GoogleGenAI } from '@google/genai'
import sharp from 'sharp'
import { providersConfig, type VideoProviderConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import { probeDuration } from '../../media/ffmpeg'
import { sleep } from '../http'
import type { VideoProvider, VideoRequest, VideoResult } from '../interfaces'

interface VeoOperation {
  done?: boolean
  error?: { message?: string; code?: number }
  response?: { generatedVideos?: { video?: unknown }[]; raiMediaFilteredReasons?: string[] }
}
export interface VeoClientLike {
  models: { generateVideos(p: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<VeoOperation> }
  operations: { getVideosOperation(p: { operation: VeoOperation }): Promise<VeoOperation> }
  files: { download(p: { file: unknown; downloadPath: string }): Promise<void> }
}

export class VeoVideoProvider implements VideoProvider {
  readonly id = 'veo'
  readonly displayName: string
  readonly model: string

  constructor(
    private readonly getKey: () => string | null,
    private readonly clientFactory: (apiKey: string) => VeoClientLike = (apiKey) => new GoogleGenAI({ apiKey }) as unknown as VeoClientLike,
    private readonly cfg: VideoProviderConfig = providersConfig.video.veo
  ) {
    this.displayName = cfg.displayName
    this.model = cfg.model
  }

  get clipSeconds(): number {
    return this.cfg.clipSeconds
  }

  estimateCost(durationSec: number): number {
    return durationSec * this.cfg.pricePerSecond
  }

  async imageToVideo(req: VideoRequest): Promise<VideoResult> {
    const key = this.getKey()
    if (!key) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ API key Google (Gemini/Veo). CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: nháº­p key á»Ÿ CÃ i Ä‘áº·t hoáº·c dÃ¹ng Mock.')
    const client = this.clientFactory(key)
    const img = await sharp(await fs.readFile(req.imagePath)).resize(1280, 1280, { fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true }).png().toBuffer()
    const config: Record<string, unknown> = {
      aspectRatio: req.aspect,
      numberOfVideos: 1,
      durationSeconds: req.durationSec,
      negativePrompt: 'gore, blood, wounds, text, watermark, sudden cuts, distorted faces'
    }
    if (this.cfg.resolution) config['resolution'] = this.cfg.resolution
    if (this.cfg.generateAudio !== undefined) config['generateAudio'] = this.cfg.generateAudio

    let op = await client.models.generateVideos({
      model: this.model,
      prompt: req.prompt,
      image: { imageBytes: img.toString('base64'), mimeType: 'image/png' },
      config
    })
    const deadline = Date.now() + (this.cfg.timeoutMs ?? 600000)
    while (!op.done) {
      if (Date.now() > deadline) throw new Error('Veo quÃ¡ thá»i gian chá» â€“ thá»­ láº¡i sau')
      await sleep(this.cfg.pollIntervalMs ?? 10000, req.signal)
      op = await client.operations.getVideosOperation({ operation: op })
    }
    if (op.error) throw Object.assign(new Error(`Veo lá»—i: ${op.error.message ?? 'khÃ´ng rÃµ'}`), { status: op.error.code })
    const video = op.response?.generatedVideos?.[0]?.video
    if (!video) {
      const why = op.response?.raiMediaFilteredReasons?.join('; ') ?? 'khÃ´ng cÃ³ video tráº£ vá»'
      throw new Error(`Veo khÃ´ng táº¡o Ä‘Æ°á»£c video (${why}). CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: sá»­a mÃ´ táº£ cáº£nh hoáº·c bá» cáº£nh khá»i danh sÃ¡ch hero.`)
    }
    const tmp = `${req.outputPath}.tmp.mp4`
    await client.files.download({ file: video, downloadPath: tmp })
    await fs.rename(tmp, req.outputPath)
    const duration = await probeDuration(req.outputPath).catch(() => req.durationSec)
    return { path: req.outputPath, duration, cost: this.estimateCost(req.durationSec), provider: this.id, model: this.model }
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\providers\http.ts ===
/**
 * fetch cÃ³ retry: exponential backoff + jitter; 429/503 tÃ´n trá»ng header Retry-After; timeout; huá»· báº±ng AbortSignal.
 * DÃ¹ng fetch cÃ³ sáºµn cá»§a Node/Electron â‡’ khÃ´ng cáº§n thÃªm thÆ° viá»‡n HTTP.
 */
export class HttpError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message: string,
    readonly status: number,
    readonly body: string,
    /** Tá»« header Retry-After (náº¿u cÃ³) â€“ hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i dÃ¹ng khi thá»­ láº¡i á»Ÿ má»©c job */
    readonly retryAfterMs: number | null = null
  ) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'HttpError'
  }
}

export interface RetryOptions {
  maxAttempts: number
  baseMs: number
  maxMs: number
  timeoutMs: number
  signal?: AbortSignal
  onRetry?: (attempt: number, delayMs: number, reason: string) => void
  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
  sleep?: (ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal) => Promise<void>
  random?: () => number
}

/** Retry-After: sá»‘ giÃ¢y hoáº·c ngÃ y giá» HTTP â‡’ mili-giÃ¢y (null náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³/khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡) */
export function parseRetryAfter(value: string | null, now = Date.now()): number | null {
  if (!value) return null
  const sec = Number(value.trim())
  if (Number.isFinite(sec) && sec >= 0) return Math.round(sec * 1000)
  const date = Date.parse(value)
  if (Number.isFinite(date)) return Math.max(0, date - now)
  return null
}

/** base Ã— 2^(attempt-1) + jitter ngáº«u nhiÃªn [0, base), khÃ´ng vÆ°á»£t max */
export function backoffDelay(attempt: number, baseMs: number, maxMs: number, random = Math.random): number {
  const exp = baseMs * Math.pow(2, Math.max(0, attempt - 1))
  return Math.min(maxMs, Math.round(exp + random() * baseMs))
}

export function isRetryableStatus(status: number): boolean {
  return status === 408 || status === 409 || status === 425 || status === 429 || status >= 500
}

function abortError(): Error {
  return new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError')
}

export function sleep(ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal?.aborted) return reject(abortError())
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
      resolve()
    }, ms)
    const onAbort = (): void => {
      clearTimeout(t)
      reject(abortError())
    }
    signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
  })
}

export async function fetchWithRetry(url: string, init: RequestInit, opts: RetryOptions): Promise<Response> {
  const doFetch = opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch
  const wait = opts.sleep ?? sleep
  const random = opts.random ?? Math.random
  let lastError: unknown = null

  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= opts.maxAttempts; attempt++) {
    if (opts.signal?.aborted) throw abortError()
    const ac = new AbortController()
    const onAbort = (): void => ac.abort()
    opts.signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
    const timer = setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), opts.timeoutMs)
    let retryDelay: number | null = null
    let reason = ''
    try {
      const res = await doFetch(url, { ...init, signal: ac.signal })
      if (res.ok) return res
      const body = (await res.text().catch(() => '')).slice(0, 2000)
      const err = new HttpError(`HTTP ${res.status}: ${body.slice(0, 300)}`, res.status, body, parseRetryAfter(res.headers.get('retry-after')))
      if (!isRetryableStatus(res.status) || attempt === opts.maxAttempts) throw err
      lastError = err
      reason = `HTTP ${res.status}`
      retryDelay = parseRetryAfter(res.headers.get('retry-after'))
    } catch (err) {
      if (opts.signal?.aborted) throw abortError()
      if (err instanceof HttpError) throw err
      // Lá»—i máº¡ng hoáº·c timeout â‡’ thá»­ láº¡i
      lastError = err
      reason = ac.signal.aborted ? 'timeout' : err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
      if (attempt === opts.maxAttempts) break
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer)
      opts.signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
    }
    const delay = Math.min(opts.maxMs, retryDelay ?? backoffDelay(attempt, opts.baseMs, opts.maxMs, random))
    opts.onRetry?.(attempt, delay, reason)
    await wait(delay, opts.signal)
  }
  throw lastError instanceof Error ? lastError : new Error(`Gá»i API tháº¥t báº¡i: ${String(lastError)}`)
}

export function createProviderRegistry(opts: {
  isMock: () => boolean
  getKey: (providerId: string) => string | null
  fetchImpl?: typeof fetch
  /** Thay provider khi kiá»ƒm thá»­ (vd video giáº£ cÃ³ tÃ­nh phÃ­ Ä‘á»ƒ thá»­ luá»“ng bÃ¡o giÃ¡) */
  overrides?: { video?: VideoProvider & { clipSeconds: number } }
}): ProviderRegistry {
  const f = opts.fetchImpl ?? fetch
  const mockTts = new MockTtsProvider()
  const mockLlm = new MockLlmProvider()
  const mockImage = new MockImageProvider()
  const openaiLlm = new OpenAiLlmProvider(() => opts.getKey('openai'), f)
  const eleven = new ElevenLabsTtsProvider(() => opts.getKey('elevenlabs'), f)
  const openaiTts = new OpenAiTtsProvider(() => opts.getKey('openai'), f)
  const gemini = new GeminiImageProvider(() => opts.getKey('gemini'))
  const mockVideo = opts.overrides?.video ?? new MockVideoProvider()
  const veo = new VeoVideoProvider(() => opts.getKey('gemini'))

  return {
    isMock: opts.isMock,
    llm: () => (opts.isMock() ? mockLlm : openaiLlm),
    tts: (providerId) => {
      if (opts.isMock() || providerId === 'mock') return mockTts
      if (providerId === 'elevenlabs') return eleven
      if (providerId === 'openai') return openaiTts
      throw new Error(`Provider giá»ng khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: "${providerId}"`)
    },
    defaultImageProvider: () => (opts.isMock() ? 'mock' : 'gemini'),
    defaultVideoProvider: () => (opts.isMock() ? 'mock' : 'veo'),
    video: (providerId) => {
      const id = providerId ?? (opts.isMock() ? 'mock' : 'veo')
      if (opts.isMock() || id === 'mock') return mockVideo
      if (id === 'veo') return veo
      throw new Error(`Provider video khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: "${id}"`)
    },
    image: (providerId) => {
      const id = providerId ?? (opts.isMock() ? 'mock' : 'gemini')
      if (opts.isMock() || id === 'mock') return mockImage
      if (id === 'gemini') return gemini
      throw new Error(`Provider áº£nh khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: "${id}"`)
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Táº¡o hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i job cá»§a app: giá»›i háº¡n theo provider (tá»« config), cháº·n khi cháº¡m ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story / thÃ¡ng,
 * ghi chi phÃ­ thá»±c táº¿ cá»§a tá»«ng job vÃ o sá»• chi phÃ­ cá»§a Story.
 */
import { providersConfig } from '../../shared/config'
import { checkBudget, formatUsdShort, storySpent } from '../../shared/logic/costEstimate'
import type { CostEntry, Job } from '../../shared/types'
import { logger } from '../logger'
import type { StoryRepository } from '../storage/storyRepo'
import { JobQueueImpl } from './jobQueue'

/** Tá»•ng chi trong thÃ¡ng hiá»‡n táº¡i trÃªn má»i Story (theo thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm ghi sá»•) */
export async function monthSpent(repo: StoryRepository, now = new Date()): Promise<number> {
  const prefix = now.toISOString().slice(0, 7)
  let sum = 0
  const add = (entries: CostEntry[]): void => {
    for (const e of entries) if (e.timestamp.startsWith(prefix)) sum += e.cost
  }
  for (const s of await repo.listStories()) {
    const b = await repo.loadBundle(s.id)
    add(b.story.costs?.entries ?? [])
    for (const r of b.releases) add(r.costs.entries)
  }
  return sum
}

const KIND: Record<Job['type'], CostEntry['kind']> = {
  image: 'image',
  video: 'video',
  tts: 'tts',
  llm: 'llm',
  stt: 'stt',
  render: 'render',
  depthMap: 'image'
}

export function createAppQueue(opts: {
  repo: StoryRepository
  persistPath?: string
  concurrency: number
  getMonthlyBudget: () => number
  onCostRecorded?: (storyId: string) => void
}): JobQueueImpl {
  const q = providersConfig.queue
  return new JobQueueImpl({
    concurrency: opts.concurrency,
    maxConcurrency: q.maxConcurrency,
    providerLimits: {
      mock: providersConfig.image.mock.maxConcurrency,
      gemini: providersConfig.image.gemini.maxConcurrency,
      veo: providersConfig.video.veo.maxConcurrency,
      elevenlabs: providersConfig.tts.elevenlabs.maxConcurrency ?? 4,
      openai: providersConfig.tts.openai.maxConcurrency ?? 6
    },
    maxAttempts: q.maxAttempts,
    backoffBaseMs: q.backoffBaseMs,
    backoffMaxMs: q.backoffMaxMs,
    persistPath: opts.persistPath,
    budget: async (job, queue) => {
      if (job.estimatedCost <= 0) return { ok: true }
      if (job.storyId) {
        const b = await opts.repo.loadBundle(job.storyId)
        const spent = storySpent(b.story.costs, b.releases.map((r) => r.costs)) + queue.inFlightCost(job.storyId)
        const chk = checkBudget(spent, job.estimatedCost, b.story.budget.limitUsd)
        if (!chk.ok) {
          return { ok: false, reason: `Story "${b.story.name}" cháº¡m ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch ${formatUsdShort(chk.limit)} (Ä‘Ã£ chi ${formatUsdShort(spent)}). TÄƒng ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch á»Ÿ mÃ n Story rá»“i báº¥m Tiáº¿p tá»¥c.` }
        }
      }
      const monthly = opts.getMonthlyBudget()
      if (monthly > 0) {
        const spent = (await monthSpent(opts.repo)) + queue.inFlightCost(job.storyId)
        if (spent + job.estimatedCost > monthly) {
          return { ok: false, reason: `Cháº¡m ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch thÃ¡ng ${formatUsdShort(monthly)} (Ä‘Ã£ chi ${formatUsdShort(spent)}). Äá»•i á»Ÿ CÃ i Ä‘áº·t rá»“i báº¥m Tiáº¿p tá»¥c.` }
        }
      }
      return { ok: true }
    },
    onCost: async (job, cost) => {
      if (!job.storyId) {
        logger.info(`Chi phÃ­ ${formatUsdShort(cost)} cho "${job.label}" (khÃ´ng gáº¯n Story)`, 'queue')
        return
      }
      await opts.repo.updateStory(job.storyId, (s) => {
        s.costs.actual += cost
        s.costs.entries.push({ provider: job.providerId, kind: KIND[job.type], units: 1, cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
      })
      opts.onCostRecorded?.(job.storyId)
    }
  })
}
