/**
 * HÃ€NG Äá»¢I JOB dÃ¹ng chung TTS / áº£nh / video.
 * - Sá»‘ luá»“ng chung 1â€“20, chá»‰nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c khi Ä‘ang cháº¡y; giá»›i háº¡n riÃªng tá»«ng provider.
 * - Retry exponential backoff + jitter; lá»—i 429/503 tÃ´n trá»ng Retry-After.
 * - Huá»· (AbortController), táº¡m dá»«ng / tiáº¿p tá»¥c.
 * - Dá»«ng cáº£ hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i khi cháº¡m ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch (job giá»¯ nguyÃªn tráº¡ng thÃ¡i "chá»").
 * - Cache theo cacheKey: job trÃ¹ng Ä‘ang chá»/cháº¡y â‡’ dÃ¹ng chung; Ä‘Ã£ xong â‡’ tráº£ káº¿t quáº£ ngay, khÃ´ng táº¡o láº¡i.
 * - LÆ°u hÃ ng chá» + cache xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a, khÃ´i phá»¥c khi má»Ÿ láº¡i app (khÃ´i phá»¥c á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Táº M Dá»ªNG Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh tá»‘n phÃ­ báº¥t ngá»).
 * KhÃ´ng dÃ¹ng worker thread: job gá»i API lÃ  I/O, cháº¡y song song báº±ng Promise trong main process.
 */
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import { backoffDelay, isRetryableStatus } from '../providers/http'
import type { EnqueueOptions, JobHandler, JobQueue, QueueState } from '../providers/interfaces'
import type { Job, JobStatus, JobType } from '../../shared/types'

export interface QueueOptions {
  concurrency: number
  maxConcurrency: number
  providerLimits?: Record<string, number>
  maxAttempts: number
  backoffBaseMs: number
  backoffMaxMs: number
  /** File lÆ°u hÃ ng chá» (bá» trá»‘ng = khÃ´ng lÆ°u) */
  persistPath?: string
  /** Kiá»ƒm tra ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch trÆ°á»›c khi cháº¡y job; ok=false â‡’ dá»«ng hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i */
  budget?: (job: Job, queue: JobQueueImpl) => Promise<{ ok: boolean; reason?: string }>
  /** Ghi chi phÃ­ thá»±c táº¿ vÃ o CostLedger */
  onCost?: (job: Job, cost: number) => Promise<void> | void
  random?: () => number
  now?: () => number
  /** Giá»›i háº¡n sá»‘ job Ä‘Ã£ xong giá»¯ trong danh sÃ¡ch */
  keepFinished?: number
  maxCacheEntries?: number
}

const TERMINAL: JobStatus[] = ['done', 'failed', 'cancelled']

/** Lá»—i cÃ³ nÃªn thá»­ láº¡i khÃ´ng: HTTP 408/409/425/429/5xx, lá»—i máº¡ng/timeout; lá»—i khÃ¡c (sai key, sai input) thÃ¬ khÃ´ng */
export function isRetryableError(err: unknown): boolean {
  const status = (err as { status?: number } | null)?.status
  if (typeof status === 'number') return isRetryableStatus(status)
  const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
  return /ECONNRESET|ETIMEDOUT|ENOTFOUND|EAI_AGAIN|fetch failed|network|timeout|socket hang up/i.test(msg)
}

export function retryAfterOf(err: unknown): number | null {
  const v = (err as { retryAfterMs?: number } | null)?.retryAfterMs
  return typeof v === 'number' && v >= 0 ? v : null
}

interface PersistShape {
  version: 1
  paused: boolean
  concurrency: number
  seq: number
  jobs: Job[]
  cache: [string, unknown][]
}

export class JobQueueImpl implements JobQueue {
  private jobs = new Map<string, Job>()
  private handlers = new Map<JobType, JobHandler<unknown, unknown>>()
  private controllers = new Map<string, AbortController>()
  private providerRunning = new Map<string, number>()
  private providerLimits = new Map<string, number>()
  private cache = new Map<string, unknown>()
  private listeners = new Set<() => void>()
  private waiters = new Map<string, ((j: Job) => void)[]>()
  private starting = 0
  private paused = false
  private budgetBlocked: string | null = null
  private concurrency: number
  private seq = 0
  private timer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null
  private persistChain: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve()

  constructor(private readonly opts: QueueOptions) {
    this.concurrency = this.clampConcurrency(opts.concurrency)
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(opts.providerLimits ?? {})) this.providerLimits.set(k, Math.max(1, v))
  }

  private now(): number {
    return this.opts.now ? this.opts.now() : Date.now()
  }

  private clampConcurrency(n: number): number {
    return Math.min(this.opts.maxConcurrency, Math.max(1, Math.round(n) || 1))
  }

  register<P, R>(type: JobType, handler: JobHandler<P, R>): void {
    this.handlers.set(type, handler as JobHandler<unknown, unknown>)
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ thÃªm job

  enqueue<P>(type: JobType, payload: P, o: EnqueueOptions): Job<P> {
    if (!this.handlers.has(type)) throw new Error(`ChÆ°a Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ handler cho job "${type}"`)
    const key = o.cacheKey ?? null
    if (key) {
      // Job trÃ¹ng Ä‘ang chá»/cháº¡y â‡’ dÃ¹ng chung
      for (const j of this.jobs.values()) {
        if (j.cacheKey === key && (j.status === 'queued' || j.status === 'running')) return j as Job<P>
      }
    }
    const now = new Date(this.now()).toISOString()
    const job: Job<P> = {
      id: randomUUID(),
      type,
      payload,
      status: 'queued',
      attempts: 0,
      error: null,
      result: null,
      estimatedCost: o.estimatedCost,
      providerId: o.providerId,
      storyId: o.storyId ?? null,
      cacheKey: key,
      label: o.label ?? type,
      seq: ++this.seq,
      progress: 0,
      actualCost: 0,
      nextRunAt: 0,
      createdAt: now,
      startedAt: null,
      finishedAt: null
    }
    // ÄÃ£ cÃ³ káº¿t quáº£ trong cache â‡’ xong ngay, khÃ´ng tá»‘n phÃ­
    if (key && this.cache.has(key)) {
      job.status = 'done'
      job.result = this.cache.get(key) ?? null
      job.progress = 1
      job.startedAt = now
      job.finishedAt = now
      job.error = null
      this.jobs.set(job.id, job)
      this.changed()
      return job
    }
    this.jobs.set(job.id, job)
    this.changed()
    this.pump()
    return job
  }

  wait(jobId: string): Promise<Job> {
    const job = this.jobs.get(jobId)
    if (!job) return Promise.reject(new Error(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y job ${jobId}`))
    if (TERMINAL.includes(job.status)) return Promise.resolve(job)
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const list = this.waiters.get(jobId) ?? []
      list.push(resolve)
      this.waiters.set(jobId, list)
    })
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn

  setConcurrency(n: number): void {
    this.concurrency = this.clampConcurrency(n)
    this.changed()
    this.pump()
  }

  setProviderLimit(providerId: string, n: number): void {
    this.providerLimits.set(providerId, Math.max(1, Math.round(n)))
    this.pump()
  }

  pause(): void {
    this.paused = true
    this.changed()
  }

  resume(): void {
    this.paused = false
    this.budgetBlocked = null
    this.changed()
    this.pump()
  }

  cancel(jobId: string): void {
    const job = this.jobs.get(jobId)
    if (!job || TERMINAL.includes(job.status)) return
    if (job.status === 'running') {
      // Handler nháº­n abort; job chuyá»ƒn "cancelled" khi handler dá»«ng
      this.controllers.get(jobId)?.abort()
      job.error = 'ÄÃ£ huá»·'
      return
    }
    this.finish(job, 'cancelled', 'ÄÃ£ huá»·')
  }

  cancelAll(): void {
    for (const j of [...this.jobs.values()]) this.cancel(j.id)
  }

  retry(jobId: string): void {
    const job = this.jobs.get(jobId)
    if (!job || (job.status !== 'failed' && job.status !== 'cancelled')) return
    Object.assign(job, { status: 'queued', attempts: 0, error: null, progress: 0, nextRunAt: 0, finishedAt: null })
    this.changed()
    this.pump()
  }

  clearFinished(): void {
    for (const [id, j] of this.jobs) if (TERMINAL.includes(j.status)) this.jobs.delete(id)
    this.changed()
  }

  list(): Job[] {
    return [...this.jobs.values()].sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
  }

  state(): QueueState {
    const counts: Record<JobStatus, number> = { queued: 0, running: 0, done: 0, failed: 0, cancelled: 0 }
    for (const j of this.jobs.values()) counts[j.status]++
    return { concurrency: this.concurrency, paused: this.paused, budgetBlocked: this.budgetBlocked, counts }
  }

  /** Tá»•ng chi phÃ­ Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh cá»§a cÃ¡c job Ä‘ang cháº¡y cá»§a má»™t Story (chÆ°a ghi vÃ o sá»•) */
  inFlightCost(storyId: string | null): number {
    let s = 0
    for (const j of this.jobs.values()) if (j.status === 'running' && j.storyId === storyId) s += j.estimatedCost
    return s
  }

  onChange(listener: () => void): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener)
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener)
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ vÃ²ng cháº¡y

  private runningCount(): number {
    let n = this.starting
    for (const j of this.jobs.values()) if (j.status === 'running') n++
    return n
  }

  private providerCapacity(providerId: string): boolean {
    const limit = this.providerLimits.get(providerId) ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
    return (this.providerRunning.get(providerId) ?? 0) < limit
  }

  private pump(): void {
    if (this.paused) return
    const now = this.now()
    let earliest = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
    for (const job of this.list()) {
      if (this.runningCount() >= this.concurrency) break
      if (job.status !== 'queued') continue
      if (job.nextRunAt > now) {
        earliest = Math.min(earliest, job.nextRunAt)
        continue
      }
      if (!this.providerCapacity(job.providerId)) continue
      void this.start(job)
    }
    // CÃ³ job Ä‘ang chá» háº¿t thá»i gian backoff â‡’ háº¹n giá» cháº¡y láº¡i vÃ²ng
    if (earliest !== Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY && !this.timer) {
      this.timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.timer = null
        this.pump()
      }, Math.max(1, earliest - now))
    }
  }

  private async start(job: Job): Promise<void> {
    // Giá»¯ chá»— ngay (Ä‘á»“ng bá»™) Ä‘á»ƒ vÃ²ng pump khÃ´ng khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng quÃ¡ sá»‘ luá»“ng trong lÃºc chá» kiá»ƒm tra ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch
    this.starting++
    this.providerRunning.set(job.providerId, (this.providerRunning.get(job.providerId) ?? 0) + 1)
    let reserved = true
    const release = (): void => {
      if (!reserved) return
      reserved = false
      this.providerRunning.set(job.providerId, Math.max(0, (this.providerRunning.get(job.providerId) ?? 1) - 1))
    }
    try {
      if (this.opts.budget) {
        const b = await this.opts.budget(job, this)
        if (!b.ok) {
          this.starting--
          release()
          this.paused = true
          this.budgetBlocked = b.reason ?? 'Cháº¡m giá»›i háº¡n ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch'
          job.error = this.budgetBlocked
          this.changed()
          return
        }
      }
      if (job.status !== 'queued') {
        this.starting--
        release()
        return
      }
      const handler = this.handlers.get(job.type)
      if (!handler) throw new Error(`ChÆ°a Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ handler cho job "${job.type}"`)
      const ac = new AbortController()
      this.controllers.set(job.id, ac)
      this.starting--
      job.status = 'running'
      job.attempts++
      job.startedAt = new Date(this.now()).toISOString()
      job.progress = 0
      this.changed()

      try {
        const { result, cost } = await handler(job.payload, {
          signal: ac.signal,
          job,
          progress: (p) => {
            job.progress = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p))
            this.changed()
          }
        })
        job.result = result
        job.actualCost += cost
        if (job.cacheKey) {
          this.cache.set(job.cacheKey, result)
          const max = this.opts.maxCacheEntries ?? 5000
          while (this.cache.size > max) this.cache.delete(this.cache.keys().next().value as string)
        }
        if (cost > 0) await this.opts.onCost?.(job, cost)
        this.finish(job, 'done', null)
      } catch (err) {
        const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
        if (ac.signal.aborted) {
          this.finish(job, 'cancelled', 'ÄÃ£ huá»·')
        } else if (job.attempts < this.opts.maxAttempts && isRetryableError(err)) {
          const delay = retryAfterOf(err) ?? backoffDelay(job.attempts, this.opts.backoffBaseMs, this.opts.backoffMaxMs, this.opts.random)
          job.status = 'queued'
          job.error = `Láº§n ${job.attempts} lá»—i (${msg.slice(0, 160)}) â€“ thá»­ láº¡i sau ${Math.round(delay / 100) / 10}s`
          job.nextRunAt = this.now() + delay
          this.changed()
        } else {
          this.finish(job, 'failed', msg)
        }
      } finally {
        this.controllers.delete(job.id)
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.starting = Math.max(0, this.starting - 1)
      this.finish(job, 'failed', err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err))
    } finally {
      release()
      this.pump()
    }
  }

  private finish(job: Job, status: JobStatus, error: string | null): void {
    job.status = status
    job.error = error
    if (status === 'done') job.progress = 1
    job.finishedAt = new Date(this.now()).toISOString()
    this.trimFinished()
    this.changed()
    const ws = this.waiters.get(job.id)
    if (ws) {
      this.waiters.delete(job.id)
      for (const w of ws) w(job)
    }
  }

  private trimFinished(): void {
    const keep = this.opts.keepFinished ?? 500
    const finished = this.list().filter((j) => TERMINAL.includes(j.status))
    for (const j of finished.slice(0, Math.max(0, finished.length - keep))) {
      if (!this.waiters.has(j.id)) this.jobs.delete(j.id)
    }
  }

  private persistTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null

  private changed(): void {
    for (const l of this.listeners) l()
    // Gom nhiá»u thay Ä‘á»•i (tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™ %) thÃ nh má»™t láº§n ghi Ä‘Ä©a
    if (this.opts.persistPath && !this.persistTimer) {
      this.persistTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.persistTimer = null
        void this.persist()
      }, 400)
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ lÆ°u / khÃ´i phá»¥c

  persist(): Promise<void> {
    const file = this.opts.persistPath
    if (!file) return Promise.resolve()
    const data: PersistShape = {
      version: 1,
      paused: this.paused,
      concurrency: this.concurrency,
      seq: this.seq,
      // Chá»‰ lÆ°u job chÆ°a xong + job lá»—i (Ä‘á»ƒ thá»­ láº¡i); job xong náº±m trong cache
      jobs: this.list().filter((j) => j.status === 'queued' || j.status === 'running' || j.status === 'failed'),
      cache: [...this.cache.entries()]
    }
    this.persistChain = this.persistChain
      .then(async () => {
        await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true })
        const tmp = `${file}.tmp`
        await fs.writeFile(tmp, JSON.stringify(data), 'utf8')
        await fs.rename(tmp, file)
      })
      .catch(() => undefined)
    return this.persistChain
  }

  async restore(): Promise<void> {
    const file = this.opts.persistPath
    if (!file) return
    let data: PersistShape
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8')) as PersistShape
    } catch {
      return
    }
    this.seq = Math.max(this.seq, data.seq ?? 0)
    this.concurrency = this.clampConcurrency(data.concurrency ?? this.concurrency)
    for (const [k, v] of data.cache ?? []) this.cache.set(k, v)
    let pending = 0
    for (const j of data.jobs ?? []) {
      if (!this.handlers.has(j.type)) continue
      if (j.status === 'running' || j.status === 'queued') {
        j.status = 'queued'
        j.nextRunAt = 0
        j.error = j.error ?? 'KhÃ´i phá»¥c sau khi má»Ÿ láº¡i app'
        pending++
      }
      this.jobs.set(j.id, j)
    }
    // CÃ³ viá»‡c dá»Ÿ dang â‡’ khÃ´i phá»¥c á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i táº¡m dá»«ng, ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng báº¥m "Tiáº¿p tá»¥c"
    this.paused = pending > 0 ? true : !!data.paused
    this.changed()
  }
}

/** Giao thá»©c tin nháº¯n giá»¯a main â‡„ utility process render */
import type { CompositionProps } from '../../shared/composition'

export interface RenderRequest {
  /** ÄÆ°á»ng dáº«n tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i tá»›i src/remotion/index.ts */
  entryPoint: string
  inputProps: CompositionProps
  outputPath: string
}

export type RenderStage = 'bundle' | 'browser' | 'render'

export interface RenderProgress {
  stage: RenderStage
  /** 0..1 trong stage hiá»‡n táº¡i */
  progress: number
}

export type WorkerInbound =
  | { type: 'render'; jobId: string; request: RenderRequest }
  | { type: 'cancel'; jobId: string }

export type WorkerOutbound =
  | { type: 'progress'; jobId: string; progress: RenderProgress }
  | { type: 'done'; jobId: string }
  | { type: 'error'; jobId: string; message: string; aborted: boolean }
  | { type: 'log'; message: string }

/** Ai cÅ©ng render Ä‘Æ°á»£c qua interface nÃ y: utility process (app) hoáº·c gá»i trá»±c tiáº¿p (smoke test) */
export interface VideoRenderer {
  render(req: RenderRequest, onProgress: (p: RenderProgress) => void, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void>
}

/**
 * LÃµi render Remotion: bundle composition (cache theo entryPoint) â†’ Ä‘áº£m báº£o cÃ³ Chrome Headless Shell
 * â†’ selectComposition â†’ renderMedia ra MP4 (H.264 + AAC).
 * Cháº¡y trong utility process (app) hoáº·c trá»±c tiáº¿p trong Node (smoke test).
 */
import { bundle } from '@remotion/bundler'
import { ensureBrowser, makeCancelSignal, renderMedia, selectComposition } from '@remotion/renderer'
import { COMPOSITION_ID } from '../../shared/composition'
import type { RenderProgress, RenderRequest } from './protocol'

const bundleCache = new Map<string, Promise<string>>()

function getBundle(entryPoint: string, onProgress: (p: RenderProgress) => void): Promise<string> {
  let cached = bundleCache.get(entryPoint)
  if (!cached) {
    cached = bundle({
      entryPoint,
      onProgress: (pct: number) => onProgress({ stage: 'bundle', progress: pct / 100 })
    })
    bundleCache.set(entryPoint, cached)
    cached.catch(() => bundleCache.delete(entryPoint))
  }
  return cached
}

export async function renderVideo(
  req: RenderRequest,
  onProgress: (p: RenderProgress) => void,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<void> {
  const serveUrl = await getBundle(req.entryPoint, onProgress)
  onProgress({ stage: 'bundle', progress: 1 })
  if (signal?.aborted) throw new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError')

  // Láº§n Ä‘áº§u: táº£i Chrome Headless Shell (~100MB), cÃ¡c láº§n sau dÃ¹ng láº¡i
  await ensureBrowser({
    onBrowserDownload: () => ({
      version: null,
      onProgress: ({ percent }) => onProgress({ stage: 'browser', progress: percent })
    })
  })
  onProgress({ stage: 'browser', progress: 1 })

  const inputProps = req.inputProps as unknown as Record<string, unknown>
  const composition = await selectComposition({ serveUrl, id: COMPOSITION_ID, inputProps })

  const { cancelSignal, cancel } = makeCancelSignal()
  const onAbort = (): void => cancel()
  signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
  try {
    await renderMedia({
      composition,
      serveUrl,
      codec: 'h264',
      audioCodec: 'aac',
      pixelFormat: 'yuv420p',
      outputLocation: req.outputPath,
      inputProps,
      cancelSignal,
      timeoutInMilliseconds: 120000,
      onProgress: ({ progress }) => onProgress({ stage: 'render', progress })
    })
  } finally {
    signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\render\renderService.ts ===
/**
 * Quáº£n lÃ½ utility process render (khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng lÆ°á»i, giá»¯ sá»‘ng Ä‘á»ƒ dÃ¹ng láº¡i bundle Remotion Ä‘Ã£ build).
 */
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
import { utilityProcess, type UtilityProcess } from 'electron'
import { logger } from '../logger'
import type { RenderProgress, RenderRequest, VideoRenderer, WorkerInbound, WorkerOutbound } from './protocol'

interface Pending {
  resolve: () => void
  reject: (err: Error) => void
  onProgress: (p: RenderProgress) => void
}

export class RenderService implements VideoRenderer {
  private child: UtilityProcess | null = null
  private pending = new Map<string, Pending>()

  constructor(private readonly workerPath: string) {}

  private ensureChild(): UtilityProcess {
    if (this.child) return this.child
    const child = utilityProcess.fork(this.workerPath, [], { serviceName: 'Folklore Render', stdio: 'pipe' })
    child.stdout?.on('data', (d: Buffer) => logger.info(d.toString('utf8').trim(), 'render'))
    child.stderr?.on('data', (d: Buffer) => logger.warn(d.toString('utf8').trim(), 'render'))
    child.on('message', (msg: WorkerOutbound) => {
      if (msg.type === 'log') {
        logger.info(msg.message, 'render')
        return
      }
      const p = this.pending.get(msg.jobId)
      if (!p) return
      if (msg.type === 'progress') p.onProgress(msg.progress)
      else if (msg.type === 'done') {
        this.pending.delete(msg.jobId)
        p.resolve()
      } else if (msg.type === 'error') {
        this.pending.delete(msg.jobId)
        const err = new Error(msg.message)
        if (msg.aborted) err.name = 'AbortError'
        p.reject(err)
      }
    })
    child.on('exit', (code) => {
      logger.warn(`Render worker thoÃ¡t (mÃ£ ${code})`, 'render')
      for (const [, p] of this.pending) p.reject(new Error(`Tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh render dá»«ng Ä‘á»™t ngá»™t (mÃ£ ${code})`))
      this.pending.clear()
      this.child = null
    })
    this.child = child
    return child
  }

  render(req: RenderRequest, onProgress: (p: RenderProgress) => void, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      if (signal?.aborted) return reject(new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError'))
      const jobId = randomUUID()
      const child = this.ensureChild()
      this.pending.set(jobId, { resolve, reject, onProgress })
      signal?.addEventListener(
        'abort',
        () => child.postMessage({ type: 'cancel', jobId } satisfies WorkerInbound),
        { once: true }
      )
      child.postMessage({ type: 'render', jobId, request: req } satisfies WorkerInbound)
    })
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.child?.kill()
    this.child = null
  }
}

/**
 * Utility process render: nháº­n lá»‡nh tá»« main qua parentPort, render báº±ng Remotion, bÃ¡o tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™.
 * TÃ¡ch riÃªng khá»i main â‡’ webpack bundle + Chrome + mÃ£ hoÃ¡ video khÃ´ng lÃ m Ä‘á»©ng UI.
 */
import { renderVideo } from './renderCore'
import type { WorkerInbound, WorkerOutbound } from './protocol'

const port = process.parentPort
const controllers = new Map<string, AbortController>()

function post(msg: WorkerOutbound): void {
  port.postMessage(msg)
}

port.on('message', (event: { data: WorkerInbound }) => {
  const msg = event.data
  if (msg.type === 'cancel') {
    controllers.get(msg.jobId)?.abort()
    return
  }
  if (msg.type === 'render') {
    const ac = new AbortController()
    controllers.set(msg.jobId, ac)
    let last = 0
    renderVideo(
      msg.request,
      (progress) => {
        // Giáº£m táº§n suáº¥t gá»­i tin (tá»‘i Ä‘a ~10 láº§n/giÃ¢y)
        const now = Date.now()
        if (now - last < 100 && progress.progress < 1) return
        last = now
        post({ type: 'progress', jobId: msg.jobId, progress })
      },
      ac.signal
    )
      .then(() => post({ type: 'done', jobId: msg.jobId }))
      .catch((err: unknown) => {
        const message = err instanceof Error ? `${err.message}\n${err.stack ?? ''}` : String(err)
        post({ type: 'error', jobId: msg.jobId, message, aborted: ac.signal.aborted })
      })
      .finally(() => controllers.delete(msg.jobId))
  }
})

/**

 *  - áº¢nh cáº£nh 16:9 / 9:16 riÃªng: prompt = artStyle + culturalVisualBible + cáº£nh + nhÃ¢n váº­t + thá»±c thá»ƒ (+ áº£nh tham chiáº¿u nhÃ¢n váº­t Ä‘Ã£ khoÃ¡)

import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import sharp from 'sharp'
import { providersConfig } from '../../shared/config'

import {
  buildCharacterViewPrompt,
  buildExpressionPrompt,
  buildScenePrompt,
  buildStatePrompt,
  MAX_CLEAR_FACES,
  pickReferences,

} from '../../shared/logic/imagePrompt'

export type ImageJobPayload =
  | { kind: 'scene'; storyId: string; sceneId: string; aspect: AspectRatio; providerId: string; autoApply: boolean; nonce: string }
  | { kind: 'sheet'; characterId: string; view: SheetView; providerId: string; storyId: string | null; nonce: string }
  | { kind: 'expression'; characterId: string; expression: string; providerId: string; storyId: string | null; nonce: string }
  | { kind: 'state'; characterId: string; stateId: string; providerId: string; storyId: string | null; nonce: string }
  | {
      kind: 'thumbnail'
      storyId: string
      releaseId: string
      aspect: '16:9' | '9:16'
      visual: string
      text: string
      sub: string
      badge: string
      providerId: string
      nonce: string
    }

export interface ImageJobResult {
  /** TÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i thÆ° má»¥c Story (cáº£nh) hoáº·c thÆ° má»¥c _characters (nhÃ¢n váº­t) */
  path: string
  kind: ImageJobPayload['kind']
  storyId: string | null
  targetId: string
}

export interface SceneGenOptions {
  providerId?: string
  regenerate?: boolean
  autoApply?: boolean
}

const MAX_VARIANTS_PER_ASPECT = 6
const aspectTag = (a: ImageAspect): string => a.replace(':', 'x')
const hash = (v: unknown): string => createHash('sha1').update(JSON.stringify(v)).digest('hex').slice(0, 12)

export class ImageService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repo: StoryRepository,
    private readonly providers: ProviderRegistry,
    private readonly queue: JobQueue,
    private readonly getAutoApply: () => boolean = () => true,
    /** BÃ¡o UI táº£i láº¡i dá»¯ liá»‡u (storyId null = thÆ° viá»‡n nhÃ¢n váº­t) */
    private readonly onChanged: (storyId: string | null) => void = () => undefined
  ) {
    queue.register<ImageJobPayload, ImageJobResult>('image', (p, ctx) => this.runJob(p, ctx))
  }

  private async promptContext(story: Story | null, lib: Library): Promise<PromptContext> {
    const channel = lib.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story?.channelPresetId) ?? lib.channelPresets[0]
    if (!channel) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ kÃªnh (ChannelPreset)')
    return {
      channel,
      folklore: lib.folklore.filter((f) => !story || story.folkloreIds.includes(f.id)),
      characters: story ? charactersForStory(story.id, lib.characters) : lib.characters
    }
  }

  private priceOf(providerId: string): number {
    return this.providers.image(providerId).estimateCost(1)
  }

  private resolveProvider(providerId?: string): string {
    return this.providers.isMock() ? 'mock' : (providerId ?? this.providers.defaultImageProvider())
  }

  /** Mtime + kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c áº£nh tham chiáº¿u â‡’ Ä‘á»•i áº£nh tham chiáº¿u thÃ¬ cache khÃ´ng cÃ²n khá»›p */
  private async refFingerprint(paths: string[]): Promise<string[]> {
    return Promise.all(
      paths.map(async (p) => {
        const st = await fs.stat(p).catch(() => null)
        return `${path.basename(p)}:${st?.size ?? 0}:${st?.mtimeMs ?? 0}`
      })
    )
  }

  // ======================================================================== áº¢nh cáº£nh

  private async scenePlan(story: Story, lib: Library, scene: SceneBankItem, aspect: AspectRatio, providerId: string, nonce: string) {
    const ctx = await this.promptContext(story, lib)
    const { prompt, negativePrompt } = buildScenePrompt(scene, ctx, aspect)
    const max = providersConfig.image.gemini.maxReferenceImages ?? 4
    const refs = pickReferences(scene, ctx.characters, max).map((r) => this.repo.resolveCharacterPath(r.path))
    const model = this.providers.image(providerId).model
    const cacheKey = `image:${hash({ prompt, refs: await this.refFingerprint(refs), model, aspect, nonce })}`
    return { ctx, prompt, negativePrompt, refs, cacheKey }
  }

  async generateScenes(storyId: string, sceneIds: string[], aspect: AspectRatio, opts: SceneGenOptions = {}): Promise<Job[]> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const providerId = this.resolveProvider(opts.providerId)
    const autoApply = opts.autoApply ?? this.getAutoApply()
    const jobs: Job[] = []
    for (const id of sceneIds) {
      const scene = story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === id)
      if (!scene) continue
      const nonce = opts.regenerate ? randomUUID() : ''
      const plan = await this.scenePlan(story, lib, scene, aspect, providerId, nonce)
      const n = story.sceneBank.indexOf(scene) + 1
      const job = this.queue.enqueue<ImageJobPayload>(
        'image',
        { kind: 'scene', storyId, sceneId: id, aspect, providerId, autoApply, nonce },
        { providerId, estimatedCost: this.priceOf(providerId), cacheKey: plan.cacheKey, storyId, label: `Cáº£nh ${n} Â· ${aspect} Â· ${scene.description.slice(0, 50)}` }
      )
      // TrÃºng cache (Ä‘Ã£ táº¡o Ä‘Ãºng áº£nh nÃ y trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³) â‡’ Ã¡p láº¡i ngay, khÃ´ng tá»‘n phÃ­
      if (job.status === 'done' && job.result) await this.applySceneResult(job.result as ImageJobResult, aspect, autoApply, providerId)
      jobs.push(job)
    }
    return jobs
  }

  private async applySceneResult(r: ImageJobResult, aspect: AspectRatio, autoApply: boolean, providerId: string): Promise<void> {
    const abs = this.repo.resolveInStory(r.storyId as string, r.path)
    const meta = await sharp(await fs.readFile(abs)).metadata().catch(() => ({ width: undefined, height: undefined }))
    const provider = this.providers.image(providerId)
    const ref: MediaRef = {
      path: r.path,
      mediaType: 'image',
      source: 'ai',
      provider: provider.id,
      model: provider.model,
      cost: provider.estimateCost(1),
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      width: meta.width,
      height: meta.height
    }
    await this.repo.updateStory(r.storyId as string, (s) => {
      const scene = s.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === r.targetId)
      if (!scene) return
      if (scene.media[aspect]?.path === r.path) return
      if (autoApply) {
        const old = scene.media[aspect]
        if (old && old.provider !== 'focus-crop') scene.variants.unshift({ id: randomUUID(), aspect, media: old, note: 'áº£nh trÆ°á»›c' })
        scene.media[aspect] = ref
        // áº¢nh 16:9 Ä‘á»•i â‡’ báº£n 9:16 cáº¯t tá»± Ä‘á»™ng cÅ© khÃ´ng cÃ²n Ä‘Ãºng, Ä‘á»ƒ pipeline cáº¯t láº¡i
        if (aspect === '16:9' && scene.media['9:16']?.provider === 'focus-crop') scene.media['9:16'] = null
        scene.approval = { status: 'pending' }
        scene.consistency = null
      } else {
        if (!scene.variants.some((v) => v.media.path === r.path)) scene.variants.unshift({ id: randomUUID(), aspect, media: ref, note: 'má»›i â€“ chá» chá»n' })
      }
      const same = scene.variants.filter((v) => v.aspect === aspect)
      for (const v of same.slice(MAX_VARIANTS_PER_ASPECT)) scene.variants.splice(scene.variants.indexOf(v), 1)
    })
    this.onChanged(r.storyId)
  }

  /** Pipeline bÆ°á»›c 6: táº¡o áº£nh 16:9 cÃ²n thiáº¿u cho má»i cáº£nh, chá» xong, bÃ¡o tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™ */
  async ensureSceneImages(storyId: string, signal: AbortSignal, onProgress: (p: number, msg: string) => void): Promise<{ created: number; reused: number }> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    if (story.sceneBank.length === 0) throw new Error('NgÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh trá»‘ng. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: báº¥m "Äá» xuáº¥t ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh" á»Ÿ mÃ n Story.')
    const missing: string[] = []
    for (const s of story.sceneBank) {
      const m = s.media['16:9']
      const ok = m ? await fs.access(this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, m.path)).then(() => true, () => false) : false
      if (!ok) missing.push(s.id)
    }
    if (missing.length === 0) return { created: 0, reused: story.sceneBank.length }
    const providerId = this.resolveProvider()
    const estimate = missing.length * this.priceOf(providerId)
    const bundle = await this.repo.loadBundle(storyId)
    const b = checkBudget(storySpent(story.costs, bundle.releases.map((r) => r.costs)), estimate, story.budget.limitUsd)
    if (!b.ok) throw new Error(`VÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story: Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh áº£nh ${formatUsdShort(estimate)} (${missing.length} áº£nh). CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: tÄƒng ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch hoáº·c báº­t Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock.`)
    logger.info(`Táº¡o ${missing.length} áº£nh cáº£nh 16:9 (${providerId}) â€“ Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh ${formatUsdShort(estimate)}`, storyId)

    const jobs = await this.generateScenes(storyId, missing, '16:9', { providerId, autoApply: true })
    const onAbort = (): void => jobs.forEach((j) => this.queue.cancel(j.id))
    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
    try {
      let finished = 0
      const results = await Promise.all(
        jobs.map((j) =>
          this.queue.wait(j.id).then((r) => {
            finished++
            onProgress(finished / jobs.length, `áº¢nh cáº£nh ${finished}/${jobs.length}`)
            return r
          })
        )
      )
      if (signal.aborted) throw new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError')
      const failed = results.filter((r) => r.status !== 'done')
      if (failed.length) throw new Error(`${failed.length} áº£nh lá»—i: ${failed[0].error ?? ''}. Xem Film Studio â†’ HÃ ng chá» Ä‘á»ƒ thá»­ láº¡i.`)
      return { created: missing.length, reused: story.sceneBank.length - missing.length }
    } finally {
      signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
    }
  }

  // ======================================================================== áº¢nh nhÃ¢n váº­t

  async generateCharacter(
    characterId: string,
    what: 'sheet' | 'expressions' | 'states',
    opts: { providerId?: string; regenerate?: boolean; storyId?: string | null } = {}
  ): Promise<Job[]> {
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const c = lib.characters.find((x) => x.id === characterId)
    if (!c) throw new Error('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y nhÃ¢n váº­t')
    const providerId = this.resolveProvider(opts.providerId)
    const storyId = c.storyId ?? opts.storyId ?? null
    const nonce = (): string => (opts.regenerate ? randomUUID() : '')
    const est = this.priceOf(providerId)
    const enqueue = (payload: ImageJobPayload, label: string, key: string): Job =>
      this.queue.enqueue<ImageJobPayload>('image', payload, { providerId, estimatedCost: est, cacheKey: key, storyId, label })

    if (what === 'sheet') {
      if (c.locked) throw new Error(`"${c.name}" Ä‘Ã£ khoÃ¡ â€“ má»Ÿ khoÃ¡ á»Ÿ mÃ n NhÃ¢n váº­t náº¿u muá»‘n táº¡o láº¡i character sheet`)
      const n = nonce()
      const front = enqueue({ kind: 'sheet', characterId, view: 'front', providerId, storyId, nonce: n }, `${c.name} Â· chÃ­nh diá»‡n`, `sheet:${hash([c, 'front', providerId, n])}`)
      // CÃ¡c gÃ³c cÃ²n láº¡i dÃ¹ng áº£nh chÃ­nh diá»‡n lÃ m tham chiáº¿u â‡’ cháº¡y sau khi chÃ­nh diá»‡n xong
      void this.queue.wait(front.id).then((j) => {
        if (j.status !== 'done') return
        for (const view of SHEET_VIEWS.filter((v) => v !== 'front')) {
          enqueue({ kind: 'sheet', characterId, view, providerId, storyId, nonce: n }, `${c.name} Â· ${SHEET_VIEW_LABEL[view]}`, `sheet:${hash([c, view, providerId, n, (j.result as ImageJobResult | null)?.path])}`)
        }
      })
      return [front]
    }
    if (!c.characterSheet.front) throw new Error(`HÃ£y táº¡o character sheet (áº£nh chÃ­nh diá»‡n) cho "${c.name}" trÆ°á»›c`)
    if (what === 'expressions') {
      if (c.expressionSheet.length === 0) throw new Error('NhÃ¢n váº­t chÆ°a cÃ³ biá»ƒu cáº£m nÃ o trong báº£ng biá»ƒu cáº£m')
      return c.expressionSheet
        .filter((e) => opts.regenerate || !e.imagePath)
        .map((e) => {
          const n = nonce()
          return enqueue({ kind: 'expression', characterId, expression: e.expression, providerId, storyId, nonce: n }, `${c.name} Â· biá»ƒu cáº£m "${e.expression}"`, `expr:${hash([c.characterSheet.front, c.appearance, e.expression, providerId, n])}`)
        })
    }
    if (c.states.length === 0) throw new Error('NhÃ¢n váº­t chÆ°a cÃ³ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i ngoáº¡i hÃ¬nh nÃ o')
    return c.states
      .filter((s) => opts.regenerate || !s.referenceImage)
      .map((s) => {
        const n = nonce()
        return enqueue({ kind: 'state', characterId, stateId: s.id, providerId, storyId, nonce: n }, `${c.name} Â· tráº¡ng thÃ¡i "${s.name}"`, `state:${hash([c.characterSheet.front, c.appearance, s, providerId, n])}`)
      })
  }

  // ======================================================================== Handler job

  /** Thumbnail / áº£nh bÃ¬a báº±ng AI: áº£nh ná»n theo Ã½ tÆ°á»Ÿng (khÃ´ng chá»¯) + app ghÃ©p chá»¯ to */
  async generateThumbnail(storyId: string, releaseId: string, concept: { text: string; visual: string }, opts: { aspect: '16:9' | '9:16'; sub: string; badge: string; providerId?: string }): Promise<Job> {
    const providerId = this.resolveProvider(opts.providerId)
    const nonce = randomUUID()
    return this.queue.enqueue<ImageJobPayload>(
      'image',
      { kind: 'thumbnail', storyId, releaseId, aspect: opts.aspect, visual: concept.visual, text: concept.text, sub: opts.sub, badge: opts.badge, providerId, nonce },
      { providerId, estimatedCost: this.priceOf(providerId), storyId, label: `Thumbnail AI Â· "${concept.text}"`, cacheKey: null }
    )
  }

  private async runThumbnail(p: Extract<ImageJobPayload, { kind: 'thumbnail' }>, ctx: JobContext): Promise<{ result: ImageJobResult; cost: number }> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(p.storyId)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const pc = await this.promptContext(story, lib)
    const prompt = [
      `Art style: ${pc.channel.artStyle}.`,
      `Setting: Vietnamese folk-horror, ${pc.channel.culturalVisualBible.slice(0, 5).join('; ')}.`,
      `Thumbnail image: ${p.visual}. One strong focal subject, high contrast, dramatic rim light, ${p.aspect === '16:9' ? 'subject on the right third, dark empty space on the left for a title' : 'subject in the upper half, dark empty space in the lower half for a title'}.`,
      'Absolutely no text, letters or logos. No gore, no blood.',
      `Avoid: ${pc.channel.negativePrompt}.`
    ].join(' ')
    const dir = path.join(this.repo.releaseDir(p.storyId, p.releaseId), 'thumbs')
    await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true })
    const base = path.join(dir, `ai_base_${p.nonce.slice(0, 8)}.png`)
    ctx.progress(0.1)
    const res = await this.providers.image(p.providerId).generate({
      prompt,
      negativePrompt: pc.channel.negativePrompt,
      aspect: p.aspect,
      outputPath: base,
      referenceImagePaths: [],
      placeholder: { title: 'THUMBNAIL', index: 0, shotType: 'thumbnail', cameraAngle: p.aspect, summary: p.visual, focusPoint: { x: p.aspect === '16:9' ? 0.7 : 0.5, y: 0.35 }, palette: pc.channel.palette },
      signal: ctx.signal
    })
    const { composeThumbnail } = await import('../media/thumbnail')
    const { platformsConfig } = await import('../../shared/config')
    const size = p.aspect === '16:9' ? platformsConfig.publishing.thumbnail.youtube : platformsConfig.publishing.thumbnail.cover
    const file = `ai_${p.nonce.slice(0, 8)}.png`
    await composeThumbnail(base, path.join(dir, file), { ...size, text: p.text, sub: p.sub, badge: p.badge, layout: p.aspect === '16:9' ? 'youtube' : 'cover', accent: pc.channel.palette[4] })
    const rel = toRelPath('releases', p.releaseId, 'thumbs', file)
    await this.repo.updateRelease(p.storyId, p.releaseId, (r) => {
      if (!r.publishing) return
      r.publishing.thumbnails.unshift({ path: rel, mediaType: 'image', source: 'ai', provider: res.provider, model: res.model, cost: res.cost, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), width: size.width, height: size.height })
      r.publishing.selectedThumbnail ??= rel
    })
    this.onChanged(p.storyId)
    return { result: { path: rel, kind: 'thumbnail', storyId: p.storyId, targetId: p.releaseId }, cost: res.cost }
  }

  private async runJob(p: ImageJobPayload, ctx: JobContext): Promise<{ result: ImageJobResult; cost: number }> {
    if (p.kind === 'thumbnail') return this.runThumbnail(p, ctx)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const provider = this.providers.image(p.providerId)

    if (p.kind === 'scene') {
      const story = await this.repo.loadStory(p.storyId)
      const scene = story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === p.sceneId)
      if (!scene) throw new Error(`Cáº£nh ${p.sceneId} khÃ´ng cÃ²n tá»“n táº¡i`)
      const plan = await this.scenePlan(story, lib, scene, p.aspect, p.providerId, p.nonce)
      const file = `${scene.id}_${aspectTag(p.aspect)}_${plan.cacheKey.slice(-8)}.png`
      const abs = path.join(this.repo.storyDir(p.storyId), 'bank', file)
      await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true })
      ctx.progress(0.1)
      const castLabel = scene.cast
        .map((m) => {
          const c = plan.ctx.characters.find((x) => x.id === m.characterId)
          const st = c?.states.find((s) => s.id === m.stateId)
          return `${c?.name ?? m.characterId}${[m.expression, st?.name].filter(Boolean).length ? ` (${[m.expression, st?.name].filter(Boolean).join(', ')})` : ''}`
        })
        .join(' Â· ')
      const res = await provider.generate({
        prompt: plan.prompt,
        negativePrompt: plan.negativePrompt,
        aspect: p.aspect,
        outputPath: abs,
        referenceImagePaths: plan.refs,
        seed: story.sceneBank.indexOf(scene) + 1 + (p.nonce ? Date.now() % 997 : 0),
        placeholder: {
          index: story.sceneBank.indexOf(scene) + 1,
          shotType: scene.shotType,
          cameraAngle: scene.cameraAngle,
          summary: scene.description,
          castLabel: castLabel + (plan.refs.length ? ` Â· ${plan.refs.length} áº£nh tham chiáº¿u` : ''),
          focusPoint: scene.focusPoint,
          palette: plan.ctx.channel.palette
        },
        signal: ctx.signal
      })
      const result: ImageJobResult = { path: toRelPath('bank', file), kind: 'scene', storyId: p.storyId, targetId: p.sceneId }
      await this.applySceneResult(result, p.aspect, p.autoApply, p.providerId)
      return { result, cost: res.cost }
    }

    // ----- nhÃ¢n váº­t
    const c = lib.characters.find((x) => x.id === p.characterId)
    if (!c) throw new Error('NhÃ¢n váº­t khÃ´ng cÃ²n tá»“n táº¡i')
    const story = p.storyId ? await this.repo.loadStory(p.storyId).catch(() => null) : null
    const pctx = await this.promptContext(story, lib)
    const dir = path.join(this.repo.charactersRoot, c.id)
    await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true })
    const front = c.characterSheet.front ? this.repo.resolveCharacterPath(c.characterSheet.front) : null

    let prompt: string
    let aspect: ImageAspect = '1:1'
    let refs: string[] = []
    let title: string
    let name: string
    if (p.kind === 'sheet') {
      prompt = buildCharacterViewPrompt(c, p.view, pctx)
      aspect = SHEET_VIEW_ASPECT[p.view]
      refs = p.view !== 'front' && front ? [front] : []
      title = `${c.name} Â· ${SHEET_VIEW_LABEL[p.view]}`
      name = p.view

      prompt = buildExpressionPrompt(c, p.expression, pctx)
      refs = front ? [front] : []
      title = `${c.name} Â· ${p.expression}`
      name = `expr-${slug(p.expression)}`
    } else {
      const st = c.states.find((s) => s.id === p.stateId)
      if (!st) throw new Error('Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i khÃ´ng cÃ²n tá»“n táº¡i')
      prompt = buildStatePrompt(c, p.stateId, pctx)
      aspect = '9:16'
      refs = front ? [front] : []
      title = `${c.name} Â· ${st.name}`
      name = `state-${slug(st.name)}`

    const file = `${name}_${hash([prompt, p.nonce, refs.length]).slice(0, 8)}.png`
    const abs = path.join(dir, file)
    ctx.progress(0.1)
    const res = await provider.generate({
      prompt,
      negativePrompt: pctx.channel.negativePrompt,
      aspect,
      outputPath: abs,
      referenceImagePaths: refs,
      placeholder: {
        title,
        index: 0,
        shotType: p.kind === 'sheet' ? SHEET_VIEW_LABEL[p.view] : p.kind === 'expression' ? 'biá»ƒu cáº£m' : 'tráº¡ng thÃ¡i',
        cameraAngle: c.role === 'entity' ? 'thá»±c thá»ƒ' : `${c.age ?? '?'} tuá»•i`,
        summary: `${c.appearance} ${c.defaultOutfit}`,

  async updateScene(storyId: string, sceneId: string, patch: Partial<Pick<SceneBankItem, 'imagePrompt' | 'description' | 'focusPoint' | 'cast' | 'approval' | 'shotType' | 'cameraAngle' | 'needsVerticalVersion'>>): Promise<Story> {

  async applyVariant(storyId: string, sceneId: string, variantId: string): Promise<Story> {
    return this.repo.updateStory(storyId, (s) => {
      const scene = s.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === sceneId)
      const v = scene?.variants.find((x) => x.id === variantId)
      if (!scene || !v) throw new Error('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y biáº¿n thá»ƒ')
      const aspect = v.aspect as AspectRatio
      const cur = scene.media[aspect]
      scene.variants = scene.variants.filter((x) => x.id !== variantId)
      if (cur && cur.provider !== 'focus-crop') scene.variants.unshift({ id: randomUUID(), aspect, media: cur, note: 'áº£nh trÆ°á»›c' })
      scene.media[aspect] = v.media
      if (aspect === '16:9' && scene.media['9:16']?.provider === 'focus-crop') scene.media['9:16'] = null
      scene.approval = { status: 'pending' }
      scene.consistency = null
    })
  }

  private async imageB64(abs: string, size: number): Promise<string> {
    const buf = await sharp(await fs.readFile(abs)).resize(size, size, { fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true }).jpeg({ quality: 80 }).toBuffer()
    return buf.toString('base64')
  }

  async checkConsistency(storyId: string, sceneIds: string[]): Promise<Story> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const llm = this.providers.llm()
    for (const id of sceneIds) {
      const scene = story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === id)
      const media = scene?.media['16:9'] ?? scene?.media['9:16']
      if (!scene || !media) continue
      const members = scene.cast
        .map((m) => ({ m, c: lib.characters.find((x) => x.id === m.characterId) }))
        .filter((x): x is { m: SceneBankItem['cast'][number]; c: Character } => !!x.c && x.c.role !== 'entity' && !!x.c.characterSheet.front)
      let result: { ok: boolean; issues: string[] }
      if (members.length === 0) {
        result = { ok: true, issues: [] }
      } else {
        const images = [{ mimeType: 'image/jpeg', base64: await this.imageB64(this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, media.path), 768), label: 'SCENE' }]
        for (const { m, c } of members) {
          images.push({ mimeType: 'image/jpeg', base64: await this.imageB64(this.repo.resolveCharacterPath(c.characterSheet.front as string), 512), label: `REFERENCE ${c.name}` })
          const st = c.states.find((s) => s.id === m.stateId)
          if (st?.referenceImage) images.push({ mimeType: 'image/jpeg', base64: await this.imageB64(this.repo.resolveCharacterPath(st.referenceImage), 512), label: `REFERENCE ${c.name} â€“ state ${st.name}` })
        }
        const res = await llm.generateJson(
          consistencyTask(
            scene.description,
            members.map(({ m, c }) => ({ name: c.name, appearance: c.appearance, outfit: c.defaultOutfit, state: c.states.find((s) => s.id === m.stateId)?.name ?? '' })),
            images
          )
        )
        if (res.cost > 0) {
          await this.repo.updateStory(storyId, (s) => {
            s.costs.actual += res.cost
            s.costs.entries.push({ provider: llm.id, kind: 'llm', units: res.inputTokens + res.outputTokens, cost: res.cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
          })
        }
        result = { ok: res.data.ok && res.data.issues.length === 0, issues: res.data.issues.map((i) => `${i.characterName}: ${i.problem}`) }
      }
      await this.repo.updateStory(storyId, (s) => {
        const sc = s.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === id)
        if (sc) sc.consistency = { checkedAt: new Date().toISOString(), ok: result.ok, issues: result.issues, characterIds: members.map((x) => x.c.id), provider: llm.id }
      })
    }
    return this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
  }

  // ======================================================================== Xuáº¥t ZIP

  async exportZip(storyId: string, outPath: string, maxEdge: number | null): Promise<{ path: string; files: number; bytes: number }> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const entries: ZipEntry[] = []
    const add = async (abs: string, name: string): Promise<void> => {
      const raw = await fs.readFile(abs).catch(() => null)
      if (!raw) return
      const data = maxEdge ? await sharp(raw).resize(maxEdge, maxEdge, { fit: 'inside', withoutEnlargement: true }).png().toBuffer() : raw
      entries.push({ name, data })
    }
    for (const [i, s] of story.sceneBank.entries()) {
      for (const a of ['16:9', '9:16'] as const) {
        const m = s.media[a]
        if (m) await add(this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, m.path), `canh/${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}_${s.id}_${aspectTag(a)}.png`)
      }
    }
    for (const c of charactersForStory(storyId, lib.characters)) {
      const folder = `nhan-vat/${slug(c.name)}`
      for (const v of SHEET_VIEWS) if (c.characterSheet[v]) await add(this.repo.resolveCharacterPath(c.characterSheet[v] as string), `${folder}/${v}.png`)
      for (const e of c.expressionSheet) if (e.imagePath) await add(this.repo.resolveCharacterPath(e.imagePath), `${folder}/bieu-cam_${slug(e.expression)}.png`)
      for (const s of c.states) if (s.referenceImage) await add(this.repo.resolveCharacterPath(s.referenceImage), `${folder}/trang-thai_${slug(s.name)}.png`)
    }
    if (entries.length === 0) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh nÃ o Ä‘á»ƒ xuáº¥t')
    const zip = createZip(entries)
    await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(outPath), { recursive: true })
    await fs.writeFile(outPath, zip)
    logger.info(`ÄÃ£ xuáº¥t ${entries.length} áº£nh â†’ ${outPath}`, storyId)
    return { path: outPath, files: entries.length, bytes: zip.length }
  }
}

 *  - Má»©c 2 parallax 2.5D: depth map local (onnxruntime) â†’ 3 lá»›p PNG; cache theo áº£nh nguá»“n cá»§a tá»«ng khung.
 *  - Má»©c 3 video AI cho cáº£nh hero: bÃ¡o giÃ¡ trÆ°á»›c, táº¡o qua hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (job "video"), lá»—i â‡’ pipeline quay vá» Má»©c 1/2.

import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import { buildVideoPrompt } from '../../shared/logic/imagePrompt'
import { charactersForStory } from '../../shared/logic/voices'

export interface VideoJobPayload {

  sceneId: string
  aspect: AspectRatio
  providerId: string
  seconds: number
  nonce: string
}

const tag = (a: AspectRatio): string => a.replace(':', 'x')
const exists = (p: string): Promise<boolean> => fs.access(p).then(() => true, () => false)

export interface HeroQuote {
  providerId: string
  seconds: number
  /** Cáº£nh cáº§n táº¡o clip má»›i */
  needed: string[]
  /** Cáº£nh Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ clip Ä‘Ãºng khung (dÃ¹ng láº¡i, khÃ´ng tá»‘n phÃ­) */
  reused: string[]
  cost: number
}

export class MotionService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repo: StoryRepository,
    private readonly providers: ProviderRegistry,
    private readonly queue: JobQueue,
    private readonly depth: DepthEstimator,
    private readonly onChanged: (storyId: string) => void = () => undefined
  ) {
    queue.register<VideoJobPayload, { path: string }>('video', (p, ctx) => this.runVideoJob(p, ctx))
  }

  // ======================================================================== Parallax

  /** TÃ¡ch lá»›p parallax cho cáº£nh á»Ÿ khung aspect (dÃ¹ng láº¡i náº¿u áº£nh nguá»“n khÃ´ng Ä‘á»•i vÃ  file cÃ²n) */
  async computeParallax(storyId: string, sceneId: string, aspect: AspectRatio, force = false): Promise<ParallaxLayers> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const scene = story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === sceneId)
    const media = scene?.media[aspect]
    if (!scene || !media) throw new Error(`Cáº£nh ${sceneId} chÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh ${aspect}`)
    const cur = scene.parallax?.[aspect]
    if (!force && cur && cur.sourcePath === media.path && (await exists(this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, cur.layers[0])))) return cur

    const h = createHash('sha1').update(media.path + media.createdAt).digest('hex').slice(0, 8)
    const base = `${scene.id}_${tag(aspect)}_${h}`
    const rel = (s: string): string => toRelPath('bank', 'parallax', `${base}_${s}.png`)
    const abs = (r: string): string => this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, r)
    await fs.mkdir(path.join(this.repo.storyDir(storyId), 'bank', 'parallax'), { recursive: true })
    const layersRel: [string, string, string] = [rel('l0'), rel('l1'), rel('l2')]
    const res = await buildParallaxLayers(
      abs(media.path),
      { depth: abs(rel('depth')), layers: [abs(layersRel[0]), abs(layersRel[1]), abs(layersRel[2])] },
      this.depth,
      aspect === '16:9' ? scene.focusPoint : { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }
    )
    const p: ParallaxLayers = { sourcePath: media.path, depthPath: rel('depth'), layers: layersRel, depthSource: res.source, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() }
    await this.repo.updateStory(storyId, (s) => {
      const sc = s.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === sceneId)
      if (!sc) return
      sc.parallax = { ...(sc.parallax ?? {}), [aspect]: p }
      if (aspect === '16:9') sc.depthMapPath = p.depthPath
    })
    this.onChanged(storyId)
    return p
  }

  /** BÆ°á»›c 17: tÃ­nh lá»›p cho danh sÃ¡ch cáº£nh; cáº£nh lá»—i tráº£ vá» Ä‘á»ƒ pipeline quay vá» Ken Burns */
  async ensureParallax(storyId: string, sceneIds: string[], aspect: AspectRatio, signal: AbortSignal, onProgress: (p: number) => void): Promise<{ failed: string[]; heuristic: number }> {
    const failed: string[] = []
    let heuristic = 0
    const uniq = [...new Set(sceneIds)]
    for (const [i, id] of uniq.entries()) {
      if (signal.aborted) throw new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError')
      try {
        const p = await this.computeParallax(storyId, id, aspect)
        if (p.depthSource === 'heuristic') heuristic++
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn(`Parallax cáº£nh ${id} lá»—i: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)} â€“ dÃ¹ng Ken Burns`, storyId)
        failed.push(id)
      }
      onProgress((i + 1) / uniq.length)
    }
    return { failed, heuristic }
  }

  // ======================================================================== Video AI (cáº£nh hero)

  async quote(story: Story, sceneBankIds: string[], aspect: AspectRatio, providerId?: string): Promise<HeroQuote> {
    const pid = providerId ?? this.providers.defaultVideoProvider()
    const provider = this.providers.video(pid)
    const needed: string[] = []
    const reused: string[] = []
    for (const id of [...new Set(sceneBankIds)]) {
      const clip = story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === id)?.heroClip[aspect]
      if (clip && (await exists(this.repo.resolveInStory(story.id, clip.path)))) reused.push(id)
      else needed.push(id)
    }
    return { providerId: provider.id, seconds: provider.clipSeconds, needed, reused, cost: provider.estimateCost(provider.clipSeconds) * needed.length }
  }

  enqueueClips(storyId: string, sceneBankIds: string[], aspect: AspectRatio, providerId: string, regenerate = false): Job[] {
    const provider = this.providers.video(providerId)
    return sceneBankIds.map((sceneId) =>
      this.queue.enqueue<VideoJobPayload>(
        'video',
        { storyId, sceneId, aspect, providerId: provider.id, seconds: provider.clipSeconds, nonce: regenerate ? randomUUID() : '' },
        {
          providerId: provider.id === 'mock' ? 'mock' : provider.id,
          estimatedCost: provider.estimateCost(provider.clipSeconds),
          storyId,
          label: `Video AI Â· cáº£nh ${sceneId} Â· ${aspect} Â· ${provider.clipSeconds}s`,
          cacheKey: regenerate ? null : `video:${storyId}:${sceneId}:${aspect}:${provider.id}:${provider.model}`
        }
      )
    )
  }

  /** Táº¡o clip cho cÃ¡c cáº£nh vÃ  chá» xong; tráº£ vá» cáº£nh thÃ nh cÃ´ng / tháº¥t báº¡i */
  async generateClips(storyId: string, sceneBankIds: string[], aspect: AspectRatio, providerId: string, signal: AbortSignal, onProgress: (p: number) => void): Promise<{ ok: string[]; failed: { id: string; error: string }[] }> {
    const jobs = this.enqueueClips(storyId, sceneBankIds, aspect, providerId)
    const onAbort = (): void => jobs.forEach((j) => this.queue.cancel(j.id))
    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
    try {
      let n = 0
      const done = await Promise.all(
        jobs.map((j) =>
          this.queue.wait(j.id).then((r) => {
            onProgress(++n / jobs.length)
            return r
          })
        )
      )
      if (signal.aborted) throw new DOMException('ÄÃ£ huá»·', 'AbortError')
      return {
        ok: done.filter((j) => j.status === 'done').map((j) => (j.payload as VideoJobPayload).sceneId),
        failed: done.filter((j) => j.status !== 'done').map((j) => ({ id: (j.payload as VideoJobPayload).sceneId, error: j.error ?? j.status }))
      }
    } finally {
      signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort)
    }
  }

  private async runVideoJob(p: VideoJobPayload, ctx: JobContext): Promise<{ result: { path: string }; cost: number }> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(p.storyId)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const scene = story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === p.sceneId)
    const media = scene?.media[p.aspect]
    if (!scene || !media) throw new Error(`Cáº£nh ${p.sceneId} chÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh ${p.aspect}`)
    const channel = lib.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story.channelPresetId) ?? lib.channelPresets[0]
    const prompt = buildVideoPrompt(scene, { channel, folklore: lib.folklore.filter((f) => story.folkloreIds.includes(f.id)), characters: charactersForStory(story.id, lib.characters) }, p.seconds)
    const file = `${scene.id}_${tag(p.aspect)}_hero_${createHash('sha1').update(media.path + p.nonce + p.providerId).digest('hex').slice(0, 8)}.mp4`
    const abs = path.join(this.repo.storyDir(p.storyId), 'bank', file)
    ctx.progress(0.05)
    const provider = this.providers.video(p.providerId)
    const res = await provider.imageToVideo({ imagePath: this.repo.resolveInStory(p.storyId, media.path), prompt, aspect: p.aspect, durationSec: p.seconds, outputPath: abs, signal: ctx.signal })
    const ref: MediaRef = {
      path: toRelPath('bank', file),
      mediaType: 'video',
      source: 'ai',
      provider: res.provider,
      model: res.model,
      cost: res.cost,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      durationSec: res.duration
    }
    await this.repo.updateStory(p.storyId, (s) => {
      const sc = s.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === p.sceneId)
      if (sc) sc.heroClip[p.aspect] = ref
    })
    this.onChanged(p.storyId)
    return { result: { path: ref.path }, cost: res.cost }
  }

export class PublishService {
  constructor(private readonly d: PublishDeps) {}

  private get repo(): StoryRepository {
    return this.d.repo
  }

  private async ctx(storyId: string, releaseId: string): Promise<{ story: Story; release: Release; lib: Library }> {
    const [story, release, lib] = await Promise.all([this.repo.loadStory(storyId), this.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId), this.repo.loadLibrary()])
    return { story, release, lib }
  }

  private emptyPackage(): PublishingPackage {
    return { aiDisclosureNeeded: true, checklist: [], thumbnails: [], selectedThumbnail: null, shorts: null, generatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }
  }

  // ======================================================================== GÃ³i xuáº¥t báº£n

  async generatePackage(storyId: string, releaseId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<{ release: Release; cost: number }> {
    const { story, release, lib } = await this.ctx(storyId, releaseId)
    const llm = this.d.providers.llm()
    const folklore = lib.folklore.filter((f) => story.folkloreIds.includes(f.id))
    const input: PublishInput = {
      platform: release.platform,
      language: release.language,
      storyName: story.name,
      premiseVi: story.premiseVi,
      folkloreNames: folklore.map((f) => (release.language === 'en' ? f.nameEn : f.nameVi)),
      scriptExcerpt: release.script.lines.slice(0, 14).map((l) => l.text).join('\n').slice(0, 2500),
      partIndex: release.partIndex,
      partCount: release.partCount
    }
    const pkg: PublishingPackage = { ...this.emptyPackage(), ...(release.publishing ?? {}) }
    let cost = 0
    if (release.platform === 'tiktok') {
      const req = tiktokPackTask(input)
      req.signal = signal
      const r = await llm.generateJson(req)
      cost += r.cost
      const partLabel = release.partCount > 1 ? `Pháº§n ${release.partIndex}/${release.partCount}` : ''
      let caption = r.data.captionVi.trim()
      if (partLabel && !caption.includes(partLabel)) caption = `${partLabel} Â· ${caption}`
      pkg.tiktok = { captionVi: caption, hashtags: normalizeHashtags(r.data.hashtags), coverConcepts: r.data.coverConcepts, partLabel }
    } else {
      const req = youtubePackTask(input)
      req.signal = signal
      const r = await llm.generateJson(req)
      cost += r.cost
      const glossary = lib.pronunciations.filter((p) => p.language === release.language)
      const desc = (intro: string, language: 'en' | 'vi'): string =>
        buildYoutubeDescription({ intro, chapters: release.chapters, glossary, folklore, fictionNote: story.fictionNoteVi, language })
      pkg.youtube = {
        titleOptions: r.data.titleOptions.map((t) => t.title),
        titleOptionsVi: r.data.titleOptions.map((t) => t.titleVi),
        description: desc(r.data.descriptionIntro, release.language),
        descriptionVi: desc(r.data.descriptionIntroVi, 'vi'),
        tags: normalizeTags(r.data.tags),
        thumbnailConcepts: r.data.thumbnailConcepts,
        shortsHook: r.data.shortsHook,
        shortsHookVi: r.data.shortsHookVi,
        shortsCta: r.data.shortsCta,
        shortsCtaVi: r.data.shortsCtaVi
      }
    }
    pkg.aiDisclosureNeeded = aiDisclosureNeeded(release)
    pkg.generatedAt = new Date().toISOString()
    const updated = await this.repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (rel) => {
      rel.publishing = pkg
      if (cost > 0) {
        rel.costs.actual += cost
        rel.costs.entries.push({ provider: llm.id, kind: 'llm', units: 1, cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
      }
    })
    await this.refreshChecklist(storyId, releaseId)
    return { release: await this.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId).catch(() => updated), cost }
  }

  async updatePackage(storyId: string, releaseId: string, pkg: PublishingPackage): Promise<Release> {
    await this.repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (r) => {
      const prev = r.publishing ?? this.emptyPackage()
      // KhÃ´ng cho renderer Ä‘á»•i danh sÃ¡ch thumbnail / Shorts (chá»‰ chá»n); checklist tay giá»¯ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i
      r.publishing = {
        ...prev,
        youtube: pkg.youtube ? { ...pkg.youtube, tags: normalizeTags(pkg.youtube.tags) } : prev.youtube,
        tiktok: pkg.tiktok ? { ...pkg.tiktok, hashtags: normalizeHashtags(pkg.tiktok.hashtags, 12) } : prev.tiktok,
        selectedThumbnail: pkg.selectedThumbnail && prev.thumbnails.some((t) => t.path === pkg.selectedThumbnail) ? pkg.selectedThumbnail : prev.selectedThumbnail,
        checklist: prev.checklist.map((c) => (c.auto ? c : { ...c, done: pkg.checklist.find((x) => x.id === c.id)?.done ?? c.done }))
      }
    })
    return this.refreshChecklist(storyId, releaseId)
  }

  // ======================================================================== Checklist

  async facts(story: Story, release: Release, lib: Library): Promise<ChecklistFacts> {
    const voices = [...new Set(release.script.lines.map((l) => resolveVoice(l.speakerId, release.language, lib, story.channelPresetId)))].filter(Boolean)
    const usedBank = new Set(release.scenes.map((s) => s.sceneBankId))
    const aspect = release.aspectRatio
    const mockMedia =
      voices.some((v) => v?.providerId === 'mock') ||
      story.sceneBank.some((b) => usedBank.has(b.id) && (b.media[aspect]?.provider === 'mock' || b.media['16:9']?.provider === 'mock')) ||
      release.scenes.some((s) => s.motion.level === 'aiVideo' && story.sceneBank.find((b) => b.id === s.sceneBankId)?.heroClip[aspect]?.provider === 'mock')
    const assets = new Map(lib.soundAssets.map((a) => [a.id, a]))
    const exportsDir = path.join(this.repo.releaseDir(story.id, release.id), 'exports')
    const files = (await fs.readdir(exportsDir).catch(() => [] as string[])).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.mp4') && !f.includes('_shorts_'))
    const p = release.publishing
    const partLabel = release.partCount > 1 ? `Pháº§n ${release.partIndex}/${release.partCount}` : ''
    return {
      voicesAllLicensed: voices.length > 0 && voices.every((v) => v?.commercialLicenseConfirmed),
      usesMockMedia: mockMedia,
      soundAllLicensed: release.soundCues.every((c) => c.kind === 'silence' || assets.get(c.assetId ?? '')?.licenseConfirmed),
      hasRender: files.length > 0,
      hasThumbnail: !!p?.selectedThumbnail,
      isSeries: release.partCount > 1,
      partLabelInCaption: !partLabel || !!p?.tiktok?.captionVi.includes(partLabel),
      fictionNoteInDescription: release.platform === 'tiktok' ? true : /fictional|hÆ° cáº¥u/i.test(p?.youtube?.description ?? ''),
      hasDescriptionOrCaption: !!(p?.youtube?.description || p?.tiktok?.captionVi)
    }
  }

  async refreshChecklist(storyId: string, releaseId: string): Promise<Release> {
    const { story, release, lib } = await this.ctx(storyId, releaseId)
    const f = await this.facts(story, release, lib)
    return this.repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (r) => {
      r.publishing ??= this.emptyPackage()
      r.publishing.checklist = buildChecklist(f, r.publishing.checklist)
    })
  }

  // ======================================================================== Thumbnail

  /** Cáº£nh lÃ m ná»n thumbnail máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh: cáº£nh hero Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn, náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ thÃ¬ cáº£nh cá»§a beat cÄƒng nháº¥t */
  pickThumbnailScene(story: Story, release: Release): string | null {
    const hero = release.scenes.find((s) => s.isHero)
    if (hero) return hero.sceneBankId
    const beats = new Map(story.outline.beats.map((b) => [b.id, b.emotionalIntensity]))
    const best = [...release.scenes].sort((a, b) => {
      const ia = beats.get(story.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === a.sceneBankId)?.beatId ?? '') ?? 0
      const ib = beats.get(story.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === b.sceneBankId)?.beatId ?? '') ?? 0
      return ib - ia
    })[0]
    return best?.sceneBankId ?? story.sceneBank[0]?.id ?? null
  }

  async quickThumbnail(storyId: string, releaseId: string, opts: { sceneBankId?: string; text?: string } = {}): Promise<Release> {
    const { story, release, lib } = await this.ctx(storyId, releaseId)
    const sceneId = opts.sceneBankId ?? this.pickThumbnailScene(story, release)
    const scene = story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === sceneId)
    const yt = release.platform === 'youtube'
    const aspect = yt ? '16:9' : '9:16'
    const media = scene?.media[aspect] ?? scene?.media['16:9']
    if (!scene || !media) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh cáº£nh Ä‘á»ƒ lÃ m thumbnail â€“ cháº¡y bÆ°á»›c 6 trÆ°á»›c')
    const concept = yt ? release.publishing?.youtube?.thumbnailConcepts[0] : release.publishing?.tiktok?.coverConcepts[0]
    const text = opts.text ?? concept?.text ?? story.name
    const folk = lib.folklore.find((f) => story.folkloreIds.includes(f.id))
    const ch = lib.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story.channelPresetId)
    const size = yt ? platformsConfig.publishing.thumbnail.youtube : platformsConfig.publishing.thumbnail.cover
    const dir = path.join(this.repo.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId), 'thumbs')
    await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true })
    const file = `quick_${createHash('sha1').update(media.path + text + Date.now()).digest('hex').slice(0, 8)}.png`
    await composeThumbnail(
      this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, media.path),
      path.join(dir, file),
      {
        ...size,
        text,
        sub: folk ? (release.language === 'en' ? folk.nameEn : folk.nameVi) : undefined,
        badge: release.partCount > 1 ? `PHáº¦N ${release.partIndex}/${release.partCount}` : undefined,
        layout: yt ? 'youtube' : 'cover',
        accent: ch?.palette[4]
      },
      aspect === '16:9' ? scene.focusPoint : { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }
    )
    const ref: MediaRef = { path: toRelPath('releases', releaseId, 'thumbs', file), mediaType: 'image', source: 'own', provider: 'quick-thumbnail', model: `scene ${scene.id} + chá»¯`, cost: 0, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), width: size.width, height: size.height }
    await this.repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (r) => {
      r.publishing ??= this.emptyPackage()
      r.publishing.thumbnails.unshift(ref)
      r.publishing.selectedThumbnail ??= ref.path
    })
    return this.refreshChecklist(storyId, releaseId)
  }

  async cutShorts(storyId: string, releaseId: string, onProgress: (p: number, msg: string) => void = () => undefined, signal?: AbortSignal, range?: { start: number; end: number }): Promise<Release> {
    const { story, release, lib } = await this.ctx(storyId, releaseId)

    const kinds = new Map(release.script.sections.map((s) => [s.id, s.kind]))
    let win = range ? { start: range.start, end: range.end } : null
    if (!win) {
      const w = selectTeaserWindow(
        release.script.lines.filter((l) => l.start != null && l.end != null).map((l) => ({ id: l.id, start: l.start as number, end: l.end as number, beatId: l.beatId, sectionKind: kinds.get(l.sectionId) ?? 'story' })),
        (b) => beats.get(b) ?? 0.5,
        { minSec: sp.minSec - 2, maxSec: sp.maxSec - sp.endCardSec }
      )
      if (!w) throw new Error('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘oáº¡n 30â€“60 giÃ¢y phÃ¹ há»£p (báº£n quÃ¡ ngáº¯n?)')
      win = { start: Math.max(0, w.start - 0.3), end: Math.min(vt.duration, w.end + 0.5) }
    }
    const len = win.end - win.start
    if (len < 10 || len > 60) throw new Error('Äoáº¡n teaser pháº£i dÃ i 10â€“60 giÃ¢y')
    const yt = release.publishing?.youtube
    const en = release.language === 'en'
    const hook = yt?.shortsHook ?? (en ? 'Never answer when the river callsâ€¦' : 'Äá»«ng tráº£ lá»i khi sÃ´ng gá»i tÃªnâ€¦')
    const cta = yt?.shortsCta ?? (en ? 'Full story on the channel' : 'Xem trá»n truyá»‡n trÃªn kÃªnh')

    onProgress(0.05, 'Cáº¯t audio teaser')
    const voiceRel = toRelPath('releases', releaseId, 'voice', 'teaser.wav')
    await cutAudio(this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, vt.mixedPath), this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, voiceRel), win.start, len, sp.endCardSec, signal)

    const ch = lib.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story.channelPresetId)
    const props = buildCompositionProps({
      story,
      release,
      glossary: lib.pronunciations.filter((p) => p.language === release.language),
      subtitleStyle: ch?.subtitleStyleByPlatform.youtubeShorts ?? platformsConfig.subtitleStyles.tiktok,
      mediaUrl: (rel, v) => buildMediaUrl(this.d.mediaBaseUrl, storyId, rel, v),
      fps: platformsConfig.fps,
      sizes: platformsConfig.aspectSizes,
      aspect: '9:16',
      scale: this.d.getRenderScale(),
      parallaxFactors: platformsConfig.parallax.layerFactors,
      window: { start: win.start, end: win.end, voiceRelPath: voiceRel, hookText: hook, ctaTitle: cta, ctaSub: ch?.name.split('/').pop()?.trim() ?? '', hookCardSec: sp.hookCardSec, endCardSec: sp.endCardSec }
    })
    const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:T]/g, '-').slice(0, 16)
    const file = `${storyId}_${releaseId}_shorts_teaser_${stamp}.mp4`
    const out = path.join(this.repo.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId), 'exports', file)
    await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true })
    logger.info(`Cáº¯t Shorts teaser ${win.start.toFixed(1)}â€“${win.end.toFixed(1)}s (${len.toFixed(1)}s + tháº» káº¿t) â†’ ${file}`, releaseId)
    await this.d.renderer.render({ entryPoint: this.d.remotionEntry, inputProps: props, outputPath: out }, (p) => onProgress(0.1 + 0.9 * (p.stage === 'render' ? p.progress : 0), `Render teaser ${Math.round(p.progress * 100)}%`), signal)
    return this.repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (r) => {
      r.publishing ??= this.emptyPackage()
      r.publishing.shorts = { start: win.start, end: win.end, path: toRelPath('releases', releaseId, 'exports', file), hookText: hook, ctaText: cta, createdAt: new Date().toISOString() }
    })
  }

  // ======================================================================== Sá»• hiá»‡u quáº£

  async addPerformanceNote(storyId: string, note: Omit<PerformanceNote, 'id'>): Promise<Story> {
    if (!(note.views >= 0) || !(note.avgWatchPercent >= 0 && note.avgWatchPercent <= 100)) throw new Error('Sá»‘ liá»‡u khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ (lÆ°á»£t xem â‰¥ 0, % xem 0â€“100)')
    return this.repo.updateStory(storyId, (s) => {
      if (!s.releaseIds.includes(note.releaseId)) throw new Error('Báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh khÃ´ng thuá»™c Story nÃ y')
      s.performanceNotes.push({ ...note, id: randomUUID() })
      s.performanceNotes.sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))
    })
  }

  async deletePerformanceNote(storyId: string, noteId: string): Promise<Story> {
    return this.repo.updateStory(storyId, (s) => {
      s.performanceNotes = s.performanceNotes.filter((n) => n.id !== noteId)
    })
  }

  // ======================================================================== Sá»• chi phÃ­

  async costRows(): Promise<{ rows: CostRow[]; budgets: Record<string, number> }> {
    const rows: CostRow[] = []
    const budgets: Record<string, number> = {}
    for (const s of await this.repo.listStories()) {
      const b = await this.repo.loadBundle(s.id)
      budgets[s.id] = b.story.budget.limitUsd
      for (const e of b.story.costs?.entries ?? []) rows.push({ ...e, storyId: s.id, storyName: s.name, releaseId: null, releaseTitle: null })
      for (const r of b.releases) for (const e of r.costs.entries) rows.push({ ...e, storyId: s.id, storyName: s.name, releaseId: r.id, releaseTitle: r.title })
    }
    return { rows, budgets }
  }

  async costReport(month: string): Promise<CostReport> {
    const { rows, budgets } = await this.costRows()
    return buildCostReport(rows, month, budgets)
  }

  async exportCostCsv(month: string, outPath: string): Promise<string> {
    const r = await this.costReport(month)
    await fs.writeFile(outPath, costCsv(r.rows), 'utf8')
    return outPath
  }

  async exportsOf(storyId: string, releaseId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    return exists(path.join(this.repo.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId), 'exports'))
  }

const pad = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, '0')

const SECTION_TITLES: Record<ScriptSectionKind, [string, string]> = {
  hook: ['Hook', 'MÃ³c má»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u'],
  coldOpen: ['Cold open', 'Má»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u láº¡nh'],
  context: ['Background', 'Bá»‘i cáº£nh'],
  story: ['The story', 'CÃ¢u chuyá»‡n'],
  folkloreExplainer: ['About the legend', 'Vá» truyá»n thuyáº¿t'],
  cliffhanger: ['Cliffhanger', 'CÃ¢u treo'],
  outro: ['Outro', 'Káº¿t']
}

export function channelOf(library: Library, story: Story): ChannelPreset {
  const ch = library.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story.channelPresetId)
  if (!ch) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y kÃªnh "${story.channelPresetId}"`)
  return ch
}

export function platformDefaultsOf(channel: ChannelPreset, release: Pick<Release, 'platform'>): PlatformDefaults {
  return channel.platformDefaults[release.platform] ?? platformsConfig.platforms[release.platform]
}

export function narratorOf(library: Library): Character {
  const n =
    library.characters.find((c) => c.scope === 'channel' && c.role === 'narrator') ??
    library.characters.find((c) => c.scope === 'channel')
  if (!n) throw new Error('KÃªnh chÆ°a cÃ³ nhÃ¢n váº­t ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ (scope "channel"). CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: thÃªm á»Ÿ mÃ n KÃªnh & NhÃ¢n váº­t.')
  return n
}

/** Cháº¡y má»™t tÃ¡c vá»¥ LLM, ghi chi phÃ­ vÃ o sá»• cá»§a Release */
export async function runLlm<T>(llm: LlmProvider, req: LlmJsonRequest<T>, ledger: Release['costs']): Promise<T> {
  const res = await llm.generateJson(req)
  if (res.cost > 0) {
    ledger.entries.push({ provider: llm.id, kind: 'llm', units: res.inputTokens + res.outputTokens, cost: res.cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
    ledger.actual += res.cost
  }
  return res.data
}

export function estimateTaskCost(llmId: string, req: LlmJsonRequest<unknown>, expectedOutputChars: number): number {
  const price = llmId === 'mock' ? providersConfig.llm.mock : providersConfig.llm.openai
  return estimateLlmCost(req.system.length + req.user.length, expectedOutputChars, price)
}

function rawToLine(releaseId: string, n: number, sectionId: string, r: RawScriptLine, language: Release['language']): ScriptLine {
  const line: ScriptLine = {
    id: `${releaseId}-l${pad(n)}`,
    sectionId,
    beatId: r.beatId,
    speakerId: r.speakerId,
    text: r.text.trim(),
    delivery: {
      tags: r.tags,
      instructions: r.instructions,
      pace: Math.round(r.pace * 100) / 100,
      pauseBeforeMs: r.pauseBeforeMs,
      pauseAfterMs: r.pauseAfterMs
    },
    takes: [],
    selectedTakeId: null,
    start: null,
    end: null,
    glossaryTermIds: [],
    approval: { status: 'pending' }
  }
  if (language !== 'vi' && r.translationVi) line.translationVi = r.translationVi.trim()
  return line
}

export function toScript(releaseId: string, out: ScriptOut, language: Release['language']): Script {
  const sections: ScriptSection[] = out.sections.map((s) => ({
    id: `${releaseId}-sec-${s.kind}`,
    kind: s.kind,
    title: s.title,
    titleVi: s.titleVi ?? SECTION_TITLES[s.kind][1]
  }))
  const lines = out.lines.map((l, i) => rawToLine(releaseId, i + 1, `${releaseId}-sec-${l.sectionKind}`, l, language))
  // Sáº¯p láº¡i theo thá»© tá»± section (LLM Ä‘Ã´i khi tráº£ láº«n lá»™n)
  const order = new Map(sections.map((s, i) => [s.id, i]))
  lines.sort((a, b) => (order.get(a.sectionId) ?? 0) - (order.get(b.sectionId) ?? 0))
  lines.forEach((l, i) => (l.id = `${releaseId}-l${pad(i + 1)}`))
  return { sections, lines }
}

function section(releaseId: string, kind: ScriptSectionKind, language: Release['language']): ScriptSection {
  const [en, vi] = SECTION_TITLES[kind]
  return { id: `${releaseId}-sec-${kind}`, kind, title: language === 'vi' ? vi : en, titleVi: vi }
}

  repo: StoryRepository
  llm: LlmProvider
  story: Story
  release: Release
  library: Library
  signal?: AbortSignal
  log: (m: string, level?: 'info' | 'warn' | 'error') => void
}
