  script: Script
  /** Release Pháº§n 2..N (chá»‰ series) */
  siblings: Release[]
  /** Id Release pháº§n cÅ© khÃ´ng cÃ²n dÃ¹ng (Ä‘Ã£ gá»¡ khá»i Story) */
  removedIds: string[]
  partCount: number
}

export async function adaptRelease(c: AdaptContext): Promise<AdaptResult> {
  const { story, release, library } = c
  if (story.outline.beats.length === 0) throw new Error('Story chÆ°a cÃ³ dÃ n Ã½ â€“ hÃ£y táº¡o vÃ  duyá»‡t dÃ n Ã½ á»Ÿ mÃ n Story')
  if (story.outline.approval.status !== 'approved') throw new Error('DÃ n Ã½ chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c duyá»‡t â€“ hÃ£y duyá»‡t á»Ÿ mÃ n Story trÆ°á»›c khi chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ')
  const channel = channelOf(library, story)
  const pd = platformDefaultsOf(channel, release)
  const narrator = narratorOf(library)
  const characters = charactersForStory(story.id, library.characters)
  const folklore = library.folklore.filter((f) => story.folkloreIds.includes(f.id))
  const glossary = library.pronunciations.filter((p) => p.language === release.language)
  const isSeries = pd.partDurationSec != null
  const base = { story, folklore, characters, narratorId: narrator.id, platform: release.platform, language: release.language, glossary }

  if (!isSeries) {
    const req = adaptScriptTask({ ...base, structure: pd.structure, targetSec: release.targetDuration })
    req.signal = c.signal
    const out = await runLlm(c.llm, req, release.costs)
    return { script: toScript(release.id, out, release.language), siblings: [], removedIds: [], partCount: 1 }
  }

  // ---------------- Series (TikTok / Shorts)
  if (release.partIndex > 1) throw new Error('ÄÃ¢y lÃ  Pháº§n ' + release.partIndex + ' â€“ hÃ£y chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ láº¡i tá»« Pháº§n 1 cá»§a series')
  const { hookSec, cliffhangerSec, toleranceRatio, minLastPartRatio } = platformsConfig.series
  const bodySec = Math.max(20, (pd.partDurationSec ?? 90) - hookSec - cliffhangerSec)
  const planned = Math.max(1, release.partCount)
  const req = adaptScriptTask({ ...base, structure: ['story'], targetSec: bodySec * planned })
  req.signal = c.signal
  const out = await runLlm(c.llm, req, release.costs)
  const rawLines = out.lines

  const est = (r: RawScriptLine): number =>
    estimateSpeechSeconds(r.text, release.language, r.pace, providersConfig.tts.mock) + (r.pauseBeforeMs + r.pauseAfterMs) / 1000
  const partIds = splitIntoParts(
    rawLines.map((r, i) => ({ id: String(i), beatId: r.beatId, estSec: est(r) })),
    { targetSec: bodySec, toleranceRatio, minLastPartRatio }
  )
  const parts = partIds.map((ids) => ids.map((i) => rawLines[Number(i)]))
  const n = parts.length
  c.log(`Chia series thÃ nh ${n} pháº§n (~${Math.round(bodySec + hookSec + cliffhangerSec)}s/pháº§n)`)

  const beatText = new Map(story.outline.beats.map((b) => [b.id, release.language === 'vi' ? b.summaryVi : b.summaryEn]))
  const hooksReq = seriesHooksTask(
    release.language,
    parts.map((p, i) => ({
      index: i + 1,
      summary: [...new Set(p.map((l) => beatText.get(l.beatId) ?? ''))].join(' / '),
      firstLine: p[0].text,
      lastLine: p[p.length - 1].text
    }))
  )
  hooksReq.signal = c.signal
  const hooks = await runLlm(c.llm, hooksReq, release.costs)

  const seriesGroupId = release.seriesGroupId ?? `series-${release.id}`
  const baseTitle = release.title.replace(/\s*â€“\s*Pháº§n\s*\d+(\/\d+)?\s*$/, '')
  const scripts: Script[] = parts.map((p, i) => {
    const rid = i === 0 ? release.id : `${release.id}-p${i + 1}`
    const endKind: ScriptSectionKind = i === n - 1 ? 'outro' : 'cliffhanger'
    const h = hooks.parts[i]
    const first = p[0]
    const last = p[p.length - 1]
    const mk = (kind: ScriptSectionKind, beatId: string, t: { text: string; tags: string[]; instructions: string }): RawScriptLine => ({
      sectionKind: kind,
      beatId,
      speakerId: narrator.id,
      text: t.text,
      tags: t.tags,
      instructions: t.instructions,
      pace: kind === 'hook' ? 1 : 0.9,
      pauseBeforeMs: kind === 'hook' ? 100 : 400,
      pauseAfterMs: kind === 'hook' ? 600 : 500
    })
    const all = [mk('hook', first.beatId, h.hook), ...p, mk(endKind, last.beatId, h.ending)]
    return toScript(rid, { sections: [{ kind: 'hook', title: 'Hook' }, { kind: 'story', title: 'Story' }, { kind: endKind, title: endKind }], lines: all }, release.language)
  })
  // TiÃªu Ä‘á» section theo ngÃ´n ngá»¯
  for (const s of scripts) s.sections = s.sections.map((sec) => ({ ...section(sec.id.replace(/-sec-.*/, ''), sec.kind, release.language) }))

  const siblings: Release[] = []
  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    const id = `${release.id}-p${i + 1}`
    let sib: Release
    try {
      sib = await c.repo.loadRelease(story.id, id)
    } catch {
      sib = { ...structuredClone(release), id, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), costs: emptyLedger(release.costs.budgetLimit) }
    }
    Object.assign(sib, {
      title: `${baseTitle} â€“ Pháº§n ${i + 1}`,
      seriesGroupId,
      partIndex: i + 1,
      partCount: n,
      script: scripts[i],
      voiceTrack: null,
      scenes: [],
      chapters: [],
      subtitles: [],
      soundCues: [],
      publishing: null,
      pipelineState: null
    })
    siblings.push(sib)
  }
  // Pháº§n thá»«a tá»« láº§n chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ trÆ°á»›c (sá»‘ pháº§n giáº£m) â‡’ gá»¡ khá»i Story
  const removedIds = story.releaseIds.filter((id) => {
    const m = new RegExp(`^${release.id}-p(\\d+)$`).exec(id)
    return m ? Number(m[1]) > n : false
  })
  release.title = `${baseTitle} â€“ Pháº§n 1`
  release.seriesGroupId = seriesGroupId
  release.partCount = n
  return { script: scripts[0], siblings, removedIds, partCount: n }
}

/** LLM viáº¿t láº¡i má»™t cÃ¢u theo chá»‰ dáº«n â€“ tráº£ vá» Release Ä‘Ã£ cáº­p nháº­t (chÆ°a lÆ°u) */
export async function rewriteLine(c: {
  llm: LlmProvider
  release: Release
  library: Library
  lineId: string
  instruction: string
}): Promise<Release> {
  const lines = c.release.script.lines
  const idx = lines.findIndex((l) => l.id === c.lineId)
  if (idx < 0) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y cÃ¢u ${c.lineId}`)
  const name = (id: string): string => c.library.characters.find((x) => x.id === id)?.name ?? id
  const fmt = (l: ScriptLine): string => `${name(l.speakerId)}: ${l.text}`
  const line = lines[idx]
  const out = await runLlm(
    c.llm,
    rewriteLineTask({
      language: c.release.language,
      platform: c.release.platform,
      before: lines.slice(Math.max(0, idx - 3), idx).map(fmt),
      line: { text: line.text, speakerName: name(line.speakerId), tags: line.delivery.tags },
      after: lines.slice(idx + 1, idx + 3).map(fmt),
      instruction: c.instruction
    }),
    c.release.costs
  )
  line.text = out.text
  if (c.release.language !== 'vi') line.translationVi = out.translationVi ?? line.translationVi
  line.delivery = { ...line.delivery, tags: out.tags, instructions: out.instructions }
  line.approval = { status: 'pending' }
  logger.info(`ÄÃ£ viáº¿t láº¡i cÃ¢u ${line.id}`, c.release.id)
  return c.release
}

const AUDIO_EXT = ['.wav', '.mp3', '.ogg', '.flac', '.m4a', '.aac']
const LICENSE_FILES = ['license.txt', 'LICENSE.txt', 'LICENSE', 'license.md', 'giay-phep.txt']

export function guessKind(rel: string, durationSec: number): SoundKind {
  const p = rel.toLowerCase()
  if (/stinger|hit|impact|riser|swell/.test(p)) return 'stinger'
  if (/music|nhac|nháº¡c|drone|score|theme/.test(p)) return 'music'
  if (/ambien|ambient|nen|ná»n|room|atmos|bg|background/.test(p)) return 'ambience'
  if (/sfx|fx|hieu-ung|hiá»‡u á»©ng|foley/.test(p)) return 'sfx'
  return durationSec >= 20 ? 'ambience' : 'sfx'
}

export function tagsFromPath(rel: string): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      rel
        .replace(/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i, '')
        .split(/[\\/_\-.\s,()]+/)
        .map((w) => w.toLowerCase())
        .filter((w) => w.length > 2 && !/^\d+$/.test(w))
    )
  ].slice(0, 12)
}

/** Bá»™ Ã¢m thanh máº«u tá»± tá»•ng há»£p báº±ng ffmpeg â€“ khÃ´ng vÆ°á»›ng báº£n quyá»n */

export class SoundService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repo: StoryRepository,
    private readonly providers: ProviderRegistry,
    private readonly getLibraryDir: () => string
  ) {}

  private async findLicense(dir: string, root: string): Promise<string> {
    let cur = dir
    for (;;) {
      for (const f of LICENSE_FILES) {
        const txt = await fs.readFile(path.join(cur, f), 'utf8').catch(() => null)
        if (txt) return txt.trim().split(/\r?\n/)[0].slice(0, 200)
      }
      if (path.resolve(cur) === path.resolve(root)) return ''
      const up = path.dirname(cur)
      if (up === cur) return ''
      cur = up
    }
  }

  /** QuÃ©t thÆ° má»¥c thÆ° viá»‡n; giá»¯ láº¡i chá»‰nh sá»­a cá»§a ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng (loáº¡i, tag, giáº¥y phÃ©p) theo Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n */
  async scan(): Promise<Library> {
    const root = this.getLibraryDir()
    await fs.mkdir(root, { recursive: true })
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const prev = new Map(lib.soundAssets.map((a) => [path.resolve(a.path), a]))
    const found: SoundAsset[] = []
    const walk = async (dir: string): Promise<void> => {
      for (const e of await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
        const abs = path.join(dir, e.name)
        if (e.isDirectory()) await walk(abs)
        else if (AUDIO_EXT.includes(path.extname(e.name).toLowerCase())) {
          const old = prev.get(path.resolve(abs))
          if (old) {
            found.push(old)
            continue
          }
          const rel = path.relative(root, abs)
          const d = await probeDuration(abs).catch(() => 0)
          const license = await this.findLicense(dir, root)
          found.push({
            id: `snd-${createHash('sha1').update(path.resolve(abs)).digest('hex').slice(0, 10)}`,
            path: abs,
            name: e.name,
            kind: guessKind(rel, d),
            tags: tagsFromPath(rel),
            durationSec: Math.round(d * 100) / 100,
            license,
            licenseConfirmed: false,
            source: rel.split(path.sep)[0] === '_tu-tao' ? 'procedural' : 'thÆ° viá»‡n ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng'
          })
        }
      }
    }
    await walk(root)
    const updated = await this.repo.updateLibrary((l) => {
      l.soundAssets = found
    })
    logger.info(`ThÆ° viá»‡n Ã¢m thanh: ${found.length} file (${found.filter((a) => a.licenseConfirmed).length} Ä‘Ã£ xÃ¡c nháº­n giáº¥y phÃ©p) â€“ ${root}`, 'sound')
    return updated
  }

  /** Táº¡o bá»™ Ã¢m thanh máº«u tá»± tá»•ng há»£p (procedural, toÃ n quyá»n dÃ¹ng thÆ°Æ¡ng máº¡i) rá»“i quÃ©t láº¡i */
  async generateStarterPack(): Promise<Library> {
    const dir = path.join(this.getLibraryDir(), '_tu-tao')
    await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true })
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(dir, 'license.txt'), 'Tá»± táº¡o báº±ng ffmpeg (procedural) â€“ toÃ n quyá»n sá»­ dá»¥ng thÆ°Æ¡ng máº¡i\n', 'utf8')
    for (const s of STARTER) {
      const out = path.join(dir, s.file)
      const exists = await fs.access(out).then(() => true, () => false)
      if (!exists) await runFfmpeg(['-f', 'lavfi', '-i', s.src, '-t', String(s.d), '-ac', '2', '-ar', '48000', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', out])
    }
    await this.scan()
    return this.repo.updateLibrary((l) => {
      for (const a of l.soundAssets) {
        const st = STARTER.find((s) => path.resolve(dir, s.file) === path.resolve(a.path))
        if (st) Object.assign(a, { kind: st.kind, tags: st.tags, licenseConfirmed: true, source: 'procedural' })
      }
    })
  }

  private designInput(story: Story, release: Release, lib: Library): DesignInput {
    const ch = lib.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story.channelPresetId) ?? lib.channelPresets[0]
    const beats = new Map(story.outline.beats.map((b) => [b.id, b.emotionalIntensity]))
    const kindOf = new Map(release.script.sections.map((s) => [s.id, s.kind]))
    return {
      duration: release.voiceTrack?.duration ?? 0,
      lines: release.script.lines
        .filter((l) => l.start != null && l.end != null)
        .map((l) => ({ id: l.id, text: l.text, start: l.start as number, end: l.end as number, beatId: l.beatId, sectionKind: kindOf.get(l.sectionId) ?? 'story' })),
      beatIntensity: (id) => beats.get(id) ?? 0.5,
      assets: lib.soundAssets.filter((a) => a.licenseConfirmed),
      palette: ch.soundPalette,
      climaxThreshold: ch.motionProfile.climaxThreshold,
      silenceBeforeClimaxSec: platformsConfig.audio.silenceBeforeClimaxSec
    }
  }

  /** Thiáº¿t káº¿ cue: LLM (hoáº·c luáº­t khi Mock), kiá»ƒm tra asset + giáº¥y phÃ©p; thÆ° viá»‡n trá»‘ng â‡’ tá»± táº¡o bá»™ máº«u */
  async design(storyId: string, releaseId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<{ cues: SoundCue[]; cost: number; note: string }> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const release = await this.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    if (!release.voiceTrack) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ voice track â€“ cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c 12')
    let lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    let note = ''
    if (!lib.soundAssets.some((a) => a.licenseConfirmed)) {
      lib = await this.generateStarterPack()
      note = 'ThÆ° viá»‡n chÆ°a cÃ³ Ã¢m thanh Ä‘Ã£ xÃ¡c nháº­n giáº¥y phÃ©p â‡’ Ä‘Ã£ táº¡o bá»™ Ã¢m thanh máº«u tá»± tá»•ng há»£p. '
    }
    const input = this.designInput(story, release, lib)
    const llm = this.providers.llm()
    try {
      const req = soundDesignTask(input, release.language)
      req.signal = signal
      const res = await llm.generateJson(req)
      return { cues: res.data, cost: res.cost, note: note + `${res.data.length} cue (${llm.id === 'mock' ? 'luáº­t tá»± Ä‘á»™ng' : 'LLM'})` }
    } catch (err) {
      if (signal?.aborted) throw err
      // LLM lá»—i â‡’ dÃ¹ng thiáº¿t káº¿ theo luáº­t, váº«n kiá»ƒm tra giáº¥y phÃ©p
      const v = validateCues(designSoundCues(input), input.assets, input.duration)
      logger.warn(`LLM sound design lá»—i (${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}) â€“ dÃ¹ng thiáº¿t káº¿ theo luáº­t`, releaseId)
      return { cues: v.cues, cost: 0, note: note + `${v.cues.length} cue (theo luáº­t â€“ LLM lá»—i)` }
    }
  }

  /** Mix giá»ng + cue â‡’ voice/mix.wav, cáº­p nháº­t voiceTrack.mixedPath + LUFS */
  async mix(storyId: string, releaseId: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Release> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const release = await this.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    const vt = release.voiceTrack
    if (!vt?.processedPath) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ voice track â€“ cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c 12')
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const ch = lib.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story.channelPresetId) ?? lib.channelPresets[0]
    const a = platformsConfig.audio
    const sfxDir = path.join(this.repo.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId), 'sfx')
    await fs.mkdir(sfxDir, { recursive: true })
    const { cues } = validateCues(release.soundCues, lib.soundAssets, vt.duration)
    const mixCues: MixCue[] = []
    for (const c of cues) {
      if (c.kind === 'silence') continue
      const asset = lib.soundAssets.find((x) => x.id === c.assetId) as SoundAsset
      // ChÃ©p asset vÃ o thÆ° má»¥c Release (minh báº¡ch nguá»“n + mang Ä‘i Ä‘Æ°á»£c)
      const local = path.join(sfxDir, `${asset.id}${path.extname(asset.path).toLowerCase()}`)
      if (!(await fs.access(local).then(() => true, () => false))) await fs.copyFile(asset.path, local)
      const len = c.end - c.start
      mixCues.push({
        path: local,
        start: c.start,
        end: c.end,
        volume: a.defaultVolume[c.kind as keyof typeof a.defaultVolume] * c.volume,
        fadeIn: c.fadeIn,
        fadeOut: c.fadeOut,
        loop: (c.kind === 'ambience' || c.kind === 'music') && asset.durationSec > 0 && asset.durationSec < len
      })
    }
    const out = path.join(this.repo.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId), 'voice', 'mix.wav')
    const res = await mixAudio(
      {
        voicePath: this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, vt.processedPath),
        duration: vt.duration,
        cues: mixCues,
        silences: cues.filter((c) => c.kind === 'silence').map((c) => ({ start: c.start, end: c.end }))
      },
      out,
      { lufs: ch.soundPalette.targetLufs, truePeak: a.truePeakDb, lra: a.lra },
      signal
    )
    const updated = await this.repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (r) => {
      if (!r.voiceTrack) return
      r.voiceTrack.mixedPath = toRelPath('releases', releaseId, 'voice', 'mix.wav')
      r.voiceTrack.mixLufs = Math.round(res.lufs * 10) / 10
      r.voiceTrack.mixStale = false
      r.voiceTrack.mixedAt = new Date().toISOString()
    })
    logger.info(`Mix xong: ${mixCues.length} lá»›p Ã¢m thanh, ${res.lufs.toFixed(1)} LUFS`, releaseId)
    return updated
  }

  /** Nghe thá»­ má»™t asset (20 giÃ¢y Ä‘áº§u, mp3 nhá») dáº¡ng data URL â€“ asset náº±m ngoÃ i thÆ° má»¥c Story */
  async preview(assetId: string): Promise<string> {
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const a = lib.soundAssets.find((x) => x.id === assetId)
    if (!a) throw new Error('KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y Ã¢m thanh')
    const tmp = path.join(this.repo.dataRoot, 'cache', `prev_${a.id}.mp3`)
    await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(tmp), { recursive: true })
    if (!(await fs.access(tmp).then(() => true, () => false))) {
      await runFfmpeg(['-i', a.path, '-t', '20', '-ac', '2', '-b:a', '96k', tmp])
    }
    return `data:audio/mpeg;base64,${(await fs.readFile(tmp)).toString('base64')}`
  }
}

/**
 * Thao tÃ¡c cáº¥p STORY (pipeline A, bÆ°á»›c 1â€“5):
 *  1. Táº¡o Story  2. LLM dÃ n Ã½  3. â˜… Duyá»‡t dÃ n Ã½  3a. LLM Ä‘á» xuáº¥t dÃ n nhÃ¢n váº­t  4. LLM Ä‘á» xuáº¥t ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh
 * Má»—i láº§n gá»i LLM: Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh chi phÃ­ trÆ°á»›c, cháº·n náº¿u vÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story, ghi chi phÃ­ thá»±c táº¿ vÃ o sá»• cá»§a Story.
 * (3b character sheet, 3c duyá»‡t/khoÃ¡ nhÃ¢n váº­t báº±ng áº£nh â€“ Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 3)
 */
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'
import { platformsConfig } from '../../shared/config'
import type { LibraryPatch, NewReleaseInput, NewStoryInput, StoryPatch } from '../../shared/ipc'
import { checkBudget, formatUsdShort, storySpent } from '../../shared/logic/costEstimate'
import { charactersForStory, suggestVoiceId } from '../../shared/logic/voices'
import { emptyLedger } from '../../shared/sampleData'
import type { Library, Release, Story } from '../../shared/types'
import { castTask, outlineTask, sceneBankTask, slug, toBeats, toCharacters, toSceneBank } from '../llm/tasks'
import { logger } from '../logger'
import type { LlmJsonRequest, LlmProvider } from '../providers/interfaces'
import type { StoryRepository } from '../storage/storyRepo'
import { channelOf, estimateTaskCost, platformDefaultsOf } from './scriptService'

export class StoryService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repo: StoryRepository,
    private readonly llm: () => LlmProvider
  ) {}

  /** Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh chi phÃ­ + cháº·n khi vÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story; cháº¡y LLM; ghi chi phÃ­ vÃ o story.costs */
  private async runStoryLlm<T>(story: Story, req: LlmJsonRequest<T>, expectedOutputChars: number): Promise<T> {
    const llm = this.llm()
    const estimate = estimateTaskCost(llm.id, req as LlmJsonRequest<unknown>, expectedOutputChars)
    const releases = (await this.repo.loadBundle(story.id)).releases
    const budget = checkBudget(storySpent(story.costs, releases.map((r) => r.costs)), estimate, story.budget.limitUsd)
    if (!budget.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        `VÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story: Ä‘Ã£ chi ${formatUsdShort(budget.spent)} + Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh ${formatUsdShort(estimate)} > ${formatUsdShort(budget.limit)}. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: tÄƒng ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch á»Ÿ mÃ n Story hoáº·c báº­t Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock.`
      )
    }
    logger.info(`LLM ${req.task} (${llm.id}) â€“ Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh ${formatUsdShort(estimate)}`, story.id)
    const res = await llm.generateJson(req)
    story.costs ??= emptyLedger(story.budget.limitUsd)
    story.costs.estimated += estimate
    if (res.cost > 0) {
      story.costs.actual += res.cost
      story.costs.entries.push({ provider: llm.id, kind: 'llm', units: res.inputTokens + res.outputTokens, cost: res.cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
    }
    return res.data
  }

  async createStory(input: NewStoryInput): Promise<Story> {
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    if (!lib.channelPresets.some((c) => c.id === input.channelPresetId)) throw new Error('KÃªnh khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i')
    if (!input.name.trim()) throw new Error('HÃ£y nháº­p tÃªn Story')
    if (input.premiseVi.trim().length < 20) throw new Error('Ã tÆ°á»Ÿng gá»‘c quÃ¡ ngáº¯n (tá»‘i thiá»ƒu 20 kÃ½ tá»±)')
    const now = new Date().toISOString()
    const id = `story-${slug(input.name)}-${randomBytes(2).toString('hex')}`
    const story: Story = {
      id,
      name: input.name.trim(),
      channelPresetId: input.channelPresetId,
      folkloreIds: input.folkloreIds,
      premiseVi: input.premiseVi.trim(),
      fictionNoteVi: '',
      outline: { beats: [], approval: { status: 'pending' } },
      characterIds: lib.characters.filter((c) => c.scope === 'channel').map((c) => c.id),
      sceneBank: [],
      releaseIds: [],
      status: 'draft',
      budget: { limitUsd: Math.max(0, input.budgetUsd) },
      performanceNotes: [],
      costs: emptyLedger(input.budgetUsd),
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now
    }
    await this.repo.ensureStoryFolders(story)
    await this.repo.saveStory(story)
    logger.info(`Táº¡o Story má»›i "${story.name}"`, story.id)
    return story
  }

  async updateStory(storyId: string, patch: StoryPatch): Promise<Story> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    if (patch.name !== undefined) story.name = patch.name.trim() || story.name
    if (patch.premiseVi !== undefined) story.premiseVi = patch.premiseVi
    if (patch.fictionNoteVi !== undefined) story.fictionNoteVi = patch.fictionNoteVi
    if (patch.budgetUsd !== undefined) {
      if (!(patch.budgetUsd >= 0)) throw new Error('NgÃ¢n sÃ¡ch pháº£i â‰¥ 0')
      story.budget.limitUsd = patch.budgetUsd
      story.costs.budgetLimit = patch.budgetUsd
    }
    if (patch.status !== undefined) story.status = patch.status
    if (patch.beats !== undefined) {
      // Sá»­a dÃ n Ã½ â‡’ cáº§n duyá»‡t láº¡i
      story.outline.beats = patch.beats.map((b, i) => ({ ...b, order: i + 1, emotionalIntensity: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, b.emotionalIntensity)) }))
      story.outline.approval = { status: 'pending' }
    }
    if (patch.sceneBank !== undefined) story.sceneBank = patch.sceneBank
    await this.repo.saveStory(story)
    return story
  }

  async generateOutline(storyId: string): Promise<Story> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const folklore = lib.folklore.filter((f) => story.folkloreIds.includes(f.id))
    const out = await this.runStoryLlm(story, outlineTask(story, folklore), 4000)
    story.outline = { beats: toBeats(out), approval: { status: 'pending' } }
    story.fictionNoteVi = out.fictionNoteVi
    await this.repo.saveStory(story)
    logger.info(`ÄÃ£ táº¡o dÃ n Ã½ ${story.outline.beats.length} beat â€“ chá» duyá»‡t`, story.id)
    return story
  }

  async approveOutline(storyId: string): Promise<Story> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    if (story.outline.beats.length === 0) throw new Error('ChÆ°a cÃ³ dÃ n Ã½ Ä‘á»ƒ duyá»‡t')
    story.outline.approval = { status: 'approved', at: new Date().toISOString() }
    await this.repo.saveStory(story)
    logger.info('â˜… ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t dÃ n Ã½', story.id)
    return story
  }

  /** 3a â€“ LLM Ä‘á» xuáº¥t dÃ n nhÃ¢n váº­t; giá»¯ nguyÃªn nhÃ¢n váº­t Ä‘Ã£ khoÃ¡, thay nhÃ¢n váº­t chÆ°a khoÃ¡ */
  async proposeCast(storyId: string): Promise<{ story: Story; library: Library }> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    if (story.outline.approval.status !== 'approved') throw new Error('HÃ£y duyá»‡t dÃ n Ã½ trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘á» xuáº¥t nhÃ¢n váº­t')
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const folklore = lib.folklore.filter((f) => story.folkloreIds.includes(f.id))
    const channelChars = lib.characters.filter((c) => c.scope === 'channel')
    const out = await this.runStoryLlm(story, castTask(story, folklore, channelChars), 5000)
    const keep = lib.characters.filter((c) => !(c.scope === 'story' && c.storyId === storyId && !c.locked))
    const created = toCharacters(out, storyId, new Set(keep.map((c) => c.id)))
    // Gá»£i Ã½ giá»ng riÃªng cho tá»«ng nhÃ¢n váº­t (cÃ¢u thoáº¡i khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng giá»ng ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ)
    const channel = channelOf(lib, story)
    for (const c of created) {
      for (const lang of ['vi', 'en'] as const) {
        const id = suggestVoiceId(c, lib.voiceProfiles, lang, new Set([channel.defaultVoiceByLanguage[lang]]))
        if (id) c.voiceProfileIdByLanguage[lang] = id
      }
      if (!c.voiceProfileIdByLanguage.vi && !c.voiceProfileIdByLanguage.en) {
        logger.warn(`ChÆ°a tÃ¬m Ä‘Æ°á»£c giá»ng phÃ¹ há»£p cho "${c.name}" â€“ hÃ£y chá»n á»Ÿ Voice Studio / mÃ n NhÃ¢n váº­t`, story.id)
      }
    }
    lib.characters = [...keep, ...created]
    story.characterIds = charactersForStory(storyId, lib.characters).map((c) => c.id)
    await this.repo.saveLibrary(lib)
    await this.repo.saveStory(story)
    logger.info(`Äá» xuáº¥t ${created.length} nhÃ¢n váº­t (character sheet + duyá»‡t/khoÃ¡ áº£nh â€“ Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 3)`, story.id)
    return { story, library: lib }
  }

  /** BÆ°á»›c 4 â€“ LLM Ä‘á» xuáº¥t ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh theo beat (thay toÃ n bá»™ ngÃ¢n hÃ ng hiá»‡n cÃ³) */
  async proposeSceneBank(storyId: string): Promise<Story> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    if (story.outline.approval.status !== 'approved') throw new Error('HÃ£y duyá»‡t dÃ n Ã½ trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘á» xuáº¥t ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh')
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const chars = charactersForStory(storyId, lib.characters)
    const out = await this.runStoryLlm(story, sceneBankTask(story, chars, channelOf(lib, story)), 9000)
    story.sceneBank = toSceneBank(out)
    await this.repo.saveStory(story)
    logger.info(`Äá» xuáº¥t ${story.sceneBank.length} cáº£nh â€“ áº£nh táº¡o á»Ÿ bÆ°á»›c 6 cá»§a pipeline Release`, story.id)
    return story
  }

  async createRelease(storyId: string, input: NewReleaseInput): Promise<Release> {
    const story = await this.repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const channel = channelOf(lib, story)
    const pd = platformDefaultsOf(channel, { platform: input.platform })
    const now = new Date().toISOString()
    const isSeries = pd.partDurationSec != null
    const label = `${pd.label} Â· ${input.language.toUpperCase()} Â· ${story.name}`
    let id = `rel-${input.platform === 'youtubeShorts' ? 'sh' : input.platform === 'youtube' ? 'yt' : 'tt'}-${input.language}`
    while (story.releaseIds.some((r) => r === id || r.startsWith(`${id}-p`))) id = `${id}-${randomBytes(1).toString('hex')}`
    const release: Release = {
      id,
      storyId,
      title: isSeries ? `${label} â€“ Pháº§n 1` : label,
      platform: input.platform,
      language: input.language,
      aspectRatio: pd.aspectRatio,
      targetDuration: isSeries ? (pd.partDurationSec ?? 90) : Math.max(60, input.targetDurationSec),
      seriesGroupId: isSeries ? `series-${id}` : null,
      partIndex: 1,
      partCount: isSeries ? Math.min(10, Math.max(1, input.plannedParts)) : 1,
      heroRatio: pd.heroRatio,
      script: { sections: [], lines: [] },
      voiceTrack: null,
      scenes: [],
      chapters: [],
      subtitles: [],
      soundCues: [],
      publishing: null,
      costs: emptyLedger(story.budget.limitUsd),
      pipelineState: null,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now
    }
    story.releaseIds.push(release.id)
    // Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i kinh doanh: cÃ³ báº£n TikTok â‡’ "Ä‘ang thá»­"; thÃªm báº£n YouTube tiáº¿ng Anh sau TikTok â‡’ "Ä‘Ã£ chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ"
    const existing = (await this.repo.loadBundle(storyId)).releases
    if (input.platform === 'youtube' && input.language === 'en' && existing.some((r) => r.platform === 'tiktok')) story.status = 'adapted'
    else if (input.platform === 'tiktok' && story.status === 'draft') story.status = 'testing'
    await this.repo.ensureStoryFolders(story)
    await this.repo.saveRelease(release)
    await this.repo.saveStory(story)
    logger.info(`ThÃªm báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh ${release.title} (ká»‹ch báº£n sáº½ chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ á»Ÿ bÆ°á»›c 8)`, storyId)
    return release
  }

  async updateLibrary(patch: LibraryPatch): Promise<Library> {
    const lib = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const unique = <T extends { id: string }>(arr: T[], what: string): T[] => {
      const seen = new Set<string>()
      for (const x of arr) {
        if (!x.id.trim()) throw new Error(`${what}: cÃ³ má»¥c chÆ°a cÃ³ id`)
        if (seen.has(x.id)) throw new Error(`${what}: trÃ¹ng id "${x.id}"`)
        seen.add(x.id)
      }
      return arr
    }
    if (patch.folklore) lib.folklore = unique(patch.folklore, 'Truyá»n thuyáº¿t')
    if (patch.pronunciations) lib.pronunciations = unique(patch.pronunciations, 'Tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn phÃ¡t Ã¢m')
    if (patch.voiceProfiles) lib.voiceProfiles = unique(patch.voiceProfiles, 'Giá»ng')
    if (patch.characters) lib.characters = unique(patch.characters, 'NhÃ¢n váº­t')
    if (patch.channelPresets) lib.channelPresets = unique(patch.channelPresets, 'KÃªnh')
    if (patch.soundAssets) lib.soundAssets = unique(patch.soundAssets, 'Ã‚m thanh')
    await this.repo.saveLibrary(lib)
    return lib
  }
}

/**
 * LÃµi giá»ng Ä‘á»c dÃ¹ng chung cho pipeline (bÆ°á»›c 11â€“12) vÃ  Voice Studio:
 * - synthesizeLine: Ã¡p tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn phÃ¡t Ã¢m â†’ chá»n giá»ng theo ngÆ°á»i nÃ³i â†’ gá»i TTS â†’ lÆ°u take (cache theo hash)
 * - processTake: xá»­ lÃ½ giá»ng theo postProcessChain cá»§a giá»ng Ä‘Ã³ (cache theo hash take + chuá»—i xá»­ lÃ½)
 */
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import { applyPronunciation } from '../../shared/logic/pronunciation'
import { resolveVoice } from '../../shared/logic/voices'
import { toRelPath } from '../../shared/media'
import type { Library, Release, ScriptLine, Story, Take, VoiceProcessChain, VoiceProfile } from '../../shared/types'
import { buildVoiceFilter, runFfmpeg } from '../media/ffmpeg'
import type { ProviderRegistry } from '../providers/registry'
import type { StoryRepository } from '../storage/storyRepo'

export async function fileExists(p: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await fs.access(p)
    return true
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

export function voiceForLine(story: Story, release: Release, line: ScriptLine, library: Library): VoiceProfile {
  const v = resolveVoice(line.speakerId, release.language, library, story.channelPresetId)
  if (!v) {
    const who = library.characters.find((c) => c.id === line.speakerId)?.name ?? line.speakerId
    throw new Error(`ChÆ°a cÃ³ giá»ng ${release.language.toUpperCase()} cho "${who}". CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: gÃ¡n giá»ng á»Ÿ mÃ n KÃªnh & NhÃ¢n váº­t hoáº·c Voice Studio.`)
  }
  return v
}

export function takeHash(text: string, voice: VoiceProfile, line: ScriptLine, providerId: string, model: string, nonce = ''): string {
  const payload = JSON.stringify({
    text,
    providerId,
    model,
    voice: voice.providerVoiceId,
    speed: voice.speed,
    instructions: voice.baseInstructions,
    settings: voice.providerSettings,
    delivery: { tags: line.delivery.tags, instructions: line.delivery.instructions, pace: line.delivery.pace },
    nonce
  })
  return createHash('sha1').update(payload).digest('hex').slice(0, 12)
}

export interface SynthArgs {
  repo: StoryRepository
  providers: ProviderRegistry
  story: Story
  release: Release
  line: ScriptLine
  library: Library
  signal?: AbortSignal
  /** KhÃ¡c rá»—ng â‡’ Ã©p táº¡o take má»›i (nÃºt "Táº¡o láº¡i") */
  nonce?: string
}

export interface SynthResult {
  take: Take
  generated: boolean
  characters: number
  cost: number
  provider: string
}

/** VÄƒn báº£n thá»±c sá»± gá»­i TTS (Ä‘Ã£ Ã¡p tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn phÃ¡t Ã¢m) + giá»ng + provider â€“ dÃ¹ng cho Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh chi phÃ­ */
export function plannedTts(story: Story, release: Release, line: ScriptLine, library: Library, providers: ProviderRegistry) {
  const voice = voiceForLine(story, release, line, library)
  const provider = providers.tts(voice.providerId)
  const { text } = applyPronunciation(line.text, library.pronunciations, release.language)
  const hash = takeHash(text, voice, line, provider.id, provider.model)
  return { voice, provider, text, hash }
}

export async function synthesizeLine(a: SynthArgs): Promise<SynthResult> {
  const { voice, provider, text } = plannedTts(a.story, a.release, a.line, a.library, a.providers)
  const hash = takeHash(text, voice, a.line, provider.id, provider.model, a.nonce ?? '')
  const takesDir = path.join(a.repo.releaseDir(a.story.id, a.release.id), 'voice', 'takes')
  await fs.mkdir(takesDir, { recursive: true })
  const fileName = `${a.line.id}_${hash}.wav`
  const abs = path.join(takesDir, fileName)
  const existing = a.line.takes.find((t) => t.hash === hash)
  if (existing && (await fileExists(abs))) {
    return { take: existing, generated: false, characters: 0, cost: 0, provider: provider.id }
  }
  const res = await provider.synthesize({ text, language: a.release.language, voice, delivery: a.line.delivery, outputPath: abs, signal: a.signal })
  const take: Take = {
    id: randomUUID(),
    path: toRelPath('releases', a.release.id, 'voice', 'takes', fileName),
    provider: res.provider,
    model: res.model,
    hash,
    duration: res.duration,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  }
  a.line.takes = [...a.line.takes.filter((t) => t.hash !== hash), take]
  return { take, generated: true, characters: res.characters, cost: res.cost, provider: res.provider }
}

export function chainHash(chain: VoiceProcessChain): string {
  return createHash('sha1').update(JSON.stringify(chain)).digest('hex').slice(0, 8)
}

/** Take Ä‘Ã£ xá»­ lÃ½ giá»ng (lá»c, nÃ©n, EQ, reverb) theo chuá»—i cá»§a giá»ng â€“ náº±m trong cache/ cá»§a Story */
export async function processTake(
  repo: StoryRepository,
  storyId: string,
  take: Take,
  chain: VoiceProcessChain,
  signal?: AbortSignal
): Promise<{ abs: string; rel: string }> {
  const name = `proc_${take.hash}_${chainHash(chain)}.wav`
  const rel = toRelPath('cache', name)
  const abs = repo.resolveInStory(storyId, rel)
  if (!(await fileExists(abs))) {
    await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(abs), { recursive: true })
    const src = repo.resolveInStory(storyId, take.path)
    const tmp = `${abs}.tmp.wav`
    await runFfmpeg(['-i', src, '-af', buildVoiceFilter(chain), '-ac', '1', '-ar', '44100', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', tmp], signal)
    await fs.rename(tmp, abs)
  }
  return { abs, rel }
}

/**
 * Voice Studio: táº¡o láº¡i Tá»ªNG CÃ‚U Ä‘á»™c láº­p, chá»n take, waveform nhá», so sÃ¡nh trÆ°á»›c/sau xá»­ lÃ½ giá»ng, nghe thá»­ giá»ng.
 * Má»i thay Ä‘á»•i giá»ng Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u bÆ°á»›c 12 trá»Ÿ Ä‘i lÃ  "Chá»" â‡’ cháº¡y láº¡i pipeline chá»‰ ghÃ©p láº¡i (take khÃ¡c dÃ¹ng cache).

import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import type { TtsPreviewInput, TtsPreviewResult } from '../../shared/ipc'
import { checkBudget, formatUsdShort, storySpent } from '../../shared/logic/costEstimate'
import { applyPronunciation } from '../../shared/logic/pronunciation'
import { resetStepsFrom } from '../../shared/pipeline'
import type { Release } from '../../shared/types'
import { readWav } from '../media/wav'
import { logger } from '../logger'
import type { ProviderRegistry } from '../providers/registry'
import type { StoryRepository } from '../storage/storyRepo'
import { fileExists, plannedTts, processTake, synthesizeLine, voiceForLine } from './voiceCore'

/** BiÃªn Ä‘á»™ Ä‘á»‰nh theo tá»«ng Ã´ (0..1) â€“ váº½ waveform nhá» */
export function computePeaks(samples: Int16Array, buckets: number): number[] {
  const n = Math.max(1, buckets)
  const size = Math.max(1, Math.floor(samples.length / n))
  const out: number[] = []
  for (let b = 0; b < n; b++) {
    let max = 0
    const end = Math.min(samples.length, (b + 1) * size)
    for (let i = b * size; i < end; i++) {
      const v = Math.abs(samples[i])
      if (v > max) max = v
    }
    out.push(Math.round((max / 32768) * 1000) / 1000)
  }
  return out
}

export class VoiceService {
  constructor(
    private readonly repo: StoryRepository,
    private readonly providers: ProviderRegistry,
    private readonly previewDir: string
  ) {}

  private async load(storyId: string, releaseId: string) {
    const [story, release, library] = await Promise.all([
      this.repo.loadStory(storyId),
      this.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId),
      this.repo.loadLibrary()
    ])
    return { story, release, library }
  }

  async regenerateLine(storyId: string, releaseId: string, lineId: string): Promise<Release> {
    const { story, release, library } = await this.load(storyId, releaseId)
    const line = release.script.lines.find((l) => l.id === lineId)
    if (!line) throw new Error(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y cÃ¢u ${lineId}`)
    const plan = plannedTts(story, release, line, library, this.providers)
    const estimate = plan.provider.estimateCost(plan.text.length)
    const bundle = await this.repo.loadBundle(storyId)
    const budget = checkBudget(storySpent(story.costs, bundle.releases.map((r) => r.costs)), estimate, story.budget.limitUsd)
    if (!budget.ok) throw new Error(`VÆ°á»£t ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch Story (Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh thÃªm ${formatUsdShort(estimate)}). TÄƒng ngÃ¢n sÃ¡ch á»Ÿ mÃ n Story.`)

    const res = await synthesizeLine({
      repo: this.repo,
      providers: this.providers,
      story,
      release,
      line,
      library,
      // Má»—i láº§n báº¥m "Táº¡o láº¡i" â‡’ má»™t take má»›i (provider tháº­t cho káº¿t quáº£ khÃ¡c nhau)
      nonce: randomUUID()
    })
    line.selectedTakeId = res.take.id
    if (res.cost > 0) {
      release.costs.actual += res.cost
      release.costs.entries.push({ provider: res.provider, kind: 'tts', units: res.characters, cost: res.cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
    }
    resetStepsFrom(release.pipelineState, 12)
    await this.repo.saveRelease(release)
    logger.info(`Táº¡o láº¡i cÃ¢u ${line.id} (${res.provider})`, release.id)
    return release
  }

  async selectTake(storyId: string, releaseId: string, lineId: string, takeId: string): Promise<Release> {
    const release = await this.repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    const line = release.script.lines.find((l) => l.id === lineId)
    if (!line?.takes.some((t) => t.id === takeId)) throw new Error('Take khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i')
    line.selectedTakeId = takeId
    resetStepsFrom(release.pipelineState, 12)
    await this.repo.saveRelease(release)
    return release
  }

  async peaks(storyId: string, relPath: string, buckets = 48): Promise<number[]> {
    const abs = this.repo.resolveInStory(storyId, relPath)
    const wav = readWav(await fs.readFile(abs))
    if (wav.bitsPerSample !== 16) return []
    const samples = new Int16Array(wav.data.buffer, wav.data.byteOffset, Math.floor(wav.data.length / 2))
    return computePeaks(samples, buckets)
  }

  /** Tráº£ vá» Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n take gá»‘c + take Ä‘Ã£ xá»­ lÃ½ (Ä‘á»ƒ nghe so sÃ¡nh trÆ°á»›c/sau) */
  async processPreview(storyId: string, releaseId: string, lineId: string): Promise<{ rawPath: string; processedPath: string }> {
    const { story, release, library } = await this.load(storyId, releaseId)
    const line = release.script.lines.find((l) => l.id === lineId)
    const take = line?.takes.find((t) => t.id === line.selectedTakeId)
    if (!line || !take) throw new Error('CÃ¢u chÆ°a cÃ³ take â€“ hÃ£y táº¡o giá»ng trÆ°á»›c')
    const voice = voiceForLine(story, release, line, library)
    const { rel } = await processTake(this.repo, storyId, take, voice.postProcessChain)
    return { rawPath: take.path, processedPath: rel }
  }

  /** Nghe thá»­ má»™t giá»ng vá»›i cÃ¢u báº¥t ká»³ (tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn phÃ¡t Ã¢m, mÃ n nhÃ¢n váº­t). Tráº£ vá» data URL Ä‘á»ƒ phÃ¡t ngay. */
  async ttsPreview(input: TtsPreviewInput): Promise<TtsPreviewResult> {
    const library = await this.repo.loadLibrary()
    const voice = library.voiceProfiles.find((v) => v.id === input.voiceProfileId)
    if (!voice) throw new Error('Giá»ng khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i')
    const provider = this.providers.tts(voice.providerId)
    const text = input.applyDictionary === false ? input.text : applyPronunciation(input.text, library.pronunciations, voice.language).text
    if (!text.trim()) throw new Error('VÄƒn báº£n trá»‘ng')
    const hash = createHash('sha1').update(JSON.stringify([text, voice, provider.id, provider.model])).digest('hex').slice(0, 14)
    await fs.mkdir(this.previewDir, { recursive: true })
    const abs = path.join(this.previewDir, `${hash}.wav`)
    let cost = 0
    if (!(await fileExists(abs))) {
      const res = await provider.synthesize({
        text,
        language: voice.language,
        voice,
        delivery: { tags: [], instructions: '', pace: 1, pauseBeforeMs: 0, pauseAfterMs: 0 },
        outputPath: abs
      })
      cost = res.cost
      if (cost > 0) logger.info(`Nghe thá»­ giá»ng ${voice.name}: ~${formatUsdShort(cost)}`, 'tts')
    }
    const buf = await fs.readFile(abs)
    return { dataUrl: `data:audio/wav;base64,${buf.toString('base64')}`, sentText: text, cost, provider: provider.id }
  }
}

 *   <dataRoot>/Truyá»‡n ma/<storyId>/story.json
 *                               /bank/            â€“ áº£nh ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh
 *                               /cache/
 *                               /releases/<releaseId>/release.json
 *                                                    /voice /sfx /exports
 * Ghi file nguyÃªn tá»­ (ghi .tmp rá»“i rename) + khoÃ¡ tuáº§n tá»± theo file Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng há»ng JSON.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'
import type { ReleaseSummary, StoryBundle, StorySummary } from '../../shared/ipc'
import { getStepDef, pipelineProgress } from '../../shared/pipeline'
import {
  createSampleLibrary,
  createSampleReleases,

  SAMPLE_STORY_ID
} from '../../shared/sampleData'
import type { Library, Release, Story } from '../../shared/types'

const SAFE_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/

/** TÃªn thÆ° má»¥c chá»©a Story â€“ cá»‘ Ã½ cÃ³ dáº¥u + khoáº£ng tráº¯ng Ä‘á»ƒ luÃ´n kiá»ƒm thá»­ Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t */
export const STORIES_FOLDER_NAME = 'Truyá»‡n ma'
/** ThÆ° má»¥c áº£nh nhÃ¢n váº­t (character sheet, biá»ƒu cáº£m, tráº¡ng thÃ¡i) â€“ náº±m trong storiesRoot Ä‘á»ƒ media server phá»¥c vá»¥ Ä‘Æ°á»£c */
export const CHARACTERS_FOLDER = '_characters'

export class StoryRepository {
  private locks = new Map<string, Promise<void>>()

  constructor(readonly dataRoot: string) {}

  get storiesRoot(): string {
    return path.join(this.dataRoot, STORIES_FOLDER_NAME)
  }

  private assertId(id: string, what: string): void {
    if (!SAFE_ID.test(id)) throw new Error(`${what} khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: "${id}"`)
  }

  storyDir(storyId: string): string {
    this.assertId(storyId, 'MÃ£ Story')
    return path.join(this.storiesRoot, storyId)
  }

  releaseDir(storyId: string, releaseId: string): string {
    this.assertId(releaseId, 'MÃ£ Release')
    return path.join(this.storyDir(storyId), 'releases', releaseId)
  }

  /** Äá»•i Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i (a/b/c) trong Story thÃ nh Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i; cháº·n thoÃ¡t ra ngoÃ i thÆ° má»¥c */
  resolveInStory(storyId: string, relPath: string): string {
    const base = this.storyDir(storyId)
    const abs = path.resolve(base, ...relPath.split('/'))
    if (abs !== base && !abs.startsWith(base + path.sep)) throw new Error(`ÄÆ°á»ng dáº«n ngoÃ i thÆ° má»¥c Story: ${relPath}`)
    return abs
  }

  private async withLock<T>(file: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const prev = this.locks.get(file) ?? Promise.resolve()
    let release!: () => void
    const next = new Promise<void>((r) => (release = r))
    this.locks.set(file, prev.then(() => next))
    await prev
    try {
      return await fn()
    } finally {
      release()
      if (this.locks.get(file) === next) this.locks.delete(file)
    }
  }

  private async writeJson(file: string, data: unknown): Promise<void> {
    await this.withLock(file, async () => {
      await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true })
      const tmp = `${file}.tmp`
      await fs.writeFile(tmp, JSON.stringify(data, null, 2), 'utf8')
      // Windows Ä‘Ã´i khi khoÃ¡ file táº¡m thá»i (antivirus/indexer) â‡’ thá»­ láº¡i vÃ i láº§n
      for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
        try {
          await fs.rename(tmp, file)
          return
        } catch (err) {
          const code = (err as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code
          if (attempt >= 5 || (code !== 'EPERM' && code !== 'EBUSY' && code !== 'EACCES')) throw err
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 60 * (attempt + 1)))
        }
      }
    })
  }

  private async readJson<T>(file: string): Promise<T> {
    return this.withLock(file, async () => JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8')) as T)
  }

  private async exists(p: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await fs.access(p)
      return true
    } catch {
      return false
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Library

  private get libraryFile(): string {
    return path.join(this.dataRoot, 'library.json')
  }

  async loadLibrary(): Promise<Library> {
    return migrateLibrary(await this.readJson<Library>(this.libraryFile))
  }

  async saveLibrary(lib: Library): Promise<void> {
    await this.writeJson(this.libraryFile, lib)
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Story

  async loadStory(storyId: string): Promise<Story> {
    return migrateStory(await this.readJson<Story>(path.join(this.storyDir(storyId), 'story.json')))
  }

  async saveStory(story: Story): Promise<void> {
    story.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString()
    await this.writeJson(path.join(this.storyDir(story.id), 'story.json'), story)
  }

  async loadRelease(storyId: string, releaseId: string): Promise<Release> {
    return migrateRelease(await this.readJson<Release>(path.join(this.releaseDir(storyId, releaseId), 'release.json')))
  }

  async saveRelease(release: Release): Promise<void> {
    release.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString()
    await this.writeJson(path.join(this.releaseDir(release.storyId, release.id), 'release.json'), release)
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Äá»câ€“sá»­aâ€“ghi nguyÃªn tá»­
  // Nhiá»u job áº£nh cháº¡y song song cÃ¹ng sá»­a story.json / library.json â‡’ pháº£i tuáº§n tá»± hoÃ¡ cáº£ chu trÃ¬nh Ä‘á»c-sá»­a-ghi.

  private mutexes = new Map<string, Promise<void>>()

  private async mutate<T>(key: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    const prev = this.mutexes.get(key) ?? Promise.resolve()
    let done!: () => void
    const next = new Promise<void>((r) => (done = r))
    const chained = prev.then(() => next)
    this.mutexes.set(key, chained)
    await prev
    try {
      return await fn()
    } finally {
      done()
      if (this.mutexes.get(key) === chained) this.mutexes.delete(key)
    }
  }

  updateStory(storyId: string, fn: (s: Story) => void | Promise<void>): Promise<Story> {
    return this.mutate(`story:${storyId}`, async () => {
      const s = await this.loadStory(storyId)
      await fn(s)
      await this.saveStory(s)
      return s
    })
  }

  updateLibrary(fn: (l: Library) => void | Promise<void>): Promise<Library> {
    return this.mutate('library', async () => {
      const l = await this.loadLibrary()
      await fn(l)
      await this.saveLibrary(l)
      return l
    })
  }

  updateRelease(storyId: string, releaseId: string, fn: (r: Release) => void | Promise<void>): Promise<Release> {
    return this.mutate(`release:${storyId}/${releaseId}`, async () => {
      const r = await this.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
      await fn(r)
      await this.saveRelease(r)
      return r
    })
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------------ áº¢nh nhÃ¢n váº­t (dÃ¹ng chung má»i Story)

  /** <storiesRoot>/_characters â€“ phá»¥c vá»¥ qua media server vá»›i "storyId" = "_characters" */
  get charactersRoot(): string {
    return path.join(this.storiesRoot, CHARACTERS_FOLDER)
  }

  resolveCharacterPath(relPath: string): string {
    const base = this.charactersRoot
    const abs = path.resolve(base, ...relPath.split('/'))
    if (!abs.startsWith(base + path.sep)) throw new Error(`ÄÆ°á»ng dáº«n ngoÃ i thÆ° má»¥c nhÃ¢n váº­t: ${relPath}`)
    return abs
  }

  async loadBundle(storyId: string): Promise<StoryBundle> {
    const story = await this.loadStory(storyId)
    const releases: Release[] = []
    for (const id of story.releaseIds) {
      try {
        releases.push(await this.loadRelease(storyId, id))
      } catch {
        // Release bá»‹ xoÃ¡/há»ng â€“ bá» qua Ä‘á»ƒ Story váº«n má»Ÿ Ä‘Æ°á»£c
      }
    }
    return { story, releases }
  }

  async listStories(): Promise<StorySummary[]> {
    await fs.mkdir(this.storiesRoot, { recursive: true })
    const entries = await fs.readdir(this.storiesRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    const out: StorySummary[] = []
    for (const e of entries) {
      if (!e.isDirectory() || !SAFE_ID.test(e.name)) continue
      if (!(await this.exists(path.join(this.storiesRoot, e.name, 'story.json')))) continue
      try {
        const { story, releases } = await this.loadBundle(e.name)
        out.push({
          id: story.id,
          name: story.name,
          status: story.status,
          updatedAt: story.updatedAt,
          costActual: (story.costs?.actual ?? 0) + releases.reduce((s, r) => s + r.costs.actual, 0),
          budgetLimit: story.budget.limitUsd,
          releases: releases.map(summarizeRelease)
        })
      } catch {
        // story.json há»ng â€“ bá» qua
      }
    }
    out.sort((a, b) => b.updatedAt.localeCompare(a.updatedAt))
    return out
  }

  /** Táº¡o cáº¥u trÃºc thÆ° má»¥c cho Story + cÃ¡c Release */
  async ensureStoryFolders(story: Story): Promise<void> {
    const dir = this.storyDir(story.id)
    await fs.mkdir(path.join(dir, 'bank'), { recursive: true })
    await fs.mkdir(path.join(dir, 'cache'), { recursive: true })
    for (const rid of story.releaseIds) {
      const rdir = this.releaseDir(story.id, rid)
      for (const sub of ['voice', 'sfx', 'exports']) await fs.mkdir(path.join(rdir, sub), { recursive: true })
    }
  }

  /**
   * Láº§n Ä‘áº§u má»Ÿ app: ghi library.json + Story máº«u. force = khÃ´i phá»¥c dá»¯ liá»‡u máº«u
   * (ghi Ä‘Ã¨ JSON, giá»¯ láº¡i file media Ä‘Ã£ táº¡o â€“ cache sáº½ dÃ¹ng láº¡i).
   */
  async ensureSampleData(force = false): Promise<void> {
    await fs.mkdir(this.storiesRoot, { recursive: true })
    // Dá»¯ liá»‡u máº«u tá»« GÄ1 (nhÃ¢n váº­t chÆ°a cÃ³ scope/cast) â‡’ nÃ¢ng cáº¥p báº±ng cÃ¡ch ghi láº¡i dá»¯ liá»‡u máº«u má»›i, giá»¯ media lÃ m cache
    if (!force && (await this.exists(this.libraryFile))) {
      const raw = await this.readJson<Library>(this.libraryFile)
      if (isLegacyLibrary(raw)) force = true
    }
    if (force || !(await this.exists(this.libraryFile))) await this.saveLibrary(createSampleLibrary())
    const storyFile = path.join(this.storyDir(SAMPLE_STORY_ID), 'story.json')
    if (!force && (await this.exists(storyFile))) return
    const story = createSampleStory()
    await this.ensureStoryFolders(story)
    await this.saveStory(story)
    for (const r of createSampleReleases()) await this.saveRelease(r)
  }
}

export function summarizeRelease(r: Release): ReleaseSummary {
  const st = r.pipelineState
  const current = st?.currentStep != null ? getStepDef(st.currentStep) : undefined
  const waiting = !!st && Object.values(st.steps).some((s) => s.status === 'waitingApproval')
  return {
    id: r.id,
    title: r.title,
    platform: r.platform,
    language: r.language,
    aspectRatio: r.aspectRatio,
    progress: pipelineProgress(st),
    running: !!st?.running,
    waitingApproval: waiting,
    currentStepLabel: current ? `BÆ°á»›c ${current.no}: ${current.title}` : st ? 'ÄÃ£ dá»«ng' : 'ChÆ°a cháº¡y'
  }
}

  return err instanceof Error && (err.name === 'AbortError' || /aborted|cancel/i.test(err.message))

export function toVietnameseError(err: unknown): string {
  const raw = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
  const code = (err as { code?: string } | null)?.code

  if (isAbortError(err)) return 'ÄÃ£ huá»· theo yÃªu cáº§u.'
  const status = (err as { status?: number } | null)?.status
  if (status === 401 || status === 403) return 'API key khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: kiá»ƒm tra/nháº­p láº¡i key á»Ÿ mÃ n CÃ i Ä‘áº·t.'
  if (status === 402 || /quota|insufficient|credits?/i.test(raw))
    return 'TÃ i khoáº£n provider háº¿t háº¡n má»©c/credit. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: náº¡p thÃªm credit hoáº·c báº­t Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock.'
  if (status === 429) return 'Provider Ä‘ang giá»›i háº¡n tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ (429) sau nhiá»u láº§n thá»­ láº¡i. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: Ä‘á»£i vÃ i phÃºt rá»“i cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c.'
  if (status === 400 || status === 422) return `Provider tá»« chá»‘i yÃªu cáº§u (${status}). CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: kiá»ƒm tra voice_id / model trong giá»ng hoáº·c file config. Chi tiáº¿t: ${raw.slice(0, 300)}`
  if (status && status >= 500) return `MÃ¡y chá»§ provider Ä‘ang lá»—i (${status}). CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: thá»­ láº¡i sau Ã­t phÃºt.`
  if (code === 'ENOENT' && /ffmpeg|ffprobe/i.test(raw))
    return `KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ffmpeg/ffprobe. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: cháº¡y láº¡i "npm install". (${raw})`
  if (code === 'ENOENT') return `KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y file hoáº·c thÆ° má»¥c. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n dá»¯ liá»‡u trong CÃ i Ä‘áº·t. (${raw})`
  if (code === 'EBUSY' || code === 'EPERM' || code === 'EACCES')
    return `File Ä‘ang bá»‹ chÆ°Æ¡ng trÃ¬nh khÃ¡c má»Ÿ hoáº·c khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n ghi. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: Ä‘Ã³ng trÃ¬nh phÃ¡t video/Explorer Ä‘ang má»Ÿ file rá»“i cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c. (${raw})`
  if (code === 'ENOSPC') return 'á»” Ä‘Ä©a Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘áº§y. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: giáº£i phÃ³ng dung lÆ°á»£ng rá»“i cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c.'
  if (/browser|chrom/i.test(raw) && /download|fetch|ENOTFOUND|ECONN/i.test(raw))
    return `KhÃ´ng táº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c Chrome Headless Shell cho Remotion. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t ná»‘i máº¡ng rá»“i cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c Render (chá»‰ cáº§n táº£i 1 láº§n). (${raw})`
  if (/ECONNREFUSED|ENOTFOUND|ETIMEDOUT|network/i.test(raw))
    return `Lá»—i máº¡ng. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t ná»‘i Internet rá»“i cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c. (${raw})`
  if (/\b401\b|unauthori[sz]ed|invalid api key/i.test(raw))
    return 'API key khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: nháº­p láº¡i key trong mÃ n CÃ i Ä‘áº·t.'
  if (/\b429\b|rate limit/i.test(raw)) return 'Provider Ä‘ang giá»›i háº¡n tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ (429). CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: giáº£m sá»‘ luá»“ng hoáº·c Ä‘á»£i vÃ i phÃºt rá»“i cháº¡y láº¡i.'
  if (/ChÆ°a triá»ƒn khai/.test(raw)) return raw
  return `Lá»—i: ${raw}. CÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½: xem panel Nháº­t kÃ½ Ä‘á»ƒ biáº¿t chi tiáº¿t, sá»­a nguyÃªn nhÃ¢n rá»“i báº¥m "Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u" Ä‘á»ƒ cháº¡y láº¡i bÆ°á»›c lá»—i.`

    webPreferences: {
      preload: path.join(__dirname, '../preload/index.js'),
      contextIsolation: true,
      nodeIntegration: false,
      sandbox: true
    }
  })
  win.once('ready-to-show', () => {
    if (!process.env['FHS_UI_SMOKE']) win.show()
  })
  // KhÃ´ng cho má»Ÿ cá»­a sá»• má»›i / Ä‘iá»u hÆ°á»›ng láº¡ trong app
  win.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
    if (/^https?:\/\//.test(url)) void shell.openExternal(url)
    return { action: 'deny' }
  })
  // Lá»—i JavaScript trong giao diá»‡n â†’ ghi vÃ o Nháº­t kÃ½/file log (dá»… gá»¡ lá»—i khi mÃ n hÃ¬nh tráº¯ng)
  win.webContents.on('console-message', (_e, level, message, line, sourceId) => {
    if (level >= 3) logger.error(`${message} (${sourceId}:${line})`, 'renderer')
  })
  win.webContents.on('render-process-gone', (_e, details) => logger.error(`Renderer dá»«ng: ${details.reason}`, 'renderer'))
  win.webContents.on('will-navigate', (e, url) => {
    if (process.env['ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL'] && url.startsWith(process.env['ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL'])) return
    e.preventDefault()
  })

  if (!app.isPackaged && process.env['ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL']) {
    void win.loadURL(process.env['ELECTRON_RENDERER_URL'])
  } else {
    void win.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, '../renderer/index.html'))
  }
  return win
}

async function bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  logger.init(path.join(app.getPath('userData'), 'logs'))

  renderService = new RenderService(path.join(__dirname, 'renderWorker.js'))

  if (app.isPackaged) {
    session.defaultSession.webRequest.onHeadersReceived((details, cb) => {
      cb({
        responseHeaders: {
          ...details.responseHeaders,
          'Content-Security-Policy': [
            "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data: blob: http://127.0.0.1:*; media-src 'self' blob: data: http://127.0.0.1:*; connect-src 'self' http://127.0.0.1:*"
          ]
        }
      })
    })
  }

  const progressEmitter = new ThrottledEmitter<{ storyId: string; releaseId: string; state: PipelineState }>(
    (_key, payload) => sendToRenderer(mainWindow, 'pipeline:progress', payload)
  )
  const dataEmitter = new ThrottledEmitter<string>((_key, storyId) =>
    sendToRenderer(mainWindow, 'data:changed', { storyId })
  , 300)

  // API key chá»‰ Ä‘á»c á»Ÿ main (giáº£i mÃ£ safeStorage khi cáº§n), khÃ´ng bao giá» gá»­i sang renderer
  const providers = createProviderRegistry({
    isMock: isMockMode,
    getKey: (id) => {
      try {
        return getApiKey(id)
      } catch {
        return null
      }
    }
  })
  logger.info(`Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™: ${isMockMode() ? 'Mock (offline, khÃ´ng tá»‘n phÃ­)' : 'API tháº­t (OpenAI / ElevenLabs / Gemini)'}`)

  // HÃ ng Ä‘á»£i job dÃ¹ng chung (áº£nh, sau nÃ y video/TTS hÃ ng loáº¡t) â€“ lÆ°u xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a, khÃ´i phá»¥c khi má»Ÿ láº¡i
  const film = getFilmSettings()
  const queue = createAppQueue({
    repo,
    persistPath: path.join(repo.dataRoot, 'queue.json'),
    concurrency: film.concurrency,
    getMonthlyBudget,
    onCostRecorded: (storyId) => dataEmitter.emit(storyId, storyId)
  })
  const notifyAll = async (storyId: string | null): Promise<void> => {
    if (storyId) return dataEmitter.emit(storyId, storyId)
    for (const s of await repo.listStories()) dataEmitter.emit(s.id, s.id)
  }
  const images = new ImageService(repo, providers, queue, () => getFilmSettings().autoDl, (sid) => void notifyAll(sid))
  appQueue = queue
  await queue.restore()
  if (!film.autoRun) queue.pause()
  else if (queue.state().counts.queued === 0) queue.resume()
  if (queue.state().paused && queue.state().counts.queued > 0) logger.warn(`KhÃ´i phá»¥c ${queue.state().counts.queued} job áº£nh Ä‘ang chá» â€“ báº¥m "Tiáº¿p tá»¥c" á»Ÿ Film Studio Ä‘á»ƒ cháº¡y`, 'queue')
  const queueSnapshot = (): QueueSnapshot => ({ state: queue.state(), jobs: queue.list(), settings: getFilmSettings() })
  const queueEmitter = new ThrottledEmitter<null>(() => sendToRenderer(mainWindow, 'queue:changed', queueSnapshot()), 300)
  queue.onChange(() => queueEmitter.emit('q', null))

  const depth = new DepthEstimator(path.join(repo.dataRoot, 'models'))
  const motion = new MotionService(repo, providers, queue, depth, (sid) => void notifyAll(sid))
  const sound = new SoundService(repo, providers, getSoundLibraryDir)
  logger.info(depth.hasModel() ? `Model depth: ${depth.modelPath}` : 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ model depth â€“ parallax dÃ¹ng depth Æ°á»›c lÆ°á»£ng (táº£i model á»Ÿ CÃ i Ä‘áº·t)', 'depth')

  const remotionEntry = path.join(app.getAppPath(), 'src', 'remotion', 'index.ts')
  const publish = new PublishService({ repo, providers, renderer: renderService, remotionEntry, mediaBaseUrl: mediaServer.baseUrl, getRenderScale })

  const runner = new PipelineRunner({
    repo,
    providers,
    images,
    motion,
    sound,
    publish,
    renderer: renderService,
    // Dev: app.getAppPath() = thÆ° má»¥c dá»± Ã¡n. ÄÃ³ng gÃ³i (GÄ6) sáº½ dÃ¹ng bundle dá»±ng sáºµn.
    remotionEntry,
    mediaBaseUrl: mediaServer.baseUrl,
    getRenderScale,
    emitProgress: (storyId, releaseId, state, force) =>
      // structuredClone: gá»­i áº£nh chá»¥p tráº¡ng thÃ¡i, trÃ¡nh renderer nháº­n object Ä‘ang bá»‹ sá»­a
      progressEmitter.emit(`${storyId}/${releaseId}`, { storyId, releaseId, state: structuredClone(state) }, force),
    emitDataChanged: (storyId) => dataEmitter.emit(storyId, storyId)
  })

  registerIpc({
    repo,
    runner,
    providers,
    stories: new StoryService(repo, () => providers.llm()),
    voices: new VoiceService(repo, providers, path.join(repo.dataRoot, 'cache', 'previews')),
    images,
    publish,
    motion,
    sound,
    depth,
    queue,
    queueSnapshot,
    getWindow: () => mainWindow,
    mediaBaseUrl: mediaServer.baseUrl,
    emitDataChanged: (storyId) => dataEmitter.emit(storyId, storyId, true)
  })
  logger.onEntry((entry) => sendToRenderer(mainWindow, 'log:entry', entry))

  mainWindow = createWindow()
  mainWindow.on('closed', () => (mainWindow = null))
  const uiSmokeDir = process.env['FHS_UI_SMOKE']
  if (uiSmokeDir) {
    const win = mainWindow
    win.webContents.once('did-finish-load', () => void runUiSmoke(win, uiSmokeDir))
  }
}

if (!app.requestSingleInstanceLock()) {
  app.quit()
} else {
  app.on('second-instance', () => {
    if (mainWindow) {
      if (mainWindow.isMinimized()) mainWindow.restore()
      mainWindow.focus()
    }
  })

  app.whenReady().then(bootstrap).catch((err: unknown) => {
    logger.error(`KhÃ´ng khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c: ${err instanceof Error ? err.stack : String(err)}`)
    app.quit()
  })

  app.on('activate', () => {
    if (BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length === 0 && mediaServer) mainWindow = createWindow()
  })

  app.on('window-all-closed', () => {
    if (process.platform !== 'darwin') app.quit()
  })

  app.on('before-quit', () => {
    appQueue?.pause()
    void appQueue?.persist()
    renderService?.dispose()
    void mediaServer?.close()
  })
}

/**
 * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ IPC handler cÃ³ kiá»ƒu. Má»—i kÃªnh trong IpcInvokeMap pháº£i cÃ³ handler tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng.

import fs from 'node:fs/promises'
import path from 'node:path'

/** CÃ³ thay Ä‘á»•i áº£nh hÆ°á»Ÿng tá»›i giá»ng (lá»i, ngÆ°á»i nÃ³i, delivery) khÃ´ng? Chá»‰ sá»­a báº£n dá»‹ch thÃ¬ khÃ´ng. */
function voiceRelevantChanged(before: Script, after: Script): boolean {
  if (before.lines.length !== after.lines.length) return true
  const key = (s: Script): string =>
    JSON.stringify(s.lines.map((l) => [l.id, l.speakerId, l.text, l.delivery, l.sectionId, l.beatId]))
  return key(before) !== key(after)
}

function sanitizeScript(release: Release, script: Script): Script {
  const takesById = new Map(release.script.lines.map((l) => [l.id, l]))
  const sectionIds = new Set(script.sections.map((s) => s.id))
  const ids = new Set<string>()
  for (const l of script.lines) {
    if (!l.id || ids.has(l.id)) throw new Error(`TrÃ¹ng hoáº·c thiáº¿u id cÃ¢u: "${l.id}"`)
    ids.add(l.id)
    if (!sectionIds.has(l.sectionId)) throw new Error(`CÃ¢u ${l.id} thuá»™c section khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i`)
    if (!l.text.trim()) throw new Error(`CÃ¢u ${l.id} Ä‘ang trá»‘ng`)
    // Giá»¯ take Ä‘Ã£ táº¡o (renderer khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c sá»­a take)
    const old = takesById.get(l.id)
    l.takes = old?.takes ?? []
    if (!l.takes.some((t) => t.id === l.selectedTakeId)) l.selectedTakeId = old?.selectedTakeId ?? null
    l.delivery.pace = Math.min(1.3, Math.max(0.6, Number(l.delivery.pace) || 1))
    l.delivery.pauseBeforeMs = Math.min(5000, Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(l.delivery.pauseBeforeMs) || 0)))
    l.delivery.pauseAfterMs = Math.min(5000, Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(l.delivery.pauseAfterMs) || 0)))
  }
  return script
}

type Handler<C extends IpcChannel> = (...args: IpcArgs<C>) => Promise<IpcResult<C>> | IpcResult<C>

function handle<C extends IpcChannel>(channel: C, fn: Handler<C>): void {
  ipcMain.handle(channel, async (_event, ...args: unknown[]) => {
    try {
      return await fn(...(args as IpcArgs<C>))
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
      logger.error(`[${channel}] ${msg}`, 'ipc')
      // Lá»—i ká»¹ thuáº­t (HTTP, file, máº¡ng) â‡’ thÃ´ng bÃ¡o tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t kÃ¨m cÃ¡ch xá»­ lÃ½
      if (err instanceof HttpError || (err as { code?: string } | null)?.code) throw new Error(toVietnameseError(err))
      throw err
    }
  })
}

export function sendToRenderer<E extends IpcEventChannel>(
  win: BrowserWindow | null,
  channel: E,
  payload: IpcEventMap[E]
): void {
  if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) win.webContents.send(channel, payload)
}

export interface IpcDeps {
  repo: StoryRepository
  runner: PipelineRunner
  stories: StoryService
  voices: VoiceService
  images: ImageService
  publish: PublishService
  motion: MotionService
  sound: SoundService
  depth: DepthEstimator
  queue: JobQueue
  queueSnapshot: () => QueueSnapshot
  getWindow: () => BrowserWindow | null
  providers: ProviderRegistry
  mediaBaseUrl: string
  emitDataChanged: (storyId: string) => void
}

const RUN_MODES: RunMode[] = ['review', 'fullAuto']

export function registerIpc(deps: IpcDeps): void {
  const { repo, runner, stories, voices } = deps

  const assertIdle = (storyId: string, releaseId: string): void => {
    if (runner.isRunning(storyId, releaseId)) throw new Error('Pipeline Ä‘ang cháº¡y â€“ hÃ£y Ä‘á»£i tá»›i Ä‘iá»ƒm duyá»‡t hoáº·c báº¥m Huá»· trÆ°á»›c khi sá»­a')
  }
  const changed = <T>(storyId: string, value: T): T => {
    deps.emitDataChanged(storyId)
    return value
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ Story (cáº¥p Story)
  handle('story:create', async (input) => {
    const s = await stories.createStory(input)
    return changed(s.id, s)
  })
  handle('story:update', async (id, patch) => changed(id, await stories.updateStory(id, patch)))
  handle('story:generateOutline', async (id) => changed(id, await stories.generateOutline(id)))
  handle('story:approveOutline', async (id) => changed(id, await stories.approveOutline(id)))
  handle('story:proposeCast', async (id) => changed(id, (await stories.proposeCast(id)).story))
  handle('story:proposeSceneBank', async (id) => changed(id, await stories.proposeSceneBank(id)))
  handle('library:update', async (patch) => {
    const lib = await stories.updateLibrary(patch)
    for (const s of await repo.listStories()) deps.emitDataChanged(s.id)
    return lib
  })

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ Release & ká»‹ch báº£n
  handle('release:create', async (storyId, input) => changed(storyId, await stories.createRelease(storyId, input)))

  handle('release:updateScript', async (storyId, releaseId, script) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    const release = await repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    const next = sanitizeScript(release, structuredClone(script))
    if (voiceRelevantChanged(release.script, next)) {
      // Sá»­a lá»i/giá»ng â‡’ cháº¡y láº¡i tá»« bÆ°á»›c 10; cÃ¢u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»•i váº«n dÃ¹ng take cÅ© (cache theo hash)
      resetStepsFrom(release.pipelineState, 10)
    }
    release.script = next
    await repo.saveRelease(release)
    return changed(storyId, release)
  })

  handle('release:rewriteLine', async (storyId, releaseId, lineId, instruction) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    const [release, library] = await Promise.all([repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId), repo.loadLibrary()])
    await rewriteLine({ llm: deps.providers.llm(), release, library, lineId, instruction })
    resetStepsFrom(release.pipelineState, 10)
    await repo.saveRelease(release)
    return changed(storyId, release)
  })

  handle('release:clearScript', async (storyId, releaseId) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    const release = await repo.loadRelease(storyId, releaseId)
    if (release.partIndex > 1) throw new Error('Chá»‰ chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ láº¡i tá»« Pháº§n 1 cá»§a series')
    release.script = { sections: [], lines: [] }
    release.voiceTrack = null
    release.scenes = []
    release.subtitles = []
    if (!resetStepsFrom(release.pipelineState, 8) && release.pipelineState) release.pipelineState.currentStep = null
    await repo.saveRelease(release)
    logger.info('ÄÃ£ xoÃ¡ ká»‹ch báº£n â€“ báº¥m "Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u" Ä‘á»ƒ LLM chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ láº¡i (bÆ°á»›c 8)', releaseId)
    return changed(storyId, release)
  })

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ Voice Studio
  handle('voice:regenerateLine', async (storyId, releaseId, lineId) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    return changed(storyId, await voices.regenerateLine(storyId, releaseId, lineId))
  })
  handle('voice:selectTake', async (storyId, releaseId, lineId, takeId) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    return changed(storyId, await voices.selectTake(storyId, releaseId, lineId, takeId))
  })
  handle('voice:peaks', (storyId, relPath, buckets) => voices.peaks(storyId, relPath, buckets))
  handle('voice:processPreview', (storyId, releaseId, lineId) => voices.processPreview(storyId, releaseId, lineId))
  handle('tts:preview', (input) => voices.ttsPreview(input))

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ HÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (Film Studio)
  const { queue, images } = deps
  const snap = deps.queueSnapshot
  handle('queue:get', () => snap())
  handle('queue:setOptions', (patch) => {
    const s = updateFilmSettings(patch)
    if (patch.concurrency !== undefined) queue.setConcurrency(s.concurrency)
    if (patch.autoRun !== undefined) {
      if (s.autoRun) queue.resume()
      else queue.pause()
    }
    return snap()
  })
  handle('queue:pause', () => (queue.pause(), snap()))
  handle('queue:resume', () => (queue.resume(), snap()))
  handle('queue:cancel', (id) => (queue.cancel(id), snap()))
  handle('queue:cancelAll', () => (queue.cancelAll(), snap()))
  handle('queue:retry', (id) => (queue.retry(id), snap()))
  handle('queue:clearFinished', () => (queue.clearFinished(), snap()))

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ áº¢nh & cáº£nh
  handle('images:generateScenes', async (storyId, sceneIds, aspect, opts) => (await images.generateScenes(storyId, sceneIds, aspect, opts)).length)
  handle('images:generateCharacter', async (characterId, what, opts) => (await images.generateCharacter(characterId, what, opts)).length)
  handle('scene:update', async (storyId, sceneId, patch) => changed(storyId, await images.updateScene(storyId, sceneId, patch)))
  handle('scene:applyVariant', async (storyId, sceneId, variantId) => changed(storyId, await images.applyVariant(storyId, sceneId, variantId)))
  handle('scene:checkConsistency', async (storyId, sceneIds) => changed(storyId, await images.checkConsistency(storyId, sceneIds)))
  // ------------------------------------------------------------ GÄ4: chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng, timeline, Ã¢m thanh
  const { motion, sound, depth } = deps
  const soundInfo = async (): Promise<{ dir: string; assets: SoundAsset[] }> => ({ dir: getSoundLibraryDir(), assets: (await repo.loadLibrary()).soundAssets })

  handle('scene:computeParallax', async (storyId, sceneId, aspect) => {
    await motion.computeParallax(storyId, sceneId, aspect, true)
    return changed(storyId, await repo.loadStory(storyId))
  })
  handle('scene:generateHeroClip', async (storyId, sceneId, aspect) => {
    const story = await repo.loadStory(storyId)
    const providerId = deps.providers.defaultVideoProvider()
    const p = deps.providers.video(providerId)
    const jobs = motion.enqueueClips(storyId, [sceneId], aspect, providerId, !!story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === sceneId)?.heroClip[aspect])
    return { jobs: jobs.length, cost: p.estimateCost(p.clipSeconds) }
  })
  handle('release:update', async (storyId, releaseId, patch) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    const r = await repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (rel) => {
      if (patch.heroRatio !== undefined && Math.abs(patch.heroRatio - rel.heroRatio) > 1e-6) {
        rel.heroRatio = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, patch.heroRatio))
        resetStepsFrom(rel.pipelineState, 16)
      }
    })
    return changed(storyId, r)
  })
  handle('release:updateScenes', async (storyId, releaseId, scenes) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    const r = await repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (rel) => {
      if (scenes.length !== rel.scenes.length || scenes.some((s, i) => s.id !== rel.scenes[i].id)) throw new Error('Danh sÃ¡ch cáº£nh khÃ´ng khá»›p â€“ táº£i láº¡i mÃ n hÃ¬nh')
      for (let i = 0; i < scenes.length; i++) {
        if (scenes[i].end - scenes[i].start < 0.3) throw new Error(`Cáº£nh ${i + 1} quÃ¡ ngáº¯n`)
        if (i > 0 && Math.abs(scenes[i].start - scenes[i - 1].end) > 0.002) throw new Error('CÃ¡c cáº£nh pháº£i liá»n nhau')
      }
      rel.scenes = rel.scenes.map((old, i) => ({ ...old, start: scenes[i].start, end: scenes[i].end, motion: scenes[i].motion, transition: scenes[i].transition, isHero: scenes[i].isHero }))
      resetStepsFrom(rel.pipelineState, 21)
    })
    return changed(storyId, r)
  })
  handle('release:updateSoundCues', async (storyId, releaseId, cues) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    const lib = await repo.loadLibrary()
    const r = await repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (rel) => {
      const v = validateCues(cues, lib.soundAssets, rel.voiceTrack?.duration ?? 0)
      if (v.problems.length) throw new Error(v.problems.join('; '))
      rel.soundCues = v.cues
      if (rel.voiceTrack) rel.voiceTrack.mixStale = true
      resetStepsFrom(rel.pipelineState, 21)
    })
    return changed(storyId, r)
  })
  handle('audio:remix', async (storyId, releaseId) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    await sound.mix(storyId, releaseId)
    return changed(storyId, await repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (rel) => void resetStepsFrom(rel.pipelineState, 21)))
  })
  handle('audio:redesign', async (storyId, releaseId) => {
    assertIdle(storyId, releaseId)
    const d = await sound.design(storyId, releaseId)
    await repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (rel) => {
      rel.soundCues = d.cues
      if (d.cost > 0) {
        rel.costs.actual += d.cost
        rel.costs.entries.push({ provider: deps.providers.llm().id, kind: 'llm', units: 1, cost: d.cost, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
      }
    })
    await sound.mix(storyId, releaseId)
    logger.info(`Thiáº¿t káº¿ láº¡i Ã¢m thanh: ${d.note}`, releaseId)
    return changed(storyId, await repo.updateRelease(storyId, releaseId, (rel) => void resetStepsFrom(rel.pipelineState, 21)))
  })
  handle('sound:info', () => soundInfo())
  handle('sound:chooseFolder', async () => {
    const win = deps.getWindow()
    const opts = { title: 'Chá»n thÆ° má»¥c thÆ° viá»‡n Ã¢m thanh', defaultPath: getSoundLibraryDir(), properties: ['openDirectory', 'createDirectory'] as ('openDirectory' | 'createDirectory')[] }
    const r = win ? await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, opts) : await dialog.showOpenDialog(opts)
    if (r.canceled || !r.filePaths[0]) return null
    setSoundLibraryDir(r.filePaths[0])
    await sound.scan()
    return soundInfo()
  })
  handle('sound:rescan', async () => {
    await sound.scan()
    return soundInfo()
  })
  handle('sound:generateStarter', async () => {
    await sound.generateStarterPack()

=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\logger.ts ===
/**
 * Logger: ghi ra file (má»—i ngÃ y 1 file) + bá»™ Ä‘á»‡m trong RAM + Ä‘áº©y vá» panel "Nháº­t kÃ½" cá»§a UI.
 * KhÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c Electron â‡’ dÃ¹ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c trong smoke test.
 */
import fs from 'node:fs'
import path from 'node:path'
import type { LogEntry } from '../shared/ipc'

type Listener = (entry: LogEntry) => void

class Logger {
  private file: string | null = null
  private buffer: LogEntry[] = []
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>()
  private nextId = 1
  private writeChain: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve()

  init(logDir: string): void {
    fs.mkdirSync(logDir, { recursive: true })
    const day = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)
    this.file = path.join(logDir, `app-${day}.log`)
  }

  get logFile(): string | null {
    return this.file
  }

  onEntry(listener: Listener): () => void {
    this.listeners.add(listener)
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener)
  }

  history(): LogEntry[] {
    return [...this.buffer]
  }

  info(message: string, scope = 'app'): void {
    this.push('info', message, scope)
  }

  warn(message: string, scope = 'app'): void {
    this.push('warn', message, scope)
  }

  error(message: string, scope = 'app'): void {
    this.push('error', message, scope)
  }

  private push(level: LogEntry['level'], message: string, scope: string): void {
    const entry: LogEntry = { id: this.nextId++, time: new Date().toISOString(), level, scope, message }
    this.buffer.push(entry)
    if (this.buffer.length > 1000) this.buffer.splice(0, this.buffer.length - 1000)
    for (const l of this.listeners) l(entry)
    const line = `${entry.time} [${level.toUpperCase()}] [${scope}] ${message}\n`
    if (level === 'error') console.error(line.trim())
    else console.log(line.trim())
    const file = this.file
    if (file) {
      // Ghi tuáº§n tá»± Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng láº«n dÃ²ng
      this.writeChain = this.writeChain.then(() => fs.promises.appendFile(file, line, 'utf8')).catch(() => undefined)
    }
  }
}

export const logger = new Logger()
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\main\progressEmitter.ts ===
/**
 * Gá»­i sá»± kiá»‡n tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™ vá» renderer, giá»›i háº¡n ~200ms/láº§n cho má»—i khoÃ¡ (trÃ¡nh lÃ m ngáº­p IPC).
 * force = gá»­i ngay (dÃ¹ng khi Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i bÆ°á»›c).
 */
export class ThrottledEmitter<T> {
  private last = new Map<string, number>()
  private timers = new Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>()
  private pending = new Map<string, T>()

  constructor(
    private readonly send: (key: string, payload: T) => void,
    private readonly intervalMs = 200
  ) {}

  emit(key: string, payload: T, force = false): void {
    const now = Date.now()
    const last = this.last.get(key) ?? 0
    if (force || now - last >= this.intervalMs) {
      const t = this.timers.get(key)
      if (t) clearTimeout(t)
      this.timers.delete(key)
      this.pending.delete(key)
      this.last.set(key, now)
      this.send(key, payload)
      return
    }
    this.pending.set(key, payload)
    if (!this.timers.has(key)) {
      this.timers.set(
        key,
        setTimeout(() => {
          this.timers.delete(key)
          const p = this.pending.get(key)
          this.pending.delete(key)
          if (p !== undefined) {
            this.last.set(key, Date.now())
            this.send(key, p)
          }
        }, this.intervalMs - (now - last))
      )
    }
  }
}

function getStore(): Store<SettingsSchema> {

      defaults: {

        soundLibraryDir: '',

      }
    })

    renderPresetId: s.get('renderPresetId'),
    apiKeyStatus: Object.fromEntries(PROVIDERS_WITH_KEYS.map((p) => [p, !!keys[p]])),
    perfMinViews: s.get('perfMinViews') ?? platformsConfig.publishing.performanceDefaults.minViews,
    perfMinAvgWatch: s.get('perfMinAvgWatch') ?? platformsConfig.publishing.performanceDefaults.minAvgWatchPercent,
