export function updateSettings(patch: { monthlyBudgetUsd?: number; renderPresetId?: string; mockMode?: boolean; perfMinViews?: number; perfMinAvgWatch?: number }): PublicSettings {
  const s = getStore()
  if (patch.perfMinViews !== undefined) s.set('perfMinViews', Math.max(0, Math.round(patch.perfMinViews)))
  if (patch.perfMinAvgWatch !== undefined) s.set('perfMinAvgWatch', Math.min(100, Math.max(0, patch.perfMinAvgWatch)))
  if (patch.mockMode !== undefined) s.set('mockMode', !!patch.mockMode)
  if (patch.monthlyBudgetUsd !== undefined) {
    if (!(patch.monthlyBudgetUsd >= 0)) throw new Error('NgÃ¢n sÃ¡ch thÃ¡ng pháº£i â‰¥ 0')
    s.set('monthlyBudgetUsd', patch.monthlyBudgetUsd)
  }
  if (patch.renderPresetId !== undefined) {
    if (!platformsConfig.renderPresets.some((p) => p.id === patch.renderPresetId)) throw new Error('Preset render khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i')
    s.set('renderPresetId', patch.renderPresetId)
  }
  return getPublicSettings()
}

export function setApiKey(providerId: string, apiKey: string): PublicSettings {
  if (!PROVIDERS_WITH_KEYS.includes(providerId)) throw new Error(`Provider khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: ${providerId}`)
  const s = getStore()
  const keys = { ...s.get('apiKeys') }
  if (!apiKey.trim()) {
    delete keys[providerId]
  } else {
    if (!safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) throw new Error('Há»‡ Ä‘iá»u hÃ nh khÃ´ng há»— trá»£ mÃ£ hoÃ¡ safeStorage â€“ khÃ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u API key an toÃ n')
    keys[providerId] = safeStorage.encryptString(apiKey.trim()).toString('base64')
  }
  s.set('apiKeys', keys)
  return getPublicSettings()
}

/** Chá»‰ dÃ¹ng trong main process (adapter tháº­t á»Ÿ GÄ2+) */
export function getApiKey(providerId: string): string | null {
  const enc = getStore().get('apiKeys')[providerId]
  if (!enc) return null
  return safeStorage.decryptString(Buffer.from(enc, 'base64'))
}

  logger.info(`UI smoke: ${shots.length} áº£nh, ${errors.length} lá»—i console`)
  app.quit()

/**
 * PRELOAD â€“ cáº§u ná»‘i an toÃ n duy nháº¥t giá»¯a renderer vÃ  main (contextIsolation: true).
 * Chá»‰ cho phÃ©p cÃ¡c kÃªnh Ä‘Ã£ khai bÃ¡o trong shared/ipc.ts (INVOKE_CHANNELS Ä‘Æ°á»£c TypeScript kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»§).
 */
import { contextBridge, ipcRenderer, type IpcRendererEvent } from 'electron'
import { INVOKE_CHANNELS, type FhsApi, type IpcEventChannel } from '../shared/ipc'

const EVENT_CHANNELS: readonly IpcEventChannel[] = ['pipeline:progress', 'data:changed', 'log:entry', 'queue:changed']

const api: FhsApi = {
  invoke: (channel, ...args) => {
    if (!INVOKE_CHANNELS.includes(channel)) return Promise.reject(new Error(`KÃªnh IPC khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: ${channel}`))
    return ipcRenderer.invoke(channel, ...args)
  },
  on: (channel, listener) => {
    if (!EVENT_CHANNELS.includes(channel)) throw new Error(`KÃªnh sá»± kiá»‡n khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: ${channel}`)
    const wrapped = (_e: IpcRendererEvent, payload: unknown): void => listener(payload as never)
    ipcRenderer.on(channel, wrapped)
    return () => {
      ipcRenderer.removeListener(channel, wrapped)
    }
  }
}

contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('api', api)

import { AbsoluteFill, Audio, Sequence, useVideoConfig } from 'remotion'
import type { CompositionProps } from '../shared/composition'
import { SceneLayer } from './SceneLayer'
import { SubtitleLayer } from './SubtitleLayer'
import { EndCard, GlossaryCards, HookCard, PartBadge, Vignette } from './Overlays'

export const FolkloreVideo: React.FC<CompositionProps> = (props) => {
  const { fps } = useVideoConfig()

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ backgroundColor: '#000' }}>
      {props.scenes.map((scene, i) => {
        const next = props.scenes[i + 1]
        const from = Math.round(scene.startSec * fps)
        const end = Math.round(scene.endSec * fps)
        // Cáº£nh káº¿ tiáº¿p "fade" â‡’ kÃ©o dÃ i cáº£nh nÃ y thÃªm má»™t Ä‘oáº¡n Ä‘á»ƒ 2 cáº£nh chá»“ng lÃªn nhau
        const overlap = next && next.transition.type === 'fade' ? Math.round(next.transition.durationSec * fps) : 0
        const duration = Math.max(1, end - from + overlap)
        return (
          <Sequence key={scene.id} from={from} durationInFrames={duration} name={`Cáº£nh ${i + 1}`}>
            <SceneLayer scene={scene} durationInFrames={duration} parallaxFactors={props.parallaxFactors ?? [0.2, 0.55, 1]} />
          </Sequence>
        )

      {props.partLabel ? <PartBadge label={props.partLabel} /> : null}

      {props.endCard ? <EndCard title={props.endCard.title} sub={props.endCard.sub} fromSec={props.endCard.fromSec} /> : null}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  )

import type { GlossaryCard } from '../shared/composition'

/** Viá»n tá»‘i quanh khung â€“ táº¡o khÃ´ng khÃ­ u Ã¡m */
export const Vignette: React.FC = () => (
  <AbsoluteFill
    style={{
      pointerEvents: 'none',
      background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at center, rgba(0,0,0,0) 45%, rgba(0,0,0,0.55) 85%, rgba(0,0,0,0.8) 100%)'
    }}

/** Tháº» chÃº thÃ­ch thuáº­t ngá»¯ tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t láº§n Ä‘áº§u xuáº¥t hiá»‡n (báº£n tiáº¿ng Anh) */
export const GlossaryCards: React.FC<{ cards: GlossaryCard[] }> = ({ cards }) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame()
  const { fps, width } = useVideoConfig()
  const time = frame / fps
  const card = cards.find((c) => time >= c.start && time < c.end)
  if (!card) return null
  const opacity = interpolate(time, [card.start, card.start + 0.4, card.end - 0.5, card.end], [0, 1, 1, 0], {
    extrapolateLeft: 'clamp',
    extrapolateRight: 'clamp'
  })
  const base = width * 0.012
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: base * 4,
        left: base * 4,
        maxWidth: width * 0.34,
        opacity,
        background: 'rgba(8,10,16,0.72)',
        borderLeft: `${Math.max(2, base * 0.35)}px solid #b3122e`,
        padding: `${base * 1.1}px ${base * 1.6}px`,
        borderRadius: base * 0.5,
        fontFamily: 'Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif',
        color: '#f4f1ea'
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ fontSize: base * 2.1, fontStyle: 'italic', fontWeight: 700, color: '#f3c77a' }}>{card.term}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: base * 1.45, marginTop: base * 0.3, lineHeight: 1.35 }}>{card.gloss}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

export const HookCard: React.FC<{ text: string; untilSec: number }> = ({ text, untilSec }) => {

  if (t > untilSec) return null
  const opacity = interpolate(t, [0, 0.25, untilSec - 0.4, untilSec], [0, 1, 1, 0], { extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp' })

    <div
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',

        left: '6%',
        right: '6%',

        textAlign: 'center',

      {text}
    </div>

export const EndCard: React.FC<{ title: string; sub: string; fromSec: number }> = ({ title, sub, fromSec }) => {

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ background: `rgba(5,6,10,${0.78 * o})`, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', opacity: o }}>

=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\remotion\SceneLayer.tsx ===
import { AbsoluteFill, Img, interpolate, OffthreadVideo, Sequence, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig } from 'remotion'
import type { CompositionScene } from '../shared/composition'
import { kenBurnsAt, parallaxLayerAt, type KenBurnsFrame } from '../shared/logic/kenBurns'

const clampOpts = { extrapolateLeft: 'clamp', extrapolateRight: 'clamp' } as const

function transformOf(kb: KenBurnsFrame): React.CSSProperties {
  return {
    transform: `translate(${kb.translateXPercent}%, ${kb.translateYPercent}%) scale(${kb.scale})`,
    transformOrigin: `${kb.originXPercent}% ${kb.originYPercent}%`
  }
}

const cover = (scene: CompositionScene): React.CSSProperties => ({
  width: '100%',
  height: '100%',
  objectFit: 'cover',
  objectPosition: `${scene.motion.focusPoint.x * 100}% ${scene.motion.focusPoint.y * 100}%`
})

/** áº¢nh tÄ©nh + Ken Burns (Má»©c 1) hoáº·c 3 lá»›p parallax (Má»©c 2) */
const Still: React.FC<{ scene: CompositionScene; t: number; factors: [number, number, number] }> = ({ scene, t, factors }) => {
  const { width } = useVideoConfig()
  if (scene.layers && scene.layers.length === 3) {
    return (
      <>
        {scene.layers.map((src, i) => (
          <AbsoluteFill key={i} style={transformOf(parallaxLayerAt(scene.motion, t, factors[i]))}>
            <Img src={src} style={cover(scene)} />
          </AbsoluteFill>
        ))}
      </>
    )
  }
  const motion = scene.motion.level === 'kenBurns' ? scene.motion : { ...scene.motion, type: 'zoomIn' as const }
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={transformOf(kenBurnsAt(motion, t))}>
      {scene.src ? (
        <Img src={scene.src} style={cover(scene)} />
      ) : (
        <AbsoluteFill
          style={{
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#6b7280',
            fontSize: width * 0.02,
            fontFamily: 'Segoe UI, Arial, sans-serif',
            background: 'linear-gradient(160deg, #0f172a, #1e1b2e)'
          }}
        >
          Thiáº¿u áº£nh â€“ {scene.label}
        </AbsoluteFill>
      )}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  )
}

/** Má»™t cáº£nh: Má»©c 1/2/3 + hiá»‡u á»©ng vÃ o cáº£nh (fade / dip-to-black / flash) */
export const SceneLayer: React.FC<{ scene: CompositionScene; durationInFrames: number; parallaxFactors: [number, number, number] }> = ({
  scene,
  durationInFrames,
  parallaxFactors
}) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame()
  const { fps } = useVideoConfig()
  const t = durationInFrames <= 1 ? 1 : frame / (durationInFrames - 1)
  const tf = Math.max(1, Math.round(scene.transition.durationSec * fps))

  const opacity = scene.transition.type === 'fade' ? interpolate(frame, [0, tf], [0, 1], clampOpts) : 1
  const blackOverlay = scene.transition.type === 'dipToBlack' ? interpolate(frame, [0, tf], [1, 0], clampOpts) : 0
  const flashOverlay = scene.transition.type === 'flash' ? interpolate(frame, [0, tf], [0.85, 0], clampOpts) : 0

  // Má»©c 3: clip video AI phÃ¡t trÆ°á»›c; cáº£nh dÃ i hÆ¡n clip â‡’ chuyá»ƒn má»m sang áº£nh tÄ©nh (Ken Burns) cho pháº§n cÃ²n láº¡i
  const clipFrames = scene.clip ? Math.min(durationInFrames, Math.max(1, Math.floor(scene.clip.durationSec * fps) - 2)) : 0
  const xf = Math.min(12, Math.floor(clipFrames / 3))
  const stillOpacity = scene.clip ? interpolate(frame, [clipFrames - xf, clipFrames], [0, 1], clampOpts) : 1

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ opacity, overflow: 'hidden', backgroundColor: '#05060a' }}>
      {scene.clip ? (
        <Sequence durationInFrames={clipFrames} layout="none">
          <AbsoluteFill>
            <OffthreadVideo src={scene.clip.src} muted style={cover(scene)} />
          </AbsoluteFill>
        </Sequence>
      ) : null}
      {stillOpacity > 0 ? (
        <AbsoluteFill style={{ opacity: stillOpacity }}>
          <Still scene={scene} t={t} factors={parallaxFactors} />
        </AbsoluteFill>
      ) : null}
      {blackOverlay > 0 ? <AbsoluteFill style={{ backgroundColor: '#000', opacity: blackOverlay }} /> : null}
      {flashOverlay > 0 ? <AbsoluteFill style={{ backgroundColor: '#fff', opacity: flashOverlay }} /> : null}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  )
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\remotion\SubtitleLayer.tsx ===
import { AbsoluteFill, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig } from 'remotion'
import type { SubtitleCue, SubtitleStyle } from '../shared/types'

/**
 * Phá»¥ Ä‘á» theo preset:
 * - youtube: chá»¯ vá»«a, ná»n má», thuáº­t ngá»¯ tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t in nghiÃªng cÃ³ mÃ u.
 * - tiktok: chá»¯ to, viá»n Ä‘en, highlight tá»«ng tá»« Ä‘ang Ä‘á»c, náº±m trong vÃ¹ng an toÃ n.
 */
export const SubtitleLayer: React.FC<{ cues: SubtitleCue[]; style: SubtitleStyle }> = ({ cues, style }) => {
  const frame = useCurrentFrame()
  const { fps, width, height } = useVideoConfig()
  const time = frame / fps
  const cue = cues.find((c) => time >= c.start && time < c.end + 0.15)
  if (!cue) return null

  const fontSize = Math.round(width * style.fontSizeRatio)
  const stroke = Math.max(1, Math.round(width * style.strokeWidthRatio))
  const shadow = style.backgroundColor
    ? `0 ${stroke}px ${stroke * 3}px rgba(0,0,0,0.8)`
    : [
        `${stroke}px ${stroke}px 0 ${style.strokeColor}`,
        `-${stroke}px ${stroke}px 0 ${style.strokeColor}`,
        `${stroke}px -${stroke}px 0 ${style.strokeColor}`,
        `-${stroke}px -${stroke}px 0 ${style.strokeColor}`,
        `0 ${stroke * 2}px ${stroke * 4}px rgba(0,0,0,0.85)`
      ].join(', ')

  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
      <div
        style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          left: `${style.horizontalCenter * 100}%`,
          top: `${style.verticalPosition * 100}%`,
          transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)',
          width: width * style.maxWidthRatio,
          display: 'flex',
          justifyContent: 'center'
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            fontFamily: style.fontFamily,
            fontSize,
            fontWeight: style.fontWeight,
            lineHeight: 1.25,
            color: style.color,
            textAlign: 'center',
            textShadow: shadow,
            textTransform: style.uppercase ? 'uppercase' : 'none',
            backgroundColor: style.backgroundColor ?? 'transparent',
            padding: style.backgroundColor ? `${fontSize * 0.18}px ${fontSize * 0.5}px` : 0,
            borderRadius: fontSize * 0.2,
            maxHeight: height * 0.3
          }}
        >
          {cue.words.map((w, i) => {
            const active = style.wordHighlight && time >= w.start && time < w.end
            const italic = style.italicGlossary && w.emphasis
            return (
              <span
                key={i}
                style={{
                  color: active ? style.highlightColor : w.emphasis ? style.emphasisColor : style.color,
                  fontStyle: italic ? 'italic' : 'normal',
                  display: 'inline-block',
                  transform: active ? 'scale(1.08)' : 'none',
                  marginRight: '0.28em'
                }}
              >
                {w.text}
              </span>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  )
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\renderer\src\components\forms.tsx ===
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { errorMessage } from '../lib/format'
import { useApp } from '../store/appStore'
import { Icon } from './Icon'

/** NÃºt cháº¡y tÃ¡c vá»¥ async: hiá»‡n "Äang cháº¡yâ€¦", khoÃ¡ nÃºt, lá»—i â‡’ toast tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t */
export function AsyncButton({
  onClick,
  children,
  className = 'btn-secondary',
  disabled,
  title,
  confirm
}: {
  onClick: () => Promise<unknown>
  children: ReactNode
  className?: string
  disabled?: boolean
  title?: string
  /** Há»i xÃ¡c nháº­n trÆ°á»›c (dÃ¹ng há»™p thoáº¡i trong app, khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng window.confirm) */
  confirm?: string
}): JSX.Element {
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [asking, setAsking] = useState(false)
  const toast = useApp((s) => s.toast)
  const run = async (): Promise<void> => {
    setAsking(false)
    setBusy(true)
    try {
      await onClick()
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }
  return (
    <>
      <button className={className} disabled={disabled || busy} title={title} onClick={() => (confirm ? setAsking(true) : void run())}>
        {busy ? <span className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-current border-t-transparent" /> : null}
        {busy ? 'Äang cháº¡yâ€¦' : children}
      </button>
      {asking ? (
        <Modal title="XÃ¡c nháº­n" onClose={() => setAsking(false)}>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-300">{confirm}</p>
          <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
            <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => setAsking(false)}>
              Huá»·
            </button>
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={() => void run()}>
              Äá»“ng Ã½
            </button>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
}

export function Modal({ title, children, onClose, wide }: { title: string; children: ReactNode; onClose: () => void; wide?: boolean }): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 p-6" onMouseDown={onClose}>
      <div
        className={`panel max-h-full w-full overflow-auto p-5 shadow-2xl ${wide ? 'max-w-3xl' : 'max-w-lg'}`}
        onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-100">{title}</h2>
          <button className="btn-ghost px-1 py-1" onClick={onClose}>
            <Icon name="x" className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function Text({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder,
  className = ''
}: {
  label?: string
  value: string
  onChange: (v: string) => void
  placeholder?: string
  className?: string
}): JSX.Element {
  const input = <input className={`input w-full ${className}`} value={value} placeholder={placeholder} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} />
  return label ? <Labeled label={label}>{input}</Labeled> : input
}

export function Area({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  rows = 2,
  placeholder,
  className = ''
}: {
  label?: string
  value: string
  onChange: (v: string) => void
  rows?: number
  placeholder?: string
  className?: string
}): JSX.Element {
  const area = (
    <textarea
      className={`input w-full resize-y leading-relaxed ${className}`}
      rows={rows}
      value={value}
      placeholder={placeholder}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
    />
  )
  return label ? <Labeled label={label}>{area}</Labeled> : area
}

export function Num({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  step = 1,
  min,
  max,
  className = 'w-24'
}: {
  label?: string
  value: number
  onChange: (v: number) => void
  step?: number
  min?: number
  max?: number
  className?: string
}): JSX.Element {
  const input = (
    <input
      type="number"
      className={`input ${className}`}
      value={Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0}
      step={step}
      min={min}
      max={max}
      onChange={(e) => onChange(Number(e.target.value))}
    />
  )
  return label ? <Labeled label={label}>{input}</Labeled> : input
}

export function Select<T extends string>({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
  className = 'w-full'
}: {
  label?: string
  value: T
  onChange: (v: T) => void
  options: { value: T; label: string }[]
  className?: string
}): JSX.Element {
  const sel = (
    <select className={`input ${className}`} value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value as T)}>
      {options.map((o) => (
        <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>
          {o.label}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  )
  return label ? <Labeled label={label}>{sel}</Labeled> : sel
}

export function Labeled({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className="mb-1 block text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">{label}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  )
}

/** Chuá»—i "a, b, c" â‡„ máº£ng */
export function ListText({ label, value, onChange, placeholder }: { label?: string; value: string[]; onChange: (v: string[]) => void; placeholder?: string }): JSX.Element {
  const [text, setText] = useState(value.join(', '))
  const [last, setLast] = useState(value)
  if (last !== value && value.join(', ') !== text.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(', ')) {
    setLast(value)
    setText(value.join(', '))
  }
  return (
    <Text
      label={label}
      value={text}
      placeholder={placeholder}
      onChange={(t) => {
        setText(t)
        const arr = t.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean)
        setLast(arr)
        onChange(arr)
      }}
    />
  )
}

const AVATAR_COLORS = ['#7c3aed', '#b3122e', '#0e7490', '#a16207', '#15803d', '#be185d', '#4338ca']

export function Avatar({ name, id, size = 24, ring }: { name: string; id: string; size?: number; ring?: boolean }): JSX.Element {
  let h = 0
  for (const ch of id) h = (h * 31 + ch.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0
  const initials = name
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(-2)
    .map((w) => w[0]?.toUpperCase())
    .join('')
  return (
    <span
      title={name}
      className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full font-bold text-white ${ring ? 'ring-2 ring-amber-300' : ''}`}
      style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: size * 0.4, background: AVATAR_COLORS[h % AVATAR_COLORS.length] }}
    >
      {initials || '?'}
    </span>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'

import { Icon } from './Icon'

export function LogPanel(): JSX.Element | null {
  const open = useApp((s) => s.logOpen)
  const logs = useApp((s) => s.logs)
  const toggleLog = useApp((s) => s.toggleLog)
  const logFile = useApp((s) => s.appInfo?.logFile)
  const endRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: 'end' })
  }, [open, logs.length])

  if (!open) return null
  return (
    <div className="flex h-56 shrink-0 flex-col border-t border-ink-600 bg-ink-950">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-ink-700 px-3 py-1.5">
        <span className="panel-title">Nháº­t kÃ½</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
          <span className="truncate">File log: {logFile ?? 'â€“'}</span>
          <button className="btn-ghost px-1 py-0.5" onClick={() => toggleLog(false)}>
            <Icon name="x" className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-auto px-3 py-1 font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-5">
        {logs.map((l) => (
          <div
            key={l.id}

export function CastEditor({ cast, characters, onChange }: { cast: CastMember[]; characters: Character[]; onChange: (c: CastMember[]) => void }): JSX.Element {
  const set = (i: number, patch: Partial<CastMember>): void => onChange(cast.map((m, k) => (k === i ? { ...m, ...patch } : m)))
  const faces = cast.filter((m) => characters.find((c) => c.id === m.characterId)?.role !== 'entity' && m.position !== 'background').length
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      {cast.map((m, i) => {
        const c = characters.find((x) => x.id === m.characterId)
        return (
          <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[24px_1.2fr_1fr_1fr_110px_1.4fr_28px] items-center gap-2">
            {c ? <Avatar name={c.name} id={c.id} size={22} ring={c.locked} /> : <span />}
            <select className="input text-xs" value={m.characterId} onChange={(e) => set(i, { characterId: e.target.value, stateId: null })}>
              {characters.map((x) => (
                <option key={x.id} value={x.id}>
                  {x.name}
                  {x.scope === 'channel' ? ' (kÃªnh)' : ''}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <select className="input text-xs" value={m.stateId ?? ''} onChange={(e) => set(i, { stateId: e.target.value || null })}>
              <option value="">(tráº¡ng thÃ¡i thÆ°á»ng)</option>
              {c?.states.map((s) => (
                <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                  {s.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <input className="input text-xs" list={`expr-${m.characterId}`} placeholder="biá»ƒu cáº£m" value={m.expression} onChange={(e) => set(i, { expression: e.target.value })} />
            <datalist id={`expr-${m.characterId}`}>
              {c?.expressionSheet.map((e) => <option key={e.expression} value={e.expression} />)}
            </datalist>
            <select className="input text-xs" value={m.position} onChange={(e) => set(i, { position: e.target.value as CastMember['position'] })}>
              {POSITIONS.map((p) => (
                <option key={p.value} value={p.value}>
                  {p.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <input className="input text-xs" placeholder="hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng" value={m.action} onChange={(e) => set(i, { action: e.target.value })} />
            <button className="btn-ghost px-1 text-red-300" onClick={() => onChange(cast.filter((_, k) => k !== i))}>
              <Icon name="x" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        )

import { getStepDef, PIPELINE_TOTAL_STEPS } from '../../../shared/pipeline'
import { formatUsd } from '../lib/format'
import { useApp, useReleases } from '../store/appStore'
import { Icon } from './Icon'

/** Thanh tráº¡ng thÃ¡i: chi phÃ­, cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock, bÆ°á»›c Ä‘ang cháº¡y, nÃºt má»Ÿ Nháº­t kÃ½ */
export function StatusBar(): JSX.Element {
  const stories = useApp((s) => s.stories)
  const appInfo = useApp((s) => s.appInfo)
  const logOpen = useApp((s) => s.logOpen)
  const toggleLog = useApp((s) => s.toggleLog)
  const releases = useReleases()
  const lastLog = useApp((s) => s.logs[s.logs.length - 1])

  const monthCost = stories.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.costActual, 0)
  const running = releases.find((r) => r.pipelineState?.running)
  const step = running?.pipelineState?.currentStep != null ? getStepDef(running.pipelineState.currentStep) : undefined
  const progress = step && running?.pipelineState ? running.pipelineState.steps[step.no]?.progress ?? 0 : 0

  return (
    <footer className="flex h-7 shrink-0 items-center gap-4 border-t border-ink-700 bg-ink-950 px-3 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
      <span>
        Chi phÃ­ thÃ¡ng: <b className="text-slate-200">{formatUsd(monthCost)}</b>
      </span>
      {appInfo?.mockMode ? (
        <span className="rounded bg-night-700/70 px-1.5 text-night-400">Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock (khÃ´ng tá»‘n phÃ­)</span>
      ) : null}
      {running && step ? (
        <span className="text-blood-300">
          â— BÆ¯á»šC {step.no}/{PIPELINE_TOTAL_STEPS}: {step.title} â€“ {Math.round(progress * 100)}%
        </span>
      ) : null}
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-slate-500">{lastLog ? lastLog.message : ''}</span>

export function Panel({
  title,
  actions,
  children,
  className = ''
}: {

  actions?: ReactNode

}): JSX.Element {

export function LangBadge({ language }: { language: Language }): JSX.Element {
  const cls = language === 'vi' ? 'bg-red-900/60 text-yellow-300 border-red-700' : 'bg-blue-950/70 text-sky-200 border-blue-800'
  return <span className={`rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold ${cls}`}>{language.toUpperCase()}</span>
}

export function PlatformBadge({ platform }: { platform: Platform }): JSX.Element {

    platform === 'tiktok'
      ? 'bg-pink-950/60 text-pink-200 border-pink-800'
      : platform === 'youtube'
        ? 'bg-red-950/60 text-red-200 border-red-800'
        : 'bg-orange-950/60 text-orange-200 border-orange-800'

}

=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\renderer\src\components\Waveform.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

const cache = new Map<string, number[]>()

/** Waveform nhá» cá»§a má»™t take (Ä‘á»‰nh biÃªn Ä‘á»™ tÃ­nh á»Ÿ main process tá»« file WAV) */
export function Waveform({ storyId, relPath, width = 160, height = 26 }: { storyId: string; relPath: string | null; width?: number; height?: number }): JSX.Element {
  const key = relPath ? `${storyId}/${relPath}` : ''
  const [peaks, setPeaks] = useState<number[] | null>(cache.get(key) ?? null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!relPath) return setPeaks(null)
    const hit = cache.get(key)
    if (hit) return setPeaks(hit)
    let alive = true
    void window.api
      .invoke('voice:peaks', storyId, relPath, 48)
      .then((p) => {
        cache.set(key, p)
        if (alive) setPeaks(p)
      })
      .catch(() => alive && setPeaks([]))
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [key, relPath, storyId])

  if (!relPath) return <div className="rounded bg-ink-900 text-center text-[10px] leading-[26px] text-slate-600" style={{ width, height }}>chÆ°a cÃ³ take</div>
  const bars = peaks ?? []
  const bw = width / Math.max(1, bars.length)
  // Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ theo Ä‘á»‰nh cá»§a chÃ­nh take â‡’ giá»ng nhá» váº«n nhÃ¬n rÃµ hÃ¬nh dáº¡ng
  const peak = Math.max(0.001, ...bars)
  return (
    <svg width={width} height={height} className="rounded bg-ink-900">
      {bars.map((p, i) => {
        const hh = Math.max(1, (p / peak) * (height - 2))
        return <rect key={i} x={i * bw + 0.5} y={(height - hh) / 2} width={Math.max(1, bw - 1)} height={hh} fill="#34d399" opacity={0.85} />
      })}
    </svg>
  )
}

  player.pause()
  player.src = src
  void player.play().catch(() => undefined)
}

  player?.pause()
}

=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\renderer\src\lib\format.ts ===
export function formatTime(sec: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (sec == null || !Number.isFinite(sec)) return 'â€“'
  const s = Math.max(0, sec)
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
  const r = Math.floor(s % 60)
  return `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}`
}

export function formatTimePrecise(sec: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (sec == null || !Number.isFinite(sec)) return 'â€“'
  const m = Math.floor(sec / 60)
  const r = (sec % 60).toFixed(2).padStart(5, '0')
  return `${m}:${r}`
}

export function formatDuration(ms: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.round(ms / 1000))
  if (s < 60) return `${s} giÃ¢y`
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
  return `${m} phÃºt ${s % 60} giÃ¢y`
}

export function formatUsd(n: number): string {
  return `$${n.toFixed(2)}`
}

export function formatBytes(n: number): string {
  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`
  if (n < 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`
  return `${(n / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`
}

export function errorMessage(err: unknown): string {
  const raw = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
  // Electron thÃªm tiá»n tá»‘ "Error invoking remote method 'x': Error: ..."
  return raw.replace(/^Error invoking remote method '[^']+': (Error: )?/, '')

import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { buildCompositionProps, type CompositionProps } from '../../../shared/composition'
import { platformsConfig } from '../../../shared/config'
import { buildMediaUrl } from '../../../shared/media'
import type { AspectRatio, Release, Story } from '../../../shared/types'

/** URL media cá»§a Story (qua media server ná»™i bá»™) */
export function useMediaUrl(storyId: string | undefined): (rel: string, v?: string) => string {
  const base = useApp((s) => s.appInfo?.mediaBaseUrl ?? '')
  return useMemo(() => (rel: string, v?: string) => (storyId ? buildMediaUrl(base, storyId, rel, v) : ''), [base, storyId])
}

/** URL áº£nh nhÃ¢n váº­t (thÆ° má»¥c dÃ¹ng chung _characters) */
export function useCharacterUrl(): (rel: string | null | undefined) => string | null {

  return useMemo(() => (rel) => (rel ? buildMediaUrl(base, '_characters', rel) : null), [base])
}

/** Props cho Remotion Player â€“ cÃ¹ng hÃ m vá»›i lÃºc render â‡’ xem trÆ°á»›c khá»›p vá»›i video xuáº¥t ra */
export function usePreviewProps(
  story: Story | null,
  release: Release | null,
  aspect: AspectRatio
): CompositionProps | null {
  const library = useApp((s) => s.library)
  const mediaUrl = useMediaUrl(story?.id)
  return useMemo(() => {
    if (!story || !release || !library || release.scenes.length === 0) return null
    const channel = library.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === story.channelPresetId)
    return buildCompositionProps({
      story,
      release,
      glossary: library.pronunciations.filter((p) => p.language === release.language),
      subtitleStyle: channel?.subtitleStyleByPlatform[release.platform] ?? null,
      mediaUrl,
      fps: platformsConfig.fps,
      sizes: platformsConfig.aspectSizes,
      aspect,
      parallaxFactors: platformsConfig.parallax.layerFactors
    })
  }, [story, release, library, mediaUrl, aspect])
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\renderer\src\lib\useLibraryDraft.ts ===
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { Library } from '../../../shared/types'
import { useApp } from '../store/appStore'

/** Báº£n nhÃ¡p thÆ° viá»‡n cho mÃ n chá»‰nh sá»­a: sá»­a thoáº£i mÃ¡i, báº¥m LÆ°u má»›i ghi xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a */
export function useLibraryDraft(): {
  draft: Library | null
  dirty: boolean
  setDraft: (fn: (lib: Library) => Library) => void
  reset: () => void
  save: (keys: (keyof Library)[]) => Promise<void>
} {
  const library = useApp((s) => s.library)
  const reloadAll = useApp((s) => s.reloadAll)
  const [draft, setDraftState] = useState<Library | null>(library)
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirty) setDraftState(library)
  }, [library, dirty])

  return {
    draft,
    dirty,
    setDraft: (fn) => {
      setDraftState((d) => (d ? fn(structuredClone(d)) : d))
      setDirty(true)
    },
    reset: () => {
      setDraftState(library)
      setDirty(false)
    },
    save: async (keys) => {
      if (!draft) return
      const patch: Partial<Library> = {}
      for (const k of keys) (patch as Record<string, unknown>)[k] = draft[k]
      await window.api.invoke('library:update', patch)
      setDirty(false)
      await reloadAll()
    }
  }
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { getStepDef, PIPELINE_TOTAL_STEPS, RELEASE_PIPELINE, stepStatusLabel } from '../../../../shared/pipeline'
import type { PipelineState } from '../../../../shared/types'
import { Icon } from '../../components/Icon'
import { ProgressBar } from '../../components/ui'
import { formatDuration } from '../../lib/format'

/** "BÆ¯á»šC 16/23: Táº¡o video AI â€“ 64%" + thá»i gian Ä‘Ã£ cháº¡y + Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh cÃ²n láº¡i */
export function ProgressHeader({ state }: { state: PipelineState | null }): JSX.Element {
  const [, tick] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state?.running) return
    const t = setInterval(() => tick((x) => x + 1), 1000)
    return () => clearInterval(t)
  }, [state?.running])

  if (!state) {
    return <div className="text-sm text-slate-400">ChÆ°a cháº¡y. Chá»n cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ rá»“i báº¥m "Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u".</div>
  }
  const def = state.currentStep != null ? getStepDef(state.currentStep) : undefined
  if (!def) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-emerald-300">
        <Icon name="check" className="h-4 w-4" /> ÄÃ£ hoÃ n táº¥t táº¥t cáº£ cÃ¡c bÆ°á»›c â€“ xem video á»Ÿ mÃ n Xuáº¥t báº£n.
      </div>
    )
  }
  const st = state.steps[def.no]
  const elapsed = st?.startedAt ? Date.now() - new Date(st.startedAt).getTime() : 0
  const p = st?.progress ?? 0
  const eta = st?.status === 'running' && p > 0.02 ? (elapsed * (1 - p)) / p : null
  const color =
    st?.status === 'failed'
      ? 'text-red-400'
      : st?.status === 'waitingApproval'
        ? 'text-amber-300'
        : st?.status === 'running'
          ? 'text-blood-300'
          : 'text-slate-300'

  return (
    <div className="space-y-1.5">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4">
        <div className={`text-base font-semibold ${color}`}>
          BÆ¯á»šC {def.no}/{PIPELINE_TOTAL_STEPS}: {def.title}
          {def.approval ? ' â˜…' : ''} â€“ {st?.status === 'running' ? `${Math.round(p * 100)}%` : stepStatusLabel(st?.status ?? 'pending')}
        </div>
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
          {st?.status === 'running' ? (
            <>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                <Icon name="clock" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> {formatDuration(elapsed)}
              </span>
              {eta != null ? <span>cÃ²n ~{formatDuration(eta)}</span> : null}
            </>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
      {st?.status === 'running' ? <ProgressBar value={p} /> : null}
      <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">{st?.error ?? st?.message ?? ''}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

  ambience: 'Ambience (ná»n)',
  sfx: 'SFX',
  stinger: 'Stinger (nháº¥n)',
  music: 'Nháº¡c ná»n',
  silence: 'Khoáº£ng láº·ng cÃ³ chá»§ Ä‘Ã­ch'
}

/** Cá»™t pháº£i AutoEdit khi chá»n má»™t sound cue trÃªn timeline */
export function SoundCuePanel({ story, release }: { story: Story; release: Release }): JSX.Element | null {
  const cueId = useApp((s) => s.selectedCueId)
  const library = useApp((s) => s.library)
  const refresh = useApp((s) => s.refresh)
  const selectCue = useApp((s) => s.selectCue)

  useEffect(() => setD(cue), [cue])
  if (!cue || !d) return null

  const assets = (library?.soundAssets ?? []).filter((a) => a.kind === d.kind && a.licenseConfirmed)
  const asset = library?.soundAssets.find((a) => a.id === d.assetId)

  const locked = !!release.pipelineState?.running

    await window.api.invoke('release:updateSoundCues', story.id, release.id, cues)
    await refresh()

        <div className="mt-1 text-slate-100">{KIND_LABEL[d.kind]}</div>

                </option>

            </AsyncButton>

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type RefObject } from 'react'
import type { PlayerRef } from '@remotion/player'
import { dragCue, lineGapSnapPoints, moveSceneBoundary, snap, type CueDragMode } from '../../../../shared/logic/timelineEdit'
import type { Release, ReleaseScene, SoundCue, Story } from '../../../../shared/types'
import { AsyncButton } from '../../components/forms'
import { errorMessage, formatTime, SHOT_LABEL } from '../../lib/format'
import { useApp } from '../../store/appStore'

const TRACK_H = 30
const LABEL_W = 92

const LEVEL_COLOR: Record<string, string> = {
  kenBurns: 'border-night-600 bg-night-700/70',
  parallax: 'border-teal-700 bg-teal-900/60',
  aiVideo: 'border-amber-600 bg-amber-900/50'
}

/** Váº¡ch phÃ¡t â€“ tá»± láº¯ng nghe Player Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng render láº¡i cáº£ timeline má»—i khung hÃ¬nh */
function Playhead({ playerRef, fps, pxPerSec, onFrame }: { playerRef: RefObject<PlayerRef | null>; fps: number; pxPerSec: number; onFrame: (f: number) => void }): JSX.Element {
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    const p = playerRef.current
    if (!p) return
    const h = (e: { detail: { frame: number } }): void => {
      setFrame(e.detail.frame)
      onFrame(e.detail.frame)
    }
    p.addEventListener('frameupdate', h)
    return () => p.removeEventListener('frameupdate', h)
  }, [playerRef, onFrame])
  return (
    <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-0 top-0 z-20 w-px bg-blood-400" style={{ left: LABEL_W + (frame / fps) * pxPerSec }}>
      <div className="absolute -left-1.5 top-0 h-3 w-3 rotate-45 bg-blood-400" />
    </div>
  )
}

/** Waveform cá»§a voice track (Ä‘á»‰nh biÃªn Ä‘á»™ tÃ­nh á»Ÿ main) */
function VoiceWave({ storyId, relPath, duration, pxPerSec }: { storyId: string; relPath: string | null; duration: number; pxPerSec: number }): JSX.Element | null {
  const [peaks, setPeaks] = useState<number[]>([])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!relPath) return
    let alive = true
    void window.api
      .invoke('voice:peaks', storyId, relPath, Math.min(4000, Math.max(200, Math.round(duration * 8))))
      .then((p) => alive && setPeaks(p))
      .catch(() => undefined)
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [storyId, relPath, duration])
  if (!peaks.length) return null
  const w = duration * pxPerSec
  const bw = w / peaks.length
  const peak = Math.max(0.001, ...peaks)
  return (
    <svg className="pointer-events-none absolute left-0 top-0" width={w} height={TRACK_H}>
      {peaks.map((p, i) => {
        const h = Math.max(1, (p / peak) * (TRACK_H - 6))
        return <rect key={i} x={i * bw} y={(TRACK_H - h) / 2} width={Math.max(0.6, bw - 0.3)} height={h} fill="#10b981" opacity={0.45} />
      })}
    </svg>
  )
}

type Drag =
  | { kind: 'boundary'; index: number; x0: number; orig: ReleaseScene[] }
  | { kind: 'cue'; id: string; mode: CueDragMode; x0: number; orig: SoundCue }

export function Timeline({ story, release, playerRef, fps }: { story: Story; release: Release; playerRef: RefObject<PlayerRef | null>; fps: number }): JSX.Element {
  const [pxPerSec, setPxPerSec] = useState(14)
  const selectedSceneId = useApp((s) => s.selectedSceneId)
  const selectedCueId = useApp((s) => s.selectedCueId)
  const selectScene = useApp((s) => s.selectScene)
  const selectCue = useApp((s) => s.selectCue)
  const toast = useApp((s) => s.toast)
  const refresh = useApp((s) => s.refresh)
  const [scenes, setScenes] = useState(release.scenes)
  const [cues, setCues] = useState(release.soundCues)
  const drag = useRef<Drag | null>(null)
  const frameRef = useRef(0)
  const onFrame = useCallback((f: number) => {
    frameRef.current = f
  }, [])
  const running = !!release.pipelineState?.running

  // Äá»“ng bá»™ khi dá»¯ liá»‡u thay Ä‘á»•i tá»« main (khÃ´ng ghi Ä‘Ã¨ lÃºc Ä‘ang kÃ©o)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!drag.current) {
      setScenes(release.scenes)
      setCues(release.soundCues)
    }
  }, [release.scenes, release.soundCues])

  const duration = release.voiceTrack?.duration ?? release.scenes[release.scenes.length - 1]?.end ?? 0
  const width = Math.max(600, duration * pxPerSec)
  const bankById = new Map(story.sceneBank.map((b) => [b.id, b]))
  const snapPts = lineGapSnapPoints(release.script.lines)
  const seek = (sec: number): void => playerRef.current?.seekTo(Math.round(sec * fps))

  const commit = async (d: Drag, nextScenes: ReleaseScene[], nextCues: SoundCue[]): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      if (d.kind === 'boundary') await window.api.invoke('release:updateScenes', story.id, release.id, nextScenes)
      else await window.api.invoke('release:updateSoundCues', story.id, release.id, nextCues)
      await refresh()
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
      setScenes(release.scenes)
      setCues(release.soundCues)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    let latestScenes = scenes
    let latestCues = cues
    const move = (e: MouseEvent): void => {
      const d = drag.current
      if (!d) return
      const dt = (e.clientX - d.x0) / pxPerSec
      if (d.kind === 'boundary') {
        let t = d.orig[d.index].start + dt
        if (!e.altKey) t = snap(t, snapPts, 0.35)
        latestScenes = moveSceneBoundary(d.orig, d.index, t)
        setScenes(latestScenes)
      } else {
        latestCues = cues.map((c) => (c.id === d.id ? dragCue(d.orig, d.mode, dt, duration) : c))
        setCues(latestCues)
      }
    }
    const up = (): void => {
      const d = drag.current
      if (!d) return
      drag.current = null
      document.body.style.cursor = ''
      const moved = d.kind === 'boundary' ? latestScenes !== d.orig : JSON.stringify(latestCues.find((c) => c.id === d.id)) !== JSON.stringify(d.orig)
      if (moved) void commit(d, latestScenes, latestCues)
    }
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', move)
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', up)
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', move)
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', up)
    }
  })

  const startDrag = (e: React.MouseEvent, d: Drag, cursor: string): void => {
    if (running) return toast('Pipeline Ä‘ang cháº¡y â€“ Ä‘á»£i tá»›i Ä‘iá»ƒm duyá»‡t Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‰nh timeline')
    e.preventDefault()
    e.stopPropagation()
    drag.current = d
    document.body.style.cursor = cursor
  }

  const cueBlock = (c: SoundCue): JSX.Element => {
    const active = c.id === selectedCueId
    const colors: Record<SoundCue['kind'], string> = {
      ambience: 'border-sky-700 bg-sky-900/50 text-sky-100',
      music: 'border-violet-700 bg-violet-900/50 text-violet-100',
      sfx: 'border-orange-700 bg-orange-900/50 text-orange-100',
      stinger: 'border-red-600 bg-red-900/60 text-red-100',
      silence: 'border-slate-500 bg-[repeating-linear-gradient(45deg,#1f2937_0,#1f2937_4px,#0b0d14_4px,#0b0d14_8px)] text-slate-300'
    }
    return (
      <div
        key={c.id}
        title={`${c.kind} Â· ${c.start.toFixed(2)}â€“${c.end.toFixed(2)}s${c.reason ? `\n${c.reason}` : ''}`}
        className={`absolute top-0.5 cursor-grab overflow-hidden rounded border px-1 text-[10px] leading-[24px] ${colors[c.kind]} ${active ? 'ring-2 ring-white' : ''}`}
        style={{ left: c.start * pxPerSec, width: Math.max(4, (c.end - c.start) * pxPerSec), height: TRACK_H - 4 }}
        onMouseDown={(e) => {
          selectCue(c.id)
          startDrag(e, { kind: 'cue', id: c.id, mode: 'move', x0: e.clientX, orig: c }, 'grabbing')
        }}
      >
        <span className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 top-0 w-1.5 cursor-ew-resize" onMouseDown={(e) => startDrag(e, { kind: 'cue', id: c.id, mode: 'start', x0: e.clientX, orig: c }, 'ew-resize')} />
        {c.kind === 'silence' ? 'láº·ng' : c.kind === 'stinger' ? 'âš¡ ' : ''}
        {c.reason?.slice(0, 40) ?? c.assetId}
        <span className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 top-0 w-1.5 cursor-ew-resize" onMouseDown={(e) => startDrag(e, { kind: 'cue', id: c.id, mode: 'end', x0: e.clientX, orig: c }, 'ew-resize')} />
      </div>
    )
  }

  const addCue = (kind: SoundCue['kind']): void =>

        await window.api.invoke('release:updateSoundCues', story.id, release.id, [...release.soundCues, cue])
        await refresh()
        selectCue(cue.id)
      } catch (err) {
        toast(errorMessage(err))
      }
    })()

  const tracks: { label: string; content: JSX.Element | null }[] = [

                  className={`absolute top-0.5 overflow-hidden rounded border px-1 text-left text-[10px] leading-[24px] text-slate-100 ${LEVEL_COLOR[s.motion.level]} ${active ? 'ring-2 ring-blood-400' : ''}`}

function CharacterImages({ c, storyId, dirty }: { c: Character; storyId: string | null; dirty: boolean }): JSX.Element {

      <div className="mt-0.5 max-w-[80px] truncate text-[10px] text-slate-500">{label}</div>

export function SoundLibraryPanel(): JSX.Element {

  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false)

    if (!r) return

    setDraft(r.assets)
    setDirty(false)

    void window.api.invoke('sound:info').then(load)
  }, [])

    setDirty(true)
  }

        dirty ? (
          <AsyncButton
            className="btn-primary text-xs"
            onClick={async () => {
              await window.api.invoke('library:update', { soundAssets: draft })
              setDirty(false)
              await reloadAll()
              toast('ÄÃ£ lÆ°u thÆ° viá»‡n Ã¢m thanh', 'info')
            }}
          >
            LÆ°u thay Ä‘á»•i
          </AsyncButton>
        ) : null
      }

        <span className="text-slate-500">ThÆ° má»¥c:</span>
        <span className="font-mono text-xs text-slate-300">{info?.dir}</span>
        <AsyncButton className="btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs" onClick={async () => load(await window.api.invoke('sound:chooseFolder'))}>
          <Icon name="folder" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Chá»n thÆ° má»¥c
        </AsyncButton>
        <AsyncButton className="btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs" disabled={dirty} onClick={async () => load(await window.api.invoke('sound:rescan'))}>
          QuÃ©t láº¡i
        </AsyncButton>

          className="btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs"

/** DÃ n Ã½ theo beat: LLM táº¡o (bÆ°á»›c 2) â†’ sá»­a â†’ â˜… duyá»‡t (bÆ°á»›c 3) */
export function OutlineEditor({ story }: { story: Story }): JSX.Element {
  const refresh = useApp((s) => s.refresh)
  const mock = useMockMode()
  const [beats, setBeats] = useState<Beat[]>(story.outline.beats)
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirty) setBeats(story.outline.beats)
  }, [story.outline.beats, dirty])

  const edit = (i: number, patch: Partial<Beat>): void => {
    setBeats((bs) => bs.map((b, k) => (k === i ? { ...b, ...patch } : b)))
    setDirty(true)
  }
  const move = (i: number, d: number): void => {

/**
 * Sá»• chi phÃ­: tá»•ng há»£p má»i khoáº£n chi (LLM, TTS, áº£nh, video) tá»« ledger cá»§a Story/Release theo thÃ¡ng.

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import type { CostReport } from '../../../shared/logic/business'
import { AsyncButton } from '../components/forms'
import { Icon } from '../components/Icon'
import { Empty, Panel } from '../components/ui'
import { formatUsd } from '../lib/format'
import { useApp } from '../store/appStore'

const KIND_LABEL: Record<string, string> = { llm: 'LLM (ká»‹ch báº£n)', tts: 'Giá»ng Ä‘á»c', image: 'áº¢nh', video: 'Video AI', stt: 'Nháº­n dáº¡ng giá»ng', render: 'Render' }

function monthOptions(n = 12): string[] {
  const d = new Date()
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => {
    const x = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth() - i, 1)
    return `${x.getFullYear()}-${String(x.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`
  })
}

function Bars({ items }: { items: { label: string; total: number; budget?: number }[] }): JSX.Element {
  const max = Math.max(0.0001, ...items.map((i) => Math.max(i.total, i.budget ?? 0)))
  return (
    <ul className="space-y-2">
      {items.map((i) => {
        const over = i.budget && i.budget > 0 && i.total > i.budget
        return (
          <li key={i.label} className="text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-2">
              <span className="truncate text-slate-300">{i.label}</span>
              <span className={over ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-slate-200'}>
                {formatUsd(i.total)}
                {i.budget ? <span className="text-slate-500"> / {formatUsd(i.budget)}</span> : null}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1 h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-ink-600">
              <div className={`h-full rounded-full ${over ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-blood-500'}`} style={{ width: `${(i.total / max) * 100}%` }} />
            </div>
          </li>
        )
      })}
    </ul>
  )
}

export function CostsScreen(): JSX.Element {
  const toast = useApp((s) => s.toast)
  const months = useMemo(() => monthOptions(), [])
  const [month, setMonth] = useState(months[0])
  const [report, setReport] = useState<CostReport | null>(null)
  const [budget, setBudget] = useState(0)

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const [r, s] = await Promise.all([window.api.invoke('costs:report', month), window.api.invoke('settings:get')])
      setReport(r)
      setBudget(s.monthlyBudgetUsd)
    } catch (e) {
      toast(String(e))
    }
  }, [month, toast])

  useEffect(() => {
    void load()
  }, [load])

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-5 p-6">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <select className="input" value={month} onChange={(e) => setMonth(e.target.value)}>
          {months.map((m) => (
            <option key={m} value={m}>
              ThÃ¡ng {m.slice(5)}/{m.slice(0, 4)}
            </option>
          ))}
          <option value="all">ToÃ n bá»™</option>
        </select>
        <button className="btn-ghost text-xs" onClick={() => void load()}>
          <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" /> LÃ m má»›i

        >
          <Icon name="folder" className="h-4 w-4" /> Xuáº¥t CSV
        </AsyncButton>
      </div>

        <Empty>Äang táº£iâ€¦</Empty>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div className="panel p-4">
              <div className="panel-title">Tá»•ng chi {month === 'all' ? '' : 'thÃ¡ng'}</div>
              <div className={`mt-1 text-3xl font-semibold ${month !== 'all' && budget > 0 && report.total > budget ? 'text-red-400' : 'text-slate-100'}`}>{formatUsd(report.total)}</div>
              {month !== 'all' ? <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">NgÃ¢n sÃ¡ch thÃ¡ng {budget > 0 ? formatUsd(budget) : 'khÃ´ng giá»›i háº¡n'}</div> : null}
            </div>

              {report.byProvider.length ? <Bars items={report.byProvider.map((s) => ({ label: s.provider, total: s.total }))} /> : <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">KhÃ´ng cÃ³.</p>}
            </Panel>
            <Panel title="Theo loáº¡i">
              {report.byKind.length ? <Bars items={report.byKind.map((s) => ({ label: KIND_LABEL[s.kind] ?? s.kind, total: s.total }))} /> : <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">KhÃ´ng cÃ³.</p>}
            </Panel>
          </div>

          <Panel title="Chi tiáº¿t">
            {report.rows.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">ChÆ°a cÃ³ khoáº£n chi nÃ o trong ká»³ nÃ y. Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ Mock khÃ´ng tá»‘n phÃ­.</p>
            ) : (

                    <tr>
                      <th className="py-1">Thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm</th>
                      <th>Story</th>
                      <th>Báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh</th>
                      <th>Loáº¡i</th>
                      <th>NhÃ  cung cáº¥p</th>
                      <th className="text-right">Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng</th>
                      <th className="text-right">Chi phÃ­</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {[...report.rows]
                      .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp))
                      .map((r, i) => (
                        <tr key={i} className="border-t border-ink-700 text-slate-300">
                          <td className="py-1 text-xs">{new Date(r.timestamp).toLocaleString('vi-VN')}</td>
                          <td className="max-w-[160px] truncate">{r.storyName}</td>
                          <td className="max-w-[200px] truncate text-xs">{r.releaseTitle ?? '(Story)'}</td>
                          <td>{KIND_LABEL[r.kind] ?? r.kind}</td>
                          <td className="text-xs">{r.provider}</td>
                          <td className="text-right">{r.units}</td>
                          <td className="text-right">{formatUsd(r.cost)}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </Panel>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

/** Sao chÃ©p vÃ o clipboard + toast */
function CopyBtn({ text, label = 'Sao chÃ©p' }: { text: string; label?: string }): JSX.Element {
  const toast = useApp((s) => s.toast)
  return (
    <button
      className="btn-ghost px-2 py-1 text-xs"
      disabled={!text}
      onClick={() =>
        void navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(
          () => toast('ÄÃ£ sao chÃ©p', 'info'),
          () => toast('KhÃ´ng sao chÃ©p Ä‘Æ°á»£c')
        )
      }
    >
      {label}
    </button>
  )
}

export function PublishScreen(): JSX.Element {

  const release = useCurrentRelease()
  const runAction = useApp((s) => s.runAction)

  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<PublicSettings | null>(null)

  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!story || !release) return

  }, [story, release])

  useEffect(() => {
    void load()
    void window.api.invoke('settings:get').then(setSettings)
  }, [load, release?.updatedAt])

  if (!story || !release) return <Empty>Chá»n Story vÃ  báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh á»Ÿ thanh trÃªn.</Empty>

        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-100">{release.title}</h2>
        {release.partCount > 1 ? <span className="chip">Pháº§n {release.partIndex}/{release.partCount}</span> : null}

// ============================================================================ GÃ³i xuáº¥t báº£n

function PackagePanel({ story, release }: { story: Story; release: Release }): JSX.Element {
  const refresh = useApp((s) => s.refresh)
  const mock = useMockMode()
  const pkg = release.publishing
  // Báº£n nhÃ¡p chá»‰nh tay â€“ Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ láº¡i khi gÃ³i trÃªn Ä‘Ä©a Ä‘á»•i (soáº¡n láº¡i / pipeline bÆ°á»›c 22)
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<PublishingPackage | null>(pkg)
  const [lang, setLang] = useState<'main' | 'vi'>('main')
  useEffect(() => setDraft(release.publishing), [release.publishing])

  const dirty = JSON.stringify(draft) !== JSON.stringify(pkg)
  const save = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (!draft) return
    await window.api.invoke('publish:update', story.id, release.id, draft)
    await refresh()
  }
  const generate = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await window.api.invoke('publish:generate', story.id, release.id)

// ============================================================================ Checklist

function ChecklistPanel({ story, release }: { story: Story; release: Release }): JSX.Element {
  const refresh = useApp((s) => s.refresh)
  const toast = useApp((s) => s.toast)
  const pkg = release.publishing
  const list = pkg?.checklist ?? []
  const doneCount = list.filter((c) => c.done).length

  const toggle = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
    if (!pkg) return
    await window.api.invoke('publish:update', story.id, release.id, {
      ...pkg,
      checklist: pkg.checklist.map((c) => (c.id === id ? { ...c, done: !c.done } : c))
    })
    await refresh()
  }

  return (
    <Panel
      title={`Checklist trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘Äƒng${list.length ? ` (${doneCount}/${list.length})` : ''}`}
      actions={
        pkg ? (
          <AsyncButton
            className="btn-ghost text-xs"
            onClick={async () => {
              await window.api.invoke('publish:refreshChecklist', story.id, release.id)
              await refresh()
            }}
          >
            <Icon name="refresh" className="h-4 w-4" /> Kiá»ƒm tra láº¡i
          </AsyncButton>
        ) : null
      }
    >
      {list.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Checklist cÃ³ sau khi soáº¡n gÃ³i xuáº¥t báº£n.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="space-y-1.5">
          {list.map((c) => (
            <li key={c.id} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm">
              <button
                disabled={c.auto}
                onClick={() => void toggle(c.id).catch((e: unknown) => toast(String(e)))}
                title={c.auto ? 'Tá»± kiá»ƒm tra â€“ báº¥m "Kiá»ƒm tra láº¡i" sau khi sá»­a' : 'ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u thá»§ cÃ´ng'}
                className={`mt-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border ${
                  c.done ? 'border-emerald-600 bg-emerald-700 text-white' : c.auto ? 'border-red-700 bg-red-950/50' : 'border-ink-400 hover:border-slate-300'
                }`}
              >
                {c.done ? <Icon name="check" className="h-3 w-3" /> : null}
              </button>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <span className={c.done ? 'text-slate-300' : 'text-slate-100'}>{c.labelVi}</span>
                {c.auto ? <span className="ml-1.5 text-[10px] text-slate-500">(tá»± Ä‘á»™ng)</span> : null}
                {c.detail ? <div className={`text-xs ${c.done ? 'text-slate-500' : 'text-amber-300'}`}>{c.detail}</div> : null}
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Panel>
  )
}

/** Script Editor: báº£n tiáº¿ng Anh 2 cá»™t Anh | Viá»‡t, báº£n tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t 1 cá»™t. Sá»­a â†’ LÆ°u â‡’ pipeline cháº¡y láº¡i tá»« bÆ°á»›c 10 (cÃ¢u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»•i dÃ¹ng cache). */
export function ScriptEditorScreen(): JSX.Element {
  const story = useCurrentStory()
  const release = useCurrentRelease()
  const releases = useReleases()
  const library = useApp((s) => s.library)
  const refresh = useApp((s) => s.refresh)
  const selectRelease = useApp((s) => s.selectRelease)
  const setScreen = useApp((s) => s.setScreen)
  const mock = useMockMode()
  const mediaUrl = useMediaUrl(story?.id)
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Script | null>(release?.script ?? null)
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    setDirty(false)
    setDraft(release?.script ?? null)
  }, [release?.id]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirty) setDraft(release?.script ?? null)
  }, [release?.updatedAt, dirty]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const speakers = useMemo(() => (story && library ? charactersForStory(story.id, library.characters) : []), [story, library])
  if (!story || !release || !draft || !library) return <Empty>Chá»n Story vÃ  báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh á»Ÿ thanh trÃªn.</Empty>

  const bilingual = release.language !== 'vi'
  const beatOrder = new Map(story.outline.beats.map((b) => [b.id, b.order]))
  const est = (l: ScriptLine): number => {
    if (!dirty && l.start != null && l.end != null) return l.end - l.start
    const v = resolveVoice(l.speakerId, release.language, library, story.channelPresetId)
    return estimateSpeechSeconds(l.text, release.language, l.delivery.pace * (v?.speed ?? 1), providersConfig.tts.mock)
  }
  const lineTotal = (l: ScriptLine): number => est(l) + (l.delivery.pauseBeforeMs + l.delivery.pauseAfterMs) / 1000
  const total = draft.lines.reduce((s, l) => s + lineTotal(l), 0)
  const st = release.pipelineState
  const waiting9 = st?.steps[9]?.status === 'waitingApproval'
  const running = !!st?.running
  const series = release.seriesGroupId ? releases.filter((r) => r.seriesGroupId === release.seriesGroupId) : []
  const dialogCount = draft.lines.filter((l) => speakers.find((s) => s.id === l.speakerId)?.scope !== 'channel').length

  const update = (fn: (lines: ScriptLine[]) => ScriptLine[]): void => {
    setDraft({ ...draft, lines: fn(draft.lines) })
    setDirty(true)
  }
  const save = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await window.api.invoke('release:updateScript', story.id, release.id, draft)
    setDirty(false)
    await refresh()
  }

  if (draft.lines.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl p-10">
        <div className="panel p-6">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-100">Báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh chÆ°a cÃ³ ká»‹ch báº£n</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
            Ká»‹ch báº£n Ä‘Æ°á»£c LLM chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ tá»« dÃ n Ã½ á»Ÿ <b>bÆ°á»›c 8</b> cá»§a pipeline (theo ngÃ´n ngá»¯, ná»n táº£ng, Ä‘á»™ dÃ i
            {release.seriesGroupId ? ', vÃ  tá»± chia pháº§n' : ''}). Pipeline sáº½ dá»«ng á»Ÿ â˜…7 Duyá»‡t storyboard, rá»“i â˜…9 Duyá»‡t ká»‹ch báº£n â€“ lÃºc Ä‘Ã³ quay láº¡i Ä‘Ã¢y Ä‘á»ƒ sá»­a.
          </p>
          {story.outline.approval.status !== 'approved' ? (
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-amber-300">DÃ n Ã½ chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c duyá»‡t â€“ hÃ£y duyá»‡t á»Ÿ mÃ n Story trÆ°á»›c.</p>
          ) : null}
          <AsyncButton
            className="btn-primary mt-4"
            disabled={running || story.outline.approval.status !== 'approved'}
            onClick={async () => {

            }}
          >

          </AsyncButton>

  }

                        update((ls) => {
                          const arr = [...ls]
                          const j = idx + d
                          if (j < 0 || j >= arr.length) return arr
                          ;[arr[idx], arr[j]] = [arr[j], arr[idx]]
                          // Äá»•i chá»— sang section khÃ¡c â‡’ cÃ¢u nháº­n section cá»§a vá»‹ trÃ­ má»›i
                          arr[j] = { ...arr[j], sectionId: ls[j].sectionId }
                          arr[idx] = { ...arr[idx], sectionId: ls[idx].sectionId }
                          return arr
                        })
                      }
                      onInsert={() =>
                        update((ls) => {
                          const arr = [...ls]
                          arr.splice(idx + 1, 0, {

                            start: null,
                            end: null,
                            glossaryTermIds: [],
                            approval: { status: 'pending' }
                          })
                          return arr
                        })
                      }
                      onDelete={() => update((ls) => ls.filter((x) => x.id !== l.id))}
                      onRewrite={async (instruction) => {
                        await window.api.invoke('release:rewriteLine', story.id, release.id, l.id, instruction)
                        await refresh()
                      }}
                    />
                  )

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { charactersForStory } from '../../../shared/logic/voices'
import type { AspectRatio, SceneBankItem } from '../../../shared/types'
import { AsyncButton, Avatar } from '../components/forms'
import { SceneEditModal } from '../components/SceneEditModal'
import { Empty } from '../components/ui'
import { imageEstimateLabel, llmEstimateLabel } from '../lib/estimate'
import { errorMessage, SHOT_LABEL } from '../lib/format'
import { useMediaUrl } from '../lib/preview'
import { useApp, useCurrentRelease, useCurrentStory, useMockMode } from '../store/appStore'

/**
 * Storyboard: duyá»‡t ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh. PhÃ­m táº¯t (khi Ä‘Ã£ chá»n má»™t tháº»):
 *   A duyá»‡t Â· R táº¡o láº¡i áº£nh khung Ä‘ang xem Â· E sá»­a prompt/cast Â· V táº¡o báº£n 9:16 váº½ riÃªng Â· â†/â†’ chuyá»ƒn tháº»
 */
export function StoryboardScreen(): JSX.Element {
  const story = useCurrentStory()
  const release = useCurrentRelease()
  const library = useApp((s) => s.library)
  const queue = useApp((s) => s.queue)
  const toast = useApp((s) => s.toast)
  const refresh = useApp((s) => s.refresh)
  const setScreen = useApp((s) => s.setScreen)
  const mock = useMockMode()
  const mediaUrl = useMediaUrl(story?.id)
  const [view, setView] = useState<AspectRatio>('16:9')
  const [filterChar, setFilterChar] = useState('')
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<SceneBankItem | null>(null)
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<{ id: string; x: number; y: number } | null>(null)
  const dragRef = useRef(drag)
  dragRef.current = drag

  const chars = useMemo(() => (story && library ? charactersForStory(story.id, library.characters) : []), [story, library])
  const scenes = useMemo(
    () => (story ? (filterChar ? story.sceneBank.filter((s) => s.cast.some((m) => m.characterId === filterChar)) : story.sceneBank) : []),
    [story, filterChar]
  )
  const providerId = mock ? 'mock' : 'gemini'

  const act = async (fn: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<void> => {
    try {
      await fn()
    } catch (err) {
      toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
  }
  const update = (sceneId: string, patch: Partial<SceneBankItem>): Promise<void> =>
    act(async () => {
      await window.api.invoke('scene:update', story!.id, sceneId, patch)
      await refresh()
    })
  const regen = (sceneId: string, aspect: AspectRatio): Promise<void> =>
    act(async () => {
      const s = story!.sceneBank.find((x) => x.id === sceneId)
      await window.api.invoke('images:generateScenes', story!.id, [sceneId], aspect, { regenerate: !!s?.media[aspect] && s.media[aspect]?.provider !== 'focus-crop' })
      toast(`ÄÃ£ Ä‘Æ°a cáº£nh vÃ o hÃ ng chá» (${aspect})`, 'info')
    })

  // PhÃ­m táº¯t
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent): void => {
      const t = e.target as HTMLElement
      if (editing || !selectedId || !story || ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(t.tagName) || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) return
      const idx = scenes.findIndex((s) => s.id === selectedId)
      const key = e.key.toLowerCase()
      if (key === 'a') void update(selectedId, { approval: { status: 'approved' } })
      else if (key === 'r') void regen(selectedId, view)
      else if (key === 'e') setEditing(story.sceneBank.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) ?? null)
      else if (key === 'v') void regen(selectedId, '9:16')
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' && idx < scenes.length - 1) setSelectedId(scenes[idx + 1].id)
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' && idx > 0) setSelectedId(scenes[idx - 1].id)
      else return
      e.preventDefault()
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  })

  if (!story || !library) return <Empty>Chá»n Story á»Ÿ thanh trÃªn.</Empty>

  const approved = story.sceneBank.filter((s) => s.approval.status === 'approved').length
  const waiting7 = release?.pipelineState?.steps[7]?.status === 'waitingApproval'
  const busyIds = new Set(
    (queue?.jobs ?? []).filter((j) => (j.status === 'queued' || j.status === 'running') && j.storyId === story.id).map((j) => (j.payload as { sceneId?: string }).sceneId)
  )
  const issues = story.sceneBank.filter((s) => s.consistency && !s.consistency.ok).length

  const pointFromEvent = (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>): { x: number; y: number } => {
    const r = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect()
    return { x: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width)), y: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height)) }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-ink-700 bg-ink-850 px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        <div className="flex overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-ink-500">
          {(['16:9', '9:16'] as const).map((a) => (
            <button key={a} onClick={() => setView(a)} className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs ${view === a ? 'bg-blood-600 text-white' : 'bg-ink-700 text-slate-300'}`}>
              {a}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <select className="input py-1 text-xs" value={filterChar} onChange={(e) => setFilterChar(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="">Má»i nhÃ¢n váº­t</option>
          {chars.map((c) => (
            <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
              {c.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
          {approved}/{story.sceneBank.length} cáº£nh Ä‘Ã£ duyá»‡t{issues ? ` Â· âš  ${issues} cáº£nh lá»‡ch nhÃ¢n váº­t` : ''}
        </span>
        <div className="flex-1" />
        <span className="hidden text-[11px] text-slate-500 xl:inline">Chá»n tháº» rá»“i báº¥m: A duyá»‡t Â· R táº¡o láº¡i Â· E sá»­a Â· V báº£n dá»c Â· â†/â†’</span>
        <AsyncButton
          className="btn-ghost text-xs"
          onClick={async () => {
            await window.api.invoke('scene:checkConsistency', story.id, story.sceneBank.filter((s) => s.media['16:9'] && s.cast.length).map((s) => s.id))
            await refresh()
          }}
        >
          Kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»“ng nháº¥t Â· {llmEstimateLabel(mock, 3000 * story.sceneBank.length, 300 * story.sceneBank.length)}
        </AsyncButton>
        <AsyncButton
          className="btn-secondary text-xs"
          onClick={async () => {
            for (const s of story.sceneBank) if (s.approval.status !== 'approved') await window.api.invoke('scene:update', story.id, s.id, { approval: { status: 'approved' } })
            await refresh()
          }}
        >
          Duyá»‡t táº¥t cáº£
        </AsyncButton>

  const release = useCurrentRelease()
  const library = useApp((s) => s.library)

/**
 * Store Zustand cá»§a renderer. Dá»¯ liá»‡u gá»‘c luÃ´n á»Ÿ main (Ä‘Ä©a); renderer chá»‰ giá»¯ báº£n sao Ä‘á»ƒ hiá»ƒn thá»‹
 * vÃ  táº£i láº¡i khi main bÃ¡o 'data:changed'. Tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™ pipeline cáº­p nháº­t trá»±c tiáº¿p qua 'pipeline:progress'.
 */
import { create } from 'zustand'
import type { AppInfo, LogEntry, PipelineProgressEvent, QueueSnapshot, StoryBundle, StorySummary } from '../../../shared/ipc'
import type { Library, Release, RunMode, Story } from '../../../shared/types'
import { errorMessage } from '../lib/format'

export type ScreenId =
  | 'home'
  | 'story'
  | 'folklore'
  | 'channel'
  | 'script'
  | 'voice'
  | 'film'
  | 'storyboard'
  | 'autoedit'
  | 'publish'
  | 'costs'
  | 'settings'

interface Toast {
  id: number
  kind: 'error' | 'info'
  message: string
}

interface AppState {
  ready: boolean
  fatalError: string | null
  appInfo: AppInfo | null
  library: Library | null
  stories: StorySummary[]
  screen: ScreenId
  currentStoryId: string | null
  currentReleaseId: string | null
  bundle: StoryBundle | null
  logs: LogEntry[]
  logOpen: boolean
  selectedSceneId: string | null
  /** Sound cue Ä‘ang chá»n trÃªn timeline (báº£ng pháº£i hiá»‡n thuá»™c tÃ­nh cue thay vÃ¬ cáº£nh) */
  selectedCueId: string | null
  toasts: Toast[]
  /** Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i job (Film Studio) â€“ cáº­p nháº­t qua sá»± kiá»‡n queue:changed */
  queue: QueueSnapshot | null

  init: () => Promise<void>
  setScreen: (s: ScreenId) => void
  selectStory: (id: string | null) => Promise<void>
  selectRelease: (id: string | null) => void
  refresh: () => Promise<void>
  /** Táº£i láº¡i AppInfo + thÆ° viá»‡n + danh sÃ¡ch Story (sau khi Ä‘á»•i cÃ i Ä‘áº·t / táº¡o Story) */
  reloadAll: () => Promise<void>
  selectScene: (id: string | null) => void
  selectCue: (id: string | null) => void
  toggleLog: (open?: boolean) => void
  toast: (message: string, kind?: Toast['kind']) => void
  dismissToast: (id: number) => void
  runAction: (fn: () => Promise<unknown>) => Promise<void>
  startPipeline: (mode: RunMode) => Promise<void>
  continuePipeline: () => Promise<void>
  cancelPipeline: () => Promise<void>
  resetPipelineFrom: (stepNo: number) => Promise<void>
}

let initialized = false
let toastId = 1

export const useApp = create<AppState>((set, get) => ({
  ready: false,
  fatalError: null,
  appInfo: null,
  library: null,
  stories: [],
  screen: 'home',
  currentStoryId: null,
  currentReleaseId: null,
  bundle: null,
  logs: [],
  logOpen: false,
  selectedSceneId: null,
  selectedCueId: null,
  toasts: [],
  queue: null,

  init: async () => {
    if (initialized) return
    initialized = true
    try {
      const api = window.api
      api.on('log:entry', (e) => set((s) => ({ logs: [...s.logs.slice(-499), e] })))
      api.on('pipeline:progress', (e: PipelineProgressEvent) => {
        const b = get().bundle
        if (!b || b.story.id !== e.storyId) return
        set({
          bundle: {
            ...b,
            releases: b.releases.map((r) => (r.id === e.releaseId ? { ...r, pipelineState: e.state } : r))
          }
        })
      })
      api.on('queue:changed', (queue) => set({ queue }))
      void api.invoke('queue:get').then((queue) => set({ queue }))
      api.on('data:changed', (e) => {
        void api.invoke('stories:list').then((stories) => set({ stories }))
        if (e.storyId === get().currentStoryId) void get().refresh()
      })

      const [appInfo, library, stories, logs] = await Promise.all([
        api.invoke('app:getInfo'),
        api.invoke('library:get'),
        api.invoke('stories:list'),
        api.invoke('log:history')
      ])
      set({ appInfo, library, stories, logs, ready: true })
      // Tá»± chá»n Story Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn + Release Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn Ä‘á»ƒ vÃ o viá»‡c ngay
      if (stories.length > 0) await get().selectStory(stories[0].id)
    } catch (err) {
      set({ fatalError: errorMessage(err), ready: true })
    }
  },

  setScreen: (screen) => set({ screen }),

  selectStory: async (id) => {
    if (!id) {
      set({ currentStoryId: null, currentReleaseId: null, bundle: null })
      return
    }
    const bundle = await window.api.invoke('story:get', id)
    const keep = bundle.releases.some((r) => r.id === get().currentReleaseId)
    set({
      currentStoryId: id,
      bundle,
      currentReleaseId: keep ? get().currentReleaseId : (bundle.releases[0]?.id ?? null),
      selectedSceneId: null
    })
  },

  selectRelease: (id) => set({ currentReleaseId: id, selectedSceneId: null }),

  refresh: async () => {
    const id = get().currentStoryId
    if (!id) return
    try {
      const [bundle, library] = await Promise.all([window.api.invoke('story:get', id), window.api.invoke('library:get')])
      set({ bundle, library })
    } catch (err) {
      get().toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
  },

  reloadAll: async () => {
    const [appInfo, library, stories] = await Promise.all([
      window.api.invoke('app:getInfo'),
      window.api.invoke('library:get'),
      window.api.invoke('stories:list')
    ])
    set({ appInfo, library, stories })
    await get().refresh()
  },

  selectScene: (id) => set({ selectedSceneId: id, selectedCueId: null }),
  selectCue: (id) => set({ selectedCueId: id, selectedSceneId: null }),
  toggleLog: (open) => set((s) => ({ logOpen: open ?? !s.logOpen })),

  toast: (message, kind = 'error') => {
    const id = toastId++
    set((s) => ({ toasts: [...s.toasts, { id, kind, message }] }))
    setTimeout(() => get().dismissToast(id), 8000)
  },
  dismissToast: (id) => set((s) => ({ toasts: s.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== id) })),

  runAction: async (fn) => {
    try {
      await fn()
    } catch (err) {
      get().toast(errorMessage(err))
    }
  },

  startPipeline: async (mode) => {
    const { currentStoryId: s, currentReleaseId: r, runAction } = get()
    if (s && r) await runAction(() => window.api.invoke('pipeline:start', s, r, mode))
  },
  continuePipeline: async () => {
    const { currentStoryId: s, currentReleaseId: r, runAction } = get()
    if (s && r) await runAction(() => window.api.invoke('pipeline:continue', s, r))
  },
  cancelPipeline: async () => {
    const { currentStoryId: s, currentReleaseId: r, runAction } = get()
    if (s && r) await runAction(() => window.api.invoke('pipeline:cancel', s, r))
  },
  resetPipelineFrom: async (stepNo) => {
    const { currentStoryId: s, currentReleaseId: r, runAction } = get()
    if (s && r) await runAction(() => window.api.invoke('pipeline:resetFrom', s, r, stepNo))
  }
}))

// Cho kiá»ƒm thá»­ UI tá»± Ä‘á»™ng (main gá»i qua executeJavaScript); store khÃ´ng chá»©a bÃ­ máº­t nÃ o
;(window as unknown as { __fhsStore: typeof useApp }).__fhsStore = useApp

const NO_RELEASES: Release[] = []

export function useReleases(): Release[] {
  return useApp((s) => s.bundle?.releases ?? NO_RELEASES)
}

export function useMockMode(): boolean {
  return useApp((s) => s.appInfo?.mockMode ?? true)
}

export function useCurrentStory(): Story | null {
  return useApp((s) => s.bundle?.story ?? null)
}

export function useCurrentRelease(): Release | null {
  return useApp((s) => s.bundle?.releases.find((r) => r.id === s.currentReleaseId) ?? null)
}
