/**
 * Sá»• hiá»‡u quáº£ & sá»• chi phÃ­ (hÃ m thuáº§n).
 */
import type { CostEntry, PerformanceNote, Platform } from '../types'

export interface PerformanceThresholds {
  minViews: number
  minAvgWatchPercent: number
}

/** Ghi chÃº tá»‘t nháº¥t cá»§a má»™t Release (lÆ°á»£t xem cao nháº¥t) */
export function bestNote(notes: PerformanceNote[]): PerformanceNote | null {
  return notes.reduce<PerformanceNote | null>((b, n) => (!b || n.views > b.views ? n : b), null)
}

export interface AdaptSuggestion {
  qualifies: boolean
  reason: string
  best: PerformanceNote | null
}

/**
 * Story cÃ³ báº£n TikTok Ä‘áº¡t ngÆ°á»¡ng (lÆ°á»£t xem + % xem trung bÃ¬nh) vÃ  CHÆ¯A cÃ³ báº£n YouTube tiáº¿ng Anh
 * â‡’ gá»£i Ã½ "Chuyá»ƒn thá»ƒ sang YouTube tiáº¿ng Anh".
 */
export function adaptSuggestion(
  notes: PerformanceNote[],
  releases: { id: string; platform: Platform; language: string }[],
  t: PerformanceThresholds
): AdaptSuggestion {
  const tiktokIds = new Set(releases.filter((r) => r.platform === 'tiktok').map((r) => r.id))
  const hasYtEn = releases.some((r) => r.platform === 'youtube' && r.language === 'en')
  const best = bestNote(notes.filter((n) => tiktokIds.has(n.releaseId)))
  if (!best) return { qualifies: false, reason: 'ChÆ°a cÃ³ sá»‘ liá»‡u báº£n TikTok', best: null }
  const ok = best.views >= t.minViews && best.avgWatchPercent >= t.minAvgWatchPercent
  if (!ok) {
    return { qualifies: false, reason: `ChÆ°a Ä‘áº¡t ngÆ°á»¡ng (${best.views.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} lÆ°á»£t xem, ${best.avgWatchPercent}% xem trung bÃ¬nh / cáº§n ${t.minViews.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} vÃ  ${t.minAvgWatchPercent}%)`, best }
  }
  if (hasYtEn) return { qualifies: false, reason: 'ÄÃ£ cÃ³ báº£n YouTube tiáº¿ng Anh', best }
  return { qualifies: true, reason: `Báº£n TikTok Ä‘áº¡t ${best.views.toLocaleString('vi-VN')} lÆ°á»£t xem, ${best.avgWatchPercent}% xem trung bÃ¬nh`, best }
}

/** Tá»· lá»‡ tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c (like + bÃ¬nh luáº­n + chia sáº») / lÆ°á»£t xem, % */
export function engagementRate(n: PerformanceNote): number {
  return n.views > 0 ? Math.round(((n.likes + n.comments + n.shares) / n.views) * 10000) / 100 : 0
}

export interface CostRow extends CostEntry {
  storyId: string
  storyName: string
  releaseId: string | null
  releaseTitle: string | null
}

export interface CostReport {
  month: string
  total: number
  byStory: { storyId: string; storyName: string; total: number; budget: number }[]
  byProvider: { provider: string; total: number }[]
  byKind: { kind: CostEntry['kind']; total: number }[]
  rows: CostRow[]
}

const round = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 10000) / 10000

/** Tá»•ng há»£p chi phÃ­ theo thÃ¡ng ("YYYY-MM") hoáº·c "all" */
export function buildCostReport(rows: CostRow[], month: string, budgets: Record<string, number>): CostReport {
  const sel = month === 'all' ? rows : rows.filter((r) => r.timestamp.startsWith(month))
  const sum = <K extends string>(key: (r: CostRow) => K): Map<K, number> => {
    const m = new Map<K, number>()
    for (const r of sel) m.set(key(r), (m.get(key(r)) ?? 0) + r.cost)
    return m
  }
  const names = new Map(rows.map((r) => [r.storyId, r.storyName]))
  return {
    month,
    total: round(sel.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cost, 0)),
    byStory: [...sum((r) => r.storyId)].map(([storyId, total]) => ({ storyId, storyName: names.get(storyId) ?? storyId, total: round(total), budget: budgets[storyId] ?? 0 })).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total),
    byProvider: [...sum((r) => r.provider)].map(([provider, total]) => ({ provider, total: round(total) })).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total),
    byKind: [...sum((r) => r.kind)].map(([kind, total]) => ({ kind, total: round(total) })).sort((a, b) => b.total - a.total),
    rows: [...sel].sort((a, b) => b.timestamp.localeCompare(a.timestamp))
  }
}

/** CSV (UTF-8 cÃ³ BOM Ä‘á»ƒ Excel Ä‘á»c Ä‘Ãºng tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t) */
export function costCsv(rows: CostRow[]): string {
  const esc = (v: string | number | null): string => {
    const s = v == null ? '' : String(v)
    return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s
  }
  const head = ['Thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm', 'Story', 'Báº£n phÃ¡t hÃ nh', 'Provider', 'Loáº¡i', 'Sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng', 'Chi phÃ­ (USD)']
  const lines = rows.map((r) => [r.timestamp, r.storyName, r.releaseTitle, r.provider, r.kind, r.units, r.cost.toFixed(4)].map(esc).join(','))
  return 'ï»¿' + [head.join(','), ...lines].join('\r\n')
}

import type { CostLedger } from '../types'

/** ~4 kÃ½ tá»± / token cho tiáº¿ng Anh; tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t cÃ³ dáº¥u tá»‘n hÆ¡n â‡’ dÃ¹ng 3 cho an toÃ n */
export function estimateTokens(text: string): number {
  return Math.ceil(text.length / 3)
}

export function estimateLlmCost(
  inputChars: number,
  expectedOutputChars: number,
  price: { pricePer1kInputTokens: number; pricePer1kOutputTokens: number }
): number {
  const inTok = Math.ceil(inputChars / 3)
  const outTok = Math.ceil(expectedOutputChars / 3)
  return (inTok / 1000) * price.pricePer1kInputTokens + (outTok / 1000) * price.pricePer1kOutputTokens
}

export function estimateTtsCost(characters: number, pricePer1kChars: number): number {
  return (characters / 1000) * pricePer1kChars
}

/** Tá»•ng Ä‘Ã£ chi cá»§a Story = chi cáº¥p Story + chi cá»§a má»i Release */
export function storySpent(storyCosts: CostLedger | undefined, releaseCosts: CostLedger[]): number {
  return (storyCosts?.actual ?? 0) + releaseCosts.reduce((s, c) => s + c.actual, 0)
}

export interface BudgetCheck {
  ok: boolean
  spent: number
  estimate: number
  limit: number
}

export function checkBudget(spent: number, estimate: number, limit: number): BudgetCheck {
  return { ok: limit <= 0 || spent + estimate <= limit + 1e-9, spent, estimate, limit }
}

export function formatUsdShort(n: number): string {
  if (n === 0) return '$0'
  if (n < 0.01) return '<$0.01'
  return `$${n.toFixed(2)}`
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\shared\logic\focusCrop.ts ===
/**
 * Cáº¯t khung theo focusPoint â€“ dÃ¹ng dá»±ng báº£n 9:16 tá»« áº£nh 16:9:
 *   tiá»n cáº£nh = vÃ¹ng cáº¯t (tá»· lá»‡ cropAspect, vd 4:5) quanh focusPoint, phÃ³ng vá»«a chiá»u ngang khung dá»c
 *   háº­u cáº£nh = vÃ¹ng cáº¯t 9:16 quanh focusPoint, phÃ³ng to + lÃ m má» + tá»‘i Ä‘i
 */
import type { FocusPoint } from '../types'

export interface CropRect {
  left: number
  top: number
  width: number
  height: number
}

const clamp = (n: number, lo: number, hi: number): number => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, n))

/** VÃ¹ng cáº¯t lá»›n nháº¥t cÃ³ tá»· lá»‡ targetAspect (rá»™ng/cao), tÃ¢m gáº§n focusPoint nháº¥t, khÃ´ng vÆ°á»£t áº£nh. */
export function computeFocusCrop(
  srcW: number,
  srcH: number,
  focus: FocusPoint,
  targetAspect: number
): CropRect {
  if (srcW <= 0 || srcH <= 0 || targetAspect <= 0) throw new Error('KÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c áº£nh/tá»· lá»‡ khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡')
  let width: number
  let height: number
  if (srcW / srcH > targetAspect) {
    height = srcH
    width = Math.round(srcH * targetAspect)
  } else {
    width = srcW
    height = Math.round(srcW / targetAspect)
  }
  width = Math.min(width, srcW)
  height = Math.min(height, srcH)
  const fx = clamp(focus.x, 0, 1)
  const fy = clamp(focus.y, 0, 1)
  const left = clamp(Math.round(fx * srcW - width / 2), 0, srcW - width)
  const top = clamp(Math.round(fy * srcH - height / 2), 0, srcH - height)
  return { left, top, width, height }
}

export interface VerticalLayout {
  foregroundCrop: CropRect
  backgroundCrop: CropRect
  fgWidth: number
  fgHeight: number
  fgLeft: number
  fgTop: number
}

export function computeVerticalLayout(
  srcW: number,
  srcH: number,
  focus: FocusPoint,
  opts: { outW: number; outH: number; cropAspect: number }
): VerticalLayout {
  const foregroundCrop = computeFocusCrop(srcW, srcH, focus, opts.cropAspect)
  const backgroundCrop = computeFocusCrop(srcW, srcH, focus, opts.outW / opts.outH)
  const fgWidth = opts.outW
  const fgHeight = Math.min(opts.outH, Math.round((opts.outW * foregroundCrop.height) / foregroundCrop.width))
  return {
    foregroundCrop,
    backgroundCrop,
    fgWidth,
    fgHeight,
    fgLeft: 0,
    fgTop: Math.round((opts.outH - fgHeight) / 2)
  }
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\shared\logic\hero.ts ===
/**
 * Chá»n cáº£nh hero (Má»©c 3 â€“ video AI) theo heroRatio cá»§a Release.
 * Æ¯u tiÃªn: cáº£nh má»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u (hook) vÃ  cáº£nh thuá»™c beat cÃ³ cÆ°á»ng Ä‘á»™ cáº£m xÃºc cao nháº¥t (cao trÃ o).
 */
export interface HeroCandidate {
  id: string
  beatId: string
}

export function heroCount(sceneCount: number, heroRatio: number): number {
  if (sceneCount <= 0 || heroRatio <= 0) return 0
  return Math.min(sceneCount, Math.max(1, Math.round(sceneCount * heroRatio)))
}

export function selectHeroScenes(
  scenes: HeroCandidate[],
  beatIntensity: (beatId: string) => number,
  heroRatio: number
): string[] {
  const count = heroCount(scenes.length, heroRatio)
  if (count === 0) return []
  const scored = scenes.map((s, index) => ({
    id: s.id,
    index,
    score: beatIntensity(s.beatId) + (index === 0 ? 0.6 : 0)
  }))
  scored.sort((a, b) => (b.score !== a.score ? b.score - a.score : a.index - b.index))
  const chosen = new Set(scored.slice(0, count).map((s) => s.id))
  // Tráº£ vá» theo thá»© tá»± trÃªn timeline
  return scenes.filter((s) => chosen.has(s.id)).map((s) => s.id)
}

/**

 * Gemini khÃ´ng cÃ³ tham sá»‘ negative prompt â‡’ ná»‘i "Avoid: â€¦" vÃ o cuá»‘i prompt.
 */
import type { AspectRatio, ChannelPreset, Character, FolkloreEntry, ImageAspect, SceneBankItem } from '../types'

export const MAX_CLEAR_FACES = 3

export type SheetView = 'front' | 'threeQuarter' | 'profile' | 'fullBody'
export const SHEET_VIEWS: SheetView[] = ['front', 'threeQuarter', 'profile', 'fullBody']
export const SHEET_VIEW_LABEL: Record<SheetView, string> = {
  front: 'chÃ­nh diá»‡n',
  threeQuarter: '3/4',
  profile: 'nghiÃªng',
  fullBody: 'toÃ n thÃ¢n'
}
export const SHEET_VIEW_ASPECT: Record<SheetView, ImageAspect> = { front: '1:1', threeQuarter: '1:1', profile: '1:1', fullBody: '9:16' }

export interface PromptContext {
  channel: ChannelPreset
  folklore: FolkloreEntry[]
  characters: Character[]
}

const SHOT_TEXT: Record<SceneBankItem['shotType'], string> = {
  wide: 'wide establishing shot',
  medium: 'medium shot',
  closeUp: 'close-up shot',
  overShoulder: 'over-the-shoulder shot',
  topDown: 'top-down overhead shot',
  insert: 'insert detail shot'
}

const SAFETY =
  'Horror through atmosphere, darkness, fog, shadows and suggestion only. No gore, no blood, no wounds, no corpses. No text, letters, captions, logos or watermarks anywhere in the image.'

function genderText(c: Character): string {
  return c.gender === 'female' ? 'woman' : c.gender === 'male' ? 'man' : 'person'
}

function describePerson(c: Character, stateId: string | null, expression: string, action: string, position: string): string {
  const st = c.states.find((s) => s.id === stateId)
  return [
    `${c.name} (${position}${c.age ? `, ${c.age}-year-old Vietnamese ${genderText(c)}` : ''}): ${c.appearance}`,
    c.defaultOutfit ? `wearing ${c.defaultOutfit}` : '',
    st ? `current state: ${st.name} â€“ ${st.description}` : '',
    expression ? `expression: ${expression}` : '',
    action ? `action: ${action}` : '',
    c.locked ? `keep face, hair and outfit identical to the reference images of ${c.name}` : ''
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('; ')
}

function describeEntity(c: Character, ctx: PromptContext, stateId: string | null, action: string): string {
  const st = c.states.find((s) => s.id === stateId)
  const design = ctx.folklore.map((f) => f.visualDesign).filter(Boolean).join(' ')
  return [
    `The supernatural entity "${c.name}" must NEVER be fully shown â€“ only suggested through darkness, shadows, reflections and small details`,
    design ? `folklore design: ${design}` : '',
    c.appearance ? `story-specific design: ${c.appearance}` : '',
    st ? `visible as: ${st.name} â€“ ${st.description}` : '',
    action ? `action: ${action}` : ''
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('; ')
}

/** Cast há»£p lá»‡ Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘Æ°a vÃ o prompt: tá»‘i Ä‘a MAX_CLEAR_FACES ngÆ°á»i rÃµ máº·t (khÃ´ng tÃ­nh háº­u cáº£nh vÃ  thá»±c thá»ƒ) */
export function castForPrompt(scene: SceneBankItem, characters: Character[]): SceneBankItem['cast'] {
  const known = scene.cast.filter((m) => characters.some((c) => c.id === m.characterId))
  let faces = 0
  return known.filter((m) => {
    const c = characters.find((x) => x.id === m.characterId)
    if (!c || c.role === 'entity' || m.position === 'background') return true
    faces++
    return faces <= MAX_CLEAR_FACES
  })
}

export function buildScenePrompt(scene: SceneBankItem, ctx: PromptContext, aspect: AspectRatio): { prompt: string; negativePrompt: string } {
  const ch = ctx.channel
  const people = castForPrompt(scene, ctx.characters)
    .map((m) => {
      const c = ctx.characters.find((x) => x.id === m.characterId)
      if (!c) return ''
      return c.role === 'entity' ? describeEntity(c, ctx, m.stateId, m.action) : describePerson(c, m.stateId, m.expression, m.action, m.position)
    })
    .filter(Boolean)
  const parts = [
    `Art style: ${ch.artStyle}.`,
    `Setting must follow Vietnamese visual culture: ${ch.culturalVisualBible.join('; ')}.`,
    `Scene: ${scene.imagePrompt}.`,
    `Camera: ${SHOT_TEXT[scene.shotType]}, ${scene.cameraAngle}. Frame ${aspect}${aspect === '9:16' ? ' vertical composition, main subject centered with headroom' : ' cinematic widescreen composition'}.`,
    ...people.map((p) => `${p}.`),
    `Colour palette: ${ch.palette.join(', ')}.`,
    SAFETY,
    `Avoid: ${ch.negativePrompt}.`
  ]
  return { prompt: parts.join('\n'), negativePrompt: ch.negativePrompt }
}

export function buildCharacterViewPrompt(c: Character, view: SheetView, ctx: PromptContext): string {
  const viewText: Record<SheetView, string> = {
    front: 'front view portrait, head and shoulders, looking at camera',
    threeQuarter: 'three-quarter view portrait, head and shoulders',
    profile: 'side profile portrait, head and shoulders',
    fullBody: 'full body standing pose, head to toe visible'
  }
  const who =
    c.role === 'entity'
      ? `${describeEntity(c, ctx, null, '')}. Concept reference image, partially hidden in darkness`
      : `Character reference sheet image. ${describePerson(c, null, 'neutral', '', 'center')}`
  return [
    `Art style: ${ctx.channel.artStyle}.`,
    who + '.',
    `${viewText[view]}, plain dark neutral background, soft even lighting, consistent identity for later scenes.`,
    SAFETY,
    `Avoid: ${ctx.channel.negativePrompt}.`
  ].join('\n')
}

export function buildExpressionPrompt(c: Character, expression: string, ctx: PromptContext): string {
  return [
    `Art style: ${ctx.channel.artStyle}.`,
    `Same character as the reference image: ${c.name}, ${c.appearance}, wearing ${c.defaultOutfit}.`,
    `Head and shoulders portrait showing the expression: ${expression}. Plain dark neutral background.`,
    SAFETY,
    `Avoid: ${ctx.channel.negativePrompt}.`
  ].join('\n')
}

export function buildStatePrompt(c: Character, stateId: string, ctx: PromptContext): string {
  const st = c.states.find((s) => s.id === stateId)
  return [
    `Art style: ${ctx.channel.artStyle}.`,
    c.role === 'entity'
      ? describeEntity(c, ctx, stateId, '') + '.'
      : `Same character as the reference image: ${c.name}, ${c.appearance}, wearing ${c.defaultOutfit}. Appearance state: ${st?.name ?? ''} â€“ ${st?.description ?? ''}.`,
    'Three-quarter body view, plain dark neutral background.',
    SAFETY,
    `Avoid: ${ctx.channel.negativePrompt}.`
  ].join('\n')
}

/** Prompt chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng cho video AI (image-to-video) â€“ chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng tinh táº¿, giá»¯ nguyÃªn bá»‘ cá»¥c vÃ  nhÃ¢n váº­t */
export function buildVideoPrompt(scene: SceneBankItem, ctx: PromptContext, seconds: number): string {
  const entity = scene.cast.some((m) => ctx.characters.find((c) => c.id === m.characterId)?.role === 'entity')
  return [
    `Animate this still image into a ${seconds}-second cinematic shot. Scene: ${scene.imagePrompt}.`,
    'Motion: very slow camera push-in; drifting fog; flickering oil-lamp light; gentle water ripples and floating leaves; characters move minimally (breathing, hair and cloth stirring in the wind, a slow head turn).',
    entity ? 'The supernatural presence only shifts subtly in the shadows or under the water â€“ never fully revealed.' : '',
    'Keep the composition, faces, outfits and Vietnamese setting exactly as in the image. No cuts, no new characters, no text.',
    SAFETY
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' ')
}

export interface RefImage {
  characterId: string
  kind: SheetView | 'expression' | 'state'
  /** ÄÆ°á»ng dáº«n tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i trong thÆ° má»¥c _characters */
  path: string
}

/**
 * Chá»n áº£nh tham chiáº¿u cho cáº£nh: CHá»ˆ nhÃ¢n váº­t Ä‘Ã£ khoÃ¡; Æ°u tiÃªn áº£nh tráº¡ng thÃ¡i â†’ áº£nh biá»ƒu cáº£m â†’ chÃ­nh diá»‡n â†’ 3/4.
 * Chia Ä‘á»u giá»¯a cÃ¡c nhÃ¢n váº­t (round-robin) cho tá»›i tá»‘i Ä‘a max áº£nh.
 */
export function pickReferences(scene: SceneBankItem, characters: Character[], max: number): RefImage[] {
  const order = { center: 0, left: 1, right: 1, background: 2 } as const
  const members = [...castForPrompt(scene, characters)].sort((a, b) => order[a.position] - order[b.position])
  const perChar: RefImage[][] = []
  for (const m of members) {
    const c = characters.find((x) => x.id === m.characterId)
    if (!c?.locked) continue
    const list: RefImage[] = []
    const st = c.states.find((s) => s.id === m.stateId)
    if (st?.referenceImage) list.push({ characterId: c.id, kind: 'state', path: st.referenceImage })
    const ex = c.expressionSheet.find((e) => e.expression === m.expression && e.imagePath)
    if (ex?.imagePath) list.push({ characterId: c.id, kind: 'expression', path: ex.imagePath })
    for (const v of ['front', 'threeQuarter'] as const) {
      const p = c.characterSheet[v]
      if (p) list.push({ characterId: c.id, kind: v, path: p })
    }
    if (list.length) perChar.push(list)
  }
  const out: RefImage[] = []
  for (let round = 0; out.length < max && perChar.some((l) => l.length > round); round++) {
    for (const l of perChar) {
      if (out.length >= max) break
      if (l[round]) out.push(l[round])
    }
  }
  return out
}

/**
 * ToÃ¡n Ken Burns (Má»©c 1) â€“ hÃ m thuáº§n, dÃ¹ng trong composition Remotion vÃ  test Ä‘Æ°á»£c.
 * Tráº£ vá» scale + dá»‹ch chuyá»ƒn (% kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c khung) táº¡i tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™ t (0..1) cá»§a cáº£nh.
 * Quy Æ°á»›c CSS: transform = translate(tx%, ty%) scale(s), transform-origin = origin.
 * Vá»›i pan: dá»‹ch tá»‘i Ä‘a (s-1)/2 * 100% Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng lá»™ mÃ©p áº£nh.
 */
import type { Easing, Motion } from '../types'

export function applyEasing(t: number, easing: Easing): number {
  const x = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t))
  switch (easing) {
    case 'linear':
      return x
    case 'easeIn':
      return x * x
    case 'easeOut':
      return 1 - (1 - x) * (1 - x)
    case 'easeInOut':
      return x < 0.5 ? 2 * x * x : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * x + 2, 2) / 2
  }
}

export interface KenBurnsFrame {
  scale: number
  translateXPercent: number
  translateYPercent: number
  originXPercent: number
  originYPercent: number
}

/**
 * PARALLAX 2.5D (Má»©c 2): 3 lá»›p [háº­u cáº£nh, chá»§ thá»ƒ, tiá»n cáº£nh] chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng khÃ¡c tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ â‡’ cáº£m giÃ¡c mÃ¡y quay 3D.
 * factor cá»§a lá»›p (0..1): lá»›p gáº§n dá»‹ch/zoom nhiá»u hÆ¡n lá»›p xa. Háº­u cáº£nh (lá»›p Ä‘áº·c) luÃ´n Ä‘á»§ lá» Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng lá»™ mÃ©p.
 */
export function parallaxLayerAt(motion: Motion, t: number, factor: number): KenBurnsFrame {
  const e = applyEasing(t, motion.easing)
  const amp = 0.05 + 0.12 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, motion.intensity))
  const f = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, factor))
  const fx = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, motion.focusPoint.x)) * 100
  const fy = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, motion.focusPoint.y)) * 100
  switch (motion.type) {
    case 'dollyIn':
      return { scale: 1.04 + amp * e * (0.35 + 0.65 * f), translateXPercent: 0, translateYPercent: 0, originXPercent: fx, originYPercent: fy }
    case 'dollyOut':
      return { scale: 1.04 + amp * (1 - e) * (0.35 + 0.65 * f), translateXPercent: 0, translateYPercent: 0, originXPercent: fx, originYPercent: fy }
    case 'truckLeft':
    case 'truckRight':
    default: {
      const scale = 1.08 + amp * 0.6
      const max = ((scale - 1) / 2) * 100 * 0.9
      const shift = (-max + 2 * max * e) * f
      return {
        scale,
        translateXPercent: motion.type === 'truckRight' ? -shift : shift,
        translateYPercent: 0,
        originXPercent: 50,
        originYPercent: 50
      }
    }
  }
}

export function kenBurnsAt(motion: Motion, t: number): KenBurnsFrame {
  const e = applyEasing(t, motion.easing)
  const amp = 0.06 + 0.14 * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, motion.intensity))
  const fx = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, motion.focusPoint.x)) * 100
  const fy = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, motion.focusPoint.y)) * 100
  const base = { translateXPercent: 0, translateYPercent: 0, originXPercent: fx, originYPercent: fy }

  switch (motion.type) {
    case 'zoomIn':
    case 'dollyIn':
      return { ...base, scale: 1.02 + amp * e }
    case 'zoomOut':
    case 'dollyOut':
      return { ...base, scale: 1.02 + amp * (1 - e) }
    case 'panLeft':
    case 'panRight':
    case 'panUp':
    case 'panDown':
    case 'truckLeft':
    case 'truckRight': {
      const scale = 1.06 + amp * 0.8
      const max = ((scale - 1) / 2) * 100 * 0.9
      const shift = -max + 2 * max * e // tá»« -max tá»›i +max
      const frame = { scale, translateXPercent: 0, translateYPercent: 0, originXPercent: 50, originYPercent: 50 }
      // MÃ¡y quay lia sang trÃ¡i â‡’ ná»™i dung trÃ´i sang pháº£i
      if (motion.type === 'panLeft' || motion.type === 'truckLeft') frame.translateXPercent = shift
      if (motion.type === 'panRight' || motion.type === 'truckRight') frame.translateXPercent = -shift
      if (motion.type === 'panUp') frame.translateYPercent = shift
      if (motion.type === 'panDown') frame.translateYPercent = -shift
      return frame
    }
    case 'aiClip':
      return { ...base, scale: 1 }
  }
}

/**
 * GÃ¡n chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng Má»©c 1 (Ken Burns) + chuyá»ƒn cáº£nh theo cÆ°á»ng Ä‘á»™ cáº£m xÃºc cá»§a beat.
 * - Beat cÄƒng tháº³ng â†’ Æ°u tiÃªn zoomIn cháº­m (Ã¡p sÃ¡t), easing easeIn.
 * - KhÃ´ng 2 cáº£nh liá»n nhau cÃ¹ng kiá»ƒu chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng.
 * - Chuyá»ƒn cáº£nh: trong beat dÃ¹ng fade, giá»¯a beat dÃ¹ng dip-to-black, cao trÃ o cÃ³ flash ngáº¯n.
 */
import type { FocusPoint, KenBurnsType, Motion, MotionProfile, ParallaxType, ShotType, Transition } from '../types'

export interface MotionSceneInput {
  beatId: string
  focusPoint: FocusPoint
}

const TENSE: KenBurnsType[] = ['zoomIn', 'panUp', 'panLeft', 'zoomOut']
const MEDIUM: KenBurnsType[] = ['panRight', 'zoomIn', 'panLeft', 'zoomOut', 'panDown']
const CALM: KenBurnsType[] = ['zoomOut', 'panLeft', 'panRight', 'panDown', 'zoomIn']

const clamp = (n: number, lo: number, hi: number): number => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, n))
const r2 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 100) / 100

/** Cá»¡ cáº£nh cÃ³ chiá»u sÃ¢u rÃµ â‡’ parallax Ä‘áº¹p; cáº£nh chi tiáº¿t / nhÃ¬n tá»« trÃªn xuá»‘ng thÃ¬ pháº³ng, khÃ´ng há»£p */
const PARALLAX_SHOTS: ShotType[] = ['wide', 'medium', 'overShoulder', 'closeUp']

/**
 * Chá»n cáº£nh dÃ¹ng parallax 2.5D theo parallaxRatio: Æ°u tiÃªn cáº£nh cÃ³ chiá»u sÃ¢u vÃ  cáº£m xÃºc vá»«aâ€“cao,
 * ráº£i Ä‘á»u (trÃ¡nh 2 cáº£nh parallax liá»n nhau náº¿u cÃ²n lá»±a chá»n). excluded = cáº£nh Ä‘Ã£ dÃ¹ng video AI.
 */
export function chooseParallaxScenes(
  scenes: { beatId: string; shotType: ShotType }[],
  beatIntensity: (beatId: string) => number,
  ratio: number,
  excluded: Set<number> = new Set()
): Set<number> {
  const count = Math.round(scenes.length * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ratio)))
  const eligible = scenes
    .map((s, i) => ({ i, ok: PARALLAX_SHOTS.includes(s.shotType) && !excluded.has(i), score: 1 - Math.abs(beatIntensity(s.beatId) - 0.7) }))
    .filter((x) => x.ok)
    .sort((a, b) => (b.score !== a.score ? b.score - a.score : a.i - b.i))
  const picked = new Set<number>()
  // LÆ°á»£t 1: khÃ´ng chá»n cáº£nh ká» cáº£nh Ä‘Ã£ chá»n; lÆ°á»£t 2: láº¥p cho Ä‘á»§ sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng
  for (const pass of [0, 1]) {
    for (const x of eligible) {
      if (picked.size >= count) break
      if (picked.has(x.i)) continue
      if (pass === 0 && (picked.has(x.i - 1) || picked.has(x.i + 1))) continue
      picked.add(x.i)
    }
  }
  return picked
}

const PARALLAX_TENSE: ParallaxType[] = ['dollyIn', 'truckLeft', 'truckRight']
const PARALLAX_CALM: ParallaxType[] = ['dollyOut', 'truckRight', 'truckLeft']

export function assignMotions(
  scenes: MotionSceneInput[],
  beatIntensity: (beatId: string) => number,
  profile: MotionProfile,
  levels: { parallax?: Set<number>; aiVideo?: Set<number> } = {}
): Motion[] {
  const result: Motion[] = []
  let prevType: string | null = null
  scenes.forEach((scene, i) => {
    const e = clamp(beatIntensity(scene.beatId), 0, 1)
    const intensity = r2(clamp(profile.baseIntensity + e * profile.intensityGain, 0.1, 1))
    if (levels.aiVideo?.has(i)) {
      prevType = 'aiClip'
      result.push({ level: 'aiVideo', type: 'aiClip', intensity, easing: 'linear', focusPoint: { ...scene.focusPoint } })
      return
    }
    if (levels.parallax?.has(i)) {
      // Cáº£nh cÄƒng tháº³ng Æ°u tiÃªn dolly vÃ o (Ã¡p sÃ¡t); cáº£nh khÃ¡c xoay vÃ²ng; khÃ´ng trÃ¹ng kiá»ƒu cáº£nh liá»n trÆ°á»›c
      const list = e >= profile.tenseThreshold ? PARALLAX_TENSE : PARALLAX_CALM
      const offset = e >= profile.tenseThreshold ? 0 : i % list.length
      let chosen: ParallaxType = list[offset]
      for (let k = 0; k < list.length; k++) {
        const c = list[(offset + k) % list.length]
        if (c !== prevType) {
          chosen = c
          break
        }
      }
      prevType = chosen
      result.push({ level: 'parallax', type: chosen, intensity, easing: e >= profile.tenseThreshold ? 'easeIn' : 'easeInOut', focusPoint: { ...scene.focusPoint } })
      return
    }
    const list = e >= profile.tenseThreshold ? TENSE : e >= 0.4 ? MEDIUM : CALM
    // Cáº£nh cÄƒng tháº³ng luÃ´n thá»­ zoomIn trÆ°á»›c; cáº£nh khÃ¡c xoay vÃ²ng theo vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘a dáº¡ng
    const offset = e >= profile.tenseThreshold ? 0 : i % list.length
    let type: KenBurnsType = list[offset]
    for (let k = 0; k < list.length; k++) {
      const candidate = list[(offset + k) % list.length]
      if (candidate !== prevType) {
        type = candidate
        break
      }
    }
    prevType = type
    result.push({
      level: 'kenBurns',
      type,
      intensity,
      easing: e >= profile.tenseThreshold ? 'easeIn' : 'easeInOut',
      focusPoint: { ...scene.focusPoint }
    })
  })
  return result
}

export function assignTransitions(
  scenes: { beatId: string }[],
  beatIntensity: (beatId: string) => number,
  profile: MotionProfile
): Transition[] {
  return scenes.map((scene, i) => {
    const d = profile.transitionDurationSec
    if (i === 0) return { type: 'dipToBlack', durationSec: d * 1.5 }
    const prev = scenes[i - 1]
    const e = beatIntensity(scene.beatId)
    if (prev.beatId !== scene.beatId) {
      if (profile.flashAtClimax && e >= profile.climaxThreshold) return { type: 'flash', durationSec: 0.18 }
      return { type: profile.transitionBetweenBeats, durationSec: d }
    }
    return { type: profile.transitionWithinBeat, durationSec: d }
  })
}

/** ToÃ¡n tÃ¡ch lá»›p parallax (hÃ m thuáº§n, cÃ³ test) */

export function percentile(values: ArrayLike<number>, p: number): number {
  const n = values.length
  if (n === 0) return 0
  // Láº¥y máº«u tá»‘i Ä‘a ~20k Ä‘iá»ƒm cho nhanh vá»›i áº£nh lá»›n
  const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(n / 20000))
  const sample: number[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i += step) sample.push(values[i])
  sample.sort((a, b) => a - b)
  const k = Math.min(sample.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.round(p * (sample.length - 1))))
  return sample[k]
}

export function smoothstep(edge0: number, edge1: number, x: number): number {
  if (edge1 <= edge0) return x >= edge1 ? 1 : 0
  const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (x - edge0) / (edge1 - edge0)))
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
}

/** Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ depth vá» 0..1 (1 = gáº§n) */
export function normalizeDepth(raw: Float32Array, higherIsNearer: boolean): Float32Array {
  let min = Infinity
  let max = -Infinity
  for (const v of raw) {
    if (v < min) min = v
    if (v > max) max = v
  }
  const range = max - min || 1
  const out = new Float32Array(raw.length)
  for (let i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {
    const v = (raw[i] - min) / range
    out[i] = higherIsNearer ? v : 1 - v
  }
  return out
}

/** Depth Æ°á»›c lÆ°á»£ng khi chÆ°a cÃ³ model: phÃ­a dÆ°á»›i khung gáº§n hÆ¡n + vÃ¹ng quanh focusPoint ná»•i lÃªn */
export function heuristicDepth(w: number, h: number, focus: { x: number; y: number }): Float32Array {
  const out = new Float32Array(w * h)
  const sx = 0.22
  const sy = 0.3
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
    const ny = y / Math.max(1, h - 1)
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      const nx = x / Math.max(1, w - 1)
      const blob = Math.exp(-(((nx - focus.x) / sx) ** 2 + ((ny - focus.y) / sy) ** 2))
      out[y * w + x] = Math.min(1, 0.5 * ny + 0.6 * blob)
    }
  }
  return out
}

/**
 * Ãp tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn phÃ¡t Ã¢m: thay thuáº­t ngá»¯ báº±ng cÃ¡ch Ä‘á»c (ttsRespelling) CHá»ˆ trong vÄƒn báº£n gá»­i TTS.
 * VÄƒn báº£n hiá»ƒn thá»‹ (ká»‹ch báº£n, phá»¥ Ä‘á») giá»¯ nguyÃªn.
 */
import type { Language, PronunciationEntry } from '../types'

function escapeRegExp(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')
}

const normKey = (s: string): string => s.normalize('NFC').toLocaleLowerCase('vi')

/**
 * Regex khá»›p nguyÃªn cá»¥m tá»«, ranh giá»›i theo chá»¯ cÃ¡i Unicode (Ä‘á»ƒ "Ä‘Ã²" khÃ´ng khá»›p trong "Ä‘Ã²n").
 * GhÃ©p táº¥t cáº£ thuáº­t ngá»¯ thÃ nh má»™t regex duy nháº¥t, cá»¥m dÃ i Æ°u tiÃªn trÆ°á»›c â†’ thay má»™t lÆ°á»£t,
 * trÃ¡nh trÆ°á»ng há»£p thay xong rá»“i bá»‹ thay tiáº¿p láº§n ná»¯a.
 */
function buildTermsRegex(terms: string[]): RegExp | null {
  const cleaned = [...new Set(terms.map((t) => t.normalize('NFC').trim()).filter((t) => t.length > 0))]
  if (cleaned.length === 0) return null
  cleaned.sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)
  const alternation = cleaned.map(escapeRegExp).join('|')
  return new RegExp(`(?<![\\p{L}\\p{N}])(${alternation})(?![\\p{L}\\p{N}])`, 'giu')
}

export interface PronunciationResult {
  text: string
  appliedIds: string[]
}

export function applyPronunciation(
  text: string,
  entries: PronunciationEntry[],
  language: Language
): PronunciationResult {
  const relevant = entries.filter((e) => e.language === language && e.term.trim().length > 0)
  const source = text.normalize('NFC')
  const re = buildTermsRegex(relevant.map((e) => e.term))
  if (!re) return { text: source, appliedIds: [] }
  const byKey = new Map(relevant.map((e) => [normKey(e.term), e]))
  const applied = new Set<string>()
  const out = source.replace(re, (match: string) => {
    const entry = byKey.get(normKey(match))
    if (!entry) return match
    applied.add(entry.id)
    return entry.ttsRespelling
  })
  return { text: out, appliedIds: [...applied] }
}

/** TÃ¬m id cÃ¡c thuáº­t ngá»¯ xuáº¥t hiá»‡n trong cÃ¢u (dÃ¹ng gáº¯n glossaryTermIds cho ScriptLine) */
export function findTermIds(text: string, entries: PronunciationEntry[], language: Language): string[] {
  return applyPronunciation(text, entries, language).appliedIds
}

/**
 * GÃ³i xuáº¥t báº£n (hÃ m thuáº§n): mÃ´ táº£ YouTube (chapters + chÃº thÃ­ch thuáº­t ngá»¯ + nguá»“n truyá»n thuyáº¿t),
 * chuáº©n hoÃ¡ hashtag/tag, checklist trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘Äƒng, nhÃ£n ná»™i dung AI.
 */
import type { Chapter, ChecklistItem, FolkloreEntry, PronunciationEntry, Release } from '../types'

export function fmtTimestamp(sec: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sec))
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600)
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)
  const r = s % 60
  return h > 0 ? `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}` : `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, '0')}`
}

/** YouTube yÃªu cáº§u chapter Ä‘áº§u 0:00, â‰¥ 3 chapter, má»—i chapter â‰¥ 10 giÃ¢y */
export function validChapters(chapters: Chapter[]): Chapter[] {
  const out: Chapter[] = []
  for (const c of [...chapters].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)) {
    const prev = out[out.length - 1]
    if (prev && c.start - prev.start < 10) continue
    out.push(out.length === 0 ? { ...c, start: 0 } : c)
  }
  return out.length >= 3 ? out : []
}

export function buildYoutubeDescription(args: {
  intro: string
  chapters: Chapter[]
  glossary: PronunciationEntry[]
  folklore: FolkloreEntry[]
  fictionNote: string
  language: 'en' | 'vi'
}): string {
  const en = args.language === 'en'
  const parts: string[] = [args.intro.trim()]
  const ch = validChapters(args.chapters)
  if (ch.length) parts.push((en ? 'Chapters:\n' : 'ChÆ°Æ¡ng:\n') + ch.map((c) => `${fmtTimestamp(c.start)} ${en ? c.title : (c.titleVi ?? c.title)}`).join('\n'))
  const gl = args.glossary.filter((g) => g.glossEn)
  if (gl.length && en) parts.push('Vietnamese terms:\n' + gl.map((g) => `â€¢ ${g.term} â€“ ${g.glossEn}`).join('\n'))
  if (args.folklore.length) {
    const lines = args.folklore.map((f) => {
      const kind = f.isDocumentedFolklore ? (en ? 'documented Vietnamese folk belief' : 'tÃ­n ngÆ°á»¡ng dÃ¢n gian cÃ³ ghi nháº­n') : en ? "channel's fiction" : 'hÆ° cáº¥u cá»§a kÃªnh'
      const src = f.sources.filter((s) => s.trim() && !s.startsWith('(')).join('; ')
      return `â€¢ ${en ? f.nameEn : f.nameVi}: ${kind}${src ? ` (${en ? 'sources' : 'nguá»“n'}: ${src})` : ''}`
    })
    parts.push((en ? 'About the legend:\n' : 'Vá» truyá»n thuyáº¿t:\n') + lines.join('\n'))
  }
  if (args.fictionNote.trim()) parts.push(en ? `Note: the village, characters and events in this story are fictional.` : `LÆ°u Ã½: ${args.fictionNote.trim()}`)
  parts.push(en ? 'Narration voice and illustrations are AI-assisted.' : 'Giá»ng Ä‘á»c vÃ  hÃ¬nh minh hoáº¡ cÃ³ sá»­ dá»¥ng AI.')
  return parts.join('\n\n')
}

/** Hashtag: bá» dáº¥u cÃ¡ch, thÃªm #, bá» trÃ¹ng, tá»‘i Ä‘a max */
export function normalizeHashtags(tags: string[], max = 8): string[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>()
  const out: string[] = []
  for (const t of tags) {
    const clean = t.trim().replace(/^#+/, '').replace(/\s+/g, '')
    if (!clean) continue
    const key = clean.toLocaleLowerCase('vi')
    if (seen.has(key)) continue
    seen.add(key)
    out.push(`#${clean}`)
    if (out.length >= max) break
  }
  return out
}

/** Tag YouTube: tá»•ng â‰¤ 500 kÃ½ tá»±, bá» trÃ¹ng */
export function normalizeTags(tags: string[], maxChars = 480): string[] {
  const out: string[] = []
  let total = 0
  for (const t of tags.map((x) => x.trim().replace(/^#/, '')).filter(Boolean)) {
    if (out.some((o) => o.toLowerCase() === t.toLowerCase())) continue
    if (total + t.length + 1 > maxChars) break
    out.push(t)
    total += t.length + 1
  }
  return out
}

  if (f.isSeries) items.push({ id: 'part-label', labelVi: 'NhÃ£n "Pháº§n X/N" cÃ³ trÃªn video vÃ  caption', done: f.partLabelInCaption, auto: true })
  items.push(
    { id: 'ai-label', labelVi: 'Báº­t nhÃ£n ná»™i dung AI khi Ä‘Äƒng (YouTube: "Altered or synthetic content" Â· TikTok: "AI-generated content")', done: manual('ai-label') },
    { id: 'spelling', labelVi: 'ÄÃ£ soÃ¡t chÃ­nh táº£ phá»¥ Ä‘á» vÃ  tÃªn riÃªng tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t', done: manual('spelling') },
    { id: 'respect', labelVi: 'ÄÃ£ xem láº¡i: khÃ´ng cháº¿ giá»…u tÃ­n ngÆ°á»¡ng, khÃ´ng gáº¯n vá»›i ngÆ°á»i tháº­t / bi ká»‹ch cÃ³ tháº­t', done: manual('respect') }
  )
  return items
}

/** Video dÃ¹ng giá»ng/áº£nh/video AI trÃ´ng nhÆ° tháº­t â‡’ cáº§n khai bÃ¡o ná»™i dung AI */
export function aiDisclosureNeeded(release: Release): boolean {

/**
 * KHá»šP Cáº¢NH THEO BEAT
 * - Gom cÃ¡c cÃ¢u liÃªn tiáº¿p cÃ¹ng beat, chia thÃ nh nhÃ³m 3â€“8 giÃ¢y (khÃ´ng cáº¯t giá»¯a cÃ¢u).
 * - Má»—i nhÃ³m = 1 cáº£nh, láº¥y tá»« ngÃ¢n hÃ ng cáº£nh cá»§a beat Ä‘Ã³, tuÃ¢n luáº­t Ä‘a dáº¡ng
 *   (khÃ´ng 2 cáº£nh liá»n nhau cÃ¹ng shotType náº¿u cÃ²n lá»±a chá»n khÃ¡c, khÃ´ng láº·p cÃ¹ng má»™t áº£nh liá»n nhau).
 * - Beat thiáº¿u cáº£nh â†’ mÆ°á»£n cáº£nh beat gáº§n nháº¥t + tráº£ vá» gá»£i Ã½ "cáº§n táº¡o thÃªm cáº£nh".
 */
import type { Beat, SceneBankItem, ShotType } from '../types'

export interface MatchLine {
  id: string
  beatId: string
  start: number
  end: number
  /** true = cÃ¢u má»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u Ä‘oáº¡n cÃ³ nhÃ¢n váº­t kÃªnh (ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ) xuáº¥t hiá»‡n trÃªn hÃ¬nh */
  narrator?: boolean
}

export interface NarratorOptions {
  /** Cáº£nh cÃ³ nhÃ¢n váº­t kÃªnh trong cast */
  isNarratorScene: (s: SceneBankItem) => boolean
}

export interface MatchOptions {
  minSec: number
  targetSec: number
  maxSec: number
  /** Cáº¯t cáº£nh sá»›m hÆ¡n Ä‘áº§u cÃ¢u káº¿ tiáº¿p má»™t chÃºt (náº±m trong khoáº£ng láº·ng) */
  cutLeadSec: number
}

export interface MatchedScene {
  id: string
  sceneBankId: string
  beatId: string
  start: number
  end: number
  lineIds: string[]
}

export interface MissingSceneHint {
  beatId: string
  available: number
  needed: number
  reason: 'noScenes' | 'reuse'
}

const r3 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000

/** Gom cÃ¢u liÃªn tiáº¿p cÃ¹ng beatId (vÃ  cÃ¹ng cá» ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ) */
export function groupByBeat(lines: MatchLine[]): MatchLine[][] {
  const groups: MatchLine[][] = []
  for (const line of lines) {
    const last = groups[groups.length - 1]
    if (last && last[0].beatId === line.beatId && !!last[0].narrator === !!line.narrator) last.push(line)
    else groups.push([line])
  }
  return groups
}

/** Chia má»™t nhÃ³m cÃ¢u thÃ nh cÃ¡c cá»¥m ~targetSec, tá»‘i Ä‘a maxSec (trá»« khi 1 cÃ¢u Ä‘Ã£ dÃ i hÆ¡n). */
export function chunkLines(lines: MatchLine[], opts: MatchOptions): MatchLine[][] {
  const chunks: MatchLine[][] = []
  let current: MatchLine[] = []
  const span = (c: MatchLine[]): number => (c.length ? c[c.length - 1].end - c[0].start : 0)
  for (const line of lines) {
    if (current.length > 0) {
      const d = span(current)
      const withNext = line.end - current[0].start
      if (d >= opts.minSec && (withNext > opts.maxSec || d >= opts.targetSec)) {
        chunks.push(current)
        current = []
      }
    }
    current.push(line)
  }
  if (current.length > 0) {
    // Cá»¥m cuá»‘i quÃ¡ ngáº¯n â†’ nháº­p vÃ o cá»¥m trÆ°á»›c (trong cÃ¹ng beat)
    if (chunks.length > 0 && span(current) < opts.minSec) {
      chunks[chunks.length - 1].push(...current)
    } else {
      chunks.push(current)
    }
  }
  return chunks
}

function pickCandidates(beatId: string, bank: SceneBankItem[], beats: Beat[]): SceneBankItem[] {
  const own = bank.filter((s) => s.beatId === beatId)
  if (own.length > 0) return own
  const order = beats.find((b) => b.id === beatId)?.order ?? 0
  // Beat gáº§n nháº¥t cÃ³ cáº£nh (Æ°u tiÃªn beat phÃ­a trÆ°á»›c khi khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch báº±ng nhau)
  const sorted = [...beats]
    .filter((b) => b.id !== beatId && bank.some((s) => s.beatId === b.id))
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const da = Math.abs(a.order - order)
      const db = Math.abs(b.order - order)
      if (da !== db) return da - db
      return a.order - b.order
    })
  const nearest = sorted[0]
  return nearest ? bank.filter((s) => s.beatId === nearest.id) : bank.slice(0, 1)
}

export function matchScenes(
  lines: MatchLine[],
  bank: SceneBankItem[],
  beats: Beat[],
  totalDuration: number,
  opts: MatchOptions,
  narrator?: NarratorOptions
): { scenes: MatchedScene[]; missing: MissingSceneHint[] } {
  if (lines.length === 0 || bank.length === 0) return { scenes: [], missing: [] }

  // Cáº£nh ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ chá»‰ dÃ¹ng cho cÃ¢u Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u narrator; cáº£nh truyá»‡n dÃ¹ng cho pháº§n cÃ²n láº¡i
  const narratorBank = narrator ? bank.filter(narrator.isNarratorScene) : []
  const storyBank = narrator ? bank.filter((s) => !narrator.isNarratorScene(s)) : bank
  const usage = new Map<string, number>()
  const missing: MissingSceneHint[] = []
  const draft: { chunk: MatchLine[]; bankItem: SceneBankItem; beatId: string }[] = []
  let prev: SceneBankItem | null = null

  for (const group of groupByBeat(lines)) {
    const beatId = group[0].beatId
    const chunks = chunkLines(group, opts)
    const useNarrator = !!group[0].narrator && narratorBank.length > 0
    const pool = useNarrator ? narratorBank : storyBank.length > 0 ? storyBank : bank
    const own = pool.filter((s) => s.beatId === beatId)
    // Cáº£nh ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ: Æ°u tiÃªn cáº£nh cÃ¹ng beat, náº¿u khÃ´ng láº¥y báº¥t ká»³ cáº£nh ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ nÃ o
    const candidates = useNarrator ? (own.length > 0 ? [...own, ...pool.filter((s) => s.beatId !== beatId)] : pool) : pickCandidates(beatId, pool, beats)
    if (!useNarrator) {
      if (own.length === 0) {
        missing.push({ beatId, available: 0, needed: chunks.length, reason: 'noScenes' })
      } else if (chunks.length > own.length) {
        missing.push({ beatId, available: own.length, needed: chunks.length, reason: 'reuse' })
      }
    }

    for (const chunk of chunks) {
      // Äiá»ƒm pháº¡t: trÃ¹ng áº£nh liá»n trÆ°á»›c (ráº¥t náº·ng) > trÃ¹ng shotType liá»n trÆ°á»›c > Ä‘Ã£ dÃ¹ng nhiá»u láº§n
      let best: SceneBankItem = candidates[0]
      let bestScore = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY
      candidates.forEach((c, idx) => {
        let score = idx * 0.01 + (usage.get(c.id) ?? 0) * 10
        if (prev && c.id === prev.id) score += 1000
        if (prev && c.shotType === prev.shotType) score += 100
        if (score < bestScore) {
          bestScore = score
          best = c
        }
      })
      usage.set(best.id, (usage.get(best.id) ?? 0) + 1)
      draft.push({ chunk, bankItem: best, beatId })
      prev = best
    }
  }

  // TÃ­nh biÃªn cáº£nh: cáº£nh Ä‘áº§u tá»« 0, cáº£nh cuá»‘i tá»›i háº¿t video, biÃªn náº±m trong khoáº£ng láº·ng giá»¯a 2 cÃ¢u
  const scenes: MatchedScene[] = draft.map((d, i) => {
    const start =
      i === 0
        ? 0
        : (() => {
            const prevEnd = draft[i - 1].chunk[draft[i - 1].chunk.length - 1].end
            const nextStart = d.chunk[0].start
            const gap = Math.max(0, nextStart - prevEnd)
            return nextStart - Math.min(opts.cutLeadSec, gap / 2)
          })()
    return {
      id: `rs-${String(i + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`,
      sceneBankId: d.bankItem.id,
      beatId: d.beatId,
      start: r3(start),
      end: 0,
      lineIds: d.chunk.map((l) => l.id)
    }
  })
  scenes.forEach((s, i) => {
    s.end = i < scenes.length - 1 ? scenes[i + 1].start : r3(Math.max(totalDuration, s.start + 0.5))
  })
  return { scenes, missing }
}

export interface DiversityItem {
  shotType: ShotType
  motionType: string
}

export interface DiversityViolation {
  index: number
  kind: 'shotType' | 'motion'
}

/** Luáº­t Ä‘a dáº¡ng: khÃ´ng 2 cáº£nh liá»n nhau cÃ¹ng shotType, khÃ´ng 2 cáº£nh liá»n nhau cÃ¹ng kiá»ƒu chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng */
export function findDiversityViolations(items: DiversityItem[]): DiversityViolation[] {
  const out: DiversityViolation[] = []
  for (let i = 1; i < items.length; i++) {
    if (items[i].shotType === items[i - 1].shotType) out.push({ index: i, kind: 'shotType' })
    if (items[i].motionType === items[i - 1].motionType) out.push({ index: i, kind: 'motion' })
  }
  return out
}

/**
 * Chá»n Ä‘oáº¡n cáº¯t Shorts teaser (30â€“60 giÃ¢y) tá»« báº£n YouTube dÃ i:
 * - Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u á»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u má»™t cÃ¢u, káº¿t thÃºc á»Ÿ cuá»‘i má»™t cÃ¢u (khÃ´ng cáº¯t giá»¯a cÃ¢u).
 * - KhÃ´ng láº¥y cold open/outro/giáº£i thÃ­ch; Æ°u tiÃªn Ä‘oáº¡n cÄƒng (tá»•ng cÆ°á»ng Ä‘á»™ cao)
 *   vÃ  Káº¾T THÃšC á»ž CÃ‚U CÄ‚NG (treo lá»­ng) Ä‘á»ƒ ngÆ°á»i xem muá»‘n xem báº£n dÃ i.

export interface TeaserLine {
  id: string
  start: number
  end: number
  beatId: string
  sectionKind: string
}

export interface TeaserWindow {
  start: number
  end: number
  lineIds: string[]
  score: number
}

export function selectTeaserWindow(
  lines: TeaserLine[],
  beatIntensity: (beatId: string) => number,
  opts: { minSec: number; maxSec: number }
): TeaserWindow | null {
  const usable = lines.filter((l) => l.sectionKind === 'story' || l.sectionKind === 'context' || l.sectionKind === 'hook')
  let best: TeaserWindow | null = null
  for (let i = 0; i < usable.length; i++) {
    for (let j = i; j < usable.length; j++) {
      // Chá»‰ xÃ©t chuá»—i cÃ¢u liá»n nhau trong ká»‹ch báº£n gá»‘c
      if (j > i && lines.indexOf(usable[j]) !== lines.indexOf(usable[j - 1]) + 1) break
      const len = usable[j].end - usable[i].start
      if (len > opts.maxSec) break
      if (len < opts.minSec) continue
      const seg = usable.slice(i, j + 1)
      const avg = seg.reduce((s, l) => s + beatIntensity(l.beatId), 0) / seg.length
      const endTension = beatIntensity(usable[j].beatId)
      // Káº¿t á»Ÿ cÃ¢u cÄƒng Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÆ°á»Ÿng; káº¿t á»Ÿ beat cao trÃ o cuá»‘i cÃ¹ng (Ä‘Ã£ "giáº£i quyáº¿t") bá»‹ trá»« nháº¹
      const score = avg * 2 + endTension + (len - opts.minSec) / (opts.maxSec - opts.minSec) * 0.2
      if (!best || score > best.score) best = { start: usable[i].start, end: usable[j].end, lineIds: seg.map((l) => l.id), score: Math.round(score * 1000) / 1000 }
    }
  }
  return best
}

/**

import type { SoundAsset, SoundCue, SoundPalette } from '../types'

export interface DesignLine {

  end: number

  climaxThreshold: number
  silenceBeforeClimaxSec: number
}

/** Tá»« khoÃ¡ trong lá»i ká»ƒ (VI + EN) â†’ tag Ã¢m thanh */
export const SFX_KEYWORDS: Record<string, RegExp> = {
  water: /nÆ°á»›c|sÃ´ng|táº¯m|cháº¿t Ä‘uá»‘i|splash|water|river|drown/i,
  'wood-creak': /mÃ¡i chÃ¨o|ghe|Ä‘Ã²|thuyá»n|vÃ¡n|káº½o káº¹t|oar|boat|plank|creak/i,
  knock: /gÃµ cá»­a|cá»­a nhÃ |knock|door/i,
  footsteps: /bÆ°á»›c chÃ¢n|dáº¥u chÃ¢n|footstep|footprint/i,
  whisper: /thÃ¬ tháº§m|tháº§m thÃ¬|kÃªu nhá»|gá»i tÃªn|whisper|call(s|ed)? (your|my|her) name/i,
  wind: /giÃ³|wind/i
}

const r2 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 100) / 100
let seq = 0
const cid = (k: string): string => `cue-${k}-${(++seq).toString(36)}`

function score(asset: SoundAsset, wanted: string[]): number {
  const tags = asset.tags.map((t) => t.toLowerCase())
  return wanted.reduce((s, w) => s + (tags.some((t) => t.includes(w.toLowerCase()) || w.toLowerCase().includes(t)) ? 1 : 0), 0)
}

function best(assets: SoundAsset[], kind: SoundAsset['kind'], wanted: string[]): SoundAsset | undefined {
  return assets
    .filter((a) => a.kind === kind && a.licenseConfirmed)
    .map((a) => ({ a, s: score(a, wanted) }))
    .sort((x, y) => y.s - x.s || x.a.name.localeCompare(y.a.name))[0]?.a
}

export function designSoundCues(input: DesignInput): SoundCue[] {
  seq = 0
  const cues: SoundCue[] = []
  const D = input.duration
  const allText = input.lines.map((l) => l.text).join(' ')
  const textTags = Object.entries(SFX_KEYWORDS)
    .filter(([, re]) => re.test(allText))
    .map(([t]) => t)

  const amb = best(input.assets, 'ambience', [...input.palette.ambienceTags, ...textTags])
  if (amb) cues.push({ id: cid('amb'), kind: 'ambience', assetId: amb.id, start: 0, end: r2(D), volume: 1, fadeIn: 2, fadeOut: 3, reason: `Ná»n khÃ´ng khÃ­ "${amb.name}" theo bá»‘i cáº£nh` })
  const music = best(input.assets, 'music', input.palette.musicTags)
  if (music) cues.push({ id: cid('mus'), kind: 'music', assetId: music.id, start: 0, end: r2(D), volume: 1, fadeIn: 4, fadeOut: 4, reason: `Nháº¡c ná»n "${music.name}" ráº¥t nhá», giá»¯ Ä‘á»™ cÄƒng` })

  // Cao trÃ o: cÃ¢u Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn cá»§a beat cÃ³ cÆ°á»ng Ä‘á»™ â‰¥ ngÆ°á»¡ng
  const climax = input.lines.find((l) => input.beatIntensity(l.beatId) >= input.climaxThreshold)
  const stinger = best(input.assets, 'stinger', input.palette.stingerTags)
  if (climax) {
    const silStart = Math.max(0, climax.start - input.silenceBeforeClimaxSec)
    if (climax.start - silStart >= 0.3) {
      cues.push({ id: cid('sil'), kind: 'silence', assetId: null, start: r2(silStart), end: r2(climax.start - 0.05), volume: 0, fadeIn: 0, fadeOut: 0, reason: 'Khoáº£ng láº·ng cÃ³ chá»§ Ä‘Ã­ch ngay trÆ°á»›c cao trÃ o' })
    }
    if (stinger) cues.push({ id: cid('stg'), kind: 'stinger', assetId: stinger.id, start: r2(climax.start), end: r2(Math.min(D, climax.start + Math.max(1, stinger.durationSec || 3))), volume: 1, fadeIn: 0, fadeOut: 0.6, reason: 'Stinger má»Ÿ mÃ n cao trÃ o' })
  }
  // CÃ¢u treo / káº¿t
  const last = [...input.lines].reverse().find((l) => l.sectionKind === 'cliffhanger' || l.sectionKind === 'outro')
  if (last && stinger && (!climax || last.start - climax.start > 8)) {
    cues.push({ id: cid('stg'), kind: 'stinger', assetId: stinger.id, start: r2(last.end), end: r2(Math.min(D, last.end + Math.max(1, stinger.durationSec || 3))), volume: 0.7, fadeIn: 0, fadeOut: 0.8, reason: 'Stinger nháº¹ sau cÃ¢u treo' })
  }

  // SFX theo tá»« khoÃ¡ â€“ chá»‰ á»Ÿ khoáº£nh kháº¯c then chá»‘t: bá» 2,5s Ä‘áº§u (cÃ¢u mÃ³c), beat Ä‘á»§ cÄƒng, cÃ¡ch nhau â‰¥10s, tá»‘i Ä‘a ~1/15s
  let lastSfx = -Infinity
  let sfxCount = 0
  const maxSfx = Math.max(2, Math.round(D / 15))
  for (const l of input.lines) {
    if (sfxCount >= maxSfx) break
    if (l.start < 2.5 || l.start - lastSfx < 10 || input.beatIntensity(l.beatId) < 0.45) continue
    // Má»™t cÃ¢u cÃ³ thá»ƒ khá»›p nhiá»u tá»« khoÃ¡ (vd "dáº¥u chÃ¢nâ€¦ cá»­a nhÃ ") â‡’ thá»­ láº§n lÆ°á»£t, láº¥y cÃ¡i cÃ³ Ã¢m thanh phÃ¹ há»£p
    let a: SoundAsset | undefined
    for (const [tag, re] of Object.entries(SFX_KEYWORDS)) {
      if (!re.test(l.text)) continue
      const cand = best(input.assets, 'sfx', [tag])
      if (cand && score(cand, [tag]) > 0) {
        a = cand
        break
      }
    }
    if (!a) continue
    const start = r2(Math.min(D - 0.5, l.start + 0.3))
    cues.push({ id: cid('sfx'), kind: 'sfx', assetId: a.id, start, end: r2(Math.min(D, start + Math.min(3, Math.max(0.5, a.durationSec || 2)))), volume: 1, fadeIn: 0.05, fadeOut: 0.3, reason: `"${l.text.slice(0, 40)}â€¦" â†’ ${a.name}` })
    lastSfx = l.start
    sfxCount++
  }
  return cues.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start)
}

/** Kiá»ƒm tra cue (tá»« LLM hoáº·c ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng): asset tá»“n táº¡i + Ä‘Ãºng loáº¡i + cÃ³ giáº¥y phÃ©p, thá»i gian há»£p lá»‡ */
export function validateCues(cues: SoundCue[], assets: SoundAsset[], duration: number): { cues: SoundCue[]; problems: string[] } {
  const problems: string[] = []
  const out: SoundCue[] = []
  for (const c of cues) {
    const start = Math.max(0, Math.min(duration, c.start))
    const end = Math.max(start, Math.min(duration, c.end))
    if (end - start < 0.1) {
      problems.push(`cue ${c.id}: quÃ¡ ngáº¯n hoáº·c ngoÃ i thá»i lÆ°á»£ng video`)
      continue
    }
    if (c.kind !== 'silence') {
      const a = assets.find((x) => x.id === c.assetId)
      if (!a) {
        problems.push(`cue ${c.id}: assetId "${c.assetId}" khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i`)
        continue
      }
      if (!a.licenseConfirmed) {
        problems.push(`cue ${c.id}: "${a.name}" chÆ°a xÃ¡c nháº­n giáº¥y phÃ©p`)
        continue
      }
      if (a.kind !== c.kind) {
        problems.push(`cue ${c.id}: "${a.name}" lÃ  ${a.kind}, khÃ´ng pháº£i ${c.kind}`)
        continue
      }
    }
    out.push({ ...c, start: r2(start), end: r2(end), volume: Math.max(0, Math.min(1.5, c.volume)), fadeIn: Math.max(0, c.fadeIn), fadeOut: Math.max(0, c.fadeOut) })
  }
  return { cues: out.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start), problems }
}

/**
 * Æ¯á»›c tÃ­nh thá»i lÆ°á»£ng Ä‘á»c cá»§a má»™t cÃ¢u.
 * Tiáº¿ng Anh: Ä‘áº¿m tá»«; tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t: Ä‘áº¿m Ã¢m tiáº¿t (má»—i Ã¢m tiáº¿t cÃ¡ch nhau dáº¥u cÃ¡ch) â†’ cÃ¹ng cÃ¡ch tÃ¡ch token.
 */
import type { Language } from '../types'

export interface SpeechRateConfig {
  unitsPerSecond: Record<Language, number>
  commaPauseSec: number
  stopPauseSec: number
  minSec: number
}

export function countSpeechUnits(text: string): number {
  return text
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter((t) => /[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(t)).length
}

export function estimateSpeechSeconds(
  text: string,
  language: Language,
  speed: number,
  cfg: SpeechRateConfig
): number {
  const units = countSpeechUnits(text)
  const rate = cfg.unitsPerSecond[language] * Math.max(0.3, speed)
  const commas = (text.match(/[,;:â€”â€“]/g) ?? []).length
  const stops = (text.match(/[.!?â€¦]+/g) ?? []).length
  const seconds = units / rate + commas * cfg.commaPauseSec + stops * cfg.stopPauseSec
  return Math.round(Math.max(cfg.minSec, seconds) * 1000) / 1000
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\shared\logic\subtitles.ts ===
/**
 * Dá»±ng phá»¥ Ä‘á» tá»« cÃ¢u Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ má»‘c thá»i gian.
 * - Má»‘c tá»«ng tá»«: chia thá»i lÆ°á»£ng cÃ¢u theo Ä‘á»™ dÃ i tá»« (GÄ6 sáº½ thay báº±ng má»‘c tháº­t tá»« Whisper).
 * - Chia cá»¥m theo preset (YouTube: dÃ i, 2 dÃ²ng; TikTok: 3â€“5 tá»«, chá»¯ to).
 * - ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u thuáº­t ngá»¯ trong tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn (báº£n tiáº¿ng Anh in nghiÃªng).
 */
import type { PronunciationEntry, SubtitleCue, SubtitleStyle, SubtitleWord } from '../types'

export interface SubtitleLineInput {
  id: string
  text: string
  start: number
  end: number
}

const r3 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000
const bare = (w: string): string =>
  w
    .normalize('NFC')
    .replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}]+|[^\p{L}\p{N}]+$/gu, '')
    .toLocaleLowerCase('vi')

export function tokenize(text: string): string[] {
  return text.split(/\s+/).filter((w) => w.length > 0)
}

/** Chia [start,end] cho cÃ¡c tá»« theo sá»‘ kÃ½ tá»± (+1 Ä‘á»ƒ tá»« ngáº¯n váº«n cÃ³ thá»i gian) */
export function wordTimings(words: string[], start: number, end: number): { start: number; end: number }[] {
  const weights = words.map((w) => bare(w).length + 1)
  const total = weights.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1
  const span = Math.max(0, end - start)
  let cursor = start
  return weights.map((wt, i) => {
    const s = cursor
    const e = i === weights.length - 1 ? end : cursor + (span * wt) / total
    cursor = e
    return { start: r3(s), end: r3(e) }
  })
}

/** Tráº£ vá» cÃ¡c cá»¥m [tá»« chá»‰ sá»‘ a, tá»›i b) â€“ Æ°u tiÃªn ngáº¯t sau dáº¥u cÃ¢u khi cá»¥m Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»§ dÃ i */
export function chunkWords(words: string[], maxWords: number, maxChars: number): [number, number][] {
  const chunks: [number, number][] = []
  let begin = 0
  let chars = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
    const w = words[i]
    const count = i - begin
    const nextChars = chars + (count > 0 ? 1 : 0) + w.length
    if (count > 0 && (count >= maxWords || nextChars > maxChars)) {
      chunks.push([begin, i])
      begin = i
      chars = w.length
    } else {
      chars = nextChars
    }
    const endsPhrase = /[,.!?â€¦;:"â€]$/.test(w)
    const len = i - begin + 1
    if (endsPhrase && len >= Math.max(2, Math.ceil(maxWords / 2)) && i < words.length - 1) {
      chunks.push([begin, i + 1])
      begin = i + 1
      chars = 0
    }
  }
  if (begin < words.length) chunks.push([begin, words.length])
  return chunks
}

/** ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u tá»« thuá»™c thuáº­t ngá»¯ (há»— trá»£ cá»¥m nhiá»u tá»« nhÆ° "cÃ¢y Ä‘a") */
export function markGlossaryWords(
  words: string[],
  glossary: PronunciationEntry[]
): { emphasis: boolean[]; termIds: string[] } {
  const keys = words.map(bare)
  const emphasis = words.map(() => false)
  const termIds = new Set<string>()
  const terms = glossary
    .filter((g) => g.glossEn)
    .map((g) => ({ id: g.id, parts: tokenize(g.term).map(bare) }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.parts.length - a.parts.length)
  for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
    for (const t of terms) {
      if (t.parts.length === 0 || i + t.parts.length > keys.length) continue
      if (t.parts.every((p, k) => keys[i + k] === p)) {
        for (let k = 0; k < t.parts.length; k++) emphasis[i + k] = true
        termIds.add(t.id)
      }
    }
  }
  return { emphasis, termIds: [...termIds] }
}

export function buildSubtitles(
  lines: SubtitleLineInput[],
  glossary: PronunciationEntry[],
  style: SubtitleStyle
): SubtitleCue[] {
  const cues: SubtitleCue[] = []
  for (const line of lines) {
    const words = tokenize(line.text)
    if (words.length === 0) continue
    const timings = wordTimings(words, line.start, line.end)
    const { emphasis } = markGlossaryWords(words, glossary)
    for (const [a, b] of chunkWords(words, style.maxWordsPerCue, style.maxCharsPerCue)) {
      const cueWords: SubtitleWord[] = []
      for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
        cueWords.push({ text: words[i], start: timings[i].start, end: timings[i].end, emphasis: emphasis[i] })
      }
      const ids = markGlossaryWords(words.slice(a, b), glossary).termIds
      cues.push({
        id: `${line.id}-c${cues.length + 1}`,
        start: cueWords[0].start,
        end: cueWords[cueWords.length - 1].end,
        text: cueWords.map((w) => w.text).join(' '),
        words: cueWords,
        glossaryTermIds: ids,
        styleId: style.id
      })
    }
  }
  return cues
}

/**
 * CHIA PHáº¦N TIKTOK/SHORTS: cáº¯t ká»‹ch báº£n dÃ i thÃ nh cÃ¡c pháº§n ~targetSec giÃ¢y.
 * - Æ¯u tiÃªn cáº¯t á»Ÿ ranh giá»›i beat (cÃ¢u chuyá»‡n liá»n máº¡ch hÆ¡n); náº¿u khÃ´ng thÃ¬ cáº¯t á»Ÿ ranh giá»›i cÃ¢u.
 * - KhÃ´ng bao giá» cáº¯t giá»¯a cÃ¢u.
 * - Pháº§n cuá»‘i quÃ¡ ngáº¯n (< minLastPartRatio Ã— target) Ä‘Æ°á»£c nháº­p vÃ o pháº§n trÆ°á»›c náº¿u khÃ´ng vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ nhiá»u.
 * targetSec á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¢y lÃ  thá»i lÆ°á»£ng THÃ‚N má»—i pháº§n (Ä‘Ã£ trá»« hook + cliffhanger).
 */
export interface PartLineInput {
  id: string
  beatId: string
  estSec: number
}

export interface SplitOptions {
  targetSec: number
  /** Cho phÃ©p lá»‡ch Â±ratio quanh target */
  toleranceRatio: number
  minLastPartRatio: number
}

export function splitIntoParts(lines: PartLineInput[], opts: SplitOptions): string[][] {
  if (lines.length === 0) return []
  const lo = opts.targetSec * (1 - opts.toleranceRatio)
  const hi = opts.targetSec * (1 + opts.toleranceRatio)
  const parts: PartLineInput[][] = []
  let cur: PartLineInput[] = []
  let curSec = 0

  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const line = lines[i]
    if (cur.length > 0) {
      const beatBoundary = cur[cur.length - 1].beatId !== line.beatId
      // ÄÃ£ Ä‘á»§ dÃ i vÃ  Ä‘ang á»Ÿ ranh giá»›i beat â‡’ cáº¯t Ä‘áº¹p
      const cutAtBeat = beatBoundary && curSec >= lo
      // ThÃªm cÃ¢u nÃ y sáº½ vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ xa â‡’ buá»™c cáº¯t á»Ÿ ranh giá»›i cÃ¢u
      const forced = curSec + line.estSec > hi && curSec >= opts.targetSec * 0.5
      if (cutAtBeat || forced) {
        parts.push(cur)
        cur = []
        curSec = 0
      }
    }
    cur.push(line)
    curSec += line.estSec
  }
  if (cur.length > 0) {
    const last = parts[parts.length - 1]
    const lastSec = (p: PartLineInput[]): number => p.reduce((s, l) => s + l.estSec, 0)
    if (last && curSec < opts.targetSec * opts.minLastPartRatio && lastSec(last) + curSec <= hi * 1.2) {
      last.push(...cur)
    } else {
      parts.push(cur)
    }
  }
  return parts.map((p) => p.map((l) => l.id))
}

/**
 * GhÃ©p cÃ¢u: tÃ­nh má»‘c start/end chÃ­nh xÃ¡c tá»«ng cÃ¢u tá»« thá»i lÆ°á»£ng take + khoáº£ng láº·ng trÆ°á»›c/sau.
 * Bá»‘ cá»¥c: [leadIn] [pauseBeforeâ‚][cÃ¢u 1][pauseAfterâ‚] [pauseBeforeâ‚‚][cÃ¢u 2]... [tail]
 */

export interface TimelineItemInput {
  id: string
  duration: number
  pauseBeforeMs: number
  pauseAfterMs: number
}

export interface TimedItem {
  id: string
  start: number
  end: number
}

export interface TimelineResult {
  items: TimedItem[]
  totalDuration: number
}

const r3 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000

export function computeLineTimings(
  inputs: TimelineItemInput[],
  opts: { leadInSec: number; tailSec: number }
): TimelineResult {
  let cursor = Math.max(0, opts.leadInSec)
  const items: TimedItem[] = []
  for (const it of inputs) {
    if (!(it.duration >= 0)) throw new Error(`Thá»i lÆ°á»£ng cÃ¢u ${it.id} khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: ${it.duration}`)
    const start = cursor + Math.max(0, it.pauseBeforeMs) / 1000
    const end = start + it.duration
    items.push({ id: it.id, start: r3(start), end: r3(end) })
    cursor = end + Math.max(0, it.pauseAfterMs) / 1000
  }
  return { items, totalDuration: r3(cursor + Math.max(0, opts.tailSec)) }
}

/**
 * Sá»­a timeline báº±ng kÃ©o tháº£ (hÃ m thuáº§n, cÃ³ test):
 * - KÃ©o biÃªn giá»¯a 2 cáº£nh liá»n nhau: cáº£nh trÆ°á»›c káº¿t thÃºc = cáº£nh sau báº¯t Ä‘áº§u (timeline luÃ´n liá»n máº¡ch).
 * - HÃºt vá» khoáº£ng láº·ng gáº§n nháº¥t giá»¯a 2 cÃ¢u (Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng cáº¯t cáº£nh giá»¯a cÃ¢u) náº¿u trong táº§m hÃºt.
 * - KÃ©o/di chuyá»ƒn sound cue, káº¹p trong thá»i lÆ°á»£ng video.

import type { ReleaseScene, SoundCue } from '../types'

const r3 = (n: number): number => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000

/** Äiá»ƒm hÃºt: giá»¯a khoáº£ng láº·ng giá»¯a cÃ¡c cÃ¢u */
export function lineGapSnapPoints(lines: { start: number | null; end: number | null }[]): number[] {
  const sorted = lines.filter((l) => l.start != null && l.end != null).sort((a, b) => (a.start as number) - (b.start as number))
  const pts: number[] = []
  for (let i = 1; i < sorted.length; i++) {
    const prevEnd = sorted[i - 1].end as number
    const nextStart = sorted[i].start as number
    if (nextStart >= prevEnd) pts.push(r3(nextStart - Math.min(0.2, (nextStart - prevEnd) / 2)))
  }
  return pts
}

export function snap(t: number, points: number[], radius: number): number {
  let best = t
  let d = radius
  for (const p of points) {
    if (Math.abs(p - t) <= d) {
      d = Math.abs(p - t)
      best = p
    }
  }
  return best
}

/**
 * Dá»i biÃªn báº¯t Ä‘áº§u cá»§a cáº£nh index (= biÃªn káº¿t thÃºc cá»§a cáº£nh index-1).
 * Má»—i cáº£nh giá»¯ tá»‘i thiá»ƒu minSec.
 */
export function moveSceneBoundary(scenes: ReleaseScene[], index: number, t: number, minSec = 0.6): ReleaseScene[] {
  if (index <= 0 || index >= scenes.length) return scenes
  const lo = scenes[index - 1].start + minSec
  const hi = scenes[index].end - minSec
  if (lo > hi) return scenes
  const nt = r3(Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, t)))
  return scenes.map((s, i) => (i === index - 1 ? { ...s, end: nt } : i === index ? { ...s, start: nt } : s))
}

export type CueDragMode = 'move' | 'start' | 'end'

export function dragCue(cue: SoundCue, mode: CueDragMode, delta: number, duration: number, minLen = 0.2): SoundCue {
  const len = cue.end - cue.start
  if (mode === 'move') {
    const start = r3(Math.min(Math.max(0, cue.start + delta), Math.max(0, duration - len)))
    return { ...cue, start, end: r3(start + len) }
  }
  if (mode === 'start') return { ...cue, start: r3(Math.min(Math.max(0, cue.start + delta), cue.end - minLen)) }
  return { ...cue, end: r3(Math.max(Math.min(duration, cue.end + delta), cue.start + minLen)) }
}

/**
 * Chá»n giá»ng cho cÃ¢u: ngÆ°á»i nÃ³i lÃ  nhÃ¢n váº­t cÃ³ giá»ng riÃªng theo ngÃ´n ngá»¯ â‡’ dÃ¹ng giá»ng Ä‘Ã³;
 * khÃ´ng cÃ³ â‡’ giá»ng máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cá»§a kÃªnh cho ngÃ´n ngá»¯ Ä‘Ã³ (ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ).
 */
import type { ChannelPreset, Character, Language, Library, VoiceProfile } from '../types'

export function resolveVoiceId(
  speakerId: string,
  language: Language,
  characters: Character[],
  channel: ChannelPreset | undefined
): string | null {
  const ch = characters.find((c) => c.id === speakerId)
  return ch?.voiceProfileIdByLanguage[language] ?? channel?.defaultVoiceByLanguage[language] ?? null
}

export function resolveVoice(
  speakerId: string,
  language: Language,
  library: Library,
  channelPresetId: string
): VoiceProfile | null {
  const channel = library.channelPresets.find((c) => c.id === channelPresetId)
  const id = resolveVoiceId(speakerId, language, library.characters, channel)
  return library.voiceProfiles.find((v) => v.id === id) ?? null
}

const RE = {
  ghost: /ghost|whisper|drown|ma da|thÃ¬ tháº§m|\bma\b|há»“n/i,
  old: /\bold\b|elder|grand|\bbÃ \b|\bÃ´ng\b|\bcá»¥\b|khÃ n/i,
  young: /young|teen|thiáº¿u|cÃ´ gÃ¡i|chÃ ng|\b1\d\b/i,
  female: /female|woman|girl|\bbÃ \b|\bcÃ´\b|chá»‹|thiáº¿u ná»¯|-f\b/i,
  male: /\bmale\b|\bman\b|\bÃ´ng\b|\banh\b|chÃ ng|-m\b/i
}

/**
 * Gá»£i Ã½ giá»ng cho nhÃ¢n váº­t má»›i theo vai (thá»±c thá»ƒ), tuá»•i, giá»›i tÃ­nh â€“ cháº¥m Ä‘iá»ƒm theo tá»« khoÃ¡ trong tÃªn/chá»‰ dáº«n giá»ng.
 * KhÃ´ng gÃ¡n giá»ng rÃµ rÃ ng khÃ¡c giá»›i. KhÃ´ng cháº¯c â‡’ null (dÃ¹ng giá»ng máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh kÃªnh, ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng tá»± chá»n).
 */
export function suggestVoiceId(c: Character, voices: VoiceProfile[], language: Language, excludeIds: Set<string>): string | null {
  let best: { id: string; score: number } | null = null
  for (const v of voices) {
    if (v.language !== language || excludeIds.has(v.id)) continue
    const t = `${v.name} ${v.baseInstructions} ${v.providerVoiceId}`
    const isGhost = RE.ghost.test(t)
    const isFemale = RE.female.test(t)
    const isMale = RE.male.test(t) && !isFemale
    if ((c.gender === 'male' && isFemale) || (c.gender === 'female' && isMale)) continue
    let score = 0
    if (c.role === 'entity') score += isGhost ? 6 : -3
    else if (isGhost) score -= 6
    if (c.age != null && c.age >= 55) score += RE.old.test(t) ? 3 : -1
    if (c.age != null && c.age < 30) score += RE.young.test(t) ? 3 : RE.old.test(t) ? -3 : 0
    if (c.gender === 'female' && isFemale) score += 1
    if (c.gender === 'male' && isMale) score += 1
    if (score > 0 && (!best || score > best.score)) best = { id: v.id, score }
  }
  return best?.id ?? null
}

/** NhÃ¢n váº­t kÃªnh (ngÆ°á»i ká»ƒ thÆ°Æ¡ng hiá»‡u) */
export function isChannelCharacter(characterId: string, characters: Character[]): boolean {
  return characters.find((c) => c.id === characterId)?.scope === 'channel'
}

/** NhÃ¢n váº­t hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trong má»™t Story: nhÃ¢n váº­t kÃªnh + nhÃ¢n váº­t thuá»™c Story Ä‘Ã³ */
export function charactersForStory(storyId: string, characters: Character[]): Character[] {
  return characters.filter((c) => c.scope === 'channel' || c.storyId === storyId)
}

/** Cáº¯t props theo khoáº£ng [start, end] rá»“i dá»i vá» 0; cáº£nh cuá»‘i kÃ©o dÃ i phá»§ tháº» káº¿t */
export function applyWindow(p: CompositionProps, w: CompositionWindow, voiceSrc: string): CompositionProps {
  const len = w.end - w.start
  const total = len + w.endCardSec
  const scenes = p.scenes
    .filter((s) => s.endSec > w.start + 0.05 && s.startSec < w.end - 0.05)
    .map((s) => ({ ...s, startSec: Math.max(0, s.startSec - w.start), endSec: Math.min(len, s.endSec - w.start) }))
  if (scenes.length) {
    scenes[0] = { ...scenes[0], startSec: 0, transition: { type: 'dipToBlack', durationSec: 0.4 } }
    scenes[scenes.length - 1] = { ...scenes[scenes.length - 1], endSec: total }
  }
  const shift = (t: number): number => Math.round((t - w.start) * 1000) / 1000
  const subtitles = p.subtitles
    .filter((c) => c.end > w.start && c.start < w.end)
    .map((c) => ({ ...c, start: Math.max(0, shift(c.start)), end: Math.min(len, shift(c.end)), words: c.words.map((x) => ({ ...x, start: shift(x.start), end: shift(x.end) })) }))
  return {
    ...p,
    durationSec: total,
    voiceSrc,
    scenes,
    subtitles,
    glossaryCards: p.glossaryCards.filter((g) => g.start >= w.start && g.start < w.end).map((g) => ({ ...g, start: shift(g.start), end: shift(g.end) })),
    partLabel: null,
    hookCard: w.hookText ? { text: w.hookText, untilSec: w.hookCardSec } : null,
    endCard: { title: w.ctaTitle, sub: w.ctaSub, fromSec: len }
  }
}

/** Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»™ng Má»©c 2/3 khi thiáº¿u lá»›p / clip â‡’ quay vá» Ken Burns tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘Æ°Æ¡ng */
function fallbackMotion(m: Motion): Motion {
  const map: Record<string, Motion['type']> = { dollyIn: 'zoomIn', dollyOut: 'zoomOut', truckLeft: 'panLeft', truckRight: 'panRight', aiClip: 'zoomIn' }
  return { ...m, level: 'kenBurns', type: map[m.type] ?? m.type }
}

const CENTER: FocusPoint = { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }

export function buildCompositionProps(args: BuildCompositionArgs): CompositionProps {
  const { story, release } = args
  const aspect = args.aspect ?? release.aspectRatio
  const size = args.sizes[aspect]
  const bankById = new Map(story.sceneBank.map((s) => [s.id, s]))

  const scenes: CompositionScene[] = release.scenes.map((rs, index) => {
    const bank = bankById.get(rs.sceneBankId)
    let media = rs.mediaOverride
    let motion: Motion = rs.motion
    if (!media && bank) {
      if (aspect === '9:16' && bank.media['9:16']) {
        media = bank.media['9:16']
        // áº¢nh dá»c Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c dá»±ng quanh focusPoint â‡’ tÃ¢m zoom á»Ÿ giá»¯a khung
        motion = { ...rs.motion, focusPoint: CENTER }
      } else {
        media = bank.media['16:9'] ?? bank.media['9:16']
      }
    }
    let layers: string[] | null = null
    let clip: CompositionScene['clip'] = null
    if (motion.level === 'parallax') {
      const p = bank?.parallax?.[aspect]
      if (p && media && p.sourcePath === media.path) layers = p.layers.map((l) => args.mediaUrl(l, p.createdAt))
      else motion = fallbackMotion(motion)
    } else if (motion.level === 'aiVideo') {
      const c = bank?.heroClip[aspect]
      if (c) clip = { src: args.mediaUrl(c.path, c.createdAt), durationSec: c.durationSec ?? 5 }
      else motion = fallbackMotion(motion)
    }
    return {
      id: rs.id,
      index,
      src: media ? args.mediaUrl(media.path, media.createdAt) : null,
      layers,
      clip,
      startSec: rs.start,
      endSec: rs.end,
      motion,
      transition: rs.transition,
      isHero: rs.isHero,
      label: bank ? `${bank.shotType} Â· ${bank.description}` : rs.sceneBankId
    }
  })

  // Tháº» chÃº thÃ­ch thuáº­t ngá»¯: chá»‰ báº£n khÃ´ng pháº£i tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t, láº§n Ä‘áº§u xuáº¥t hiá»‡n
  const glossaryCards: GlossaryCard[] = []
  if (release.language !== 'vi') {
    const seen = new Set<string>()
    for (const cue of release.subtitles) {
      for (const id of cue.glossaryTermIds) {
        if (seen.has(id)) continue
        const entry = args.glossary.find((g) => g.id === id)
        if (!entry?.glossEn) continue
        seen.add(id)
        glossaryCards.push({ start: cue.start, end: cue.start + 4.5, term: entry.term, gloss: entry.glossEn })
      }
    }
  }

  const lastSceneEnd = scenes.length ? scenes[scenes.length - 1].endSec : 0
  const durationSec = Math.max(1, release.voiceTrack?.duration ?? lastSceneEnd)
  const voice = release.voiceTrack?.mixedPath ?? null
  const isVerticalPlatform = release.platform === 'tiktok' || release.platform === 'youtubeShorts'

  const props: CompositionProps = {
    fps: args.fps,
    width: Math.round(size.width * (args.scale ?? 1)),
    height: Math.round(size.height * (args.scale ?? 1)),
    durationSec,
    aspect,
    voiceSrc: voice ? args.mediaUrl(voice, release.voiceTrack?.mixedAt ?? String(release.voiceTrack?.duration ?? '')) : null,
    scenes,
    subtitles: release.subtitles,
    subtitleStyle: args.subtitleStyle,
    glossaryCards,
    partLabel: isVerticalPlatform && release.partCount >= 1 ? `Pháº§n ${release.partIndex}/${release.partCount}` : null,
    parallaxFactors: args.parallaxFactors ?? [0.2, 0.55, 1],
    hookCard: null,
    endCard: null
  }
  return args.window ? applyWindow(props, args.window, args.mediaUrl(args.window.voiceRelPath, String(args.window.start))) : props
}

/**
 * GhÃ©p URL media cho renderer (Remotion Player) vÃ  cho Chrome headless khi render.
 * Má»i media Ä‘Æ°á»£c phá»¥c vá»¥ bá»Ÿi má»™t HTTP server ná»™i bá»™ (127.0.0.1) trong main process:
 *   <base>/<storyId>/<Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i Ä‘Ã£ encode tá»«ng Ä‘oáº¡n>?v=<phiÃªn báº£n>
 * Encode tá»«ng Ä‘oáº¡n Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n cÃ³ dáº¥u tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t / khoáº£ng tráº¯ng váº«n hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng.
 */
export function buildMediaUrl(baseUrl: string, storyId: string, relPath: string, version?: string): string {
  const encoded = relPath
    .split('/')
    .filter((s) => s.length > 0)
    .map((s) => encodeURIComponent(s))
    .join('/')
  const v = version ? `?v=${encodeURIComponent(version)}` : ''
  return `${baseUrl}/${encodeURIComponent(storyId)}/${encoded}${v}`
}

/** Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i vá» dáº¡ng "a/b/c" */
export function toRelPath(...parts: string[]): string {
  return parts
    .join('/')
    .replace(/\\/g, '/')
    .split('/')
    .filter((p) => p.length > 0 && p !== '.')
    .join('/')
}
=== FILE: D:\User\Tool\YTB\src\shared\migrate.ts ===
/**
 * NÃ¢ng cáº¥p dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u tá»« phiÃªn báº£n cÅ© (GÄ1) lÃªn data model hiá»‡n táº¡i â€“ cháº¡y má»—i láº§n Ä‘á»c file, idempotent.
 */
import type { CastMember, Character, CostLedger, Library, PublishingPackage, Release, SceneBankItem, SoundAsset, Story, ThumbnailConcept } from './types'

type Legacy<T> = Partial<T> & Record<string, unknown>

export function isLegacyLibrary(lib: Library): boolean {
  return lib.characters.some((c) => (c as Legacy<Character>).scope === undefined)
}

export function migrateCharacter(c: Legacy<Character>, fallbackStoryId: string | null): Character {
  const scope = c.scope ?? (c.role === 'narrator' ? 'channel' : 'story')
  return {
    id: String(c.id),
    name: String(c.name ?? ''),
    role: c.role ?? 'character',
    scope,
    storyId: scope === 'story' ? (c.storyId ?? fallbackStoryId) : null,
    age: c.age ?? null,
    gender: c.gender ?? 'unknown',
    appearance: c.appearance ?? String(c.description ?? ''),
    defaultOutfit: c.defaultOutfit ?? '',
    description: String(c.description ?? ''),
    referenceImages: c.referenceImages ?? [],
    characterSheet: c.characterSheet ?? { front: null, threeQuarter: null, profile: null, fullBody: null },
    expressionSheet: c.expressionSheet ?? [],
    states: c.states ?? [],
    voiceProfileIdByLanguage: c.voiceProfileIdByLanguage ?? {},
    locked: c.locked ?? false,
    approval: c.approval ?? { status: 'pending' }
  }
}

export function migrateSceneItem(s: Legacy<SceneBankItem>): SceneBankItem {
  const legacyIds = (s['characterIds'] as string[] | undefined) ?? []
  const cast: CastMember[] =
    s.cast ?? legacyIds.map((id) => ({ characterId: id, stateId: null, expression: String(s.expression ?? ''), position: 'center', action: '' }))
  const item = { ...s, cast, needsVerticalVersion: s.needsVerticalVersion ?? false } as SceneBankItem & { characterIds?: string[] }
  delete item.characterIds
  return item
}

/** GÄ5: gÃ³i xuáº¥t báº£n cÃ³ thÃªm thumbnail, Shorts; Ã½ tÆ°á»Ÿng thumbnail thÃ nh dáº¡ng cÃ³ cáº¥u trÃºc */
export function migrateRelease(r: Release): Release {
  const p = r.publishing as (PublishingPackage & Record<string, unknown>) | null
  if (p) {
    p.thumbnails ??= []
    p.selectedThumbnail ??= null
    p.generatedAt ??= new Date(0).toISOString()
    const fix = (arr: unknown[] | undefined): ThumbnailConcept[] =>
      (arr ?? []).map((x) => (typeof x === 'string' ? { text: x, visual: x } : (x as ThumbnailConcept)))
    if (p.youtube) p.youtube.thumbnailConcepts = fix(p.youtube.thumbnailConcepts as unknown[])
    if (p.tiktok) p.tiktok.coverConcepts = fix(p.tiktok.coverConcepts as unknown[])
  }
  return r
}

export function migrateStory(story: Story): Story {
  const s = story as Story & { costs?: CostLedger }
  s.costs ??= { estimated: 0, actual: 0, budgetLimit: story.budget?.limitUsd ?? 0, entries: [] }
  s.performanceNotes ??= []
  s.fictionNoteVi ??= ''
  s.sceneBank = (s.sceneBank ?? []).map((x) => migrateSceneItem(x as Legacy<SceneBankItem>))
  return s
}

export function migrateLibrary(lib: Library): Library {
  lib.characters = lib.characters.map((c) => migrateCharacter(c as Legacy<Character>, null))
  for (const ch of lib.channelPresets) {
    for (const pd of Object.values(ch.platformDefaults)) pd.narratorSections ??= []
    ch.motionProfile.parallaxRatio ??= 0.3
  }
  // GÄ4: SoundAsset cÃ³ thÃªm loáº¡i, thá»i lÆ°á»£ng, xÃ¡c nháº­n giáº¥y phÃ©p
  lib.soundAssets = (lib.soundAssets ?? []).map((a) => {
    const x = a as Legacy<SoundAsset>
    return {
      id: String(x.id),
      path: String(x.path ?? ''),
      name: x.name ?? String(x.path ?? '').split(/[\\/]/).pop() ?? 'sound',
      kind: x.kind ?? 'sfx',
      tags: x.tags ?? [],
      durationSec: x.durationSec ?? 0,
      license: x.license ?? '',
      licenseConfirmed: x.licenseConfirmed ?? false,
      source: x.source ?? ''
    }
  })
  return lib
}

/**

 * BÆ°á»›c 1â€“5 (táº¡o Story, LLM dÃ n Ã½, duyá»‡t dÃ n Ã½, Ä‘á» xuáº¥t cáº£nh, Æ°á»›c tÃ­nh chi phÃ­) Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ sáºµn trong dá»¯ liá»‡u máº«u.

import type { PipelineState, RunMode, StepState } from './types'

export const PIPELINE_TOTAL_STEPS = 23

export interface StepDef {
  no: number
  key: StepKey
  title: string
  /** â˜… Ä‘iá»ƒm duyá»‡t â€“ dá»«ng láº¡i chá» ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng báº¥m "Tiáº¿p tá»¥c" (bá» qua khi Full Auto) */
  approval: boolean
  /** Ghi chÃº cho GÄ1 (vd: bÆ°á»›c nÃ y dÃ¹ng dá»¯ liá»‡u máº«u / sáº½ lÃ m á»Ÿ GÄ nÃ o) */
  phaseNote?: string
}

export const RELEASE_PIPELINE: StepDef[] = [

export function getStepDef(no: number): StepDef | undefined {
  return RELEASE_PIPELINE.find((s) => s.no === no)
}

export function createInitialPipelineState(mode: RunMode): PipelineState {
  const steps: Record<number, StepState> = {}
  for (const def of RELEASE_PIPELINE) steps[def.no] = { status: 'pending', progress: 0 }
  return { mode, running: false, currentStep: null, steps, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() }
}

/**
 * ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u cÃ¡c bÆ°á»›c tá»« stepNo trá»Ÿ Ä‘i vá» "Chá»" (sau khi sá»­a ká»‹ch báº£n/giá»ng).
 * Tráº£ vá» true náº¿u cÃ³ thay Ä‘á»•i. Media Ä‘Ã£ táº¡o váº«n cÃ²n trong cache â‡’ cháº¡y láº¡i chá»‰ táº¡o pháº§n thay Ä‘á»•i.
 */
export function resetStepsFrom(state: PipelineState | null, stepNo: number): boolean {
  if (!state) return false
  let changed = false
  for (const def of RELEASE_PIPELINE) {
    const st = state.steps[def.no]
    if (def.no >= stepNo && st && st.status !== 'pending') {
      state.steps[def.no] = { status: 'pending', progress: 0 }
      changed = true
    }
  }
  if (changed && state.currentStep != null && state.currentStep >= stepNo) state.currentStep = null
  // Quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh duyá»‡t cÃ³ Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n cá»§a cÃ¡c bÆ°á»›c bá»‹ Ä‘áº·t láº¡i cÅ©ng háº¿t hiá»‡u lá»±c
  if (state.approvals) for (const k of Object.keys(state.approvals)) if (Number(k) >= stepNo) delete state.approvals[Number(k)]
  return changed
}

export function isStepFinished(state: StepState | undefined): boolean {
  return !!state && (state.status === 'done' || state.status === 'skipped')
}

export function isPipelineComplete(state: PipelineState | null): boolean {
  if (!state) return false
  return RELEASE_PIPELINE.every((d) => isStepFinished(state.steps[d.no]))
}

/** Tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™ tá»•ng 0..1 (dÃ¹ng cho danh sÃ¡ch Release á»Ÿ Trang chá»§) */
export function pipelineProgress(state: PipelineState | null): number {
  if (!state) return 0
  let sum = 0
  for (const d of RELEASE_PIPELINE) {
    const st = state.steps[d.no]
    if (isStepFinished(st)) sum += 1
    else if (st?.status === 'running') sum += st.progress
  }
  return sum / RELEASE_PIPELINE.length
}

export function stepStatusLabel(status: StepState['status']): string {
  switch (status) {
    case 'pending':
      return 'Chá»'
    case 'running':
      return 'Äang cháº¡y'
    case 'done':
      return 'Xong'
    case 'skipped':
      return 'Bá» qua'
    case 'waitingApproval':
      return 'Chá» duyá»‡t'
    case 'failed':
      return 'Lá»—i'
    case 'cancelled':
      return 'ÄÃ£ huá»·'
  }
}
