const defaultChain: VoiceProcessChain = { highpassHz: 80, compressor: { thresholdDb: -18, ratio: 3 }, warmthDb: 2, reverbAmount: 0.12 }
const ghostChain: VoiceProcessChain = { highpassHz: 180, compressor: { thresholdDb: -22, ratio: 4 }, warmthDb: -2, reverbAmount: 0.4 }

function voice(
  id: string,
  name: string,
  language: 'en' | 'vi',
  providerVoiceId: string,
  baseInstructions: string,
  toneHz: number,
  chain: VoiceProcessChain = defaultChain,
  defaultTags: string[] = []
): VoiceProfile {
  return {
    id,
    name,
    language,
    providerId: 'mock',
    providerVoiceId,
    baseInstructions,
    defaultTags,
    speed: 1,
    // toneHz chá»‰ dÃ¹ng cho Mock Ä‘á»ƒ nghe phÃ¢n biá»‡t giá»ng cÃ¡c nhÃ¢n váº­t
    providerSettings: { toneHz },
    postProcessChain: chain,
    commercialLicenseConfirmed: false
  }
}

function cast(
  characterId: string,
  expression = '',
  position: CastMember['position'] = 'center',
  action = '',
  stateId: string | null = null
): CastMember {
  return { characterId, stateId, expression, position, action }
}

function scene(
  n: number,
  beatId: string,
  shotType: ShotType,
  cameraAngle: string,
  description: string,
  imagePrompt: string,
  focus: [number, number],
  castList: CastMember[] = [],
  needsVerticalVersion = false
): SceneBankItem {
  return {
    id: `s${String(n).padStart(2, '0')}`,
    beatId,
    description,
    shotType,
    cameraAngle,
    cast: castList,
    folkloreIds: castList.some((c) => c.characterId === 'char-ma-da') ? ['folk-ma-da'] : [],
    expression: castList[0]?.expression ?? '',
    imagePrompt,
    needsVerticalVersion,
    media: { '16:9': null, '9:16': null },
    focusPoint: { x: focus[0], y: focus[1] },
    depthMapPath: null,
    heroClip: { '16:9': null, '9:16': null },
    variants: [],
    approval: { status: 'pending' }
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Ká»‹ch báº£n
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function delivery(tags: string[], instructions: string, pace = 1, pauseBeforeMs = 250, pauseAfterMs = 450): Delivery {
  return { tags, instructions, pace, pauseBeforeMs, pauseAfterMs }
}

interface RawLine {
  section: string
  beat: string
  text: string
  d: Delivery
  vi?: string
  speaker?: string
}

function buildLines(prefix: string, raw: RawLine[]): ScriptLine[] {
  return raw.map((r, i) => {
    const l: ScriptLine = {
      id: `${prefix}-l${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
      sectionId: r.section,
      beatId: r.beat,
      speakerId: r.speaker ?? CHANNEL_NARRATOR_ID,
      text: r.text,
      delivery: r.d,
      takes: [],
      selectedTakeId: null,
      start: null,
      end: null,
      glossaryTermIds: [],
      approval: { status: 'pending' }
    }
    if (r.vi !== undefined) l.translationVi = r.vi
    return l
  })
}

  | 'pending'

// ============================================================================
// JOB (hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i dÃ¹ng chung TTS / áº£nh / video)
// ============================================================================

export type JobType = 'tts' | 'image' | 'video' | 'llm' | 'stt' | 'render' | 'depthMap'
export type JobStatus = 'queued' | 'running' | 'done' | 'failed' | 'cancelled'

export interface Job<P = unknown, R = unknown> {
  id: string
  type: JobType
  payload: P
  status: JobStatus
  attempts: number
  error: string | null
  result: R | null
  estimatedCost: number
  /** Provider dÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ giá»›i háº¡n sá»‘ luá»“ng riÃªng (gemini, elevenlabs, mockâ€¦) */
  providerId: string
  storyId: string | null
  /** hash(input + model + params + reference) â€“ khÃ´ng táº¡o trÃ¹ng */
  cacheKey: string | null
  /** MÃ´ táº£ ngáº¯n hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trong Film Studio */
  label: string
  /** Sá»‘ thá»© tá»± hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trÃªn tháº» */
  seq: number
  progress: number
  actualCost: number
  /** Thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm sá»›m nháº¥t Ä‘Æ°á»£c cháº¡y láº¡i (backoff sau lá»—i) */
  nextRunAt: number
  createdAt: string
  startedAt: string | null
  finishedAt: string | null
}

/** Káº¿t quáº£ kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»“ng nháº¥t nhÃ¢n váº­t báº±ng LLM nhÃ¬n áº£nh (so áº£nh cáº£nh vá»›i character sheet) */
export interface ConsistencyCheck {
  checkedAt: string
  ok: boolean
  /** VÃ­ dá»¥: "Ãšt Lan: sai mÃ u Ã¡o (pháº£i lÃ  Ã¡o bÃ  ba xanh)" */
  issues: string[]
  characterIds: string[]
  provider: string
}
